Our code and dataset are available at https://pku-value-lab.github.io/EmbodiedCLBench-homepage.

</details>


### [35] [Travel Back To Move Forward: Retrospective Exploration and Evolving for Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13058)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Lei Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Yu Wang,Zhiyuan Yao,Zihan Wang,Xierui Song,Fuli Feng,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为ReTRAIL的框架，用于解决大型语言模型（LLM）代理在长时序环境中遇到的早期错误导致级联失败的问题。ReTRAIL允许代理回溯环境状态，同时保留有效进展和从废弃探索中学习到的知识，从而实现从无效状态中恢复并指导后续决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时序环境中，LLM代理容易因早期错误而陷入死胡同。需要一种机制让代理能够主动从这些不利状态中恢复，并将探索经验转化为未来决策的指导。

Method: ReTRAIL框架结合了环境状态回溯和知识前移。它训练一个共享策略，利用两种互补信号：1) State-Anchored GRPO（一种强化学习算法）用于比较从回溯状态开始的不同后续路径，以改进执行；2) 分支进展奖励用于引导代理通过后续探索的进展进行反思。

Result: 在MineSweeper、Sokoban、ALFWorld和ScienceWorld四个交互式基准测试中，实验表明：简单的回溯原始方法效果有限，甚至可能损害性能。然而，ReTRAIL框架能够使代理从低效状态回溯，并利用从废弃分支中获得的见解来指导后续探索，显著提升了任务解决能力。

Conclusion: 将环境回溯与经验重用相结合，对于LLM代理解决长时序任务至关重要。ReTRAIL框架通过在任务进行中耦合回溯和经验利用，有效地提升了代理在复杂长时序环境中的表现。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in long-horizon environments where early mistakes can trigger cascading failures and trap agents in dead-end trajectories. Agents must therefore be able to proactively recover from such states and turn their exploratory experience into guidance for subsequent decisions. We introduce ReTRAIL, a framework that enables agents to rewind the environment to earlier states while preserving valid progress and carrying forward knowledge learned from abandoned exploration. The environment state moves backward while the agent's knowledge continues to move forward. ReTRAIL trains a shared policy with two complementary signals: State-Anchored GRPO compares alternative continuations from revisited states to improve execution, while a branch-advancement reward guides reflection through the progress of subsequent exploration. Experiments across four interactive benchmark MineSweeper, Sokoban, ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that simply adding a rewind primitive provides limited benefit and can even degrade performance. In contrast, ReTRAIL enables agents to rewind from unproductive states and use insights from abandoned branches to guide subsequent exploration. These results demonstrate the value of coupling environment rewinding with experience reuse within an ongoing task for long-horizon task solving. The code is available at https://github.com/whi497/ReTRAIL.

</details>


### [36] [LongHarness: Advancing Agentic Reasoning over 100M-Token Corpora via Stateful Evidence Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13075)
*Songtao Huang,Zhijie Zhong,Xiaohan He,Shuaiyu Zhang,Zhiyin Yu,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Yutao Fan,Xiangchao Yan,Bo Zhang,Shiyang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LongHarness的代理框架，通过显式管理证据和结论的演变，解决了长上下文推理中的探索和证据漂移问题，并在多个基准测试中显著提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文推理代理存在探索无界和证据-结论漂移的问题，即代理在检索信息时容易陷入无效循环，且新获得的证据或纠正无法有效更新现有结论。

Method: LongHarness通过“证据-结论协同演进”机制，利用未解决的结论指导探索，新证据则更新结论；并结合“证据生命周期管理”，确保证据的时效性和可追溯性。该框架模型无关，可与不同基础模型结合。

Result: 在OfficeQA Pro V1/V2、AA-LCR和LongBench v2等基准测试中，使用GLM-5.3-Flash时，LongHarness在OfficeQA Pro上的精确匹配率从15.8%提升至60.2%（V1）和4.4%提升至61.1%（V2）。与最先进的代理框架相比，它在V1上领先Claude Code 12.0个百分点，在V2上领先Codex 18.9个百分点。使用Qwen3.8-Flash时，LongHarness在V1上达到61.7%，领先Claude Code 8.3个百分点，领先Codex 12.0个百分点。

Conclusion: LongHarness通过显式管理证据和结论的关系，有效解决了长上下文推理中的挑战，显著提升了代理的性能，为未来的长上下文推理代理研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Corpus-level long-context reasoning requires agents to acquire and integrate evidence across document collections that exceed their context windows. Existing agent harnesses enable incremental access to these corpora, but acquired evidence and evolving conclusions often remain entangled in a growing interaction history. This implicit state management can lead to two coupled failure modes: unbounded exploration, where retrieval expands the interaction history without resolving decision-relevant information gaps, and evidence-state drift, where previously acquired evidence or subsequent corrections fail to inform the current conclusion. We present LongHarness, a model-agnostic harness that makes the interaction between acquired evidence and evolving conclusions explicit. Its core mechanism, Evidence-Conclusion Coevolution, uses unresolved conclusions to guide exploration toward missing information, while newly acquired evidence strengthens or revises the conclusions that directed the search. LongHarness couples this process with Evidence Lifecycle Management, which keeps accumulated evidence current and traceable throughout the interaction. Together, these mechanisms focus retrieval on remaining information needs and keep conclusions aligned with current evidence. We evaluate LongHarness on OfficeQA Pro V1 and V2, AA-LCR, and LongBench v2 using GLM-5.3-Flash and Qwen3.8-Flash. Compared with direct reasoning using the same backbone, LongHarness improves GLM-5.3-Flash exact match on OfficeQA Pro from 15.8% to 60.2% on V1 and from 4.4% to 61.1% on V2. Against state-of-the-art agent harnesses, it surpasses Claude Code by 12.0 percentage points on V1 and Codex by 18.9 points on V2. With Qwen3.8-Flash, LongHarness reaches 61.7% on V1, outperforming Claude Code by 8.3 points and Codex by 12.0 points.

</details>


### [37] [SWEAR: Section-Wise Efficient Audio Reasoning via Self-Rated Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13081)
*Yixuan Li,Minghao Lv,Xuenan Xu,Zihao Zheng,Mengyue Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SWEAR的新的音频-语言模型（LALM）推理框架，通过多维度的难度评估和强化学习，实现了在提高准确性的同时显著减少模型生成的token数量，并且针对不同难度的样本进行了自适应的推理优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LALM在平衡准确性和效率方面，其难度评估过于粗糙，忽略了影响推理复杂性的多种因素。作者希望通过更精细的难度评估来改进这一状况。

Method: 该研究从离线评估中提取了五维度的难度分数和压缩的推理轨迹。在此基础上，提出了SWEAR框架，该框架在监督微调过程中学习难度信号，并使用强化学习在推理的不同阶段动态调整推理分配。

Result: 在MMAU-test-mini和MMAR数据集上的实验表明，SWEAR在提高模型准确率的同时，显著减少了生成的token数量。对于简单样本，它缩短了推理时间，而对困难样本的性能没有造成牺牲。

Conclusion: SWEAR是一个有效的难度感知推理框架，能够通过更精细的难度评估和自适应的推理策略，在效率和准确性之间取得更好的平衡，特别是能够有效降低计算成本。

Abstract: Recent work on large audio-language models (LALMs) explores adaptive reasoning to balance accuracy and efficiency. However, existing approaches often rely on coarse-grained difficulty estimation, overlooking the diverse factors that determine reasoning effort. We address this limitation by deriving five-dimensional difficulty scores and compressed reasoning traces from complementary offline assessments. Based on this characterization, we propose SWEAR, a difficulty-aware reasoning framework that learns difficulty signals during supervised fine-tuning and uses reinforcement learning to adapt reasoning allocation across reasoning stages. Experiments on MMAU-test-mini and MMAR show that SWEAR improves accuracy while substantially reducing generated tokens, shortening reasoning for easy instances without sacrificing performance on difficult ones.

</details>


### [38] [Attention Sees, MLP Refuses: Tracing the Attention-to-MLP Safety Pathway in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13078)
*Zixuan Weng,Haoqi He,Weifei Jin,Yue Huang,Yang Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员通过分析Transformer模型中的残差流更新，区分了有害信息识别和拒绝性回答生成，发现注意力机制主要负责识别有害性（尤其是在早期层），而MLP模块则更侧重于生成选择性的拒绝回答。后训练增强了MLP的拒绝生成能力，但并未系统性提高有害性识别能力。引入了一种结合注意力感知得分P和MLP决策得分D的Sentinel方法，用于检测越狱和过度拒绝，并在多个模型上取得了较低的错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和改进安全对齐的大语言模型（LLMs）在面对越狱攻击和过度拒绝良性请求时的失败机制，需要区分模型识别有害信息的能力和生成拒绝性回答的能力。

Method: 该研究将问题重构为“通路”问题，并在Transformer的残差流更新上进行量化。通过区分模块更新暴露的有害性信息和其产生的可读拒绝证据变化，来分析注意力（attention）和MLP模块的作用。研究使用了18个基础-Instruct模型对（Qwen、Llama、Gemma）以及6个支持推理的模型。此外，还进行了因果干预实验，并通过引入Sentinel模型（结合注意力感知得分P和MLP决策得分D）来评估越狱和过度拒绝。

Result: 有害性信息在注意力更新中更容易被解码，尤其是在早期层。MLP更新在对齐模型中对有害性选择性拒绝的生成贡献更大。后训练加强了所有18个模型对的MLP拒绝生成能力，但并未系统性提高有害性解码能力，且正向MLP生成在后训练后更集中在深层。在支持推理的模型中，有害性在推理过程中仍可解码，而强烈的拒绝生成集中在回答的开始。因果干预表明，将下游MLP更新重置为干净值可以显著减少因上游注意力干预引起的拒绝变化。Sentinel方法通过联合使用P和D信号，比单一信号路由和固定的提示词，在八个模型上实现了更低的总体错误率。

Conclusion: 注意力机制和MLP模块在大语言模型安全对齐中扮演着不同的角色，前者侧重于有害性识别，后者侧重于拒绝性回答的生成。后训练会影响这些模块的行为，但可能以牺牲识别有害性为代价来增强拒绝能力。Sentinel方法通过结合感知和决策信号，为理解和缓解LLMs的越狱和过度拒绝问题提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreaks and can over-refuse benign requests. Understanding these failures requires distinguishing harmfulness recognition from the computation that writes refusal. We reframe this as a pathway question and make it measurable on residual-stream updates in decoder-only Transformers, separating the harmfulness information a module update exposes from the change it produces in readable refusal evidence. Across 18 Base-Instruct pairs spanning Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, harmfulness is decoded more readily from attention updates, particularly in early layers, whereas MLP updates contribute more strongly to harm-selective refusal writing in aligned models. Post-training strengthens MLP refusal writing in all 18 pairs without systematically improving harmfulness decodability, while positive MLP writing becomes more concentrated in deeper layers after post-training. Across six reasoning-enabled models, harmfulness remains decodable during reasoning while strong refusal writing concentrates at answer onset, a pattern that persists with reasoning disabled. Causal interventions further support this pathway: resetting downstream MLP updates to their clean values substantially reduces the refusal shift caused by upstream attention interventions. Building on these findings, we introduce Sentinel, which combines an attention-derived perception score P with an MLP-derived decision score D. Their joint profiles characterize jailbreaks and over-refusals, and conditional routing with both signals achieves a lower aggregate error rate than single-signal routing and evaluated fixed reminders across eight models.

</details>


### [39] [When Faster Decisions Do Not Make Better Agents: An Empirical Study of Jev in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13092)
*Yuchen Ling,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员评估了将Jev（一种可加速LLM决策的技术）集成到AI代理中，以了解其对任务效率和可靠性的影响。结果显示，Jev降低了决策成本和延迟，但对整体任务成功率的影响因应用场景和代理工作流程而异，有时甚至会因额外的交互或失败的恢复机制而抵消成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Jev技术能够降低LLM决策的成本和延迟，但尚不清楚这种局部优化是否能转化为AI代理整体任务的效率和可靠性。因此，研究旨在调查Jev在实际代理任务执行中的效率转移效应。

Method: 研究人员在ALFWorld（具身任务）和AndroidWorld（移动应用使用）两个场景中，对五个AI代理进行了共计4500次任务执行的评估。评估指标包括任务有效性、决策级和任务级资源使用以及执行行为。

Result: Jev的集成显著降低了所有代理的决策成本和延迟。然而，任务级别的收益因工作流程而异。在ALFWorld中，任务成功率接近基线水平，但在AndroidWorld中的两个移动代理任务成功率下降更为明显。辅助推理和额外的交互抵消了部分决策成本节约，而恢复失败和过早完成限制了任务的成功交付。甚至有一个代理在每次尝试的支出减少的情况下，成功完成任务的平均成本却增加了。

Conclusion: 将Jev等高效决策模型集成到AI代理工作流程中时，必须重视维护进度、支持恢复机制以及验证任务完成情况。评估Jev等技术的实际价值应侧重于成功的任务交付、恢复行为以及每成功交付结果的资源消耗，而不仅仅是决策层面的成本节约。

Abstract: AI agents depend on repeated decisions to accomplish tasks, with large language models (LLMs) commonly serving as their core decision makers. The recent emergence of Jev offers an opportunity to make these decisions faster and at lower cost. However, it remains unclear whether such local savings can be translated into efficient and reliable task completion. In this paper, we investigate this efficiency transfer across five agents in two representative application settings, embodied tasks in ALFWorld and mobile app use in AndroidWorld. Across 4,500 task executions, we jointly evaluate task effectiveness, decision-level and task-level resource use, and execution behavior. Experimental results show that Jev integration substantially reduces decision cost and latency across all five agents, with task-level gains varying by workflow. Task success remains close to baseline levels on ALFWorld and declines more substantially in two mobile agents. Auxiliary reasoning and additional interactions absorb decision savings, while failed recovery and premature completion limit successful delivery. Mean cost per successful outcome increases in one agent even as spending per attempt falls. These findings highlight the importance of preserving progress, supporting recovery, and verifying completion when integrating efficient decision models into agent workflows. Their practical value should be assessed through successful task delivery, recovery behavior, and resources per delivered outcome.

</details>


### [40] [VETR: Execution-Grounded Self-Improving Agent for Symbolic Graphics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13098)
*Yingbo Ma,Kuangshi Ai,Gordon Dai,Chenglong Yu,Chaoli Wang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VETR的框架，用于改进依赖可执行世界表征进行符号图形推理的视觉代理。VETR通过追踪和验证程序执行轨迹中的中间状态来识别和修正导致失败的操作，从而实现了代理的递归自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉代理在符号图形推理方面，虽然可以通过反复交互来优化场景，但缺乏从执行轨迹中提取可操作证据的机制，无法判断哪些符号编辑在程序执行过程中得以保留、消失或产生干扰。这限制了代理的进一步改进。

Method: VETR框架通过在每次任务中保留对齐的程序/渲染记录和运行时观察，并引入一个执行精炼器。该精炼器将观察到的写入和状态变化编译成约束性替代程序，并验证其终点和保留条件。一个普通的视觉验证器则评估目标相关性。修复构建不依赖额外的语言模型调用，但可能需要额外的执行。

Result: 在BlenderGym（280个任务）和BlenderBench（27个任务）上的评估表明，VETR在使用GPT-5.6-luna和Qwen3.8-27B时，其性能持续优于现有的精炼策略，包括BlenderAlchemy和VIGA。对照消融实验评估了固定生成器交互限制下，执行精炼和角色非对称证据组织的作用。

Conclusion: VETR框架通过利用程序执行轨迹中的验证信息，有效地实现了对可执行世界表征的递归自我改进，从而在符号图形推理任务中提高了视觉代理的性能。

Abstract: Visual agents for symbolic graphics reasoning depend on executable world representations, yet continued improvement remains bottlenecked by ambiguous supervision from final renders. Recent agentic systems refine scenes through repeated interaction, but they lack mechanisms to recursively extract actionable evidence from execution traces, overlooking which proposed symbolic edits survived, disappeared, or interfered during program execution. To address this, we propose VETR (Verified Execution Trajectories for Refinement), a framework that enables agentic recursive self-improvement over executable world representations for symbolic graphics reasoning. Within each task, VETR retains aligned program/render records with bounded runtime observations and introduces an execution refiner that compiles observed writes and state changes into constrained alternative programs. The refiner validates their endpoints and preservation conditions against captured runtime state; the ordinary visual verifier then assesses goal relevance. Repair construction makes no additional language-model calls, but can require additional executions. Evaluations on BlenderGym (280 tasks) and BlenderBench (27 released tasks) with GPT-5.6-luna and Qwen3.8-27B show that VETR consistently outperforms existing refinement strategies, including BlenderAlchemy and VIGA. Controlled ablations evaluate the contributions of execution refinement and role-asymmetric evidence organization under fixed generator-interaction caps.

</details>


### [41] [Does Location Matter? A Black-Box Causal Audit Reveals Model-Dependent Audio Grounding in LALM Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13112)
*Mohammad Khalooei,Mohammad Sabokrou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的因果方法来评估和减轻大型音频语言模型（LALM）中的对象幻觉问题。通过隔离和移除声学证据，并结合对比解码技术，研究表明该方法能有效提高模型准确性，并揭示了不同模型之间存在不同的因果接地机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型（LALMs）存在对象幻觉（识别不存在的声音）的问题，现有的缓解方法（如音频感知解码 AAD）存在局限性。作者希望找到一种更直接、更具因果性的方法来诊断和解决这个问题。

Method: 研究引入了一种黑盒因果测试方法，通过 CLAP 相似度定位声学证据区域，然后通过频谱门控移除该区域（或使用随机窗口作为对照），并与全零“空白”波形进行对比。此外，还提出了一种对比解码方法，结合空白和语言先验对比，并通过门控和集成来提高模型准确性。

Result: 在 Qwen2-Audio-7B 模型上，局部消融的原始 AUROC 低于空白消融，但固定时长后，局部消融优于随机移除。对比解码方法将 Qwen2-Audio 的准确率从 57.1% 提高到 71.9%，优于单独的 AAD。不同模型之间的因果接地机制存在差异，无法通用。

Conclusion: 提出的因果测试方法能够有效地隔离和评估 LALMs 中的声学证据。结合对比解码的方法可以显著提高模型的准确性，克服对象幻觉问题。研究还发现，不同 LALMs 具有不同的因果接地机制，需要针对特定模型进行调整。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) frequently exhibit object hallucination, affirming absent sounds. The dominant mitigation, Audio-Aware Decoding (AAD), contrasts logits under real audio against an all-zero "blank" waveform, flagged by prior review as out-of-distribution. We introduce a black-box causal test that isolates the claimed evidence itself: localize a claim's evidence region via CLAP similarity, remove it by spectral gating, and compare against a random-window control across a 2x2x2 grid on three LALMs. On Qwen2-Audio-7B, localized ablation's raw AUROC trails blank's (0.613 vs. 0.721); this is a duration confound, not a localization failure, since blank removes the entire clip versus the grid's 2-4 s. Holding duration fixed, localization beats random removal at every cell on both models. Raw detection value remains model-dependent, especially on Audio Flamingo 3. The same evidence corrects decoding: contrastive decoding combining blank and language-prior contrasts, gated and ensembled, raises Qwen2-Audio accuracy from 57.1% to 71.9%, beating AAD alone. A unified causal-grounding profile does not transfer between models, indicating different grounding regimes. The source code and a detailed report of our findings, including identified limitations, are available at https://github.com/khalooei/causa.

</details>


### [42] [DRYLAB-Bench: Benchmarking the Biosecurity Risk of Agentic LLM Mutation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13116)
*Yuzhen Wang,Chunxiao Li,Baichen Le,Lewen Yan,Jie Zhang,Yucheng T. Yang,Xing-Ming Zhao,Lijun Li,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了DRYLAB-Bench，一个首次用于评估大型语言模型（LLM）在生物设计中的高风险变异设计能力的基准测试。研究发现，即使没有工具，LLM也能生成高风险的设计，而集成生物设计工具（BDTs）会显著放大这种风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生物设计方面展现出巨大潜力，但同时也带来了工程增强病原体等双重使用风险。现有评估未能充分量化LLM执行危险生物学修改的实际风险，特别是当它们与生物设计工具（BDTs）结合时。

Method: 研究引入了DRYLAB-Bench基准测试，包含30个源自生物安全指南的危险变异设计场景。该基准在一个多轮、工具增强的环境中评估LLM代理，LLM会协调BDTs迭代优化设计。使用Biosecurity Risk Score (BRS)量化风险，并利用ProteinGym和ViroGym的实测数据进行验证。

Result: 对15个前沿LLM模型的评估显示，有67.6%的单次尝试设计超过了平均水平，21.4%达到了最高风险十分位数。当模型配备BDTs后，风险显著增加，平均BRS提升了0.116，最高风险十分位数的比例上升到23.5%。

Conclusion: LLM在生物设计领域存在显著的基线威胁，并且使用BDTs会进一步放大这些风险。DRYLAB-Bench提供了一种安全且可扩展的方法来量化和评估这种高风险的生物设计能力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) acquire advanced biological design capabilities, they offer substantial research benefits but simultaneously introduce severe dual-use risks, such as engineering enhanced pathogens or drug-resistant bacteria. While biosecurity frameworks recognize these threats, the actual risk of LLMs executing dangerous biological modifications remains unquantified, particularly when augmented with specialized biological design tools (BDTs). Existing evaluations primarily assess hazardous knowledge possession rather than practical application, overlook risk amplification from BDTs, and rely on unreliable, expensive, or hazardous validation methods. To address these gaps, we introduce DRYLAB-Bench (Design Risk Yielded by LLM Agent in Biodesign), the first comprehensive benchmark evaluating the high-risk biological mutation design capabilities of LLM agents. Moving beyond basic question-answering, DRYLAB-Bench tasks agents with 30 genuinely hazardous mutation-design scenarios distilled from biosecurity guidelines. It evaluates models in a multi-round, tool-augmented environment where LLMs orchestrate BDTs to iteratively refine candidates. Crucially, it scores designs directly against 187,695 empirical wet-lab measurements from ProteinGym and ViroGym using our proposed Biosecurity Risk Score (BRS), quantifying risk-associated effects while enabling scalable and safe ground-truth verification. Evaluations of 15 frontier models reveal substantial baseline threats: without tools, 67.6% of one-shot designs exceed the average measured variant, and 21.4% reach the highest-risk decile. Equipping models with BDTs significantly amplifies this risk, raising the pooled risk by \(ΔBRS=+0.116\) and the top-decile share to 23.5%. Code: https://github.com/drylab-bio/DRYLAB-Bench

</details>


### [43] [Locate, Judge, Read: Role-Specialized Tool Agents over a Knowledge-Point Index for Open-Domain Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13128)
*Wenlin Lin,Fanzhe Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowForge 是一个新颖的检索增强问答系统，它将任务分解为独立的阶段（检索、阅读），通过多项创新技术（知识点索引、独立代理、桥接判断、证据池、格式检查器）提高了在大规模异构文档集合上的问答性能，并在 M3DocVQA、MultiHop-RAG 和 MMLongBench-Doc 等基准测试中取得了显著的 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强代理将检索和阅读任务混淆，导致效率低下。研究旨在解决大规模异构文档集合上的问答难题，提高检索和阅读的协同效率。

Method: KnowForge 采用流水线式设计，将任务分解为独立的组件：1. 混合检索知识点索引；2. 仅能搜索的 Locator 代理；3. 强制进行二次检索的桥接判断；4. 类型化、大小受限的证据池；5. 仅能阅读文本、图像和表格的 Deep Reader 代理；6. 具备格式检查器的合成器。

Result: KnowForge 在 M3DocVQA 上达到 F1 47.5 (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B) 和 53.5 (DeepSeek-V4-Flash)，显著优于现有系统。在 MultiHop-RAG 上达到 73.5 准确率，优于 RAG 和 GraphRAG。在 MMLongBench-Doc 上将同一模型的性能从 34.8 提升至 47.0，与 GPT-4o 相当。消融实验证明了各组件的有效性，特别是 Deep Reader 的重要性。

Conclusion: KnowForge 通过将检索和阅读任务解耦并引入专门的代理和机制，有效提升了在大规模异构文档集合上的问答能力。其模块化设计和 SOTA 性能表明了其在未来大规模文档问答系统中的潜力。

Abstract: Question answering over large heterogeneous document collections requires first finding the right passages and then reading them, yet current retrieval-augmented agents give one model both kinds of tools and let it schedule itself. We present KnowForge, a pipeline separated by job: a hybrid-retrieval knowledge-point index; a Locator agent that can only search; a code-scheduled bridge judge that forces the missing second hop; a typed, size-bounded evidence pool that merges every search's ranking; a Deep Reader agent that can only read text, images and tables; and a synthesizer with a format checker. With a self-hosted Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (3B active), KnowForge reaches F1 47.5 on the full open-domain M3DocVQA (2,441 questions, 3,368 PDFs), +11.0 over the published M3DocRAG system and +7.1 over one-shot retrieve-and-answer on the same index and model, and 53.5 with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On MultiHop-RAG it reaches 73.5 accuracy, above the strongest published RAG and GraphRAG results (71.2); on the single-document MMLongBench-Doc it lifts the same model from 34.8 to 47.0, level with GPT-4o reading page images (46.3). Per-job ablations on the full benchmarks attribute the gain: the Deep Reader is worth 3.8, 9.8 and 0 points on the three corpora, tracking how visual each is; merging both agents into one with all tools and twice the rounds loses 1.9 F1 because it keeps searching instead of reading; a round-budget sweep shows a step at four Locator rounds and a plateau beyond; query latency scales with model calls rather than corpus size. We serve the three indexed benchmarks as read-only knowledge bases (free registration) and release all prompts and the runner code: https://github.com/metask-ai/knowforge-bench

</details>


### [44] [Looking Ahead and Back: Anticipatory Generative Modeling for Road-Network Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13131)
*Zhenhao Huang,Li Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Foresight的新型生成模型，通过引入“预见-选择-更新-再预见”的循环机制，解决了现有轨迹生成模型在交叉路口处选择下一个出口路径的难题，并能生成更符合实际道路网络的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹生成方法（如扩散模型）主要模拟车辆位置序列，在处理交叉路口的路径选择问题上存在不足，可能生成偏离道路网络的轨迹。因此，需要一种能够显式建模交叉路口处行为并实现长远预见的生成方法。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于生成式路径规划的图约束生成范式——“预期生成模型”（Anticipatory Generative Modeling）。具体实现上，构建了一个名为Foresight的概率模型，该模型通过一个“预见-选择-更新-再预见”的循环过程，在连续生成历史轨迹的同时，实现对未来走向目的地的路径的长期预判。

Result: 在大量实验中，Foresight模型在轨迹生成任务上取得了优于现有最先进基线模型的性能。

Conclusion: Foresight模型通过将轨迹生成从单纯的位置模拟转向行为建模，并引入长期路径预判机制，有效解决了车辆在道路网络中交叉路口的路径选择问题，生成了更具现实意义的轨迹。

Abstract: Trajectory generation underpins urban applications such as traffic management and urban planning. Within road networks, vehicle movement along road segments is largely deterministic, whereas transitions at intersections are critical--multiple outgoing paths may appear equally plausible, yet they can lead to substantially different downstream routes. Existing generative methods such as diffusion models simulate location sequences and fail short of next transition selection, even producing trajectories that drift off the road network. To fill this gap, we advocate a fundamental shift: moving beyond location simulation toward behavior modeling on the road network. In this paper, we revisit trajectory generation through the lens of generative route planning, and introduce a new graph-constrained generative paradigm, Anticipatory Generative Modeling, which resolves next transition selection by incorporating the Anticipation of how the future route may unfold toward the destination. Concretely, we propose a novel generative model named Foresight that formulates a probabilistic model for an "anticipate--select--update--re-anticipate" loop that enables longer-term foresight while continuing sequential generation along the historical trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Foresight wins state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [45] [DP-MIND: Differentially private memory-guided Long context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13132)
*Zhihao Liu,Yitian Cheng,Shuang Liu,Rui Wen,Yuke Hu,Zhan Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DP-MIND 是一种内存驱动的框架，通过分块处理和强化学习优化的内存来为百万 token 规模的输入实现差分隐私（DP）的推理，并在不暴露原始上下文的情况下进行局部去噪，以提高隐私和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私（DP）推理方法难以处理长上下文（百万 token 级别）输入，存在上下文容量有限和隐私扰动导致效用严重下降的问题，而 LLM-as-a-Service 的普及使得用户查询的隐私保护尤为重要。

Method: DP-MIND 采用内存驱动的框架，将输入分块处理，通过一个紧凑的内存保存任务相关信息。通过强化学习优化内存构建策略，以平衡最终答案的正确性和内存的一致性。此外，还引入了一个局部去噪模块来恢复被扰动破坏的答案片段。

Result: DP-MIND 能够实现百万 token 级别的长上下文差分隐私推理，并在多项长上下文基准测试中，在可扩展性和实用性方面持续优于现有的 DP 和长上下文推理基线。

Conclusion: DP-MIND 成功地将差分隐私推理扩展到了百万 token 规模的上下文，提供了一种在保护长上下文隐私的同时保持模型效用的有效方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Service has become a dominant paradigm for accessing large language models without requiring local deployment. However, the process raises severe privacy concerns when user queries may include sensitive or confidential contexts. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled approach to protecting such inputs, but existing DP inference methods are primarily designed for short- or moderate-context settings and struggle with million-token-scale inputs due to limited context capacity and severe utility degradation under DP perturbation.
  In this paper, we propose DP-MIND, a memory-guided framework that scales differentially private inference to million-token contexts. DP-MIND enables private long-context inference by locally perturbing the input and processing it chunk by chunk with a compact memory that preserves task-relevant information across the sequence. To make this memory robust to DP noise, we optimize the memory construction policy directly on perturbed inputs using reinforcement learning, guided by both final answer correctness and memory consistency. We further recover perturbation-corrupted answer spans through a local denoising module without exposing the original context to the external model. We provide theoretical privacy analysis of DP-MIND and further conduct extensive empirical analysis across multiple long-context benchmarks with context lengths up to the million-token scale, demonstrating that DP-MIND consistently outperforms existing DP and long-context inference baselines in both scalability and utility.

</details>


### [46] [Competence-Aware Orchestration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13144)
*Songmiao Wang,Yuchen Wang,Chenglin Yu,Hongxia Yang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于LLM能力感知的多智能体系统（MAS）编排框架，通过动态调整智能体间的协作和通信来优化性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统在协作方面存在挑战，即如何根据当前查询的难度自适应地调整协作程度，因为过度的协作会增加成本并导致冗余交互。

Method: 提出一个两阶段的、能力感知的框架。第一阶段对LLM主干在当前查询下的任务相关能力进行建模。第二阶段利用该能力信号来调整角色激活和通信图。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，该方法平均得分达到93.81%。对于高能力LLM，在提高平均准确率的同时，令牌成本降低了36.26%；对于低能力LLM，比现有方法平均提高了4.58个点。该趋势在未见过的LLM上同样成立。

Conclusion: 所提出的能力感知框架能够根据LLM主干的能力自适应地调整多智能体系统的协作策略，从而在不同能力水平的LLM上实现性能和效率的优化。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing complex reasoning through role specialization and collaboration, yet a key challenge remains: how can collaboration be adapted to a backbone's competence on the current query? This problem matters because more collaboration is not always better--richer coordination may help on difficult cases, but it also incurs additional token cost and redundant interactions. We propose a two-stage competence-aware framework for automatic MAS orchestration. It first models the LLM backbone's task-conditioned competence for the current query, and then uses this signal to adapt role activation and the communication graph. Across six benchmarks, our method achieves an average score of 93.81%. Experiments with six LLM backbones of varying competence levels further show that our method adjusts collaboration according to the inferred competence: for higher-competence backbones, it improves average accuracy from 87.22 to 89.97 while reducing token cost by 36.26%; for lower-competence backbones, it yields a further average gain of 4.58 points over existing methods. The same trend also holds on unseen backbones.

</details>


### [47] [VIGOR: Verifiable Industrial Geometry Optimization and Repair in Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13145)
*Fuqiang Wang,Zheng Guo,Xiaoming Wu,Hanlin Kong,Nayu Liu,Chuanting Zhang,Daojun Liang,Jingxuan Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了VIGOR模型，旨在解决大型语言模型生成的工业场景程序在可执行性与工程可行性之间的差距，通过监督生成、基于执行的验证和分阶段几何精炼，有效解决了局部和全局的工程约束冲突，显著提高了场景生成的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的工业场景程序虽然可以执行，但可能存在违反耦合边界、碰撞、间隙、通道或结构约束的问题，即存在“可执行性-工程可行性差距”。现有方法未能有效弥合这一差距，导致局部合理的放置可能导致全局不可行。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含1,749个可构建场景、可执行程序和94个最终状态几何检查的数据集。提出了VIGOR模型，结合了监督场景程序生成、基于执行的验证和分阶段几何精炼。具体包括：监督微调实现完整的程序生成；局部投影解决独立的、可修正的违规；联合冲突图修复协调残余的多对象冲突，同时保持结构关系。

Result: VIGOR在Complete Feasibility上达到了96.86%，在Overall score上达到了91.20，比最强的基线模型高出10.42个百分点。实验证明，VIGOR能够可靠地生成满足工程约束的工业场景程序。

Conclusion: 在受控的工业布局条件下，可靠的场景程序生成不仅需要可执行的程序，还需要显式的验证和对耦合工程约束的协同解决。VIGOR模型展示了在这种场景下解决可执行性-工程可行性差距的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate structured layouts and executable scene programs from natural-language instructions. However, executability does not imply engineering feasibility: an industrial scene program may execute successfully while its final state still violates coupled boundary, collision, clearance, aisle, or structural constraints. We identify this mismatch as the executability-engineering-feasibility gap, where locally plausible placements can remain globally infeasible because corrections to one device may introduce violations elsewhere. To make this gap measurable in a controlled setting, we construct an execution-grounded industrial scene-program dataset with 1,749 constructible scenarios, complete executable programs, and 94 final-state geometric checks per scenario. We further propose VIGOR, which combines supervised scene-program generation with execution-grounded verification and staged geometric refinement. Supervised fine-tuning establishes complete program generation, local projection resolves independently correctable violations, and joint conflict-graph repair coordinates residual multi-object conflicts while preserving structural relations. Experiments show that VIGOR achieves 96.86% Complete Feasibility and an Overall score of 91.20, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 10.42 points in Overall. These results show that, under controlled industrial layout conditions, reliable scene-program generation requires not only executable programs, but also explicit verification and coordinated resolution of coupled engineering constraints.

</details>


### [48] [Quality, Not Just Outcome: A Structural Scoring Framework for Selecting Coding-Agent Trajectories for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13152)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Shicheng Li,Linli Yao,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为TQS的轨迹质量评分框架，用于评估代码生成代理的交互轨迹，并使用其过滤数据以训练代理，结果表明此方法比随机或低分轨迹训练的模型效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码生成代理训练方法（监督微调）仅基于最终测试通过的结果来筛选轨迹，忽略了过程中可能存在的效率低下模式，这些模式会被模型模仿。因此，需要一种新的方法来评估和利用轨迹的“过程质量”。

Method: 提出TQS（Trajectory Quality Score）框架，该框架直接分析轨迹的结构，不依赖LLM判断，运行时间为线性。TQS通过信息焦点、意图-行动一致性以及策略动态这三个结构化收敛信号来表征轨迹质量。研究人员使用155,144条成功轨迹，根据TQS对轨迹进行排序，并筛选出排名靠前的轨迹来微调代码生成代理。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified基准测试中，使用TQS筛选出的排名靠前轨迹训练的模型，其性能一致优于使用随机选择或排名靠后的轨迹训练的模型。

Conclusion: 过程级别的轨迹质量是代码生成代理监督的重要因素。结构化的轨迹过滤（如TQS）是一种有效且可扩展的替代LLM数据选择的方法。

Abstract: Open coding agents are commonly trained through supervised fine-tuning on interaction trajectories whose final patches pass the benchmark test suite. However, this outcome-level filtering criterion ignores process quality: trajectories that eventually succeed may still contain inefficient interaction patterns such as repetitive file inspection, unstable tool usage, or delayed modification behavior, all of which become part of the imitation target. We propose TQS (Trajectory Quality Score), a structural scoring framework for coding-agent trajectories that operates directly on trajectory structure, requires no LLM judge, and runs in linear time. TQS characterizes trajectory quality through structural signals organized around structured convergence: information focus, intent--action alignment, and policy dynamics. Using 155,144 successful trajectories from a public open-license corpus, we rank trajectories by TQS and fine-tune coding agents on filtered subsets. Models trained on top-ranked trajectories consistently outperform those trained on random or bottom-ranked subsets on SWE-Bench-Verified. Our results suggest that process-level trajectory quality is an important factor in coding-agent supervision and that structural trajectory filtering provides an effective and scalable alternative to LLM-based data selection.

</details>


### [49] [RoleWorld: Knowledge Transfer in World Modeling with Functional Role Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13172)
*Boyuan Xiao,Pu Wang,Ji Feng,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RoleWorld的符号世界建模方法，通过将对象行为抽象为可复用的原型和功能角色，实现了LLM代理在不同环境下的高效知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在知识迁移到新环境时存在局限，尽管其模型（如符号规则或程序）在结构上支持迁移，但仍将知识绑定在特定环境的对象上。研究旨在克服这一限制，实现更通用的知识迁移。

Method: RoleWorld方法利用一个“教师LLM”从交互轨迹中提取功能角色原型和基于角色的可执行迁移规则，并生成一个“技能”指导“演员LLM”进行决策。演员LLM在新环境中，在无需教师的情况下，利用该技能、当前角色分配和参数估计，并在不确定时主动测试角色实例假设。模型更新仅限于角色分配和参数估计，不更新LLM参数，迁移规则保持不变。

Result: 在MARS基准测试中，RoleWorld在对象行为显著改变的环境中表现出鲁棒的泛化能力，表明功能角色抽象在知识迁移方面是有效的。

Conclusion: 功能角色抽象是知识迁移在世界建模中的有效组成部分，RoleWorld通过可复用原型和参数值实现了LLM代理在不同环境下的鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: World models enable large language model (LLM) agents to anticipate action effects and make informed decisions. For generalization, it is desirable for such world models to transfer efficiently to new environments with similar interaction structure and underlying dynamics. Recent approaches encode world dynamics as symbolic rules or executable programs, whose compositional structure in principle affords transfer, but still organize such knowledge around environment-specific objects. To address this limitation, we introduce RoleWorld, a symbolic world modeling approach which uses reusable prototypes and environment-specific parameter values as functional role abstractions to represent how objects behave in interactions. From interaction trajectories, a teacher LLM extracts role prototypes and executable transition rules defined over role instances, together with a skill that guides the actor LLM to use role information in decision making. In a target world, without the teacher, the actor uses this skill together with its current role assignments and parameter estimates, and actively tests a role instance hypothesis through interaction when its uncertainty affects the current decision. Feedback from each action refines role assignments and estimates parameter values online, with no LLM parameter updates and the transition rules held fixed. Experiments on MARS benchmark show that RoleWorld enables robust generalization across environments with significantly altered object behavior, suggesting that functional role abstractions can serve as an effective component for knowledge transfer in world modeling. Project Page: https://future-item.github.io/RoleWorld.

</details>


### [50] [JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13194)
*Mengdi Liu,Chenghao Jia,Hong Chang,Mingjie He,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JANUS 是一个研究框架，通过预测未来结果和反思过去经验来加速自主研究，克服了传统方法中的延迟和搜索空间爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的“生成-执行-反馈”范式在自主研究中存在反馈延迟和搜索空间爆炸的问题，阻碍了研究的进展。

Method: JANUS 提出了一个“预测-验证-演化”循环，利用一个共演化的世界模型来预测未来结果。研究代理在演化图中提出候选，世界模型根据预测价值、信息增益和探索效用对其进行评分，然后在执行前进行选择、优化或修剪。接收真实反馈后，JANUS 将经验提炼为演化技能，使研究代理和世界模型能够持续共演化。

Result: 在 MLS-Bench-Lite 和真实研究任务上的评估表明，JANUS 在演化效率和研究上限方面显著优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: JANUS 通过整合预测和反思，实现了研究代理和世界模型的持续共演化，有效解决了自主研究中的关键挑战，并取得了优越的性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.

</details>


### [51] [Forgetting is Not Always Bad: A Neuro-Inspired Memory Repair Mechanism for Poisoned LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13267)
*Lei Liu,Yunji Liang,Xiaowen Zhang,Jingqi Liu,Qi Li,Bin Guo,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DREAM的LLM代理内存修复框架，该框架通过选择性遗忘受污染的记忆来抵御内存中毒攻击，同时保持良好记忆的效用，并且效率比现有方法更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM内存中毒防御方法效率低下且计算开销高，因此需要一种更有效的内存修复方法。

Method: DREAM框架包含三个阶段：1. 扰动诱导的隐式重激活以暴露恶意记忆的结构脆弱性；2. 基于多维激活模式的自适应异常诊断以检测拓扑异常；3. 动态内存修复模块，选择性抑制有害记忆并强化良性记忆。

Result: DREAM将后门中毒的攻击成功率降低了95%以上，同时保持了良性记忆的强大效用。在注入攻击方面也取得了具有竞争力的鲁棒性。与A-MemGuard相比，DREAM的运行时速提高了2.13倍，令牌消耗减少了37.7%。在受污染的多代理系统中，DREAM的任务成功率为92.96%。

Conclusion: DREAM是一种有效的、训练无关的内存修复框架，能够选择性地使受污染的记忆衰减，从而抵御内存中毒攻击，并且在效率和性能上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are highly vulnerable to memory-poisoning attacks. Existing defenses primarily rely on external modules for either static memory isolation or continuous online auditing, resulting in low memory efficiency and high computational overhead. To address these limitations, inspired by neuroscientific mechanisms that weak reactivation can destabilize memories and induce decay in the absence of reinforcement, we propose an agent-intrinsic training-free memory repair framework, DREAM (Dynamic Reactivation for Engram Attenuation in Memory), that enables selective functional forgetting of poisoned memories. Specifically, DREAM implements a three-stage pipeline: perturbation-induced implicit reactivation to reveal the structural fragility of malicious memories, adaptive anomaly diagnosis based on multi-dimensional activation patterns to detect topological anomalies, and a dynamic memory repair module that selectively suppresses harmful memories while reinforcing benign ones. Extensive experiments across diverse attackers and real-world tasks demonstrate that DREAM reduces attack success rates by over 95\% against backdoor poisoning while preserving strong benign utility. DREAM also achieves competitive robustness against injection attacks. In terms of runtime and token consumption, DREAM achieves up to a 2.13$\times$ speedup and a 37.7\% reduction in token consumption compared with A-MemGuard. Furthermore, DREAM also achieves a task success rate of 92.96\% on poisoned multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [52] [AdaC-GRPO: Towards Mastering Long-Horizon Visual Sudoku Puzzles via Adaptive Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13184)
*Sida Li,Yuanxin Liu,Linli Yao,Shicheng Li,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了FG-Sudoku基准和AdaC-GRPO方法，以解决大型多模态模型（LMM）在处理长时域视觉逻辑推理任务（如视觉数独）时遇到的困难，并取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）在处理长时域多模态任务方面效果不佳，特别是在视觉数独这种需要多步推理的任务上。现有的大型多模态模型（LMM）在复杂长距离依赖上表现不佳，导致标准RLVR训练受阻。

Method: 提出了FG-Sudoku，一个按网格大小和推理步骤划分的细粒度视觉数独基准。提出了AdaC-GRPO，一种自适应课程分组相对策略优化方法，通过跟踪模型的“近侧发展区”（ZPD）动态构建训练批次，克服了标准RLVR的奖励稀疏问题。

Result: AdaC-GRPO使Qwen2.5-VL-7B的性能接近Gemini-2.5-Flash，缩小了与Gemini-2.5-Pro的62.2%的准确率差距。该方法在InternVL-3.5和Qwen3-VL上也展现了泛化能力。

Conclusion: AdaC-GRPO是一种有效的解决LMM在长时域多模态推理任务中RLVR奖励稀疏问题的训练策略，显著提升了模型在视觉数独等任务上的性能，并具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR) enhances reasoning in large language models, its efficacy in long-horizon multimodal tasks remains underexplored. Visual Sudoku puzzles pose a representative challenge, requiring multi-step deductive reasoning over both local constraints and global dependency chains. To this end, we introduce FG-Sudoku, a fine-grained benchmark categorized by grid size and required deductive steps. Evaluations reveal that frontier MLLMs fail on complex long-range dependencies (e.g., 9*9 puzzles). During post-training, this near-zero success rate causes severe reward sparsity, halting standard RLVR. We propose Adaptive Curriculum Group Relative Policy Optimization (AdaC-GRPO) to overcome this. Unlike rigid or prior-based schedules, AdaC-GRPO dynamically constructs training batches by tracking the model's "Zone of Proximal Development" via online rollout accuracy. Experiments demonstrate that AdaC-GRPO enables Qwen2.5-VL-7B to approach the performance of Gemini-2.5-Flash while bridging 62.2% of the overall accuracy gap to Gemini-2.5-Pro, with its generalizability further validated across InternVL-3.5 and Qwen3-VL.

</details>


### [53] [BoundaryEvo: Data-Model Co-Evolution at the Capability Boundary for Long-Chain Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13285)
*Jiaming Su,Suorong Yang,Xudong Li,Tengchao Yang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BoundaryEvo的两阶段数据-模型协同演进框架，用于从大型教师模型中蒸馏长链推理轨迹，以提升小型语言模型的推理能力。该框架通过能力启动阶段预先获得基础推理能力，然后进入能力边界协同演进阶段，动态选择学生模型尚未掌握但教师模型能可靠解决的样本进行训练，从而在有限的训练数据下有效提高模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法在蒸馏前固定训练集，未能考虑推理样本的有效性会随着学生模型的演进而变化，尤其是在训练数据量有限时，这种不匹配会带来高昂的代价。已掌握的样本提供的监督有限，而学生模型能力之外的样本又难以学习。

Method: BoundaryEvo框架包含两个阶段：1. 能力启动（Capability Priming）：直接蒸馏少量教师生成的长推理轨迹，不依赖额外模型进行筛选，让学生模型初步获得推理能力。2. 能力边界协同演进（Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution）：重复选择学生模型尚未掌握但教师模型能够可靠解决的问题，并随着学生模型的演进更新训练数据。

Result: 在1,000个训练样本的情况下，BoundaryEvo在11个推理基准测试中持续优于静态和动态数据选择基线。在IMO-AnswerBench上，它将Qwen3.5-4B的准确率从62.9%提升到75.3%；在AMO-Bench上，准确率从26.8%提升到46.0%。

Conclusion: BoundaryEvo框架通过协同演进数据和模型，能够有效地利用有限的训练数据提升小型语言模型的推理能力，解决了现有方法在数据选择上的局限性。

Abstract: Distilling long-chain reasoning trajectories from large teacher models provides an effective way to improve the reasoning ability of small language models. However, existing methods typically select a fixed training set before distillation, overlooking that the usefulness of a reasoning example changes as the student evolves. This mismatch becomes especially costly when only a small number of teacher trajectories can be used for training. Besides, examples already mastered provide little new supervision, while examples far beyond the capability of student may be difficult to learn from. Training data should therefore adapt as model capability evolves, yet reliable data selection is difficult when the student is initially weak. To address these challenges, our key idea is to first establish the basic reasoning capability and then adapt the training data to the evolving capability boundary of the student. We propose BoundaryEvo, a two-stage data--model co-evolution framework. In Capability Priming, we directly distill a small set of teacher-generated long reasoning trajectories without using a separate model to judge and filter them, allowing the student to acquire an initial reasoning capability before capability-aware data selection. In Capability-Boundary Co-Evolution, we repeatedly select questions that the current student has not mastered but the expert can reliably solve, and update the training data as the student evolves. With only 1,000 training examples, BoundaryEvo consistently outperforms static and dynamic data selection baselines across 11 reasoning benchmarks, raising the original Qwen3.5-4B accuracy from 62.9 to 75.3 on IMO-AnswerBench and from 26.8 to 46.0 on AMO-Bench. The code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [54] [Masked Diffusion Language Agents for Tool-Integrated Chemical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13330)
*Mengdi Liu,Chenghao Jia,Hong Chang,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemDiffAgent，一种新颖的掩码扩散语言代理，用于化学推理任务，通过迭代去噪和优化的训练框架，克服了自回归模型的局限性，并在化学任务上取得了显著的性能提升，甚至在某些方面超越了大型前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的化学推理代理虽然集成了外部工具，但受限于自回归范式，在双向分子理解、全局工具规划和多源证据整合方面存在困难。研究的动机是克服这些限制，设计一个更强大的化学推理代理。

Method: ChemDiffAgent将化学推理重新构建为迭代去噪过程，通过交错的推理和工具使用轨迹进行。它通过更难的填充子问题训练和灵活的任意顺序解码来增强代理推理能力。研究还开发了一个两阶段的训练后框架，包括代理监督微调和方差缩减偏好优化。

Result: ChemDiffAgent在化学任务上的综合性能比自回归代理提高了约20%。在相同预算和评估下，其8B模型在某些方面取得了与GPT 5.5和Claude Opus 4.7等大型模型相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: ChemDiffAgent是首个用于工具集成化学推理的掩码扩散语言代理，它通过迭代去噪和优化的训练策略，有效解决了现有模型的局限性，并在各项化学任务中展现出优越的性能，预示着扩散模型在复杂任务代理领域的光明前景。

Abstract: Designing tool-integrated reasoning agent systems for complex chemical tasks remains a fundamental challenge. Although recent chemical agents have demonstrated promising performance through the integration of external tools, they are still built on the autoregressive paradigm, which is inherently constrained by causal attention and left-to-right generation. As a result, they often struggle with bidirectional molecular understanding, global coordinated tool planning and multi-source evidence integration. To address this challenge, we introduce ChemDiffAgent, the first masked diffusion language agent for tool-integrated chemical reasoning. It reformulates chemical reasoning as iterative denoising over interleaved reasoning and tool-use trajectories, enabling tool-use decisions to be jointly determined under full-trajectory context and thereby better aligning the agent's decision boundary with its knowledge boundary. We further analyze how this paradigm benefits agentic reasoning through harder infilling subproblems during training and flexible arbitrary-order decoding during inference. Then we develop a two-stage post-training framework, comprising agentic supervised fine-tuning and variance-reduced preference optimization, to enhance tool-use and reasoning capabilities. We also construct a comprehensive benchmark covering six chemistry tasks across both single-turn and multi-turn tool-use scenarios. Under matched budgets and evaluation, ChemDiffAgent consistently outperforms autoregressive agents, achieving an overall improvement of approximately 20%. Notably, our 8B model achieves performance comparable to, in some cases surpassing, significantly larger frontier models, such as GPT 5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7. Code is available at https://github.com/bilemond/ChemDiffAgent.

</details>


### [55] [AlphaNIST: Emergent Assembly Skills from Binary Task Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13354)
*Octi Zhang,Iretiayo Akinola,Rosario Scalise,Mateo Guaman Castro,Patrick Yin,Tobias Widmer,Miles Macklin,Chris Amevor,Dylan Turpin,Dirk Van Gelder,Jan Carius,Ankur Handa,Gavriel State,Yunrong Guo,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaNIST 使用大规模强化学习在 NIST Assembly Task Board #1 上实现了无需演示、任务分解或奖励塑形的机器人装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索是否可以通过仅提供稀疏的二进制任务奖励和任务多样性，让机器人像AlphaGo Zero从头学习围棋一样，自主发现完成复杂的机器人装配任务的策略，而不是依赖人工设计的指导。

Method: 采用大规模强化学习（RL）方法，从零开始训练一个单一的策略，使其能够处理NIST Assembly Task Board #1上的所有20个装配任务。在训练过程中，仅提供稀疏的二进制任务成功奖励，并且允许任务以任意顺序执行，甚至可以在同一场景中完成多个装配对。

Result: 在模拟环境中，该方法达到了98.2%的总体训练成功率，无需演示、进度塑形、脚本化原语或针对每个任务的调优。学习到的策略展现出了灵巧和规划能力，例如选择对后续装配有利的抓取点，利用周围几何形状重新定向零件，以及能够在不依赖显式规划器的情况下，在不同任务间切换并完成装配。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大规模强化学习结合稀疏奖励，能够让机器人在复杂的机器人装配任务中自主发现有效的策略，甚至展现出类似规划的行为，这为未来在更广泛的机器人操作任务中减少人工干预提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: The NIST Assembly Task Board #1 remains a challenging benchmark for learning-based methods in robotic manipulation. Over short horizons, it demands precise, contact-rich control of small, tight-fitting parts. Over long horizons, it requires recovery and coordination across extended action sequences. Demonstrations, task decomposition, and dense progress rewards have enabled research advances by providing guidance on how to assemble. But how much of the strategy must be specified? The progression from AlphaGo to AlphaGo Zero showed that RL at scale can discover superhuman strategies without prescribing how to win. AlphaNIST asks whether a sparse binary task reward and task diversity admitted by this task-agnostic reward can similarly let a robot discover how to assemble. In simulation, we train a single policy from scratch across all 20 assembly tasks on the NIST Assembly Task Board #1, reaching 98.2% overall training success without demonstrations, progress shaping, scripted primitives, or per-task tuning. The framework also trains policies to complete ten assembly pairs in a single episode without a prescribed order, with task success rewarded only when all ten pairs are simultaneously complete. The learned policies discover dexterous and planning-like behaviors: choosing grasps for later assembly, using surrounding geometry to reorient parts, and preparing one part, assembling another, then returning to finish the first without an explicit planner. For videos, see our website: https://alpha-nist.github.io/

</details>


### [56] [SWE-Critix: Post-Training Generalist Verifiers for Assessing Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13356)
*Jiajun Hu,Chun Yong Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SWE-Critix，一个用于训练代码生成代理行为通用验证器的后训练流程。该验证器在给定任务和执行轨迹后，能够预测成功标签并生成解释性文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前代码生成代理在端到端软件工程任务中缺乏可靠的评估标准，需要能够可靠判断和解释其行为的验证器，尤其是在处理长时程任务时。

Method: SWE-Critix采用监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）相结合的后训练流程，在CodeForge-Preview的轨迹数据集上训练30B模型。验证器接收任务和执行轨迹，输出完成标签和解释性文本。

Result: 在测试集上，SWE-Critix将Qwen3-30B-A3B模型的分类准确率从57%提升到SFT后的66%，RL后的78%。

Conclusion: SWE-Critix是一个有效的通用验证器训练流程，能够显著提高代码生成代理轨迹的评估准确性和可解释性。研究者公开发布了模型、数据集和训练代码。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly deployed for end-to-end software engineering tasks, where a readily available oracle evaluator that defines the success criteria is often absent. This motivates verifications for coding agents that can reliably judge and explain their behavior. However, verification is non-trivial as coding agents are increasingly capable of solving long-horizon software engineering tasks. Their execution trajectories can be extremely long, containing hundreds of iterative interactions with code repositories and external tools. Existing verifiers either rely on general-purpose LLMs without specialized training or train dedicated critics that predominantly focus on producing scalar rewards, highlighting the relatively limited exploration of training-based generalist verification. We introduce SWE-Critix, a post-training pipeline for training generalist verifiers of coding-agent trajectories. Given a task and its execution trajectory, the learned verifier predicts the completion label and generates a natural-language analysis that explains its judgment. Our pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) and trains a series of 30B models on trajectories from CodeForge-Preview. On a held-out test set, SWE-Critix improves classification accuracy from 57% for the base Qwen3-30B-A3B reasoning model to 66% after SFT and 78% after RL. We release our models, datasets, and training code to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [57] [RegulaTutor: A Theory-Informed Framework for Long-Term Personalized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13399)
*Yonggang Li,Boqian Han,Rui Sheng,Yushi Sun,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RegulaTutor 是一个基于理论的框架，用于长期个性化辅导，通过将学习者历史与教学策略联系起来，并结合自适应学习（SRL）和 CAMM 框架，来改进学习者诊断和教学质量。PairRoute 是一种改进程序，可用于识别和修复系统中的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的辅导系统在个性化响应方面依赖于记忆和检索，但将历史证据与教学策略联系起来的教学原理不够明确。需要一个更具理论依据的框架来指导长期个性化辅导。

Method: RegulaTutor 框架结合了学习者历史证据、学习者解释和面向学习者的教学。它借鉴了自适应学习（SRL）研究，并采用认知、情感、元认知和动机（CAMM）框架来推导出基于证据的学习者解释和教学策略。PairRoute 是一种离线改进程序，利用中间执行记录来区分系统故障，并提出针对性的改进。

Result: RegulaTutor 在历史证据准确性、学习者诊断和教学质量方面平均有所提高。PairRoute 改进程序在模型训练过程中显著提高了历史证据的准确性。

Conclusion: RegulaTutor 有潜力通过不断发展的教学策略和有针对性的系统改进，来支持终身个性化教育。PairRoute 能够有效地细化和改进辅导系统。

Abstract: Long-term personalized tutoring requires language models to translate a learner's evolving history into appropriate instructional support. Existing LLM-based tutoring systems use memory and retrieval to personalize responses, but the pedagogical rationale linking historical evidence to teaching strategies is not always explicit. We introduce RegulaTutor, a theory-informed framework for long-term personalized tutoring that connects historical evidence, learner interpretation, and learner-facing instruction. Guided by self-regulated learning (SRL) research, its tutoring harness operationalizes a cognitive, affective, metacognitive, and motivational (CAMM) framework for deriving evidence-supported learner interpretations and teaching policies. The resulting policies retain links to their supporting evidence and are updated as new observations arrive. This staged workflow also supports systematic refinement through PairRoute, an offline procedure that uses intermediate execution records to distinguish harness, model, and mixed failures. PairRoute proposes and evaluates targeted harness revisions, then constructs reviewed stage-level supervision for confirmed model failures remaining under the selected harness. We focus this refinement on historical evidence acquisition, which underpins personalized instruction and allows outputs to be checked against learning records and reference answers. RegulaTutor improves historical-evidence accuracy, learner diagnosis, and judged teaching quality on average across evaluated model configurations. PairRoute results show an endpoint improvement in standalone-model historical-evidence accuracy over the refinement trajectory. Together, these results highlight RegulaTutor's potential to support lifelong personalized education through instructional policies that evolve with learner histories and targeted improvements to the tutoring system.

</details>


### [58] [Web$hell: A Unified Interface for Agentic Web Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13416)
*Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Sangam Lee,Donguk Kwon,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are becoming direct users of the open web. As they take on increasingly complex and information-intensive tasks, effective web access is becoming a core capability for agentic problem solving. Existing approaches have advanced agentic web access through different access models, including search-augmented agents, browser-based agents, and, more recently, filesystem-based agents. Nevertheless, as users expect agents to complete long-horizon, end-to-end tasks, they increasingly need to conduct broad information gathering, perform browser actions, and revisit acquired evidence within a single trajectory. Yet our preliminary analyses reveal that existing access models struggle to meet these demands. They primarily support either information gathering or browser interaction, while combining their capabilities can involve substantial costs or performance trade-offs. To this end, we present Web\$hell, an interface that unifies web access under a single access model. Using a common schema defined by WebContract, WebCompiler compiles live webpages into addressable information and action objects. These objects are maintained in WebCache and handled through a shared set of operations, providing agents with consistent, operable, and persistent web access. Through extensive experiments across web search, web navigation, and deep research benchmarks, we show that Web\$hell achieves strong task performance at lower cost. Our results further indicate the importance of interface design for effective web access, with Web\$hell opening new possibilities for more capable and efficient LLM agents on the web.

</details>


### [59] [Local Diagnostics of Learned Event Representations for Anomaly Detection at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13437)
*Donatella Genovese,Philip Ploner,Simone Scardapane,Stefano Giagu,Thea Klaeboe Aarrestad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上进行异常检测的方法，利用神经网络学习低维表示来识别与标准模型不符的碰撞事件，并介绍了一种局部诊断流程来区分新物理、模型错误或探测器效应，并识别用于后续搜索的观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上进行新物理搜索时，模型无关的异常检测策略很有前景，但学习到的低维表示会牺牲物理解释性。为了将异常检测纳入发现流程，需要对标记的事件进行局部诊断，以区分新物理、模型错误或探测器效应，并确定用于后续搜索的观测量。

Method: 研究人员在COLLIDE-2V数据集上评估了监督式和自监督式神经网络嵌入策略。他们使用在学习到的潜在空间中训练的自编码器的重建误差作为异常分数。此外，他们还引入了一个局部诊断流程，该流程将高斯混合模型拟合到标准模型潜在空间，并在没有过程标签的情况下通过高层观测量来剖析每个组件，从而定位标记的事件并按局部区分能力对观测量进行排名。

Result: 在所考虑的配置和基准信号中，监督式目标（利用训练期间的模拟过程标签）通常优于自监督式方法。最佳配置的监督式方法比直接在运动学特征上训练的自编码器表现更好，在10%的背景接受率下，所有保留下来的过程的信号效率都得到了提高。局部诊断流程恢复了保留过程的定义运动学，并且通过将异常分数与同一观测量相关联，量化了分数跟随它们的紧密程度，揭示了最能区分的观测量并非分数最紧密跟随的观测量的情况。

Conclusion: 监督式神经网络嵌入策略在LHC异常检测中表现优于自监督式方法，并能提高信号效率。引入的局部诊断流程能够有效地区分潜在的新物理信号和模型/探测器效应，并能识别出用于定向搜索的关键观测量。

Abstract: Anomaly detection, the identification of collision events that deviate from Standard Model expectations, is one of the most promising model-agnostic strategies for new-physics searches at the Large Hadron Collider. Searching for rare events in learned low-dimensional representations removes the need to commit to hand-crafted observables a priori, but sacrifices the immediate physical interpretation those observables provide. For anomaly detection to enter a discovery pipeline, flagged events must therefore be diagnosed locally, both to distinguish potential new physics from mismodeling or detector effects and to identify observables for targeted follow-up searches. We evaluate supervised and self-supervised neural embedding strategies on COLLIDE-2V, a 750-million-event dataset simulated under realistic High-Luminosity LHC conditions, using the reconstruction error of an autoencoder trained in the learned latent space as the anomaly score. Across the configurations and benchmark signals considered, supervised objectives, which exploit simulation process labels during training, generally outperform their self-supervised counterparts and, in their best configurations, improve on an autoencoder trained directly on kinematic features, raising the signal efficiency at 10% background acceptance on all held-out processes. We further introduce a local diagnostic pipeline that fits a Gaussian Mixture Model to the SM latent space, profiles each component through high-level observables without process labels, localizes the flagged events, and ranks observables by their local discriminating power. The ranking recovers the defining kinematics of the held-out processes, while correlating the anomaly score with the same observables quantifies how closely the score tracks them, revealing cases where the most discriminating observable is not the one the score follows most closely.

</details>


### [60] [Rollout-Time Intervention: Unlocking Agentic Reinforcement Learning Beyond Policy Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13438)
*Chen Xi,Ding Ruixue,Wang Zhigaoyuan,Zhang Fanrui,Zhang Qiang,Chen Boli,Wang Shihang,Wang Tianfu,Ren Ailin,Wang Pengyong,Xie Pengjun,Xiong Hui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI) 的新方法，通过在强化学习（RL）训练过程中利用奖励信号（如评估标准）来纠正错误轨迹，从而生成更有效的训练样本，克服了现有 LLM Agent RL 方法的固有局限性，并在多项基准测试中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM Agent RL 方法在处理超出模型当前能力范围的任务时，由于有效轨迹极其稀疏，容易导致训练崩溃。研究的动机是找到一种方法来生成更多成功的轨迹，从而提升模型的学习效率和性能。

Method: RTI 方法在采样（rollout）阶段利用奖励侧信息（评估标准）来干预错误的轨迹，将其转化为成功的轨迹。虽然这些干预轨迹本质上是 off-policy 的，但通过过滤掉分布差异过大的轨迹，它们被证明可以有效地指导策略优化。

Result: 在五个基准测试上的大量实验表明，RTI 持续优于 RL 基线方法。经过 RTI 后训练的策略在性能上可以接近更强大的 LLM，并最重要的是，能够超越其原始能力边界。

Conclusion: RTI 是一种有效的方法，可以生成超出策略内在探索能力的成功轨迹，从而克服 LLM Agent RL 中的激励稀疏性问题，并显著提高模型的性能，使其能够解决更复杂的任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents, enabling stronger reasoning, planning, and tool-use in interactive environments. However, existing agent RL methods remain fundamentally constrained by an on-policy reasoning bottleneck: when tasks exceed the model's current capability boundary, effective trajectories become extremely sparse and training often collapses. We introduce Rollout-Time Intervention (RTI), a technique designed to elicit successful trajectories beyond the policy's intrinsic exploration capacity. RTI operates by leveraging reward-side evidence (i.e., evaluation rubrics) during rollout to convert erroneous trajectories into successful ones, providing effective learning signals for tasks that the policy rarely solves on its own. Although intervention trajectories are inherently off-policy, we find that they can effectively guide policy optimization when trajectories with excessive distributional shifts are filtered out. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that RTI consistently outperforms RL baselines, enabling the post-trained policy to approach the performance of substantially stronger contemporary LLMs and, more importantly, transcend its original capability boundary. The code is available at https://github.com/Alibaba-NLP/qqr.

</details>


### [61] [Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13455)
*Max van Duijn,Michiel van der Meer,Aske Plaat,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个关于能够直接在现实世界中行动并与其他 LLM 互动的大型语言模型（LLM）的书籍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 LLM 直接作用于现实世界以及 LLM 之间相互作用的可能性及其影响。

Method: 本书将探讨 agentic LLM 的工作原理、行动方式、互动方式以及它们对用户意味着什么。

Result: LLM 结合了与现实世界互动和相互作用的能力。

Conclusion: agentic LLM 结合了大型语言模型的力量与在现实世界中行动的能力，并可能对用户产生重大影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have taken the world by storm. Their ability to communicate with us in our own language, and to reason about our world, has made them a favorite tool for writing, answering questions, and much more. Their answers already influence our decisions and our actions. What would happen if LLMs themselves could act in the world directly? What would happen if LLMs could interact with each other? Agentic LLMs combine the power of large language models with the ability to act in the real world. In this book we will discuss how agentic LLMs work, how they act, how they interact, and what this means for us, as users.

</details>


### [62] [SqueezeBench: Saturation-Resilient Evaluation of Mathematical Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13461)
*Pablo Manrique,Hai Xia,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SqueezeBench 是一个新颖的数学基准测试，通过动态提高问题难度来克服饱和问题，每次成功提交都会提升记录，迫使后续提交超越现有最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数学问题解决基准测试存在饱和问题，即问题一旦被解决就无法重复使用。这阻碍了对 AI 在数学领域持续进步的衡量。

Method: SqueezeBench 引入了可计算函数及其机器检查证明作为记录。提交的内容需要提供一个新函数，证明其能界定目标函数，并且在至少一个点上优于当前记录。系统自动接受和更新记录，并由两个独立的 Lean 内核进行验证。

Result: 在十个组合数学问题上，语言模型智能体在有限时间内成功提交了 142 次，显著提高了现有记录，其中一些问题被多次改进。即使是基于早期模型的智能体也能在不超出已有文献的情况下取得进展。

Conclusion: SqueezeBench 作为一个动态且可扩展的基准测试，能够有效地衡量 AI 在数学领域的进步，并通过不断提高难度来避免饱和。语言模型在 SqueezeBench 上表现出强大的能力，并能独立于现有文献进行创新。

Abstract: AI systems are making rapid progress in mathematics, but this progress is hard to measure. Benchmarks of solved problems saturate, and in benchmarks of open problems each problem can be resolved for the first time only once. We present SqueezeBench, a benchmark that is resilient to saturation: a success raises the bar instead of removing the problem. Each problem fixes an integer function $f(n)$ whose values are known only for small $n$, and the benchmark keeps a lower and an upper record on it. A record is a computable Lean function with a machine-checked proof that it bounds $f$. A submission proposes a new function and proves that it bounds $f$, that it is no worse than the current record at any $n$, and that it is strictly better at some $n$. Once accepted, it becomes the record that the next submission has to beat. The system thus chooses the bound as well as its proof, and it may improve the record at a single $n$ or its growth rate. At every $n$, the gap between the two records shows how far the certified bounds are from determining $f(n)$. Every proof is checked by two independent Lean kernels, so no referee needs to check the mathematics. The checks also reject a function that computes $f$ by exhaustive search at small $n$, and a maintainer only reads the submitter's report and looks for such a search at larger $n$. We release ten problems from extremal combinatorics with twenty proved initial records and the admission tools. As a baseline, agents based on a frontier language model, with at most one hour per record, had 82 submissions accepted and improved every record, without going beyond the published literature. Agents based on two newer models then started from these records, had 60 more submissions accepted and improved 19 of the 20 records again. For eight of the ten problems, at least one record is still asymptotically behind the best published bound.

</details>


### [63] [A Local Agentic AI Framework to Generate Edge Case Scenarios for Safety Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13479)
*Victoria J. Hodge,Colin Paterson,Rob Alexander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于本地化、代理式AI框架的自适应边缘场景生成方法，利用语言模型（LM）通过“思考-行动-观察-更新-继续”循环生成、评估和优化边缘场景，以提高自主系统的可靠性和鲁棒性，并为安全论证提供可追溯证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主系统边缘场景生成方法依赖人工干预或规则，限制了多样性。语言模型（LM）的快速发展为自动化生成提供了潜力，但现有方法仍有不足。

Method: 采用本地化LM在笔记本电脑上构建代理式AI框架，通过“思考、行动、观察、更新、继续”的循环生成场景。利用系统需求、描述和环境描述作为知识库，通过上下文提示引导LM生成真实场景。引入独立的LM进行场景批判和优化，并在仿真环境中运行生成场景收集评估指标。通过聚类分析和文本分析来识别功能差距及其触发条件。

Result: 所提出的框架能够生成多样化且真实的边缘场景，并通过仿真评估和分析，识别出系统的功能差距和触发条件。生成的场景为安全论证提供了可追溯的证据。

Conclusion: 灵活、自适应的本地化代理式AI框架在自动化边缘场景生成和产生可追溯的安全论证证据方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Generating edge case scenarios is crucial for training, testing and safety assuring autonomous systems. This identifies hazardous failures to increase system reliability and robustness, ensures the system can still function safely on the boundaries of its operational design domain and learns the expected system behaviour for these edge cases. The rapid advancement in the generation and analysis capabilities of language models (LMs) has expanded their applicability and recent work has investigated LM scenario generation. However, many recent approaches rely on human-in-the-loop or rule-based strategies. This restricts the LM's ability to generate diverse and edge case scenarios; the long tail of the distribution. Our work uses local LMs in a laptop-based agentic AI framework for adaptive scenario generation. This is cheap, fully-controlled, ensures data privacy and security. It uses think, act, observe, update, and continue loops with lightweight JSON encoding to generate scenarios. We use the system requirements, system description and environment description as knowledge bases in contextual prompts for an LM to ensure scenario realism. A second, independent LM critiques each scenario using domain specific criteria so it can be refined. The refined scenarios are run in simulation and evaluation metrics collected. These metric scores are analysed and similar scenarios are created for edge cases (exploitation) or novel scenarios are generated to explore the scenario space. Post-hoc clustering using metric scores and thematic-type analysis of the rich-text scenario descriptions produced by the LM pinpoints functional gaps and their triggering conditions. This creates traceable evidence for the safety assurance case. Our work highlights the potential of a flexible and adaptive local agentic AI framework for automating edge case generation and producing traceable safety assurance evidence.

</details>


### [64] [Branching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13481)
*Dongyang Chen,Shudong Liu,Shubo Li,Duoxun Tang,Jinwei Liang,Zehuan Wu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 BranchOPD 的新方法，通过选择不确定性高的决策点，并对共享前缀进行重采样，从而在数学和agent任务中提高了On-policy distillation (OPD)的效率和效果。同时引入了protocol-boundary repair (PBR)来解决生成重复和浪费的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OPD方法在有限采样预算下，由于每次决策只有一个延续，导致监督覆盖范围有限。需要一种更有效的方法来提高监督的质量和覆盖面。

Method: BranchOPD方法包括：1. 选择信息量高的决策点（基于学生不确定性和师生分歧）。2. 从共享前缀重采样学生策略的延续。3. 对主轨迹和分支应用token级别的教师监督。4. 引入protocol-boundary repair (PBR)来解决生成问题。

Result: BranchOPD在ALFWorld和WebShop上减少了学生生成token的成本（分别约19%和31%）。在数学任务上，BranchOPD比Vanilla OPD的平均准确率（avg@16）高出18.55个百分点，在ALFWorld任务上成功率高出13.8个百分点。实验证明，在不确定的决策点进行分支比随机选择或在回合开始时进行分支效果更好。

Conclusion: BranchOPD通过智能地选择决策点并重采样分支，有效提高了OPD的效率和性能。PBR进一步解决了生成过程中的实际问题。BranchOPD在多个基准测试中均优于现有方法，表明其在提升策略学习效率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but a single trajectory realizes only one continuation at each decision, limiting supervision coverage under a finite sampling budget. We propose BranchOPD (Branching On-Policy Distillation), which selects informative decision points using student uncertainty and teacher-student disagreement, resamples student-policy continuations from shared prefixes, and applies token-level teacher supervision to both main trajectories and branches. BranchOPD operates at token-level decisions in mathematics and within-turn decisions in agent tasks. Shared-prefix reuse reduces student-generated token costs by approximately 19% on ALFWorld and 31% on WebShop relative to seven independent trajectories. We also introduce protocol-boundary repair (PBR), which uses targeted supervision under protocol constraints to mitigate repetition and wasted generation. BranchOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD and Multi-Rollout OPD (n=7) across all evaluated benchmarks, with gains over Vanilla OPD of 18.55 percentage points in mean mathematics accuracy over 16 sampled answers (avg@16) and 13.8 points in mean ALFWorld ID/OOD success. Mechanistic analyses and selection ablations show that branching at uncertain decisions expands subsequent behavior coverage and outperforms random selection and turn-initial branching.

</details>


### [65] [Humanize MLE Agents: Multi-Agent Session Alternation Beyond Single-Agent Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13494)
*Changye Li,Zijian Zhang,Muheng Li,Yiduo Pang,Tingjun Wu,Menghan Li,Yitong Liu,Hongkai Guo,Mengchi Chen,Chenyu He,Kaisheng Ma,Wei Xu,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为HMA的新型机器学习工程代理工作流，通过交替执行和重置上下文来克服现有代理在长任务中的效率衰减问题，并在Kaggle数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的代理在处理长时限任务时，随着执行时间的增加，其推理能力和进展速度会下降（长时限减速），并且随着底层模型改进，其定制化管理器的优势会消失（管理器陈旧）。这表明需要一种工作流级别的方法，使代理能够跨会话建立在彼此的工作之上。

Method: 将代理会话建模为上下文重置半马尔可夫决策过程（CR-SMDP），推导出使交替代理优于单代理的条件，以及实验预算上限，并确定会话限制如何维持这种优势。基于此分析，提出Humanize MLE Agents（HMA），该方法让两个代理在共享工作空间中交替执行，每个会话最多进行k次实验，并在会话间重置上下文。

Result: 在75个Kaggle MLE-bench任务上，HMA实现了78.2%的任何奖牌率，比最强的开源基线高8.0个百分点，且时间预算仅为其四分之一。在五个异构代理对的六种配置中，HMA平均比单独的基线提高了6.4个百分点的任何奖牌率。在核优化案例研究中，HMA达到了878个周期，接近社区排行榜的领先者。

Conclusion: HMA是一种有效的工作流级别方法，通过代理交替执行和上下文重置，能够克服长时限任务中的效率衰减问题，并在机器学习工程等领域取得了优于现有方法的性能，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based agent approaches for long-horizon machine learning engineering (MLE) often use context engineering, implemented through custom harnesses that manage tools, skills, and accumulated experience. However, we find that as single-session execution lengthens, the agent's reasoning intensity declines alongside slowing progress, summarized as long-horizon deceleration, and the benefits of these custom harnesses vanish as the underlying model improves, summarized as harness staleness. These findings suggest that their benefits may not persist across versions, which motivates a workflow-level approach in which agents build on one another's work across sessions. We model these alternating agent sessions as a context-reset semi-Markov decision process (CR-SMDP) and establish sufficient conditions for alternation to exceed single-agent reference limits, derive an explicit experiment-budget bound, and characterize when session caps preserve this advantage. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Humanize MLE Agents (HMA), which alternates two agents on a shared workspace, limits each session to at most k experiments and resets the context between sessions, without additional task-specific harness or prior knowledge supplements. Across 75 Kaggle tasks in MLE-bench, HMA reaches a state-of-the-art any-medal rate of 78.2%, exceeding the strongest open-source baseline by 8.0 points with a quarter of its time budget. Moreover, among six configurations spanning five heterogeneous agent pairs, HMA improves any-medal rate over the constituent baselines by 6.4 points on average, demonstrating cross-pair generality. A supplementary case on kernel optimization reaches 878 cycles, within 1.6% of the community leaderboard leader, demonstrating applicability beyond MLE.

</details>


### [66] [Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13510)
*Hamza Shafiq,Jialu Tang,Ting Dang,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PRISM的多模态语言模型，用于整合结构化测量、生理波形、长期病史和人群背景信息，以改进临床预后预测。PRISM在MDS-ED基准测试中，在诊断、病情恶化、ICU入院和死亡率预测方面均取得了显著优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预后预测需要整合多种异构的医疗数据，现有方法在处理这些多模态数据方面存在不足，作者希望开发一种能够有效集成结构化测量、生理波形、长期病史和人群背景信息的新模型。

Method: PRISM是一个多模态语言模型，它结合了：1. 每种特征的数值分词；2. 对长期ECG信号进行时间加权的聚合；3. 一个双模态的人群记忆库。检索机制在表示空间中进行，模型接收相似训练集样本的平均池化嵌入，而不是将单个检索记录输入提示。PRISM还利用了96小时的预到达窗口数据。

Result: 在MDS-ED基准测试中，PRISM在诊断、病情恶化、ICU入院和死亡率预测方面分别取得了86.2%、93.1%、91.8%和92.8%的AUROC。与UniPACT和MDS-ED等基线模型相比，PRISM在仅使用基准窗口输入时表现相当，但在使用相同的五个证据流和冻结编码器进行紧凑融合的模型上，PRISM的AUROC（0.9092）显著高于融合模型（0.7652）。去除预到达历史和检索机制会分别降低AUROC 1.97和0.99个百分点。在外部MC-MED队列上，PRISM的微调性能（0.8611 AUROC）优于本地训练的梯度提升模型（0.8227）和仅源模型（0.7841）。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持结构感知的多模态集成和监督式外部适应对于提高临床预后预测的有效性。PRISM模型在整合多模态数据方面表现出强大的能力，并为未来的临床决策支持系统提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.

</details>


### [67] [Causal Effect Identifiability in Causal Abstractions: A Strict Hierarchy of Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13520)
*Clément Yvernes,Marianne Clausel,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在因果图集合层面研究因果效应可识别性的方法，而不是在单个因果图层面，以解决因果图不完全指定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果推断中，通常需要完全指定的因果图，但在复杂或高维数据中这不现实。因果抽象可以缓解这个问题，但其可识别性研究仍有待深入。

Method: 作者在因果图集合层面形式化了因果效应可识别性的概念，并构建了一个层级结构来阐明这些概念之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现，因果效应可以在一个因果图集合上被识别，即使不存在一个在所有兼容图上都适用的单一因果推导。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了现有文献中的不足和模糊之处，并为因果抽象下的因果效应可识别性研究提供了新的视角和理论基础。

Abstract: Identifying causal effects from observational data typically requires a fully specified causal graph, an assumption that is often unrealistic in complex or high-dimensional settings. Causal abstractions address this limitation by retaining only partial causal information, thereby defining equivalence classes of compatible causal graphs. In this paper, we study causal effect identifiability directly at the level of these graph collections, rather than at the level of individual causal diagrams. We formalize several notions of identifiability in this setting and organize them into a hierarchy that clarifies their relationships and differences. In particular, we establish that a causal effect may be identified over a collection of graphs without admitting a single causal derivation that holds uniformly across all compatible graphs. These results help reveal gaps and clarify ambiguities in the existing literature.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Domain-Routed Supervision: Cross-Teacher Guarding for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13521)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Tianrui Zhu,Sule Bai,Xinwei Yang,Ruiqi Liu,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Yipeng Sun,Gu Simiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Road MOPD 的新方法，用于解决多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）中教师间梯度冲突和能力退化的问题，通过引入非路由教师作为辅助批评者，利用令牌级别的信用分配和梯度投影来协调多教师监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MOPD 方法虽然能将领域特定数据分配给专门的教师，但所有教师仍作用于共享参数，当教师在响应风格和领域专业知识上存在显著差异时，会产生梯度冲突，导致性能下降。

Method: Road MOPD 引入了非路由教师作为辅助跨领域批评者，并在同一学生轨迹上进行监督。它通过令牌级别的信用分配来协调多教师监督，利用教师间的一致性来增强共同支持的令牌校正，并削弱冲突信号。同时，它采用预防性多教师梯度投影，利用来自同一轨迹的辅助梯度来约束潜在冲突的更新，并自适应地控制投影强度，以保留目标域的学习信号。

Result: 在实验中，Road MOPD 减轻了劣势领域的性能下降，在高冲突情况下提高了超过5个百分点，并在低冲突和三领域设置下提升了整体性能。

Conclusion: Road MOPD 是一种有效的解决 MOPD 中教师间冲突和能力退化问题的方法，通过引入跨教师的批评和协调机制，提高了模型的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) by routing domain-specific data to specialized teachers, integrating their capabilities through dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, domain routing only distinguishes the supervision sources for different domains; updates from all domains still act on shared parameters. When teachers differ substantially in response style and domain expertise, supervision that is effective in isolation may still lead to persistent gradient conflicts and capability degradation during joint optimization. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-Teacher Guarding for MOPD (Road MOPD), which introduces non-routed teachers as auxiliary cross-domain critics on the same student trajectories and coordinates multi-teacher supervision through token-level credit assignment and gradient updates.
