Specifically, cross-teacher-guided credit assignment uses teacher agreement to strengthen token-level corrections jointly supported by the teachers and attenuate conflicting signals; preventive multi-teacher gradient projection uses auxiliary gradients from the same trajectories to constrain potentially conflicting updates and adaptively controls projection strength to retain target-domain learning signals. In experiments, Road MOPD mitigates degradation in the disadvantaged domain, gains over 5 percentage points under high conflict, and improves overall performance in low-conflict and three-domain settings.

</details>


### [69] [MAGMA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Embodied Agents under Dynamic Tasks and Execution Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13534)
*Loan Bernat,Matthieu Grard,Ariane Herbulot,Florent Lamiraux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGMA-Bench 是一个用于评估多机器人系统在动态环境下的长期任务执行能力的基准测试，它引入了在线扰动和异构机器人协调，并展示了现有模型在此方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在动态任务执行和多机器人协调方面存在不足，未能充分评估这两者的结合，因此需要一个能够同时处理长期任务、动态环境和异构机器人协调的基准。

Method: 引入MAGMA-Bench基准，该基准支持架构无关性，并通过在线扰动（如执行失败、任务中断、任务更新）和异构机器人能力来评估代理。它使用结构化的技能级别接口来分离任务级别推理、感知和低级控制。评估了本地可部署的开源规划器、多代理状态跟踪策略和前沿模型。

Result: 即使是最强大的模型，在干净的测试用例中也未能成功完成相当一部分任务，并且在在线扰动下成功率进一步下降。监督式学习可以显著提升小型模型的性能，但在更长期的任务和多机器人任务中，性能仍然远低于要求。

Conclusion: 在动态多机器人环境中进行长期任务推理和协调仍然是一个重大挑战。现有模型在面对执行失败、任务中断和任务更新等在线扰动时表现不佳，并且在处理异构机器人能力方面存在困难。尽管可以通过监督学习进行改进，但其在复杂场景下的性能仍需提高。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied agents must maintain task-relevant information, coordinate heterogeneous robots, and adapt their decisions as execution unfolds. Existing benchmarks investigate dynamic task execution and embodied multi-agent coordination, but provide limited evaluation of their intersection. We introduce MAGMA-Bench, an architecture-agnostic benchmark for persistent task reasoning in dynamic multi-robot environments. MAGMA-Bench evaluates agents across increasing task horizons and controlled online perturbations, including execution failures, task interruptions, and mission updates, with scenarios requiring coordination across robots with heterogeneous capabilities. It uses a structured skill-level interface to separate task-level reasoning from perception and low-level control. We evaluate locally deployable open-weight planners, multi-agent state-tracking strategies, and frontier models. Even the strongest evaluated models fail on a substantial fraction of clean episodes, and their success rates decrease further under online perturbations. Supervised adaptation greatly improves a small model, but performance remains substantially lower on longer and multi-robot missions. We release the benchmark, 261,646 task-level training examples, and an architecture-agnostic expert-trace generation pipeline.

</details>


### [70] [Constructing Pareto Frontiers of Optimal Reasoning Termination Methods for Overthinking in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13570)
*Weixin Guan,Liang Li,Shiyu Xie,Jiapeng Liu,Bing Li,Peng Fu,Chengyang Fang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Can Ma,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为步骤截断探测（STP）的方法来量化大型语言模型（LLM）在推理过程中出现的“过度思考”现象，并评估了动态路由、固定预算和动态提前退出等策略的缓解潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型评估方法在测试时扩展推理能力时，未能有效衡量“过度思考”的严重性以及不同缓解策略的理论潜力。

Method: 1. 提出步骤截断探测（STP）：沿固定采样轨迹，重复生成从不同前缀开始的答案，以确定达到最大准确率的最早位置。2. 定义过度思考率和准确率下降：基于STP结果，量化超出最大准确率位置后生成的思考令牌比例和准确率下降幅度。3. 统一框架：将动态路由、固定预算和动态提前退出策略纳入统一框架，量化其理论缓解潜力。4. 构建OTBench：一个包含70.5B推理令牌和相关探测测量数据的基准测试集。

Result: 通过STP测量，作者能够为每种策略构建在通用归一化思考令牌预算下的令牌-准确率帕累托前沿。OTBench基准测试已在六种模型和四个基准组上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 本文成功地量化了LLM的过度思考现象，并提供了一个评估不同推理终止策略有效性的框架和基准测试，为优化模型的推理效率和准确性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Reasoning models can benefit from test-time scaling, but continued reasoning may become ineffective or harmful, a phenomenon termed overthinking. Evaluations against full thinking compare accuracy and token usage but lack references for overthinking severity and strategies' theoretical mitigation potential. To characterize overthinking at the model-task level, we first introduce Step-wise Truncation Probing (STP), repeatedly generating answers from multiple retained prefixes along each fixed sampled reasoning trajectory. Using each trajectory's earliest measured position attaining maximum empirical accuracy as a reference, we define Overthinking Ratio and Accuracy Degradation as the fraction of thinking tokens generated afterward and the accuracy decrease from this reference to full thinking, respectively. Secondly, we then formulate dynamic routing, fixed budgets, and dynamic early exit within a unified framework to quantify the theoretical mitigation potential of different termination strategies. Based on this unified framework, we use the complete STP measurements to construct a token-accuracy Pareto frontier for each strategy under a common normalized thinking-token budget. We further develop OTBench, a reusable benchmark built from the STP process, which produced 70.5B reasoning tokens and the corresponding probing measurements across six models and four benchmark groups.

</details>


### [71] [MENTOR: Learning from Failure with On-Policy Pedagogical Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13594)
*Guanlan Yue,Hang Yu,Xiaoyong Huang,Ruipeng Li,Wei Zhang,Bingchang Liu,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MENTOR的多智能体框架，通过实时诊断失败原因并生成教学性提示来解决强化学习中的“零奖励崩溃”问题，并在数学和代码生成任务中取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的从可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在面对任务难度突然增加时容易出现“零奖励崩溃”，导致训练失败。现有解决方案未能提供适应性的指导来克服这种崩溃。

Method: 提出了一种名为MENTOR的多智能体框架，它充当一个实时的“导师”，能够诊断失败的根本原因，并动态生成有针对性的教学性提示来指导学习代理的下一次尝试。MENTOR可以作为即插即用模块集成到现有的策略优化流程中。

Result: MENTOR在数学和代码生成任务上有效缓解了零奖励崩溃，并且优于现有的最先进基线。在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct上，相对于基础模型，MENTOR取得了高达78.3%的平均相对提升。

Conclusion: MENTOR通过实时的、适应性的教学干预，成功地解决了RLVR中的零奖励崩溃问题，并为大型推理模型的训练提供了一种有效的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a key driver for Large Reasoning Models, but it is vulnerable to "zero-reward collapse", a training failure where a sudden increase in task difficulty causes the learning signal to vanish. We argue that the conventional approach of discarding these failed trajectories is wasteful, as they contain valuable diagnostic information about the model's cognitive gaps, a bright side that can be leveraged for recovery. Existing solutions, however, often regress to static, "off-policy-style" interventions like pre-defined hint libraries or perform post-hoc refinement, failing to provide the adaptive, forward-looking guidance needed to escape the collapse. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift towards on-policy pedagogical intervention and introduce MENTOR, a multi-agent framework that acts as a live mentor. MENTOR diagnoses the root cause of failure in real-time and dynamically generates targeted, instructional prompts to guide the learning agent's next attempt. As a plug-and-play module, MENTOR integrates seamlessly into existing policy optimization pipelines like GRPO without modifying the training infrastructure. Extensive experiments on math and code generation show that MENTOR effectively mitigates zero-reward collapse, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, it achieves massive average relative gains of up to 78.3% on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct over its base model.

</details>


### [72] [Humanize Olympiad Agents: A Multi-Session Workflow for Olympiad Problem Solving with Solve, Review, Wiki, Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13632)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Jing Xiong,Hongzhou Lin,Juihui Chung,Zijian Zhang,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOA（Humanize Olympiads Agent）框架，通过引入独立评审、结构化知识库和验证器，协调多个LLM代理会话，以提高在长期科学和奥林匹克竞赛任务上的表现。HOA在IMO、IPhO、IOI 2026和PutnamBench上取得了优异成绩，并能有效弥合不同基础模型的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在解决长期、复杂的科学和奥林匹克竞赛任务时存在局限性，如早期错误传播、领域知识缺失以及对任务的误解。作者希望通过一个系统性的框架来克服这些挑战。

Method: HOA框架通过三个反馈渠道协调多个LLM代理会话：1. 独立评审员评估并提供反馈；2. 结构化知识库提供任务相关知识；3. 验证器检查生成的证明。文章还评估了使用GPT-5.6 Sol和Kimi-K3两个模型的HOA。

Result: 使用GPT-5.6 Sol，HOA在IMO、IPhO和IOI 2026上获得满分，在IChO 2026上接近满分，并解决了PutnamBench上的所有问题。使用Kimi-K3，HOA在IMO、IPhO和IOI上获得满分，在IChO上达到97.1%。HOA在Lean-Eval排行榜上排名第一，解决了82.6%的问题。

Conclusion: HOA框架通过引入系统性反馈机制，显著提高了LLM代理在复杂推理任务上的表现，即使是基础模型能力存在差异，也能取得与领先闭源模型相当甚至更优的成果，证明了方法论的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly improving on long-horizon scientific and olympiad tasks. Recent systems have produced a Lean-formalized resolution of the Navier--Stokes problem and autonomous solutions to four open questions in the Erdős Conjectures database. Yet neither system releases both its underlying model and complete inference stack. More broadly, a single agent session remains brittle on difficult problems: early errors can propagate, missing domain knowledge can stall progress, and apparent success can mask a misinterpretation or weakening of the original task. We introduce HOA (Humanize Olympiads Agent), an open, flow-level framework that coordinates multiple agent sessions through three complementary feedback channels. First, an independent reviewer evaluates each candidate and provides actionable feedback. Second, a structured wiki supplies task-specific knowledge that may be missing or unreliable in model pretraining. Third, a verifier checks generated Lean proofs against trusted challenge statements. With GPT-5.6 Sol, HOA achieves perfect scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI 2026, near-full marks on the IChO 2026 problem set, and solves all 672 problems (100%) on PutnamBench. On the Lean-Eval leaderboard, HOA ranks first, solving 251 of 304 problems (82.6%). We further evaluate HOA with open models. Using Kimi-K3, HOA achieves full scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI, and a gold-level score of 97.1% on the IChO. These results closely match the closed-source GPT-5.6 Sol + /Goal baseline on the IMO and IPhO, while exceeding it on the IChO and IOI, demonstrating that systematic feedback can help bridge substantial differences in base-model capability.

</details>


### [73] [Learning Size Independent Constraint Models from Input Data and Positive and Negative Example Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13640)
*Helmut Simonis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个约束获取工具，该工具能够从包含正反例的输入数据中生成独立于问题规模的约束程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是开发一种能够自动学习和生成约束模型的工具，以解决多个问题实例，并且生成的模型能够泛化到新的、未见过的数据。

Method: 该系统首先通过识别典型约束模式（包括全局和二元约束）来构建特定规模的模型，然后将这些模型泛化为一个独立的模型，该模型可以处理不同规模的输入数据。最终输出为MiniZinc代码和模型描述。

Result: 该工具在PTHG21挑战赛的示例问题上进行了评估，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该约束获取工具能够从示例中学习并生成可泛化的、独立于规模的约束程序，为解决约束满足问题提供了一种可行的方法。

Abstract: In this paper we describe a Constraint Acquisition Tool that generates size independent constraint programs from input data and positive and negative example solutions of multiple problem instances. The system finds size specific models based on typical constraint patterns over global and binary constraints, then generalizes the models to a size independent model that can be run on unseen input data. The system produces MiniZinc code as well as a textual description of the model. We evaluate the tool on the example problems of the PTHG21 Challenge and show the feasibility of the approach.

</details>


### [74] [A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Tuning of Specialized Neural Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13663)
*Ivan Moloshnikov,Dmitry Kunitsyn,Alexander Sboev,Maksim Skorokhodov,Roman Rybka,Yuanchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体大语言模型（LLM）的框架，用于自动调整机器学习模型中的关键组件，无需更改整体网络结构，并在两个不同领域的任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法通常需要专家手动选择方法类别和关键组件，效率低下。本研究旨在开发一个可迁移的框架，实现关键组件的自动调优。

Method: 构建了一个包含分析师、编码员和记录员三个角色的多智能体LLM框架，配备实验日志和沙盒执行环境。该框架在语义上狭窄但开放的空间中操作，仅为指定组件生成代码，不改变整体网络结构。

Result: 在MNIST和Digits分类任务中，准确率分别提高了13%和6%。在求解非线性薛定谔方程（GNLSE）和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky（KS）方程的边界值问题时，误差分别降低了约2491倍和100多倍（GNLSE），质量漂移降低了14倍（KS）。

Conclusion: 该框架能够跨不同性质的任务自动调整关键组件，且无需改变其内部结构，展示了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Many machine learning approaches are two-level: an expert selects a class of methods, within this class the choice of key components is critical and usually manual. This paper addresses the automatic tuning of these components and proposes a framework transferable across models of different nature. The multi-agent large language model (LLM) framework has three roles---analyst, coder, and recorder~---with an experiment log and a sandboxed execution environment. Unlike systems that optimize the entire ML script or search architectures in a discrete space, the framework operates over an open but semantically narrow space: the agent generates code only for the specified components, leaving the overall network structure unchanged. The approach is validated in two directions: tuning a classification spiking neural network with correlation learning, and solving high-order differential equations with Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN). Across five tasks---spiking classification on MNIST and Digits, and boundary-value problems for the generalized nonlinear Schrödinger equation (GNLSE) and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equations---the agent outperforms the baselines: classification accuracy is 13 percentage points (MNIST) and 6 percentage points (Digits) higher; the error against the analytical single-soliton solution is reduced approximately 2491-fold; the invariant-preservation error drops approximately 325-fold in the selected GNLSE run with a Gaussian perturbation and by more than 100-fold on average over repeated initializations; and the mass drift drops 14-fold for the KS problem with a Gaussian perturbation. The framework thus tunes key components on tasks of different nature without changing its internal structure.

</details>


### [75] [A Step Harder, Not a Step Too Far: The Benefits and Limits of Curriculum Difficulty Progression in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13669)
*Ronghan Li,Fei Chen,Mingyu Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: Curriculum learning typically organizes training samples from easy to hard. In GRPO, medium-difficulty samples are attractive for online selection because they are more likely to yield informative group-relative learning signals, and prioritizing them can improve training efficiency. However, it remains unclear whether persistently selecting such samples also leads to the strongest final model performance, or whether training can benefit from progressing toward harder samples. Meanwhile, sample difficulty changes as the policy evolves, so fixed difficulty estimates can become misaligned with the current policy. We therefore re-estimate sample difficulty under the current policy and, inspired by the easy-to-hard principle of curriculum learning, design and evaluate a staged curriculum that progressively shifts the active difficulty band toward harder samples. Experiments on GSM8K show that persistent medium-difficulty selection does not achieve the strongest observed performance. Across three random seeds, progressing to a moderately harder difficulty band improves test accuracy to $0.6591 \pm 0.0077$, compared with $0.6449 \pm 0.0024$ for the fixed-medium curriculum. However, further progression to a harder difficulty band reduces accuracy to $0.6405 \pm 0.0053$. These results show that staged difficulty progression can outperform persistent medium-difficulty selection, but its benefit is non-monotonic. Effective GRPO curricula can therefore benefit from progressing beyond medium difficulty, while avoiding excessive progression toward overly difficult samples.}

</details>


### [76] [Adaptive DHiSparse: Speculative Decoding over a Load-Adaptive KV Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13708)
*Naji Alazhar,Wassel Alazhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Adaptive DHiSparse的新型长上下文服务方法，该方法通过分层稀疏KV缓存和动态内存分配，显著提高了模型在长上下文和高并发场景下的吞吐量，并支持投机解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文服务方法（如HiSparse）在GPU显存耗尽之前就达到了硬件吞吐量的瓶颈，并且不支持像DSpark和DFlash2这样的投机解码技术。需要一种更高效的KV缓存管理和投机解码支持的方法。

Method: Adaptive DHiSparse通过以下方式工作：1. 支持分层稀疏KV上的投机解码。2. 根据负载动态调整KV历史的驻留策略，在GPU内存充足时保留更多历史，内存紧张时减少保留。3. 通过精确的融合检索来恢复未保留的历史。4. 沿用了DSpark和DFlash2的投机解码机制，如共享缺失行检索、窗口有界环状草稿KV和跨层组的验证阶段重用。

Result: 在100K上下文和128并发下，GLM-5.3模型使用Adaptive DHiSparse（DFlash2）的吞吐量比HiSparse提高了1.45倍，比非投机适应性服务提高了1.27倍。在相同延迟限制下，并发度可提高4倍，输出令牌数可提高2.3至3.7倍。在500K上下文和512并发下，DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash模型使用Adaptive DHiSparse（DSpark）的输出是使用DSpark的驻留服务的1.85倍。在负载变化时，可选驻留将主机获取量从14.2%降至1.4%，并在压力下释放内存。

Conclusion: Adaptive DHiSparse是一种高效的长上下文服务方法，它通过结合分层稀疏KV缓存、动态内存驻留策略和投机解码支持，克服了现有方法的局限性，在长上下文和高并发场景下实现了显著的性能提升，并能有效应对不同负载情况。

Abstract: Long-context serving runs out of KV memory before the hardware reaches its useful throughput. HiSparse stores history in host memory and fetches only the selected entries, but it uses one cache budget at every load and does not handle draft-model speculative decoding such as DSpark and DFlash2. We present Adaptive DHiSparse, which supports speculative decoding over hierarchical sparse KV and adapts residency to load. It protects the required working set, retains additional history while GPU memory is spare, and recovers the rest through exact fused retrieval. DSpark and DFlash2 keep their proposal and acceptance rules: queries share missing-row retrieval, draft KV uses a window-bounded ring, and verification staging is reused across layer groups. At 100K context and concurrency 128, GLM-5.3 with DFlash2 delivers 793 output tokens/s per decode GPU, 1.45$\times$ HiSparse and 1.27$\times$ non-speculative adaptive serving. Held to the same latency bounds as HiSparse, it sustains up to 4$\times$ the concurrency and 2.3 to 3.7$\times$ the output, depending on the per-token bound. On DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash at 500K context and concurrency 512, Adaptive DHiSparse with DSpark delivers 1.85$\times$ the output of resident serving with DSpark. In a load step, optional residency reduces host fetches from 14.2% to 1.4% of selected rows at low load and releases memory under pressure.

</details>


### [77] [A Baseline Assessment for the Agentic Web Technology Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13703)
*Vagner Figueredo de Santana,Diogo Cortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 MASTIF（多智能体系统测试框架）来评估 LLM 驱动的 Agentic Web 技术，并提供了对不同 LLM、协议和框架组合的基线评估。结果表明，选择特定任务的技术栈可以提高任务完成率、效率和社会价值稳定性，并减少冗余和完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 相关技术快速发展，缺乏对不同协议、框架和库如何协同工作的评估，导致开发者和组织在构建 Agentic Web 解决方案时面临挑战（如技能、治理、基础设施）。

Method: 提出 MASTIF（Multi-Agent System TestIng Framework）系统，并使用该系统对 3 种 LLM、3 种协议和 4 种智能体实现框架在 300 个 Mind2Web 基准任务上进行了基线评估。

Result: 结果表明，选择特定任务的技术栈可以在任务完成率、效率得分和社会价值稳定性得分方面带来提升，并减少任务的冗余度和完成时间。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个 Agentic Web 技术评估的基线，包括指标和结果，以帮助设计、评估和开发多智能体系统的研究人员和开发者应对复杂且快速发展的环境。

Abstract: The fast pace in which protocols, frameworks, and technologies are being proposed for LLM-based agents results in a complex scenario for anyone aiming to create state-of-the-art AI solutions. Without an assessment on how different protocols, frameworks, and libraries work together to compose an Agentic Web solution, developers and organizations face different challenges (e.g., skilling, governance, infrastructure) even before becoming part of the Agentic Web ecosystem. In this context, we propose a system for performing Agentic Web evaluations called MASTIF (Multi-Agent System TestIng Framework) and present a baseline assessment of Agentic Web technologies, including 3 LLMs, 3 protocols, and 4 agent implementation frameworks, against 300 Mind2Web benchmark tasks. Results suggest that selecting a task-specific technology stack may result in gains of task completion rate, efficiency score, and social value stability scores; and reductions in verbosity and time to complete tasks. With the presented results, we expect to present a baseline in terms of metrics and results for this complex and fast evolving scenario for those aiming at designing, assessing, and developing multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [78] [UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13783)
*Nazar Andrushko,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究使用视觉语言模型（VLMs）来检测乌克兰战争时期迷因（memes）中的说服性技巧，并引入了一个名为 UkrMeme 的新数据集，同时评估了不同输入模式和解释方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迷因在在线交流，尤其是在政治冲突背景下，作为一种结合视觉和文本的传播信息的方式，发挥着重要作用。本研究旨在研究乌克兰战争时期迷因中的说服性技巧检测。

Method: 研究者引入了 UkrMeme 数据集，包含 200 个乌克兰战争时期的迷因，并标注了说服性技巧。同时，他们还创建了 PTM-UA，这是 Propaganda 950 benchmark 的乌克兰语翻译版本。研究采用零样本提示（zero-shot prompting）设置，评估了 VLMs 在图像、文本和多模态输入上的表现，并使用了分层 F1 指标。最后，研究比较了 Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) 和模型生成的 token 归因方法，并分析了两种解释方法所强调的证据的语义类别。

Result: 研究评估了 VLMs 在检测乌克兰迷因说服性技巧方面的能力，并比较了不同输入模式（图像、文本、多模态）的性能。同时，研究还对两种解释方法（LRP 和 token 归因）进行了比较分析。

Conclusion: 本研究为乌克兰战争时期迷因的说服性技巧检测提供了新的数据集和评估框架，并深入分析了不同 VLMs 和解释方法的有效性，为理解和对抗在线宣传提供了有益的见解。

Abstract: Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.

</details>


### [79] [A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13744)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Ayush Rai,Ekansh Verma,Noel E. O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有 on-policy self-distillation 方法未能有效利用评分信号来纠正错误，我们提出 critique-conditioned self-distillation，通过引入显式的纠错步骤来提高性能，并证明其在测试时和 cold-start 场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 on-policy self-distillation 方法依赖于一个评分教师来提供密集的信号，但我们发现该信号在定位和纠正错误方面存在结构性缺陷，无法有效指导策略改进。

Method: 1. 分析了现有 self-distillation 方法中评分信号（KL散度和token log-ratio）是否具有纠错能力，发现它们无法定位错误或将概率移出错误token。 2. 提出了 critique-conditioned self-distillation：首先生成对rollout的 critique，然后将评分教师的条件设为 critique，从而生成更具纠错能力的信号。 3. 在 GRPO 中测试了 critique 的有效性，包括在测试时恢复错误rollout和在cold-start场景下提供额外优势信号。

Result: 1. 现有评分信号无法有效定位错误或将概率移出错误token。 2. critique-conditioned self-distillation 生成的信号能够定位错误，并在错误span内进行纠正，并将概率推向修正性token。 3. critique 在测试时可以显著提高模型解决数学问题的能力，并在cold-start GRPO中提供比可验证奖励更强的优势信号。

Conclusion: 仅凭评分信号不足以实现有效的 on-policy self-distillation。通过引入显式的 critique 步骤，我们可以生成更具信息量和纠错能力的信号，从而显著提升模型在复杂推理任务中的表现，尤其是在数据稀疏或需要精细错误修正的场景下。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

</details>


### [80] [Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13840)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为PAGE的框架，利用人工智能（包括生成模型）来分析大量的漫画数据，以促进漫画研究。该框架能够解析漫画页面、文本和书目信息，并使用视觉语言模型进行注释，然后基于这些注释进行跨档案的变化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的漫画研究方法耗时且局限于单本书籍的研究。作者认为，利用人工智能技术可以对海量的漫画数据进行整体性研究，从而促进对漫画这一重要视觉媒介的理解。

Method: PAGE框架通过以下步骤实现自动化分析：1. 将漫画扫描件解析为分格、文本和书目记录。2. 使用视觉语言模型和编码手册对每页进行注释。3. 将部分注释结果与人工标注进行比对。4. 控制出版商和类型因素，分析档案中的变化。该框架在1938-1956年的美国漫画书和87卷日本漫画上进行了验证。

Result: 通过PAGE框架，研究人员能够：1. 结合文本搜索和视觉阅读，研究大众媒体如何描绘政治权力。2. 衡量整个行业的页面视觉形式，并识别个体艺术家的风格。3. 追踪不同漫画传统中的对话者，并与人工标注进行核对。

Conclusion: 人工智能可以作为漫画研究的有力工具，不仅能够深化对漫画媒介的理解，还能提高档案的可搜索性、为视障读者提供便利、帮助保存脆弱的漫画资料以及识别未署名的艺术家。作者呼吁将人工智能纳入漫画研究、保护和传播的未来议程中。

Abstract: Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938--1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page's visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

</details>


### [81] [X-AgentRed: Red-Teaming Personal Agents in Evolving Multi-Service Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13886)
*Yeonsung Jung,Zhiyuan Wang,Zifan Wang,Vítor Albiero,Benjamin Newman,Edoardo Debenedetti,Ivan Evtimov,Ninareh Mehrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-AgentRed 是一个创新的自主红队框架，通过构建工具依赖图并利用执行反馈，有效地发现了个人智能代理在使用工具时可能出现的有害的跨服务组合路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队方法在发现智能代理可能产生的有害结果时存在局限性，因为它们依赖于LLM的生成能力，容易受到训练数据的偏见影响，并且可能忽视新颖的跨服务交互。

Method: X-AgentRed 构建了一个包含工具依赖关系、潜在危害和执行先决条件的定向图。然后，它从图中采样“配方”来创建模拟场景，并在模拟执行中收集反馈。通过工具替换和先决条件精炼，框架能够自我改进，以发现更多有害路径并提高工具覆盖率。

Result: 在四种前沿的智能代理模型上，X-AgentRed 比 STAC 发现了多出 1.77 倍的有害路径，并且工具覆盖率平均高出 2.99 倍，显示出更强的发现能力和多样性。

Conclusion: X-AgentRed 是一种有效的自主红队框架，通过系统地探索工具之间的依赖关系和利用执行反馈，能够更全面地发现个人智能代理在使用工具时可能产生的有害跨服务交互，克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Personal agents increasingly connect to services across the web and applications by using tools. As the effects of their actions accumulate and interact across services, unintended or harmful outcomes can arise when agents fulfill benign user requests. Proactively identifying these compositional failures requires systematically exploring diverse cross-service paths. Existing red-teaming methods often simply prompt LLMs to identify potentially harmful paths from task and environment descriptions or decompose harmful objectives specified by humans or LLMs into seemingly benign action combinations. However, generation is biased toward patterns learned during training, potentially favoring familiar tool combinations and leaving novel cross-service interactions underexplored. To address this, we propose X-AgentRed, a self-evolving red-teaming framework that discovers harmful tool call paths through a graph of tool dependencies and expands and refines them using execution feedback. By analyzing each tool's effects, potential harms, and preconditions for execution, the red-teaming agent constructs a directed graph that captures how one tool's effects can satisfy another's execution preconditions. Recipes sampled from this graph are instantiated as realistic scenarios in which agents can produce harmful outcomes. Execution feedback enables self-improvement by diversifying paths that produce harm through tool substitution and refining preconditions at blocked steps in unsuccessful paths to enable further execution. Across four frontier agentic models, X-AgentRed achieves $1.77\times$ the harmful path rate of STAC and explores a more diverse set of tools, yielding $2.99\times$ the tool coverage on average.

</details>


### [82] [RATED: Reliability-Aware AI Agent Tool Retrieval via Execution Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13889)
*Weiliang Chen,Song Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 RATED 的工具检索框架，该框架通过结合语义匹配和基于执行动态的可靠性自适应来提高 AI 代理对可用工具的检索准确性，并在 EcoAgent 基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 工具检索方法主要关注语义相关性，而忽略了工具在执行时的可用性，导致 AI 代理行为不可靠。

Method: RATED 框架结合了：1. 使用冻结的双编码器和对比学习进行语义匹配。2. 使用轻量级上下文多臂老虎机模块，根据实时执行反馈对候选工具进行重新排序。

Result: 在 EcoAgent 基准测试中，RATED 相较于 ToolLLM API 检索器，在真实查询上实现了 52.2% 的相对 MRR 提升。在 API 故障率 30% 的严峻场景下，故障选择率从 86.2% 降低到 2.9%，且推理延迟增加不到 1 毫秒。

Conclusion: RATED 框架能够有效地结合语义匹配和动态可靠性适应，显著提高了 AI 代理在工具可用性动态变化时的检索性能，同时保持了低延迟。

Abstract: As AI agents increasingly rely on external tools, accurate retrieval is essential for reliable behavior. Existing methods optimize semantic relevance but largely ignore tool availability during execution. To address this gap, we propose RATED (Reliability-Aware Tool Retrieval via Execution Dynamics), a retrieval framework that combines robust semantic matching with dynamic reliability adaptation. RATED employs a frozen dual-encoder with contrastive fine-tuning for semantic matching, and a lightweight contextual bandit module that re-ranks candidates based on real-time execution feedback. This design enables rapid adaptation to runtime tool failures without expensive model retraining. We introduce EcoAgent, a benchmark of 5,172 real-world tools and over 210,000 query-tool pairs for offline retrieval and dynamic reliability evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that RATED achieves a 52.2% relative MRR improvement over the ToolLLM API retriever on real-world queries, and reduces fault selection rate from 86.2% to 2.9% under severe API outage scenarios with a 30% failure rate, while incurring negligible latency overhead of less than 1 ms over encode-only inference. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Elliotes/EcoAgent.

</details>


### [83] [Can Language Models Play the Wikipedia Game?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13948)
*Alexander Hickey,Joanna Knight,James Bishop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员利用维基百科游戏（Wikipedia Game）作为基准，评估了不同语言模型在信息检索能力上的表现，发现大型语言模型（LLMs）和句子嵌入模型在检索相似性上表现可比，但均未显著超越人类玩家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型虽然能处理海量文档，但在需要专业领域知识的信息检索任务上表现受限。研究旨在评估和比较不同语言模型在这类任务上的能力，并提供一个可在本地运行的基准测试框架。

Method: 1. 使用维基百科游戏作为信息检索的类比任务，玩家通过点击链接在文章间跳转至目标文章。2. 训练和评估了包括句子嵌入模型和大型语言模型（LLMs）在内的多种模型。3. 在五种维基百科语言和五种学术文献数据集上进行测试。4. 构建了基于OpenAlex的维基百科页面和论文引文的本地大型图谱，以支持本地化测试。5. 将模型表现与人类玩家和TF-IDF基准进行比较。

Result: 1. 尽管GPT-3.5 mini、GPT-4o、QwQ:32B和DeepSeek-R1:70B等最佳模型取得了与人类相当的成功，但没有模型能够显著超越人类玩家。2. 模型在不同语言和学科领域上的表现及排名存在差异，这表明模型的通用性和特定领域性能的权衡。

Conclusion: 维基百科游戏能够有效地评估语言模型在信息检索方面的能力，尤其是在需要领域知识的任务中。虽然大型语言模型在某些方面取得了与人类接近的性能，但仍有提升空间，并且模型在不同语言和领域上的表现不尽相同，提示用户在实际应用中需考虑这些因素。

Abstract: Language models increasingly support or automate information retrieval from document collections too large to index. This task is difficult when it requires specialist linguistic or scientific knowledge. We used the Wikipedia Game to test language models on an analogous information retrieval task. In the game, players must reach a target article using only article links. We compared sentence embedding models and LLMs across five Wikipedia languages and five academic-literature datasets. We construct very large local graphs of Wikipedia pages and of paper citations using OpenAlex and provide code to build and navigate them. This allows the study of language models' navigation capabilities without relying on third-party APIs. We compared the models with human and TF-IDF benchmarks. The Wikipedia Game format enables robust comparisons across techniques, languages, and domains. No model significantly outperformed humans, although the best models (GPT-o3 mini, GPT-4o, QwQ:32B, and DeepSeek-R1:70B) achieved comparable success. Performance and rankings varied across languages and topics, with implications for practitioners utilising these techniques.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [CORAL-KV: Compact Cross-Layer Prediction and Residual Coding for Autoregressive Video KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12514)
*Albert Ge,Akash Haridas,Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Ian Colbert,Frederic Sala,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Coral-KV 的视频 KV 缓存压缩框架，通过利用多层级联关系来预测并量化残差，有效缓解了长视频生成中的内存瓶颈，并在不显著增加延迟的情况下提高了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 KV 缓存压缩方法（如基于聚类的方法）未能充分利用跨层级联关系，且易导致重建误差累积，影响长视频生成质量。作者发现视频模型中不同缓存层之间存在强烈的依赖关系，这促使了 Coral-KV 的研究。

Method: Coral-KV 框架首先利用当前层的缓存状态来预测后续层的键值（KV）向量，然后对预测残差进行量化。其核心是一种紧凑的块对角加低秩（block-DPLR）预测器，其中块对角部分处理单个注意力头内部的通道关系，低秩部分则捕捉头之间的依赖关系。该框架还支持在视频间重用预测器或为特定视频微调轻量级预测器以提高保真度。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 和 MAGI-1 数据集上的评估显示，Coral-KV 的可重用配置在分别降低 48% 和 37% 缓存内存的同时，PSNR 分数分别提高了 0.32 dB 和 4.95 dB。视频自适应配置在 MAGI-1 上可将 PSNR 提高 3.77 dB，同时减少 33.7% 的内存。此外，Coral-KV 在长时间生成中保持了更高的重建保真度，减少了时间不连续性，并且生成延迟与现有方法相当。

Conclusion: Coral-KV 框架通过引入跨层级联预测和残差量化，成功解决了现有 KV 缓存压缩方法在长视频生成中存在的误差累积问题，实现了内存效率和生成质量的显著提升，并提供了灵活的保真度-内存权衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models maintain a growing attention key-value (KV) cache, making memory a key bottleneck for long video generation. Recently, clustering-based quantization has emerged as an effective method to compress video KV caches by grouping similar key and value vectors then quantizing residual errors. However, when later frames attend to the compressed history, small reconstruction errors can compound as the video grows. Beyond the within-layer similarity exploited by clustering, we find strong dependencies across cache layers in video models. This motivates Coral-KV - Calibrated Offline, Residual-coded, and Adapted Locally - a KV-cache compression framework that uses cache states in each layer to predict keys and values in subsequent layers, then quantizes the remaining prediction residuals. We construct compact block-diagonal-plus-low-rank (block-DPLR) predictors: block-diagonal components model channel relationships within each attention head, while a global low-rank term models dependencies between heads. Coral-KV exposes a flexible fidelity-memory trade-off: it can reuse its predictors across videos or refit lightweight block-diagonal predictors to the current video for higher fidelity. Across extensive evaluations on both HY-WorldPlay and MAGI-1, our reusable configuration improves PSNR over QVG-4 by 0.32 dB and 4.95 dB, respectively, while using 48% and 37% less cache memory. Against QVG-Pro, our video-adapted configuration improves MAGI-1 PSNR by 3.77 dB while using 33.7% less cache memory. Coral-KV also maintains higher reconstruction fidelity over extended generation and reduces temporal discontinuities while retaining comparable generation latency.

</details>


### [85] [TraceICL: Thread-Resolved Demonstration Curation with Consistency Calibration for Robust Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12516)
*Yigit Yilmaz,Elena Petrova,Mehmet Kaya,Lucia Rossi,Amir Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AVA的训练无关框架，用于改进冻结的大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的多模态上下文学习（ICL），通过解决演示绑定错误、冗余和噪声问题，提高了在五个基准测试上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态ICL方法存在演示绑定错误、冗余演示和噪声答案等问题，导致其性能脆弱。研究旨在解决这些问题，提高多模态ICL的鲁棒性和有效性。

Method: AVA框架包含三个阶段：1. 线程解析分解器（TRD）分割和重组上下文，恢复图像-文本绑定。2. 影响评分演示选择器（IDS）评估候选演示的影响力，去除冗余并排序。3. 弱到强一致性校准（WSC）模块通过弱模型重构置信度对演示进行评分，并在解码时软加权其贡献。整个过程无需梯度计算，LVLM保持冻结。

Result: 在五个视觉-语言基准测试上，AVA比最强的基线平均提高了1.7个点，在VizWiz上最高提升2.4个点，缩小了ScienceQA-IMG的性能差距，并在30%的噪声演示下仍能保持大部分性能提升。延迟开销为26%，可通过离线缓存分摊。

Conclusion: AVA是一种有效的训练无关框架，可以显著提高冻结LVLM的多模态ICL性能，解决演示绑定、冗余和噪声问题，同时保持LVLM的冻结状态。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal in-context learning (ICL) lets frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) acquire new tasks from a handful of image-text-answer demonstrations, yet it remains fragile: demonstrations bind to the wrong images, redundant exemplars crowd out informative ones, and noisy answers are copied verbatim into predictions. We present AVA, a training-free framework that organizes the demonstration pipeline of multimodal ICL into three composable stages. A Thread-Resolved Decomposer (TRD) segments the interleaved context into per-sample threads and re-stitches them with explicit boundaries, restoring image-text binding. An Influence-scored Demonstration Selector (IDS) estimates each candidate's marginal influence with one extra forward pass, removes redundancy, and orders the survivors by descending value. A Weak-to-Strong Consistency Calibration (WSC) module scores each demonstration by a weak sibling model's reconstruction confidence and softly reweights its contribution during decoding. On five vision-language benchmarks with interleaved 4-shot prompts, AVA improves the strongest same-setting baseline by +1.7 points on average, with gains of up to +2.4 points on VizWiz, shrinks the require-visual performance gap on ScienceQA-IMG, and retains most of its gain under 30% noisy demonstrations, at a 26% latency overhead that an offline cache amortizes. No gradients are ever computed; the LVLM stays frozen.

</details>


### [86] [TerraAgent: Demonstration-Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Long-Context Remote Sensing Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12517)
*Jingwen Huang,Yiming Fang,Hanxiao Wu,Boyuan He,Keyi Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AVA框架，用于提升遥感视觉问答（RS-VQA）的准确性和视觉依赖性，解决了当前RS-MLLMs评估指标的不足，能够区分基于图像的回答和仅基于先验知识的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥感多模态大语言模型（RS MLLMs）主要依赖准确率评估，无法判断回答是否真正依赖图像证据，导致模型可能仅凭先验知识答题，且在处理长上下文、多尺度、多时相数据时容易出现错误累积。

Method: AVA是一个训练无关（training-free）的框架，包含三个模块：GDR（GSD分桶、场景图演示检索与过滤）、MACE（查询分解为证据类型子问题DAG，调用感知工具，利用证据账本记录溯源）和CVA（通过消融、替换等反事实扰动审计最终答案，计算视觉依赖分数，并进行校准和强制重新查询）。

Result: 在RSVQA-LR/HR、VRSBench和LHRS-Bench数据集上，AVA将平均准确率提升了最高2.40个点，视觉依赖分数从0.28提升至0.49，先验知识回答率从46.2%降至21.7%，幻觉率从17.1%降至9.8%。

Conclusion: AVA框架通过改进演示检索、子问题分解、多工具融合以及反事实审计，有效解决了RS-VQA中的图像证据依赖性问题，显著提高了回答的准确性、视觉依赖性和可信度，同时保持了计算效率且无需微调。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual question answering (RS-VQA) requires answers that truly depend on image evidence, yet current remote sensing multimodal large language models (RS MLLMs) are still evaluated mainly by accuracy, which cannot reveal whether a correct answer was produced without using the image. This setting introduces three challenges: demonstrations retrieved across different ground sample distances (GSD), sensors, and city textures may be poorly matched; real queries often involve long visual contexts from multi-scale tiles, multi-temporal frames, and retrieved knowledge, where evidence can be diluted and errors can propagate through multi-step reasoning; and prior-only answering remains invisible to standard metrics. We present AVA, a training-free framework that wraps a frozen RS MLLM with three modules. GDR performs GSD-bucketed, scene-graph-based demonstration retrieval and filters demonstrations whose answers are unsupported by their images. MACE decomposes each query into an evidence-typed sub-question DAG, routes sub-questions to suitable perception tools, and records verified provenance in a shared evidence ledger with bounded re-perception. CVA audits the final answer through counterfactual perturbations of cited evidence, including ablation and swap, and computes a visual-dependency score for confidence calibration and evidence-forcing re-query. On RSVQA-LR/HR, VRSBench, and LHRS-Bench, AVA improves average accuracy by up to 2.40 points over the strongest same-setting baselines, raises the visual-dependency score from 0.28 to 0.49, reduces the prior-only answer rate from 46.2 percent to 21.7 percent, and lowers the hallucination rate from 17.1 percent to 9.8 percent, with 2.6x the cost of a single backbone forward pass and no fine-tuning.

</details>


### [87] [MIRAGE: Metric Inference and Reliability-Aware Gaussian Estimation from Feed-Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12568)
*Xinzhe Wang,Changjian Jiang,Kaiwen Song,Xudong Li,Kerui Ren,Ran Yi,Lizhuang Ma,Chunhua Shen,Linning Xu,Tao Lu,Mulin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE 融合了前馈 3D 先验和 LiDAR-IMU 传感信息，实现了高精度度量尺度估计和高质量的高斯重建，解决了现有方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3D 重建方法缺乏度量尺度和长期轨迹稳定性，而 LiDAR-IMU 方法虽然度量精度高但缺乏图像一致性。因此，需要一种能结合两者优势的方法。

Method: MIRAGE 将前馈视觉几何与 LiDAR-IMU 度量运动相结合，并通过对应关系支持和跨模态一致性进行在线姿态校正。

Result: MIRAGE 在 Oxford Spires、M2DGR 和 INS 数据集上实现了精确的度量轨迹估计，优于 COLMAP。此外，作者还构建了一个新的数据集，用于评估大规模室内场景的单目重建方法。

Conclusion: MIRAGE 能够同时实现准确的度量姿态估计和高保真度的高斯重建，克服了单一模态方法的局限性，并为大规模室内单目重建提供了一个新的基准。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D priors enable high-quality reconstruction from image streams but often lack reliable metric scale and suffer from long-term trajectory drift. In contrast, LiDAR--inertial estimation provides metrically accurate motion but lacks the image-space consistency needed for high-fidelity reconstruction. We propose $MIRAGE$, which anchors feed-forward visual geometry to LiDAR--IMU metric motion and regulates online pose corrections according to correspondence support and cross-modal agreement, jointly enabling accurate metric pose estimation and high-fidelity Gaussian reconstruction. Experiments on Oxford Spires, M2DGR and INS show that MIRAGE achieves accurate metric trajectory estimation, outperforming COLMAP, a widely used but computationally expensive reference for vision-only benchmarks. Motivated by the scarcity of long-horizon monocular benchmarks for large-scale indoor reconstruction, we further build a multimodal data-collection platform and use MIRAGE to establish accurate 6-DoF reference trajectories for a new vision-only benchmark. We benchmark recent monocular large-scale reconstruction methods on this dataset, providing a systematic evaluation of their performance in long-horizon indoor scenes.

</details>


### [88] [Scaling Multi-Teacher Distillation for Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12574)
*Sofiène Boutaj,Pierre Marza,Varun Belagali,Dimitris Samaras,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种改进的多教师知识蒸馏方法，通过学习教师特定的表示来解决教师数量增加导致的学生模型性能下降问题，并释放了一系列名为OMNI的高效基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的 MTD 方法在教师数量增加时性能不佳，而数字病理学等领域需要利用多个强大的基础模型。因此，研究动机是提高 MTD 方法的可扩展性，使其能有效处理多个教师模型。

Method: 提出了一种新的 MTD 方法，其核心是学习教师特定的表示。具体实现包括：引入可学习的教师 token、定制的注意力机制、额外的混合专家层以及对比损失。这些设计旨在帮助学生模型更好地区分和学习每个教师的知识。

Result: 改进后的 MTD 方法在教师数量增加时表现更好，能够训练出比教师模型更紧凑但性能相当甚至更优的学生模型。在大量的任务（39个，涵盖图块和幻灯片级别）和基础模型（超过10个）上进行了实证验证。研究人员发布了一系列名为 OMNI 的新模型，它们继承了10个 SOTA 教师模型的知识。

Conclusion: 学习教师特定的表示是 MTD 方法可扩展的关键。所提出的方法能够有效整合多个教师的知识，训练出高效且性能强大的学生模型。此外，研究分析了学习到的教师特定表示的互补性，解释了它们在下游任务中易于聚合的原因。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation allows transferring knowledge from multiple "teacher" networks to a single "student" network. This method is promising in fields such as digital pathology, where many powerful foundation models were proposed recently. However, as we show in this paper, the standard multi-teacher distillation approach reacts poorly to an increase in the number of teachers used to train a student encoder. We demonstrate that learning teacher-specific representations is a key to scaling in MTD. Importantly, different design choices, such as learnable teacher tokens, a tailored attention scheme, additional mixture-of-experts layers and a contrastive loss, are proposed to better learn such teacher-specific representations. We show that our method better scales with respect to the number of considered teachers, allowing us to train compact student encoders, up to 10x more efficient than larger teacher foundation models, while matching their performance and even outperforming them on a large set of tile-level and slide-level tasks. We conduct a thorough empirical validation, evaluating more than 10 foundation models on 39 tasks spanning tile and slide levels. As a result, we release a new collection of strong and efficient foundation models, named OMNI, trained from the knowledge of 10 state-of-the-art foundation models. We finally conduct an analysis of the learned teacher-specific representations, highlighting their complementarity and explaining why they can be easily aggregated at downstream time.

</details>


### [89] [MemReg: Streaming Outdoor LiDAR Point Cloud Registration with Hybrid Memory Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12624)
*Jiuming Liu,Mengmeng Liu,Guangming Wang,Lihao Liu,Michael Ying Yang,Yunpeng Zhang,Francesco Nex,Hao Cheng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 MemReg 的新型多帧户外点云配准网络，通过引入与姿态相关的内存缓冲区来增强流式 LiDAR 扫描的配准性能，有效解决了传统配准方法中忽略时间关联性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外 LiDAR 配准方法多采用两帧配准范式，忽略了流式点云序列中的时间相关性，导致在数据稀疏、低重叠等情况下性能受限。

Method: 设计了两种混合内存缓冲区（隐式内存特征缓冲区和显式内存姿态缓冲区）来存储和更新姿态相关的时序特征。开发了一个动态历史加权模块，自适应地融合当前帧和历史帧的姿态相关特征。

Result: 在 KITTI、nuScenes 和 Apollo-Southbay 三个户外数据集上的实验表明，MemReg 达到了最先进的性能，优于所有两帧配准方法和多帧 SLAM 系统。此外，该方法在室内配准任务（3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, ScanNet）上也表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的 MemReg 方法通过利用长时序 LiDAR 数据中的全局上下文信息，有效提升了户外点云配准的鲁棒性和准确性，尤其是在处理稀疏测量、过滤异常值和低重叠问题上。该方法在多种数据集和场景下均表现出优越的性能和良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Prior outdoor LiDAR registration works are commonly limited by the pair-wise input paradigm, which neglects temporal correlations inherent within streaming sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-frame outdoor point cloud registration network for streaming LiDAR scans enhanced with pose-related memory buffers. The key observation is that long-term temporal LiDAR sequences can provide rich global contextual information to complete sparse measurements, filter outliers, and address low-overlap problems, thereby boosting registration performance. Specifically, two hybrid memory buffers are designed, including an implicit memory feature buffer and an explicit memory pose buffer, to store and dynamically update pose-related temporal features. Moreover, a novel dynamic history weighting module is developed to adaptively fuse current and history pose-related features. Extensive experiments on three outdoor datasets, including KITTI, nuScenes, and Apollo-Southbay, demonstrate state-of-the-art performance of MemReg, surpassing all previous pair-wise methods and also multi-frame SLAM systems. Our method also generalizes surprisingly well to multiview indoor registration scenarios with rather competitive performance on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, and ScanNet. Code will be released at https://github.com/IRMVLab/MemReg.

</details>


### [90] [Mind the Temporal Gap: Evaluating Video Models for Temporal Understanding in Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12575)
*Shiva Sreeram,Erfan Aasi,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Alaa Maalouf,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了现代视频-语言模型在驾驶等安全关键场景中对时间动态的捕捉能力，发现在标准提示、视觉提示、少样本提示和思维链提示下，模型仍不可靠且受类别偏差影响。相比之下，轻量级的 LoRA 微调和基于中间视觉特征的探针可以恢复强大的运动识别能力，表明模型内部存在运动相关线索，但最终的视频到语言路径未能有效利用。研究结论是，在驾驶场景中，现有的视频-语言模型在时间推理方面仍然不可靠，更像是受偏差驱动的帧级识别器，而非可靠的时间世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频-语言模型被广泛应用于视频推理，但其在安全关键领域（如驾驶）对时间动态的捕捉能力尚不明确。研究旨在探究这些模型在驾驶场景下的时间推理鲁棒性。

Method: 作者通过控制的自身运动任务，要求模型识别驾驶视频中的动作。他们测试了多种提示策略（标准、视觉、少样本、思维链）和模型（新旧版本），并对比了轻量级 LoRA 微调和基于中间视觉特征的简单探针的效果。

Result: 发现标准提示策略在识别驾驶动作方面不可靠且依赖于特定任务和模型，存在持续的类别偏差。较新的模型在少样本学习方面表现出任务特定增益。而 LoRA 微调和中间视觉特征探针能显著提高运动识别性能，表明模型内部存在有效的运动线索，只是未能被最终的视频到语言路径充分利用。

Conclusion: 在驾驶场景下，现有的视频-语言模型在时间推理方面仍不可靠，其行为更像是受偏差驱动的帧级识别器，而非可靠的时间世界模型。模型内部的运动信息并未被有效地用于生成语言输出。

Abstract: Modern video-language models are increasingly treated as general-purpose video reasoners, yet it remains unclear whether they robustly capture temporal dynamics in safety-critical settings such as driving. We study this question through controlled ego-motion tasks that require identifying actions from driving videos. Across video-language models, we find that standard prompting, visual prompting, few-shot prompting, and chain-of-thought prompting remain unreliable and highly task- and model-dependent, with persistent class biases, although newer models show substantial task-specific few-shot gains. In contrast, lightweight LoRA finetuning and simple probes trained on intermediate visual features recover strong motion-discriminative performance, indicating that motion-relevant cues are often present inside the models but are not reliably used by the final video-to-language pathway. These results suggest that, in driving, current video-language models remain unreliable temporal reasoners and often behave more like frame-level, bias-driven recognizers than dependable temporal world models.

</details>


### [91] [ForcingBooster: Adaptive Patch-wise Denoising for Fast Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12625)
*Zekai Gu,Peng Wang,Jiahao Lu,Yongsen Mao,Cheng Lin,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 \methodname{} 的新框架，通过动态分配去噪步骤到视频的各个区域来提高视频生成效率，使易处理区域提前停止去噪，而复杂区域获得更多处理，从而在不显著降低图像质量的情况下，将去噪步骤减少约 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视频扩散模型在生成视频时，对所有空间区域分配相同的计算资源，这忽略了视频内容的空间异质性（即某些区域比其他区域更容易去噪）。这种统一的计算分配导致对易处理区域的计算冗余，而对难处理区域的去噪又不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为 \methodname{} 的通用框架，该框架能够动态地将去噪步骤分配给视频的各个区域。易处理区域可以提前停止去噪以提高效率，而难处理区域则获得更多的精炼步骤。该方法通过一个轻量级的路由模块实现，兼容大多数现有的少步自回归扩散模型，并且只需要少量训练。

Result: 通过实验证明，\methodname{} 能够显著减少在视频块上应用的去噪步骤总数，平均减少约 50%，同时保持与四步基线模型相当的视觉质量。

Conclusion: \methodname{} 是一种有效且轻量级的框架，可以动态地将去噪计算分配给视频的不同区域，从而在加速视频生成的同时，保持高质量的视觉效果，解决了现有视频扩散模型计算分配不均的问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have become a leading approach to real-time interactive video generation and video world modeling. However, existing methods typically allocate computation uniformly across space when generating the next video chunk, updating all patches with the same denoising schedule and number of function evaluations. This ignores the spatial heterogeneity of video content: static, simple, or recurring regions are often easier to denoise, while other regions require further refinement or additional context. A uniform budget thus leads to redundant computation on easy patches, whereas reducing the fixed step count can leave difficult patches insufficiently refined. We introduce \methodname{}, a general framework that dynamically allocates denoising steps to individual patches: easy patches stop early to improve efficiency, while difficult patches receive more refinement steps. This design accelerates generation with negligible quality degradation. Our method is compatible with most existing few-step autoregressive diffusion models, requiring only a lightweight routing module with modest training. Experiments demonstrate that \methodname{} reduces the total number of denoising steps applied across video patches by about 50\% while maintaining visual quality comparable to the four-step baseline.

</details>


### [92] [Feature Competition and Its Representational Origins in Vision Language Models: Evidence from the Stroop Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12650)
*Nurbüke Teker,Rui Xiao,Zeynep Akata,Shuchen Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究发现视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理具有冲突文本和颜色信息的图像时，倾向于优先考虑文本信息，而不是颜色信息。这种偏差在模型内部的表示空间中也有体现，文本线索比颜色线索更强且更具可分离性。研究人员通过实验证明了这种特征不平衡的影响，并强调了理解不同视觉特征对 VLM 行为的影响的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当图像包含多个与问题相关的特征时，视觉语言模型（VLM）如何做出选择并生成响应是一个尚未解决的问题。研究者希望借鉴心理学中的 Stroop 任务来研究 VLM 在面对此类冲突信息时的行为。

Method: 研究者将 Stroop 任务改编为一个基准测试，并使用对比和生成式 VLM 进行实验。他们分析了模型在行为层面的响应，并深入研究了模型内部的表示空间，以理解不同特征（文本和颜色）的影响力差异。

Result: 研究发现，在 Stroop 图像中，VLM 普遍表现出对文本信息的偏好，即使文本和颜色信息相冲突。在嵌入空间中，文本线索比颜色线索具有更强的表示和更高的可分离性。这种差异也体现在干预实验中，引导模型关注文本比引导模型关注颜色更容易。

Conclusion: 本研究在行为和表示层面都证明了同一图像中的不同视觉特征对 VLM 行为具有不平等的 影响。VLM 会对不同特征赋予不同程度的显著性，这对于理解 VLM 在实际应用中的表现至关重要。

Abstract: When you ask a vision-language model (VLM) a question with an image, it answers your question based on the image. But to act means to choose: saying one thing means choosing to respond not say another. This becomes an issue when the image contains multiple features that equally relate to the question at hand: how would VLMs choose to respond? This problem is precisely studied by the classical Stroop task in psychology: among a set of deliberately designed images whose text and color conflicts (e.g., \textcolor{blue}{RED}), participants are instructed to report the color. We adapt this psychological paradigm to a benchmark and prompted a range of contrastive and generative VLMs on their behavior. Across models, we observe a persistent bias toward the feature of text in Stroop images: when text and color disagree, models preferentially generate responses centered to the text rather than color. We then opened the black box and examined the source of this bias and why certain features influences the model's behavior more than others. In the embedding space, we found that textual cues are represented more strongly and separably than color cues. This representational asymmetry predicts intervention success: steering models toward text-related directions is substantially easier than steering them toward color-related directions. Using a classical psychological paradigm, we demonstrate, at both behavioral and representational levels, that distinct features within the same image can exert unequal influence on VLM behavior. Our findings suggest that VLMs assign different degrees of saliency to different features within the same image, highlighting the need to understand how cue-specific saliency shapes their performance in widely used downstream applications.

</details>


### [93] [WoodBR-Cross: A public wood-image dataset and individual-tree-disjoint benchmark for species identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12676)
*Daniel Casanova,Alexandre Bahia Gontijo,Arnaldo Candido Junior,Silvana Nisgoski,Pedro Luiz de Paula Filho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个名为 WoodBR-Cross 的木材图像基准数据集，用于解决现有木材图像识别基准中可能存在的训练集和测试集来自同一棵树的问题。通过对数据集进行精心划分，并使用多种深度学习模型进行实验，结果表明该数据集能够更准确地评估模型在未见过树木上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有木材图像识别基准可能因训练集和测试集包含来自同一棵树的图像而导致性能估计过于乐观。研究旨在创建一个更具挑战性、更能反映实际应用场景的基准。

Method: 构建了包含 4,875 张图像的 WoodBR-Cross 数据集，并将其划分为三个任务：所有类别的图像分区（A）、物种子集图像分区（B1）以及物种子集的独立树木分区（B2）。在 B2 任务中，同一棵树的所有图像都被分配到同一数据集中，确保训练、验证和测试集之间没有共享的树木。评估了 ResNet-50、ConvNeXt-Tiny 和 ViT-B/16 等模型，并使用了手工特征和冻结特征的基线模型进行对比。

Result: 在 B2 任务中，经过验证选择的 ConvNeXt-Tiny 模型在树木级别 macro-F1 上达到了 0.8621 ± 0.0317 的性能。使用冻结的 DINOv2 特征则取得了 0.9000 ± 0.0722 的性能。这些结果是跨越三个种子平均折叠均值计算得到的。

Conclusion: WoodBR-Cross 数据集及其评估方法能够更真实地估计模型在识别来自未见过树木上的木材图像时的性能，为木材图像识别领域的研究提供了更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Wood-image benchmarks can yield optimistic estimates when images from the same tree occur in both training and test sets. WoodBR-Cross contains 4,875 transverse-surface images in 38 operational taxonomic classes, linked to 303 distinct trees identified by numeric catalogue witness codes (codigos-testemunho) in the Brazilian Forest Service collection. A species-level subset comprises 3,406 images, 20 classes and 214 trees. Three tasks compare duplicate-aware image partitions for all classes (A), image partitions for the species subset (B1), and individual-tree-disjoint partitions for that subset (B2). In B2, all images from each tree are kept together, so no tree is shared across training, validation or test within a fold. ResNet-50, ConvNeXt-Tiny and ViT-B/16 were evaluated with three outer folds, internal validation and five training seeds. Handcrafted and frozen-feature baselines complement border-only and randomized-label controls. Validation-selected ConvNeXt-Tiny achieved B2 tree-level macro-F1 of 0.8621 +/- 0.0317; frozen DINOv2 features yielded 0.9000 +/- 0.0722. Values are means and standard deviations across three seed-averaged fold means. Results estimate performance on previously unseen trees from this collection.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging Human and Model Attention: Explainability Analysis of CNN, Mamba, and ViT Architectures with Gaze-Based Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12703)
*Marco Stefani,Edoardo Parpaiola,Matteo Bino,Gianluca Caregnato,Francesco Roncolato,Daniel Fusaro,Loris Nanni,Alberto Pretto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了CNN、ViT和ViM在理解人类视觉感知方面的差异，通过眼动追踪数据量化了模型注意力与人类注意力的对齐程度，并分析了不同架构和训练策略的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险应用需要精确理解深度学习模型的决策过程，现有研究多为定性分析，缺乏量化比较模型注意力与人类视觉感知的对齐程度。

Method: 使用眼动追踪数据作为人类视觉感知的“地面真实”，采用标准化的归一化流程将模型归因图和注视热图映射为空间概率分布，并通过Jensen-Shannon相似度、卡方相似度和Pearson相关系数评估模型注意力和人类注视的对齐程度。实验涵盖了细粒度分类、医学成像和情绪识别任务，并分析了不同XAI方法、骨干网络冻结和集成策略的影响。

Result: CNN在一般场景下表现出更全局的注意力，其注意力图与人类感知有较好的相关性。ViT则展现出细粒度的、基于Patch的注意力，常聚焦于判别性微特征。ViM在医学任务中解释性强，但在自然图像任务中表现出偏差，虽然支持重叠度高，但线性相关性低。骨干网络冻结和集成策略会影响模型的解释性。

Conclusion: 模型架构和训练策略显著影响模型注意力与人类视觉注意力的对齐程度。ViT和ViM展现出与CNN不同的注意力机制，ViM在特定任务上存在可解释性偏差，揭示了不同模型在模拟人类视觉感知方面的优势和局限性。

Abstract: High-risk applications of deep learning require precise characterization of model decision-making. We conduct a systematic explainability evaluation across Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Vision Mamba (ViM). Unlike predominantly qualitative studies, we quantify the alignment between model attention and human visual perception using eye-tracking ground truth. We formalize this comparison via a standardized normalization pipeline mapping post-hoc attribution maps and gaze heatmaps onto spatial probability distributions. Alignment is evaluated across fine-grained classification, medical imaging, and emotion recognition using architecture-specific XAI methods. We measure fidelity using Jensen-Shannon similarity, Chi-square similarity, and Pearson correlation, three complementary estimators capturing support overlap, mass discrepancy, and structural co-variation. Our results reveal distinct attention dynamics: CNNs tend to exhibit a more global focus, with spatially more diffuse saliency maps that correlate well with human perception in general contexts, while ViTs demonstrate fine-grained, patch-level attention, often focused on discriminative microfeatures. The Mamba architecture shows biased behavior: it achieves strong explainability in medical tasks but exhibits divergent intensity distributions in natural images; our analytical characterization of the metrics makes this dissociation explicit, since near-unit support overlap is attained simultaneously with a vanishing linear correlation. We further analyze the effect of backbone freezing and ensemble strategies on model interpretability. The results highlight how architectural and training choices influence the alignment between model saliency and human visual attention.

</details>


### [95] [Zebra3D: Efficient Hybrid Foundation Models For Scalable 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12727)
*Utkarsh Saxena,Ruijing Yang,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Akash Haridas,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zebra3D 提出了一种高效的 3D 基础模型注意力框架，通过将早期层替换为稀疏线性注意力，并保留后期层的稠密注意力，显著提高了处理大规模视图集时的重建效率和速度，并且无需从头开始训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈式 3D 基础模型在处理大量图像视图时，由于全局注意力计算的二次成本，扩展性受到限制。研究人员希望提高这些模型的效率，使其能够处理更多视图。

Method: Zebra3D 采用层级稀疏-线性混合注意力框架。它观察到不同层对注意力替换的敏感度不同，早期层对线性注意力更宽容，而中后期层注意力更集中。因此，Zebra3D 将线性注意力应用于宽容层，将瓦片稀疏注意力应用于敏感层，并使用地标摘要保留未选定的上下文。该方法支持无训练转换（通过仿射拟合）和可选的后转换训练（结合任务监督、输出和关系特征蒸馏）。

Result: Zebra3D 在 VGGT、HunyuanWorld-Mirror 和 $π^3$ 三个基础模型上都取得了显著效果。即使在很高的注意力稀疏度下，也能保持接近密集注意力模型的重建和相机姿态精度。无训练转换的变体性能也接近密集模型。与输入视图数量呈正相关，在 1024 个视图时，端到端速度提升高达 7.1 倍。经过后转换训练的 Zebra3D-VGGT 在 RealEstate10K 数据集上取得了非常接近密集模型（仅差 1.1 个百分点）的 AUC@30 结果，同时实现了 94% 的平均注意力稀疏度。

Conclusion: Zebra3D 是一种有效的、无需从头训练的框架，可以显著提高 3D 基础模型处理大规模视图集时的效率和速度。它通过利用不同层注意力敏感度的差异，实现了高性能和高稀疏度的平衡，为大规模 3D 重建任务提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct scenes from unposed images, but the quadratic cost of global attention limits their scalability to large view sets. We present Zebra3D, a layerwise sparse-linear hybrid attention framework that improves the efficiency of pretrained 3D foundation models without training from scratch. Across VGGT, HunyuanWorld-Mirror, and $π^3$, we observe a broadly consistent depth-wise pattern: early stages are relatively tolerant to linear replacement, while sensitive middle-to-late stages tend to exhibit more concentrated attention. Zebra3D exploits this structure by assigning linear-centered attention to tolerant stages and tile-sparse attention to sensitive stages, using landmark summaries to preserve unselected context. Closed-form affine fitting enables training-free conversion, while optional post-conversion training combines task supervision with output and relational feature distillation. Across all three backbones, Zebra3D achieves strong reconstruction and camera-pose accuracy at high attention sparsity, with training-free variants remaining close to their dense counterparts. End-to-end speedups increase with input view count, reaching up to $7.1\times$ at 1,024 views. After post-conversion training, Zebra3D-VGGT reaches 80.5\% RealEstate10K AUC@30 at 94\% mean attention sparsity, within 1.1 percentage points of dense VGGT fine-tuned on the same data and schedule.

</details>


### [96] [Reference-Free Food Volume Estimation from Egocentric Stereo Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12735)
*Vivan Kumar,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种利用可穿戴设备拍摄的立体视频，自动进行食物体积估算的方法，无需用户干预或参照物，实现了9.1%的平均绝对百分比误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于立体视觉的食物份量估算方法需要用户使用手机拍摄多张图像或放置参照物进行标定，存在用户负担，阻碍了被动膳食摄入监测的发展。

Method: 利用可穿戴设备采集的立体视频，结合FoundationStereo进行零样本立体匹配，SAM 2进行食物分割，以及在关键帧上进行TSDF融合，从而重建出真实物理尺度的食物三维模型。利用可穿戴设备出厂校准的立体基线和板载视觉惯性测程(VIO)估计的相机姿态，实现无需场景参照物的全自动重建。

Result: 在五种记录的食物项上进行了初步的体积估算，取得了9.1%的平均绝对百分比误差。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够从单次立体录制中生成视觉和度量上精确的、任何盘装食物项的三维网格，并能准确估算其体积，为被动膳食摄入监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current stereo vision-based approaches for food portion estimation are hindered by the user burden of using a mobile phone to capture multiple images or placing a reference object in the scene for scale calibration. As a step towards passive dietary intake monitoring, we introduce a method that uses stereo video from a wearable egocentric device to reconstruct food items in true physical scale to accurately estimate volume. By utilizing FoundationStereo for zero-shot stereo matching, SAM 2 for food segmentation, and TSDF fusion over a set of keyframes, our method yields a visually and metrically accurate 3D mesh of any plated food item from a single stereo recording. We leverage the unique offerings of wearables, such as a factory-calibrated stereo baseline and camera poses estimated on-device via visual-inertial odometry (VIO), to perform fully automatic reconstruction without a scale reference object in the scene. We show strong initial volume estimation results across five recorded food items, achieving a mean absolute percentage error of 9.1%.

</details>


### [97] [Shift-Aware Identity-Guided Latent Refinement for Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12783)
*Kun Li,Sami Sebastian Brandt,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SAIL的新型框架，用于解决Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS)任务中的目标漂移问题，通过解耦帧级动态和全局语义锚点，以及使用带有连续记忆更新的解码器来提高模型在多模态交互中的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Referring Audio-Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS)方法在目标漂移（由于遮挡、声音衰减或身份模糊导致模型错误转移焦点）的情况下，难以保持一致性。

Method: 提出Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL) 框架，采用双token策略解耦帧级动态和全局语义锚点，设计了一个移位感知细化模块来纠正漂移，并利用一个解码器整合细化后的嵌入与连续记忆更新，以保持时空连贯性和身份一致性。

Result: 在两个Ref-AVS基准测试上，SAIL框架显著优于现有的AVS、Ref-VOS和Ref-AVS方法。

Conclusion: SAIL框架能够有效解决Ref-AVS中的目标漂移问题，并通过多模态交互保持时空连贯性和身份一致性，从而取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Referring Audio--Visual Segmentation (Ref-AVS) is an emergent multimodal task requiring the precise identification and segmentation of a specific object based on a natural language expression grounded in both visual and auditory cues. Unlike traditional AVS, which segments generic sound sources, or referring video object segmentation (Ref-VOS), which relies solely on visual--textual grounding, Ref-AVS necessitates fine-grained tri-modal interaction across vision, audio, and language. However, existing methods often fail to maintain consistency when subjected to target shift, a phenomenon where the model erroneously transfers its focus between objects due to transient occlusions, acoustic fading, or identity ambiguities. To address this, we propose a novel Shift-Aware Identity-guided Latent (SAIL) refinement framework. It introduces a dual-token strategy that decouples frame-level dynamics from a global semantic anchor to explicitly model target-shift cues. We design a shift-aware refinement module that rectifies drift by aligning tokens with domain-specific evidence. Finally, we leverage a decoder that integrates the refined embeddings with a continuous memory update to maintain spatio-temporal coherence and identity consistency throughout the video sequence. Extensive experiments conducted on two Ref-AVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method, significantly outperforming existing AVS, Ref-VOS, and Ref-AVS approaches. The code will be released after publication.

</details>


### [98] [Domain Adaptation of SAM 2.1 for Building-Component Segmentation in Image-Based 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12786)
*Ehsan Agha Ebrahimi,Yong-Cheol Lee,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过在HBD数据集上微调Segment Anything Model (SAM 2.1)，提高了建筑图像的多类别2D分割精度，进而改进了基于神经辐射场（NeRF）的3D分割效果，为3D建筑自动重建提供了更准确的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在3D环境中获得一致的2D分割掩码分组，需要提升建筑图像的2D分割精度，以便更好地应用于3D重建。利用大型视觉基础模型（如SAM）并对其进行微调是提高分割性能的有效途径。

Method: 研究者首先在包含2,679张标注图像（来自42栋建筑，11个类别）的HBD数据集上微调了SAM 2.1模型。然后，使用IoU、Dice和mIoU等指标评估了微调后模型相对于基线模型SegFormer在2D图像分割上的表现。接着，将微调前后的SAM 2.1模型应用于GARField框架，通过在新渲染的图像中进行3D分割分组对比，评估微调对3D分割效果的影响。

Result: 微调SAM 2.1后，2D图像的多类别分割mIoU从49.9%提高到69.6%。在与SegFormer的比较中，微调SAM 2.1在某些表面积较大的物体类别上显示出性能提升。在GARField框架下，微调后的SAM 2.1能实现用户选择分组尺度下的更好分组效果。

Conclusion: 在HBD数据集上微调SAM 2.1能够显著提高建筑图像的2D多类别分割精度，并进一步优化GARField框架中的3D分割分组能力，为3D建筑自动重建提供了更准确、更可靠的分割模型。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of building components using image-based scanning requires appropriate segmentation of the objects in the 2D building images. In order to benefit from consistent grouping of the 2D masks in 3D environments, vision foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model have been utilized by Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) to perform 3D segmentation. This study fine-tuned SAM 2.1 on the HBD dataset, consisting of 2,679 annotated images from 42 buildings, to enhance multi-class segmentation of 2D building images, covering 11 classes (Wall, Window, Ceiling, Door, Column, Beam, Floor, Opening, Curtain Wall, Roof, and Lift). The model was evaluated using per-class IoU, Dice, and mIoU metrics, and the results show an improvement in mIoU from 49.9% to 69.6%, indicating a meaningful improvement in class segmentation and providing a more accurate model for distilling the masks in 3D. SegFormer was then used as a baseline, generating per-class masks for comparison with SAM 2.1, which indicates improvement in some object classes with larger surface area. The masks in the building images were grouped in the neural radiance field, and the pretrained SAM 2.1 was compared with the fine-tuned SAM 2.1 in the newly rendered images. This comparison shows that fine-tuning improves the grouping of building components at a user-selected grouping scale. This advances the application of Group Anything with Radiance Fields (GARField) in 3D, enabling better segmentation of building structures for 3D auto-reconstruction.

</details>


### [99] [Spend Where It Matters: Particle Search with MH Refinement for Budget-Constrained Audio-Visual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12811)
*Shivanshu Shekhar,Raghavendra Addanki,Tong Zhang,Uttaran Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Spend Where It Matters (SWIM) 的两阶段、无需训练、与奖励无关的框架，用于在有限计算预算下优化音频-视觉生成任务，通过自适应粒子分配和Metropolis-Hastings（MH）改进，提高了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励引导推理时对齐方法在优化多个对齐目标时需要大量昂贵的模型评估，计算成本高。粒子搜索和MH算法各有优缺点，需要结合以提高效率。

Method: SWIM框架分为两个阶段：1. 自适应粒子分配：利用在线方差估计，根据去噪检查点的效用动态分配粒子，最小化估计方差。2. MH精炼：利用去噪检查点提供的优秀候选者，通过局部噪声-去噪提议进一步优化奖励。

Result: 在JavisBench-Mini上，SWIM在相似的计算量下，比单独的粒子搜索（BoN）和MH（EvoSearch）方法取得了更高的奖励。在SD1.5图像生成任务中，自适应粒子分配也优于均匀分配。

Conclusion: SWIM框架通过结合自适应粒子分配和MH精炼，在有限计算预算下有效地提高了联合音频-视觉生成和其他图像生成任务的质量，证明了其在奖励引导推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Reward-guided inference-time alignment can improve joint audio-visual generation, but optimizing multiple alignment objectives requires many expensive model evaluations, making existing methods costly under limited compute. Particle-based search enables global exploration but incurs estimation error with a finite population, while Metropolis-Hastings (MH) can refine candidates locally but benefits from a strong initialization. We introduce \textbf{S}pend \textbf{W}here \textbf{I}t \textbf{M}atters (\modelname), a two-stage, training-free, reward-agnostic framework that combines particle search with MH refinement under a fixed compute budget. In the first stage, we adaptively allocate particles across denoising checkpoints using online variance estimates, minimizing estimation variance while concentrating compute where it is most useful. This search provides a promising candidate for MH refinement, which uses local noise-and-denoise proposals on completed outputs to further improve reward. By starting from a strong candidate, MH can recover additional reward without requiring a larger particle population or costly global exploration. We show that, at matched compute, combining particle search with MH refinement achieves higher rewards than either approach alone. On \textsc{JavisBench-Mini}, at closely matched compute, \modelname achieves mean relative gains across seven metrics of $30.7\%$ over BoN and $14.1\%$ over EvoSearch with JavisDiT++, and $9.2\%$ over BoN and $17.0\%$ over EvoSearch with LTX-2. Beyond joint audio-visual generation, we also demonstrate the benefits of adaptive particle allocation on SD1.5 image generation, improving AestheticScore by $2.2\%$ and ImageReward by $6.0\%$ over uniform allocation.

</details>


### [100] [Structure-Aware Matching for Robust UAV Thermal-to-Satellite Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12835)
*Chun Liu,Yuxin Wang,Zhenxiang Huo,Linlin Zhu,Tao Xu,Henggang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SAMF的结构感知匹配框架，用于在GNSS受限和低可见度条件下，通过匹配热成像图和卫星图像来实现无人机热成像地理定位。SAMF通过方向结构增强模块（DSEM）和模态感知仿射重映射模块（MARM）来解决跨模态差异，并在新的Thermal-UAV-Paired基准数据集上进行了验证，取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于外观的无人机热成像地理定位方法难以应对热成像图和卫星图像之间显著的颜色、亮度和视角差异。现有方法主要关注全局模态转移，忽略了两种图像视图中固有的不变场景结构。因此，研究的动机是利用这些稳定的跨模态结构线索来改进地理定位的可靠性。

Method: 该研究提出了SAMF（Structure-Aware Matching Framework），一个利用稳定跨模态结构线索来改进无人机热成像地理定位的框架。具体包括：1. 方向结构增强模块（DSEM），通过捕捉方向性和低频空间模式来增强结构表示。2. 模态感知仿射重映射模块（MARM），对增强后的特征进行仿射校正，以促进跨模态对齐。此外，还引入了Thermal-UAV-Paired基准数据集。

Result: SAMF在Boson-nighttime和Thermal-UAV-Paired两个数据集上都取得了最先进的性能。消融实验证明了DSEM和MARM的有效性和协同互补性。

Conclusion: SAMF框架通过显式利用不变的跨模态结构线索，能够有效解决无人机热成像地理定位中的跨模态差异问题，并在GNSS受限和低可见度条件下实现了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: UAV thermal-to-satellite geo-localization localizes UAVs by matching real-time thermal images against georeferenced satellite images, enabling autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied and low-visibility conditions. However, substantial cross-modal disparities in color, brightness and viewpoint hinder conventional appearance-based methods from establishing reliable correspondences. While existing approaches predominantly address global modality shifts, they overlook the invariant scene structures inherently shared between thermal and satellite views. To bridge this gap, we propose SAMF, a Structure-Aware Matching Framework that explicitly exploits stable cross-modal structural cues to improve UAV thermal geolocalization. Specifically, the Directional Structure Enhancement Module (DSEM) strengthens robust structural representations by capturing directional and low-frequency spatial patterns, and the Modality-Aware Affine Remapping Module (MARM) subsequently applies affine correction to these enhanced features, further promoting cross-modal alignment. Additionally, we introduce the Thermal-UAV-Paired benchmark derived from the Thermal-UAV dataset. Extensive experiments on both the Boson-nighttime and Thermal-UAV-Paired datasets show that SAMF achieves state-of-the-art performance. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness and synergistic complementarity of DSEM and MARM. The code and dataset are released at: https://github.com/YuxinWang879/SAMF.git.

</details>


### [101] [APEX: Asynchronous Prefix Denoising for Extreme Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12843)
*David Dinkevich,Nisan Chiprut,Yoav HaCohen,Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了APEX模型，一种用于极度压缩潜在空间（高达128倍）的扩散模型。APEX通过分层潜在空间和异步去噪策略，有效缓解了压缩带来的重建-生成困境，并在ImageNet数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能，尤其是在计算资源有限的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型在极度压缩潜在空间时，会面临重建质量和生成能力之间的权衡。增加通道数可以改善重建，但会增加生成难度。研究动机是开发一种能够实现高压缩率同时保持良好生成和重建性能的方法。

Method: APEX模型结合了两种关键技术：1. 分层潜在空间：潜在空间被组织成一个层次结构，通道前缀从结构信息到细节信息排序。2. 异步去噪：一个共享的去噪器在不同的局部时间步长上处理不同的通道组，而无需增加额外的潜在token。

Result: 在ImageNet-512数据集上，APEX在64倍和128倍压缩率下，均优于现有方法。在相同的训练和引导采样计算量下，APEX的性能也优于一个256-token的模型，并且在较低的计算预算下，优势更为明显。

Conclusion: APEX通过其独特的分层潜在空间和异步去噪机制，成功解决了极度压缩下扩散模型的重建-生成困境，实现了在低比特率下的高性能，为开发更高效的扩散模型提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Fewer latent tokens promise cheaper diffusion. Yet aggressive latent compression creates a reconstruction-generation dilemma: adding channels improves reconstruction but makes generation harder. We introduce APEX, short for Asynchronous Prefix denoising for EXtreme compression, which pairs a hierarchical latent space with asynchronous denoising over channel groups. Our autoencoder orders channel prefixes from structure to detail, while one shared denoiser generates them at staggered local times without adding tokens. On ImageNet-512, APEX outperforms existing methods at every evaluated channel count under 64x and 128x compression. At matched training and guided sampling compute, it also outperforms a 256-token model at every evaluated budget, with larger gains at lower compute. Code is available at https://daviddinkevich.github.io/APEX.

</details>


### [102] [CaptchaAgent: Verifiable Visual Reasoning for CAPTCHA Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12866)
*Shangquan Sun,Ruoyu Chen,Zhirui Liu,Xiang Liu,Chenghao Qian,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 CaptchaAgent 的结构化视觉推理框架，用于解决交互式 CAPTCHA。它首先构建一个视觉中间表示（V-IR），显式记录实体、属性和关系，然后进行局部证据精炼和反事实一致性验证，以提高解决 CAPTCHA 的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端视觉语言模型在解决需要精细感知、组合关系推理和浏览器交互的 CAPTCHA 时存在脆性问题，任何细微的错误都可能导致最终动作的失败。

Method: CaptchaAgent 框架首先将屏幕截图转换为视觉中间表示（V-IR），该表示显式记录了实体、属性和空间/拓扑关系。随后，通过符号化 ASCII 协议强制模型确定具体的对象集和计数。在此基础上，进行局部证据精炼，提高模糊区域的分辨率；进行反事实一致性验证，检查在任务不变的变换下动作的稳定性。

Result: 在 Open CaptchaWorld 数据集上，CaptchaAgent 相比基线浏览器代理，Pass@1 提高了 21.3-25.8 个百分点。在 Oedipus-data 基准测试上，Pass@1 平均提高了 9.2-12.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: CaptchaAgent 框架能显著提高 CAPTCHA 求解的准确性，并揭示了精确计数、连续几何和顺序执行是当前自动化求解 CAPTCHA 的主要难点，为设计更具鲁棒性的 CAPTCHA 提供了指导。

Abstract: Modern reasoning CAPTCHAs require fine-grained perception, compositional relation reasoning, and grounded browser interaction. End-to-end vision-language model agents remain brittle in this setting because a missed object, incorrect count, or confused relation can propagate directly to the final action. To address this problem, we present CaptchaAgent, a structured visual reasoning framework for interactive CAPTCHA solving. Instead of predicting an action directly from a screenshot, CaptchaAgent first constructs a visual intermediate representation (V-IR) that explicitly records entities, attributes, and spatial or topological relations. A symbol-only ASCII protocol further requires the parser to commit to a concrete object set and count. Building on this global representation, localized evidence refinement revisits ambiguous regions at higher effective resolution, while counterfactual consistency verification tests whether the resulting action remains stable under task-preserving transformations. Across three proprietary backbones on Open CaptchaWorld, CaptchaAgent improves Pass@1 by 21.3-25.8 percentage points over a commercially available browser agent baseline evaluated with the same backbones. On the separate offline Oedipus-data benchmark, it further improves average Pass@1 by 9.2-12.3 percentage points over the Oedipus solver across three matched backbones. The intermediate traces show that exact counting, continuous geometry, and ordered execution remain major sources of automation difficulty for the evaluated agents, providing practical guidance for designing more resilient compositional CAPTCHAs.

</details>


### [103] [Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI): A Mamba-based Framework for Rotational Fruit Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12868)
*Hao Tian,Jie Hu,Guoqi Shan,Fan Wang,Wenzhuo Zhang,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种低成本、高效率、轻量级的深度学习水果分拣系统S$^2$FI，适用于中小型种植者，解决了现有大型系统成本高昂和人工分拣效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球果蔬行业面临小规模种植者在采后分拣上面临成本高昂（大型智能分拣系统）或效率低下、标准不一致、劳动力需求高（人工分拣）的困境，需要一个介于两者之间的解决方案。

Method: 提出Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI)架构，结合3D卷积编码器和基于Mamba的主干网络，以有效提取时空特征，自适应地权衡果实旋转过程中的信息帧，并以线性复杂度模拟长程依赖，以减少运动模糊和遮挡。硬件设计采用双传送带和弯曲轨道，支持家用电源、快速部署和轻量级嵌入式部署。同时，构建了一个包含6460个样本的基于传送带的水果品质视频数据集。

Result: 在测试集上的实验表明，所提出的方法在准确性、效率和实用性之间取得了最佳的平衡，优于比较基线，实现了准确性、速度和实用性的优异平衡。

Conclusion: S$^2$FI系统通过结合创新的深度学习架构和经济实用的硬件设计，为中小型果蔬种植者提供了一个有效且负担得起的水果分拣解决方案，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: The global fruit and vegetable industry confronts severe post-harvest sorting challenges for small and medium-scale growers. Large intelligent sorting systems are too costly, while manual sorting suffers from low efficiency, inconsistent standards, and high labor demand. To bridge this gap between expensive equipment and inefficient manual work, this study develops a low-cost, high-efficiency, lightweight fruit sorting system driven by deep learning, suitable for small and medium growers. We propose the Selective Spatio-Temporal Fruit Inspector (S$^2$FI) architecture, which combines a 3D convolutional encoder with a Mamba-based backbone.It efficiently extracts spatiotemporal features, adaptively weighs informative frames during fruit rotation, and models long-range dependencies with linear complexity, reducing motion blur and occlusion.
