The hardware uses a dual-conveyor and curved rail design, supporting household power, fast deployment, and lightweight embedded deployment to improve affordability. We also built a conveyor-based fruit quality video dataset with 6,460 samples. Experiments on the held-out test set show that our method achieves the best trade-off among accuracy, efficiency, and practicality, outperforming the compared baselines on the test set, achieving an excellent balance of accuracy, speed, and practicality for real-world sorting.

</details>


### [104] [ROSETTA: Rotation Subspace Estimation for Training-free Transmodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12871)
*Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ROSETTA的训练无关的校准层，用于解决不同传感器或编码器训练出的特征空间中的坐标系不一致问题，通过期望最大化算法估计局部旋转，实现跨模态、跨设备、跨表示和跨骨干网络的匹配，并且查询时间开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当查询需要与使用不同传感器或编码器构建的数据库进行匹配时，独立的编码器会在特征空间中引入不同的坐标系。现有的方法未能提供一个统一的参考系来对齐所有编码器，也缺乏能够完全弥合这一差距的单一训练过程。

Method: 研究表明，两个特征空间之间的相对变换是白化特征空间中的一个空间保持旋转，其角速度呈现白噪声特性。然而，编码器输出之间的交叉协方差具有较低有效秩，这限制了只有一小部分旋转参数。基于此，作者提出ROSETTA，一个训练无关的校准层，它通过期望最大化（EM）算法来估计局部旋转，而无需在查询时进行协同观测。

Result: ROSETTA在三个公开的驾驶数据集上进行了验证，涵盖了激光雷达、摄像头、热成像和事件相机等多种模态。实验证明了ROSETTA在跨模态、跨设备、跨表示和跨骨干网络设置下的有效性，并且在每次查询时仅带来毫秒级的开销。

Conclusion: ROSETTA是一种有效的、无需训练的校准方法，能够处理不同编码器引入的特征空间坐标系不一致问题，并在实际应用中实现了高效和准确的跨模态匹配。

Abstract: When a query must be matched against a database built with a different sensor or processed by a different encoder, the independently trained encoders impose distinct coordinate systems on the feature space. No canonical reference frame exists to which all encoders can be aligned, and no single training procedure has fully closed this gap. We show that the relative transformation between any two such systems is a spatially preserving rotation in whitened feature space, whose angular velocity exhibits white-noise characteristics: large in norm, temporally uncorrelated, and unpredictable from the features themselves. However, the cross-covariance between encoder outputs has low effective rank, constraining only a small fraction of the rotation parameters, therefore local alignment is tractable. ROSETTA is a training-free calibration layer that estimates the local rotation via an expectation-maximization procedure, requiring no coobservation at query time. We validate ROSETTA on three public driving datasets spanning LiDAR, camera, thermal, and event modalities in cross-modal, cross-device, cross-representation, and cross-backbone settings, with milliseconds overhead per query.

</details>


### [105] [Win-Win Fusion: Multi-Modal Image Fusion with Visual-Semantic Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12887)
*Hao Zhang,Xuhui Zuo,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为W2F的多模态图像融合新范式，通过视觉和语义的相互强化来解决现有方法在两个领域之间的优化差距，以提升感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态图像融合方法在视觉和语义领域之间存在优化差距，限制了感知能力的提升。作者旨在克服这一长期存在的障碍。

Method: W2F通过以下方式实现视觉-语义协同：1. 增强特征互补性：使用分割引导融合损失引入对象感知结构引导，并利用嵌入式信息恢复先验增强视觉融合为语义分支提供可靠感知证据的能力。2. 引入多模态信息交互机制：采用交互式门控混合注意力（IGM-Att）和渐进耦合注意力（PC-Att）来确保全局和局部的场景表示。3. 构建协同机制：通过特征搜索模块提取互补的关键特征，并通过梯度校正策略实现优化层面的协同，通过一致性学习提升视觉和语义融合的性能。

Result: W2F在视觉吸引力和决策准确性方面均超越了最先进的方法。

Conclusion: W2F提出的视觉-语义相互强化范式能够有效解决多模态图像融合中的优化差距问题，并显著提升融合图像的视觉和语义性能。

Abstract: Existing multi-modal image fusion (MMIF) methods generally face an optimization gap between the visual and semantic domains, limiting the enhancement of perception capabilities. To overcome this long-standing barrier, this work proposes W2F, a new visual-semantic mutual reinforcing paradigm for MMIF. First, we reinforce the foundation for visual-semantic collaboration by amplifying feature complementarity through two targeted designs. Specifically, the segmentation-guided fusion loss introduces object-aware structural guidance into visual fusion, while the embedded information restoration prior strengthens the ability of visual fusion to provide reliable perceptual evidence for the semantic branch under degraded conditions. Then, we introduce a strong multi-modal information interaction mechanism composed of interactive gated mixed attention (IGM-Att) and progressive coupling attention (PC-Att). These modules ensure comprehensive scene representation from both local and global perspectives, providing informational support for visual-semantic collaboration. Finally, we construct a collaborative mechanism to couple visual fusion and semantic fusion. It employs a feature search module to extract complementary key features from both tasks, enriching the representational potential of visual and semantic information. Meanwhile, a gradient correction strategy ensures collaboration from the optimization perspective, enhancing performance for both visual and semantic fusion through consistency learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that W2F surpasses state-of-the-art methods in both visual appeal and decision accuracy. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/HaoZhang1018/W2F.

</details>


### [106] [MeshBind: Point-to-Mesh Binding for Non-Rigid Scene Flow Automatic Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12895)
*Yinqi Chen,E Li,Shuaijun Wang,Weiyuan Peng,Meiying Zhang,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MeshBind的自动标注方法，利用多视图重建的网格模型和局部绑定技术，生成非刚性物体的每点场景流，并发布了相应的SFB数据集和工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶和机器人应用中，获取非刚性物体（如行人）的真实场景流标注非常困难。

Method: 开发了一个多视图模块来重建和配准铰接网格模型，一个局部框架绑定技术来将点连接到网格，以及一个三层流程配准管道（包括自身运动补偿、盒子绑定和网格绑定）来生成全场景每点流。

Result: 在SFB数据集上进行微调，可以将三向端点误差（EPE）降低2-3倍，并将非刚性EPE降至与刚性EPE相同的水平。

Conclusion: MeshBind方法能够有效地生成非刚性物体的每点场景流，并在SFB数据集上验证了其在提高场景流估计精度方面的有效性。

Abstract: Scene flow estimation is essential for many autonomous driving (AD) and robotic applications, but obtaining ground-truth scene flow for non-rigid objects such as pedestrians is highly difficult. We present MeshBind, an automatic annotation method that uses mesh models to generate per-point scene flow for non-rigid objects. The novelty of this work is threefold: 1) developing a multi-view module that reconstructs an articulated mesh model from six surround cameras and registers it to the LiDAR frame; 2) developing a local-frame binding technique that attaches each point to the mesh via a per-triangle tangent-normal frame, invariant under rigid motion yet adaptive to articulated deformation; 3) developing a three-layer flow registration pipeline that combines ego-vehicle motion compensation, box binding, and mesh binding to produce full-scene per-point flow. We implement MeshBind in SF-Studio, an interactive annotation tool that visualises flow and allows human mesh corrections with automatic recomputation. We also release SFB (SUScape-Flow Benchmark), a large-scale benchmark built on the public SUScape dataset, covering 41k frames with per-point non-rigid pedestrian annotations and a Rigid/Semi-Rigid/Non-Rigid evaluation protocol. Experiments show that fine-tuning on SFB reduces three-way endpoint error (EPE) by 2-3x, and brings non-rigid EPE to the same level as rigid EPE.

</details>


### [107] [AD-SRF: Attack-Defense Semantic Reinforcement for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12897)
*Hao Zhang,Zhiqian Zheng,Linfeng Tang,Xinyu Xiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AD-SRF的攻击-防御语义增强框架，用于多模态图像融合，通过解耦视觉质量和语义属性的优化，并引入攻击-防御博弈机制，以提升融合图像的语义属性，同时保持其视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像融合方法在视觉质量和语义属性之间存在固有冲突，难以平衡两者。

Method: 该框架包含两个部分：1. 基于Transformer的恢复性视觉融合网络，用于生成高质量的融合图像；2. 攻击-防御博弈机制，通过迭代对抗，增强融合图像的语义信息，并无损地将其整合回融合图像中。

Result: AD-SRF框架在视觉质量和语义属性方面均取得了显著提升，并且在下游分割任务上的性能得到了增强。

Conclusion: AD-SRF框架能够有效地解决多模态图像融合中视觉质量与语义属性的冲突，实现两者的双赢，并且能够提升下游任务的性能。

Abstract: Existing image fusion methods struggle to balance visual quality and semantic attributes due to the inherent conflict between their optimization objectives. To address this challenge, we propose an attack-defense semantic reinforcement framework for multi-modal image fusion, termed AD-SRF. By decoupling the optimization of these two aspects and then losslessly reintegrating them, this framework injects rich semantic attributes into the fused image without compromising its visual quality. First, we develop a transformer-based restorative visual fusion network as a strong visual baseline. Equipped with the cross-modal retrieval module and the information restoration-fusion collaborative loss, it produces highly visible fused images even under degraded conditions. Second, we introduce an attack-defense game mechanism to strengthen the semantic attributes of the fused images. The attacker learns perturbation patterns that impair downstream segmentation, whereas the defender restores missing semantics from the original inputs and reintegrates them into the fused image through an imperceptible semantic map. Through iterative adversarial interaction, this mechanism drives the defender to continually strengthen the fused image's semantic attributes, boosting downstream segmentation performance without compromising visual quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AD-SRF achieves a win-win performance in both visual quality and semantic attributes. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/HaoZhang1018/AD-SRF.

</details>


### [108] [A Lightweight Velocity Correction Framework for Improving Instruction Following in Few-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12899)
*Yating Gao,Zhenlong Zhu,Wen Zihao,Lenan Zhang,Jiexiong Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang,Yixiong Zou,Takuya Maekawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的校正框架，用于改进冻结的少步数文本到图像生成器处理复杂提示的能力，通过在生成过程中学习残差调整，而无需改变生成器参数或增加前向传播次数，并在不增加额外延迟的情况下显著提高了图像生成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少步数文本到图像生成器在处理包含多个对象、交互、空间关系、计数和属性绑定等复杂提示时表现不佳。研究动机是寻找一种方法来减少这些错误，同时保持生成器的参数不变并维持原有的前向传播次数。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的校正框架，该框架包含一个参数量小于2000万的校正器，用于对一个冻结的60亿参数生成器进行残差调整。校正器学习在选定的采样步骤中调整生成器预测的速度，从而更新中间图像表示。利用“On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision”技术，在实际达到的状态下推导出残差目标。校正器与生成器并行运行，仅训练校正器，且不涉及奖励优化或重蒸馏。

Result: 该框架在处理复杂场景分布时，相比于冻结的Z-Image-Turbo，在配对指令遵循评估中实现了+17.60个百分点的NetWin提升。同时，端到端的额外延迟仅为26.9毫秒（0.84%），且骨干网络评估次数没有增加。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级校正框架能够有效地改进冻结的少步数文本到图像生成器处理复杂提示的能力，而无需改变生成器本身或增加计算成本，是第一个实现这种轻量级速度-残差适应的框架，并保持了原始采样计划和前向传播次数。

Abstract: Few-step text-to-image generators produce high-quality images with few network forward passes, yet struggle with complex prompts involving multiple subjects, interactions, spatial relations, counting, and attribute binding. Can these errors be reduced without changing the generator's parameters or increasing its forward-pass count? We propose a lightweight correction framework for a frozen 6B-parameter generator. The generator predicts velocities that determine how its intermediate image representation is updated; a corrector with fewer than 20M parameters learns residual adjustments at selected sampling steps. Because earlier corrections change the states encountered at later steps, our On-Trajectory Flow-Bridge Supervision derives residual targets at the states actually reached during corrected generation. A single corrector is shared across selected steps and runs in parallel with the generator. Only the corrector is trained, requiring neither reward optimization nor re-distillation. To our knowledge, this is the first framework for lightweight velocity-residual adaptation of fully frozen few-step text-to-image generators that preserves their original sampling schedule and forward-pass count. On our target complex-scene distribution, the framework achieves a +17.60-percentage-point NetWin over frozen Z-Image-Turbo in pairwise instruction-following evaluation, with only 26.9 ms (0.84%) additional end-to-end latency per image and no increase in backbone evaluations.

</details>


### [109] [JMT-YOLO: Multi-task Domain Adaptation via Segmentation-Guided Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12901)
*Zhenhua Yu,Jiahao Zhang,Wenchao Zhang,Ou Ye,Hengxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JMT-YOLO是一种联合多任务学习框架，通过引入语义分割和掩码机制，改进了领域自适应目标检测在复杂场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域自适应目标检测方法在复杂环境（如恶劣天气、遮挡）下性能下降，原因是细粒度特征建模不足和实例级对齐受限。

Method: 基于Mean Teacher架构，引入辅助语义分割任务和掩码机制。分割头采用多尺度上下文感知和密集连接结构，掩码一致性损失通过随机掩码操作引导网络学习结构上下文并改进实例级对齐。

Result: 在Pascal VOC到Clipart1k、Cityscapes到Foggy Cityscapes、KITTI到Cityscapes三个基准数据集上，JMT-YOLO分别取得了46.9%、57.3%和61.0%的检测性能。

Conclusion: JMT-YOLO通过联合多任务学习和创新的掩码机制，有效提升了领域自适应目标检测在复杂条件下的性能，优于现有SOTA方法。

Abstract: Domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) aims to reduce performance degradation due to distribution shifts between training (source) and testing (target) datasets. However, owing to insufficient modeling of fine-grained features and limited instance-level alignment, existing approaches become less effective under complex environmental conditions, such as extreme weather conditions and object occlusion. We overcome these challenges by proposing JMT-YOLO, a novel joint multi-task learning framework for DAOD. JMT-YOLO is based on the Mean Teacher architecture. It introduces an auxiliary semantic segmentation task and a masking mechanism to enhance the modeling of foreground-background semantic boundaries in the target domain. Specifically, fine-grained feature degradation is reduced by introducing a multiscale context-aware segmentation head equipped with a dense connection structure to effectively capture local and global contextual features. A mask-consistency loss is formulated by applying random masking operations to target images, thereby guiding the network to learn structural context and improving instance-level feature alignment. Extensive experiments on three benchmark domain adaptation tasks (Pascal VOC to Clipart1k, Cityscapes to Foggy Cityscapes, and KITTI to Cityscapes) demonstrate that JMT-YOLO achieves detection performances of 46.9%, 57.3%, and 61.0%, respectively. These results demonstrate superior performance over existing state-of-the-art and baseline methods, thereby validating the effectiveness and generalization of JMT-YOLO.

</details>


### [110] [BLEND: Balancing Personalization vs. Generalization in Federated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12907)
*Payam Abdisarabshali,Fardis Nadimi,Kasra Borazjani,Naji Khosravan,Richeng Jin,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为BLEND的新框架，用于增强联邦视觉语言模型（FedVLMs）的个性化和泛化能力，通过选择性地微调和聚合VLM参数，并引入了一种新的混合损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FedVLMs在提升模型个性化和泛化能力方面存在挑战，需要一种能够同时兼顾这两者的框架。

Method: BLEND框架采用选择性VLM参数微调和聚合策略，包括共享的全局视觉和文本适配器，以及每个客户端特有的个性化视觉投影适配器。此外，引入了一种新的混合损失函数，该函数结合了全局和个性化适配器的输出来实现个性化，并通过零样本VLM投影头实现原则性的锚点设计来促进泛化。

Result: 实验结果表明，BLEND在各种系统设置下持续优于最先进的基线方法，有效地平衡了个性化和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BLEND框架能够有效地提升FedVLMs的个性化和泛化能力，为该领域的研究提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated vision-language models (FedVLMs) represent one of the emerging frontiers of machine learning, aiming to integrate federated learning within the fine-tuning pipelines of vision-language models (VLMs). In this work, we introduce BLEND, a new framework designed to jointly enhance the personalization and generalization capabilities of FedVLMs. BLEND proposes a selective VLM parameter fine-tuning and aggregation strategy that consists of (i) global vision and text adapters shared across clients, and (ii) a personalized vision projection adapter tailored to each client. BLEND further introduces a novel loss function that blends the outputs of the global and personalized adapters to achieve personalization, while simultaneously promoting generalization through a principled anchor design implemented via the projection heads of the zero-shot VLM. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BLEND consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting its ability to effectively balance personalization and generalization across diverse system settings. (Code is available at: https://github.com/payamsiabd/BLEND.git)

</details>


### [111] [What Egocentric Video Embeddings Encode and How to Combine Them with Inertial Sensors for Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12926)
*Kansuke Habano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了 Egocentric 视频和惯性传感器的嵌入表示，发现视频嵌入主要反映相机视线方向，而惯性信号则区分个体活动。研究提出了一种基于视频图的惯性信号传播方法，显著提高了活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对 Egocentric 视频和惯性传感器数据如何结合以及嵌入表示的含义研究不足，特别是在头部佩戴相机的情况下。本研究旨在探索这些嵌入的含义，并开发更有效的融合方法。

Method: 研究使用了 VideoMAEv2、CLIP、DINOv2 和 VC-1 等模型对 Egocentric 视频嵌入进行主成分分析，并考察了头部姿态和场景布局对其的影响。同时，研究分析了惯性信号在区分个体活动中的作用。最后，提出了一种在移除视频图的领先成分后，利用惯性信号在视频图上传播的方法来提高活动识别性能。

Result: 研究发现，视频嵌入的主导方向主要由相机视线决定。头部姿态可以解释 Egocentric 相机的嵌入，但不能解释 Exocentric 相机。惯性信号能区分不同个体的活动。提出的惯性信号传播方法在 WEAR 数据集上，将识别性能从 0.648 提高到 0.852。

Conclusion: Egocentric 视频嵌入和惯性信号具有不同的作用：视频嵌入有助于识别同一组活动片段，而惯性信号则用于识别不同活动。提出的基于视频图的惯性信号传播方法是一种有效的融合策略，优于简单的模型融合或在图中添加惯性边缘的方法。

Abstract: Egocentric video and body-worn inertial sensors are increasingly recorded together, and the video is usually encoded by pretrained models. What these embeddings represent when the camera sits on the head, and how they should be combined with inertial sensing, has received little attention. Across VideoMAEv2, CLIP, DINOv2 and the egocentric VC-1, the leading principal directions of the embedding are dominated by where the camera looks. Measured head orientation explains them on Ego-Exo4D for egocentric (R^2 0.49-0.70) but not exocentric cameras, and the scene layout explains them on WEAR. The part that the limbs' inertial signals predict separates activities across people. In a set-level probe, the rest separates exercise sets within a participant but not activities across people. This is consistent with separate roles: the video finds the clips of the same set, and the inertial classifier labels them. On WEAR, with temporal order removed and one random limb per one-second clip, we propagate inertial predictions over a within-participant video graph whose leading components are removed. This raises subject-wise cross-validated macro-F1 from 0.648 to 0.852. In our comparisons, propagation helps only over the video graph, and fusing the modalities in one classifier, or adding inertial edges to the graph, performs worse.

</details>


### [112] [UniDeblur: Unified Motion Deblurring via Fourier Convolution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12941)
*Xintian Mao,Jiansheng Wang,Huaiyuan Wang,Qingli Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于傅里叶卷积学习的统一运动去模糊框架，通过构建多源数据集和数据处理流程，并在傅里叶空间进行模糊自适应全局滤波学习，以解决现有方法在不同场景下泛化能力差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊方法多专注于单一场景，导致在面对不同相机和图像内容时性能下降。因此，需要一个能够处理多样化模糊模式的统一去模糊框架。

Method: 1. 构建多源数据集并进行数据筛选和质量控制。 2. 提出基于傅里叶卷积学习的统一运动去模糊框架，在傅里叶空间进行模糊自适应全局滤波学习，而非依赖像素级映射和空间域内核估计。

Result: 所提出的统一模型能够有效地处理来自不同来源和场景的运动模糊，在同域和分布外（OOD）基准测试中均取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶卷积学习的统一去模糊框架能够有效克服现有方法的局限性，在处理多样化的运动模糊问题上展现出优越的泛化能力和性能。

Abstract: Image deblurring is a fundamental task in computer vision, yet existing methods mainly specialize in isolated scenarios. Consequently, the deblurring performance of these models drops significantly across scenarios with different cameras and image contents. This motivates the development of a unified motion deblurring framework that can handle diverse blur patterns. To this end, we construct a multi-source dataset and develop a data pruning and quality control pipeline. Then, to restore blur images with various motion blur kernels across different scenarios, we propose a unified motion deblurring framework via Fourier Convolution Learning. Existing deblurring networks usually rely on pixel-level mappings and overlook the convolutional nature of blur. Thus, the network mostly fits the image content itself rather than learning the convolution process, making it difficult to handle multi-source blur images. Instead of relying on spatial mapping, we perform blur-adaptive global filter learning in Fourier space, which avoids the limited spatial support of kernel estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model effectively handles motion blurs across diverse sources and scenes, achieving competitive performance across both in-domain and OOD benchmarks. Code will be released at github.com/DeepMed-Lab-ECNU/Single-Image-Deblur.

</details>


### [113] [Video Codec-Guided Training-Free Token Compression for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12959)
*Xihua Sheng,Ruijie Liu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用视频编码器信息进行训练的视频大语言模型（VLLM）的无训练 token 压缩方法，通过编码信息指导 token 保护和时间融合，有效降低了特征比较次数，并取得了与现有 SOTA 方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLLM 在处理长视频序列时效率低下，需要对视觉 token 进行压缩。然而，现有的无训练压缩方法在特征比较方面仍然计算量巨大，尤其是在帧内 token 保护和帧间时间 token 合并时。

Method: 提出了一种视频编解码器引导的无训练 token 压缩方法。该方法将视频编解码器中的参考帧依赖、运动矢量和残差信息与视觉 token 网格对齐。残差信息用于构建 token 保护的候选池，运动信息则沿参考帧链传播，为稀疏采样帧之间的时间 token 合并构建局部候选。最后，在这些局部候选内进行视觉特征相似度比较来做出压缩决策，整个过程不修改基础 VLLM。

Result: 在三个代表性 VLLM 和四个视频理解基准测试上，该方法取得了与 SOTA 无训练基线 FlashVID 相当的性能，并在某些情况下超越了它。同时，该方法显著减少了 token 保护和时间匹配所需的特征比较次数。

Conclusion: 视频编解码器信息可以有效地提高视频 token 压缩的准确性-复杂度权衡。所提出的方法利用现有的编码信息，以低成本的方式显著降低了 VLLM 推理时的计算复杂度，同时保持了高水平的性能。

Abstract: Video large language models (VLLMs) rely on long visual token sequences to understand complex temporal content, which makes visual token compression important for efficient inference. Existing training-free compression methods mainly reduce the number of tokens sent to the language model, but the compression process itself can still require many visual feature comparisons, especially for intra-frame token protection and inter-frame temporal token merging. This paper proposes a video codec-guided training-free token compression method for VLLMs that uses coding information already available in compressed video as a low-cost search prior. The method aligns reference-frame dependencies, motion vectors, and residuals with the visual token grid. Residual information forms compact candidate pools for token protection, while motion information is propagated along reference-frame chains to construct localized candidates for temporal token merging between sparsely sampled frames. Local visual feature similarity comparison is then used within these candidates to make the final compression decisions, without modifying the base VLLM. Experiments on three representative VLLMs and four video-understanding benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art training-free baseline FlashVID, and in some settings surpasses it, while substantially reducing the feature comparisons required by token protection and temporal matching. These results demonstrate that codec information can effectively improve the accuracy-complexity tradeoff of video token compression.

</details>


### [114] [In-Context Learning Can Help Vision-Language Models Overcome Training Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12942)
*Kun Wang,Xindi Wu,Sanghyuk Chun,Olga Russakovsky,Esin Tureci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明，当前的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在遇到违反训练时学习到的统计规律的输入图像时，会表现出错误。通过受控的视觉上下文学习（in-context learning），可以有效地纠正这些错误，表明模型实际上具备相关的视觉能力。这种方法在多个任务和模型家族中都显示出显著的性能提升，并能将模型注意力从先验知识引导到实际视觉线索上。然而，当底层视觉能力本身不足时，这种方法的提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视觉语言模型（VLMs）在训练过程中学习到的统计规律，当输入图像违反这些规律时，模型可能会给出错误的响应。区分这些由先验知识引起的错误与模型本身视觉能力缺失造成的错误是一个挑战。

Method: 使用受控的视觉上下文学习（controlled visual in-context learning）来鼓励模型关注视觉证据。通过在五个视觉中心任务上进行实验，比较了上下文学习对与先验知识冲突的反事实样本和与先验知识一致的样本的影响。同时分析了模型的视觉表示，以研究注意力重定向和对先验线索的依赖性变化。

Result: 在与先验知识冲突的反事实样本上，上下文学习带来了显著的性能提升（Qwen3-VL 235B 提升 27.2%，Qwen3-VL 32B 提升 15.1%），而在与先验知识一致的样本上，准确率几乎没有变化。这种提升效果在五个 VLM 家族中普遍存在，增幅在 9.7% 到 30.3% 之间。分析表明，上下文学习能将注意力引向视觉线索，同时减少对支持先验知识的线索的依赖。当模型本身视觉能力较弱或缺失时，上下文学习的效果有限。

Conclusion: 当前的 VLM 实际上拥有标准评估方法可能忽略的视觉基础能力。受控的评估设计对于诊断和提升模型能力至关重要。视觉上下文学习是一种有效的方法，可以纠正由模型先验知识引起的错误，并揭示模型潜在的视觉能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) learn strong statistical regularities during training, which can make them fail to properly respond to visual evidence when input images violate those regularities. It is challenging to disambiguate these prior-induced errors from errors caused by the model missing the underlying visual capabilities. In this work, we use controlled visual in-context learning to encourage the model to focus on the visual evidence, and demonstrate that this alone is frequently sufficient to correct the errors -- suggesting that the models in fact do possess the relevant visual capabilities. Specifically, we show that across five vision-centric tasks, demonstrations yield large gains on prior-conflicting counterfactual examples ($+27.2\%$ for Qwen3-VL 235B and $+15.1\%$ for Qwen3-VL 32B) while leaving accuracy on prior-aligned examples nearly unchanged. These findings generalize across five VLM families with gains from $+9.7\%$ to $+30.3\%$. We analyze visual representations and find that demonstrations indeed redirect attention toward grounding cues while reducing reliance on cues that support the learned prior. Finally, we find that in-context learning is limited when the underlying visual capability is weak or absent. Together, these results show that current VLMs possess visual grounding capabilities that standard evaluations may overlook, underscoring the need for controlled evaluation design in diagnosing and improving model capability.

</details>


### [115] [MVGraph: Benchmarking LVLMs on Evidence Composition across Multi-View Visual Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12961)
*Yingjie Zhu,Han Cheng,Xuefeng Bai,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MVGraph，一个用于评估大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多图推理能力上的基准。该基准包含8k个实例，涵盖8个图论任务，并针对性地评估了LVLMs在跨视图证据对齐和组合方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理基准仅限于单图结构，未能充分探索跨视图证据的对齐和组合，而现实世界中证据常分散在多个相互关联的视图中。

Method: 构建了一个包含8k个实例、8个图论任务的多图推理基准MVGraph，其中每个实例都无法从单一视图解决。通过29个诊断任务和64k个实例，将多图推理分解为感知、对齐、组合和推理四个能力进行评估，并提供中间表示以供评估。在15个LVLMs上进行了评估。

Result: 评估结果表明，多图推理对LVLMs来说具有挑战性，其中跨视图边对齐和关系级组合是主要的瓶颈。此外，时间约束和视图数量的增加会进一步暴露模型的局限性。

Conclusion: MVGraph基准有效地揭示了LVLMs在多图推理方面的不足，特别是跨视图信息整合能力有待提高。研究为未来LVLMs在处理复杂、分布式的视觉信息方面提供了重要的评估框架和研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce MVGraph, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models (LVLMs) on multi-graph reasoning where the evidence required by a query is distributed across multiple interrelated visual graph views. Although such evidence commonly resides in source-specific graphs, temporal snapshots, or local observations, existing graph reasoning benchmarks remain confined to a single self-contained structure, assessing only graph perception and reasoning while leaving the alignment and composition of evidence across views underexplored. MVGraph comprises 8k instances spanning 8 \textit{graph-theoretic} tasks, abstracted from two multi-graph scenarios (partial and temporal graph views). Each problem is programmatically constructed and verified to be unsolvable from a single view. Beyond end-to-end evaluation, we decompose multi-graph reasoning into four complementary capabilities---Perception, Alignment, Composition, and Reasoning---and design 29 diagnostic tasks with 64k instances to probe each one, along with golden intermediate representations for evaluating the intermediate process. Across 15 LVLMs, we reveal the difficulty of multi-graph reasoning and identify cross-view edge alignment and relation-level composition as prominent bottlenecks through stage-oriented diagnostics. Meanwhile, temporal constraints and increasing view counts expose further capability boundaries. Code and data are available at https://github.com/AAAndy-Zhu/MVGraph.

</details>


### [116] [MotionCoach: Constructing Multimodal Guidance for Video Professional Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12963)
*Siyi Qian,Hanqing Yang,Weihuang Lin,Zihao Yang,Yichen Lu,Yuanhong Zheng,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MotionCoach，一个多模态智能体，能够为视频编辑模型生成专业动作的详细指导（提示词和参考视频），以克服现有模型在理解和生成细粒度专业动作方面的不足，并在VPMEBench基准上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑模型在生成专业动作时存在困难，例如对“Stoppie”指令无法准确执行前轮抬起。这主要是由于缺乏详细的动作描述，导致模型无法捕捉专业动作的精细技术和时间结构。

Method: 1. 构建一个多模态智能体MotionCoach，该智能体能够利用多轮工具进行交互并进行自我验证。 2. 训练MotionCoach生成“接地”的提示词（grounded prompt）和参考视频（reference clip），作为对视频编辑模型的可靠且充分的多模态指导。 3. 建立数据管道来构建训练数据。 4. 在MotionCoach-SFT-10k上进行冷启动训练，并使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)在MotionCoach-RL-2k上进行强化学习训练，奖励机制包括动作指导提示词、动作输出视频和格式。 5. 引入VPMEBench基准，包含314个专业动作编辑任务。

Result: 在Seedance 2.0上，MotionCoach将整体得分从55.1提高到65.6；在MiniMax-H3上，得分从51.2提高到64.5。这表明MotionCoach能够显著提升专业动作视频编辑的效果。

Conclusion: 通过学习构建多模态指导，MotionCoach能够实现精确的专业动作编辑，而无需重新训练现有的视频编辑模型，有效解决了专业动作编辑中的挑战。

Abstract: Video editing models achieve strong performance in modifying appearance, backgrounds, styles, and motions, empowered by large-scale training data. Beyond general scenarios, professional motions are significant in performance, sports, and martial arts. This paper focuses on professional motion editing, which involves fine-grained motion technique and temporal structure. Given professional motion names but lacking detailed descriptions, existing models often fail to generate professional motions, such as incorrectly lifting the front wheel under a Stoppie instruction. To address this challenge, we introduce MotionCoach, a trained multimodal agent that uses multi-turn tools and performs self-verification, producing a grounded prompt and a reference clip as reliable and sufficient multimodal guidance to inject external professional motion knowledge into video editing models. To supply training data, we build a data pipeline to construct trajectories. We cold-start the agent on MotionCoach-SFT-10k and apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on MotionCoach-RL-2k with rewards on the motion guidance prompt, the motion outcome video, and the format. For evaluation, we introduce VPMEBench, comprising 314 professional motion editing tasks with fine-grained references and annotations. Extensive experiments show that MotionCoach improves the overall score from 55.1 to 65.6 on Seedance 2.0 and from 51.2 to 64.5 on MiniMax-H3. Learning to construct multimodal guidance enables precise professional motion editing without retraining video editing models.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Representations to Guide Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12978)
*Zihao Pan,Mengting Chen,Cheng Shi,Taihang Hu,Zhengrong Yue,Zikang Wang,Yuan Wang,Wei Li,Sibei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，图像生成过程中，外部对齐的语义信息会丢失，而架构分离未能有效提升表示的生成能力。作者提出表示引导（Representation Guidance, RG）方法，通过低维瓶颈强制生成相关信息进入紧凑表示，并用该表示指导解码器生成，从而在保持 Transformer 架构简洁性的同时，提升了语义和空间结构，并实现了优于现有方法的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉表示研究主要关注理解能力，但其对生成任务的有效性尚不明确。作者旨在探究什么使得表示更适合生成任务，并基于此改进生成模型。

Method: 作者首先通过实验分析了像素扩散 Transformer 中的表示质量与生成效用的关系，发现外部对齐的语义信息在像素合成中可能丢失，且架构分离未能显著提升表示的生成能力。在此基础上，提出表示引导（RG）方法，该方法通过引入一个低维瓶颈迫使信息编码到紧凑表示中，并利用该表示修改流匹配目标来指导解码器生成。

Result: RG 方法在 ImageNet 256x256 数据集上，RG-L/16 以更少的参数量超越了更大的像素扩散 Transformer 模型。RG-XL/16 达到了 1.55 的 FID 分数。在 512x512 分辨率下，RG 取得了 1.74 的 FID。此外，RG 方法在文本到图像生成任务上，于 GenEval 上达到 0.89，在 DPG-Bench 上达到 87.1。

Conclusion: 一个有用的生成表示需要同时具备良好的形成和积极的利用。RG 方法通过直接形成和利用内部表示，在保持 Transformer 架构简洁性的同时，提升了生成模型的性能，并在图像生成和文本到图像生成任务上取得了 SOTA 结果。

Abstract: Visual representations are largely developed for understanding, yet what makes them useful for generation remains unclear. We study this question in pixel diffusion transformers and uncover two key insights. First, better representation quality does not necessarily translate into greater generative utility, as externally aligned semantics can be progressively lost during pixel synthesis. Second, architectural separation with specialized pixel decoders mainly redistributes semantic and spatial modeling, without necessarily yielding representations better suited to generation. These insights suggest that a useful generative representation must be both properly formed and actively exploited. Motivated by these insights, we introduce Representation Guidance (RG), which directly forms and exploits internal representations while retaining a plain Transformer architecture. A low-dimensional bottleneck forces generation-relevant information into a compact representation, which is then explicitly used to guide decoder generation by modifying the flow-matching target according to its induced prediction offset. RG learns clearer semantic and spatial structure while scaling efficiently in both parameters and computation. On ImageNet 256x256, RG-L/16 with only 459M parameters outperforms substantially larger pixel diffusion transformers, while RG-XL/16 achieves an FID of 1.55. RG further reaches an FID of 1.74 at 512x512 and extends to text-to-image generation, achieving 0.89 on GenEval and 87.1 on DPG-Bench.

</details>


### [118] [The Evidence Anchoring Boundary: Where Visual Interventions Matter and When They Help in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12981)
*Qiji Zhou,Junyi Hu,Peiyu Wang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，在多模态大语言模型中，视觉干预（如视觉残差编辑）在特定解码器层（证据锚定边界，EAB）之后效果会显著下降，甚至可能损害正确答案。基于此，研究提出了校准选择性路由，它只在校准表明干预对特定问题类型有益时才应用干预，从而提高了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，视觉干预虽然可以减少模型幻觉，但其有效性随模型层数变化，且可能损害正确答案。因此，作者希望了解视觉干预在哪些层仍然有效，以及何时能真正改善预测结果。

Method: 1. 对同一视觉残差编辑在 successive 解码器层应用，并匹配其在不同深度的 RMS 强度，观察干预效果的变化，定义了证据锚定边界（EAB）。 2. 比较了不同类型问题（存在、属性、关系）上的反事实减法干预效果。 3. 基于以上观察，开发了校准选择性路由（calibrated selective routing）方法。

Result: 1. 视觉干预效果随着模型深度的增加而持续下降，在 EAB 之后效果显著减弱。2. 在 EAB 之前的干预效果比 EAB 之后的干预效果大 13.78 到 26.10 倍。3. 反事实减法干预可能修正错误偏好，保持原决策，或移除支持正确答案的偏好。4. 校准选择性路由相比普遍应用减法，在属性准确率上提高了 1.56 到 1.81 个百分点。

Conclusion: 视觉干预的效果高度依赖于其在模型中的应用深度，存在一个关键的“证据锚定边界”（EAB）。在此边界之后，干预的效果会迅速衰减。校准选择性路由是一种更优的干预策略，通过根据问题类型选择性地应用干预，可以有效提升模型性能，避免普遍干预带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Visual interventions provide a training-free way to reduce hallucinations in multimodal large language models. However, an intervention may have little leverage at some layers and may damage a correct answer even when it changes the output. We investigate where visual interventions retain leverage and when they improve predictions. First, we apply the same visual-residual edit at successive decoder layers, matching its RMS strength across depth. Across six checkpoints from three model families, we observe a persistent drop in intervention effect and define its operational onset for this edit as the \textbf{Evidence Anchoring Boundary (EAB)}. With boundaries fixed on calibration queries, held-out effects on disjoint evaluation queries before EAB are $13.78\times$--$26.10\times$ larger than those after it. Comparison with intermediate readouts shows that the edit can lose leverage before answer preferences stabilize. Second, we compare counterfactual subtraction across presence, attribute, and relation questions. Subtraction can correct a misleading preference, leave a decision unchanged, or remove a preference supporting the correct answer. Based on these observations, we develop calibrated selective routing, which preserves the original prediction unless calibration supports an intervention for the question type. It improves full-set attribute accuracy by $1.56$--$1.81$ percentage points across three checkpoints, while universal subtraction is neutral or harmful.

</details>


### [119] [Learning Features, Not Layouts: Feature-Axis Attention as a Transfer Prior for Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13012)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过将 Vision Transformer 中的全局注意力从空间轴转移到特征轴，可以显著提高模型在医学图像上的迁移能力，尤其是在纹理比几何形状更具辨识度的任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然图像预训练模型迁移到医学图像领域时，在域偏移、模态异质性和标签稀疏性方面表现不可靠。作者认为，这部分归因于 Vision Transformer 中将全局建模完全依赖于空间自注意力，而这种方式更适合自然图像的布局先验，不适用于医学图像中普遍存在的跨扫描仪可复现的特征相关性和纹理信号。

Method: 作者提出了 FAD-ViT（Feature-Axis Decoupled Vision Transformer）模型，该模型严格解耦了空间轴和特征轴的全局注意力。通过使特征轴注意力成为主要的全局算子，并控制注意力轴，作者能够独立评估特征轴注意力的效果。在 23 个分类、分割和低数据任务上进行了全面评估，比较了不同注意力轴（空间 vs. 特征）在迁移学习中的表现。

Result: 将全局注意力从空间轴转移到特征轴，在从头训练（from-scratch）时的性能基本不变，但显著增强了模型的迁移能力。使用相同的 ImageNet 预训练，转移增益最高可达约三倍，并在所有保留的分割任务上均有持续改进。与标准模型和通道注意力基线模型相比，FAD-ViT 的参数量和计算量显著降低。这种优势在纹理比几何形状更具辨识度的任务中尤为明显，表明预训练特征的复用程度更高。

Conclusion: 全局注意力的轴（空间 vs. 特征）是影响医学图像迁移学习的关键设计因素。将全局注意力转向以通道（特征）为主，是一种简单、低成本且有原则性的策略，可以有效提升模型在医学图像领域的迁移性能。

Abstract: Natural-image-pretrained foundation models are increasingly adapted to medical imaging, yet transfer remains unreliable under domain shift, modality heterogeneity, and label scarcity. We trace part of this fragility to an overlooked design choice. Standard vision transformers place all global modeling on self-attention over spatial tokens, an object-layout prior well suited to natural scenes but not to medical images, where the discriminative signal lies in feature correlations and texture that recur across scanners while layout does not. We ask whether the axis of global attention, spatial versus feature, is itself a lever for transferable medical representations. Existing channel-aware hybrids confound this question, adding channel attention to a spatially-mixed backbone and leaving the axes coupled and the feature axis unmeasurable in isolation. We design FAD-ViT, a backbone that strictly decouples the two axes, making feature-axis attention the primary global operator and the attention axis a controlled variable. Evaluating comprehensively across 23 classification, segmentation, and low-data tasks under matched tokenization, capacity, and protocol, we find that shifting global attention from the spatial to the feature axis leaves from-scratch performance essentially unchanged, yet markedly strengthens transfer. Identical ImageNet pretraining yields up to roughly three times the transfer gain, with consistent improvements on every held-out segmentation task at up to a quarter the parameter count and far lower compute than standard and channel-attention baselines. The advantage is largest where texture rather than geometry is discriminative, tracking greater reuse of pretrained features. The axis of global attention is thus a design lever for medical transfer, and making it channel-primary is a simple, cheap, and principled choice.

</details>


### [120] [Unified Generative Video Compression with Autoregressive and Bidirectional Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13023)
*Tianyu Zhang,Shuai Huo,Dengchao Jin,Hewei Liu,Meng Lei,Tianwen Wu,Jiawen Gu,Chao Zhou,Li Li,Dong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGVC是一个统一的生成式视频编码器，能够从单一比特流支持两种解码模式：UniGVC-BI用于高质量的随机访问解码，UniGVC-AR用于低延迟流式传输。它通过共享的层次化潜在编码器和知识蒸馏实现了高效压缩和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散模型在超低比特率下能生成高质量视频，但存在部署上的权衡。双向（BI）模型质量高但延迟大且需要完整序列，自回归（AR）模型延迟低但压缩效率受限。这两种模式分别适用于实时流媒体和事后高质量回放，但需要不同的模型，增加了复杂性。

Method: UniGVC提出了一种统一的视频编码框架，支持两种解码模式。UniGVC-BI采用具有密集注意力机制的双向Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 来实现高质量的随机访问解码。UniGVC-AR采用带有窗口注意力的自回归DiT来实现低延迟流式传输。两种模式共享一个层次化潜在编码器，并利用知识蒸馏，让UniGVC-BI作为教师模型指导UniGVC-AR的训练。此外，还引入了一个小型条件视频解码器来进一步降低流媒体延迟。

Result: 在1080p视频基准测试中，UniGVC-BI在率失真感知权衡方面表现最佳，在0.0005 bpp的极低比特率下也能生成时间稳定的视频。UniGVC-AR在解码1080p视频时速度达到36.85 FPS，比UniGVC-BI快250倍以上，同时保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: UniGVC成功地在一个统一的框架下解决了视频扩散模型在低比特率视频压缩中的性能与延迟权衡问题，通过灵活的解码模式适应了实时流媒体和高质量回放的不同需求，并在性能和效率上取得了显著的提升。

Abstract: Video diffusion models can reconstruct visually compelling content from severely compressed representations at ultra-low bitrates, but they impose a rigid deployment trade-off. Bidirectional (BI) models achieve high quality through dense full-sequence attention, yet require the entire sequence before reconstruction and incur substantial latency. Autoregressive (AR) models enable low-delay streaming, but their restricted temporal context reduces compression efficiency. This tension is particularly relevant when a video stream must be decoded in real time yet is also stored for later, quality-oriented playback, making AR and BI models natural choices for the live and replay stages, respectively. To support both stages, we present UniGVC, a Unified Generative Video Codec that supports two decoding modes from a single bitstream. UniGVC-BI employs a BI-DiT with dense attention for quality-oriented random-access decoding, whereas UniGVC-AR uses windowed AR-DiT for low-delay streaming. Both paths share a hierarchical latent codec that recurrently produces the same conditions with ultra-low bitrates, allowing UniGVC-BI to serve as a conditional teacher for single-step distillation of UniGVC-AR. A tiny conditional video decoder further reduces streaming latency while preserving most of the reconstruction quality. On standard 1080p benchmarks, UniGVC-BI achieves the best overall rate-distortion-perception trade-off among the evaluated methods and produces temporally stable video at rates as low as 0.0005 bits per pixel. Meanwhile, UniGVC-AR remains competitive while decoding 1080p video at 36.85 FPS, over $250\times$ faster than UniGVC-BI. Code and models will be released later.

</details>


### [121] [What Visual Representations Do World Action Models Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13024)
*Kai Liu,Jiangran Lyu,Tingyu Deng,Yixin Zheng,Jingtian Wan,Wenbo Cui,Mi Yan,Yizhou Zhou,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统地比较了不同的视觉表示、集成机制和特征组合对世界动作模型（WAMs）的影响，发现通用的视觉表示和通道联合预测效果最佳，并提出了 R^2-WAM，在 RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上均取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然利用了预训练视频生成模型的先验知识，但其变分自编码器（VAE）的表示更侧重于视觉重建而非操作。虽然外部视觉表示可以提升操作能力，但现有方法将表示选择与集成机制耦合，难以区分各自的影响。因此，研究旨在系统地比较不同视觉表示、集成机制及组合的效果，为改进 WAMs 提供指导。

Method: 研究人员在受控设置下，系统地比较了五种集成机制、不同视觉表示（包括通用的和特定任务的）以及它们各自的特征组合。通过实验评估了不同方法在WAMs上的表现，并分析了成功率的来源。

Result: 研究发现：1）强大的通用视觉表示能带来更广泛的鲁棒性提升，且性能可匹敌甚至超越特定任务预训练的表示。2）通道联合预测（channel-wise joint prediction）取得了最高的平均成功率，这归因于未来特征预测以及动作专家有效利用新增的视觉信息。3）特征组合的增益取决于其对任务相关信息的贡献，同一来源的信息可能对某些组合有益，对另一些则有害。4）最佳组合的额外增益仍小于引入强通用视觉表示对 VAE-only 基线带来的初始提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，未来的 WAMs 研究应优先考虑更强的通用视觉表示和更有效的集成机制。特征组合可以作为一种辅助的改进途径。研究提出的 R^2-WAM 实例化了这些发现，并在 RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上达到了最先进的性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) inherit powerful priors from pretrained video generation models, but their variational autoencoder (VAE) representations are optimized for visual reconstruction rather than manipulation. External visual representations can improve manipulation, but existing methods couple representation choices with different integration mechanisms, obscuring their individual effects. We systematically compare visual representations, five integration mechanisms, and feature combinations under controlled settings. Our study yields three main findings. First, strong general-purpose visual representations provide broader robustness gains and can match or outperform representations pretrained for specific visual capabilities. Second, channel-wise joint prediction achieves the highest average success rate; further analyses suggest that future-feature prediction and the action expert's effective use of added visual information contribute to its gains. Third, additional sources should be selected for the task-relevant information they contribute beyond existing representations, as the same source can help one combination but harm another. However, even the best evaluated composition provides a smaller additional gain than the initial improvement from adding a strong general-purpose representation to the VAE-only baseline. We instantiate the resulting Representation Recipe as R$^2$-WAM. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop with an 84.7% success rate and reaches 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0, improving over the VAE-only baselines by 5.9 and 2.2 percentage points, respectively. Taken together, these findings suggest that future work should prioritize stronger general-purpose visual representations and more effective mechanisms for integrating them into WAMs, with feature composition serving as a supplementary route to improvement.

</details>


### [122] [Dataset-Specific Encoding of Image Content Underlies Cross-Dataset Failure in Brain MRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13027)
*Minh Sao Khue Luu,Bair N. Tuchinov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，尽管预训练的脑部 MRI 基础模型（foundation models）在不同数据集上表现不佳，但问题并非源于模型编码了数据来源信息，而是因为模型将 MRI 序列的内容信息编码在了不同的方向上。只有少数模型（如 3DINO 和 SAM-Med3D）能够稳定地编码序列信息并进行跨数据集迁移，而一些专门为脑部设计的模型表现甚至不如未训练的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为，脑部 MRI 基础模型在新的数据集上表现不佳的原因是模型“冻结”的特征编码了数据来源信息。作者希望探究导致这一问题的根本原因。

Method: 作者评估了八种不同的“冻结”特征表示在 17 个公开数据集上的表现。他们通过分析模型对 MRI 序列本身的识别能力，以及内容信息在不同数据集中的编码方向来解释模型的迁移能力。作者还尝试使用 INLP、LEACE、CORAL 或对抗训练等方法移除数据集信号，并观察其对序列迁移能力的影响。

Result: 研究发现，所有模型都能识别出特定数据集内的 MRI 序列，但在面对未见过的数据集时，大多数模型表现不佳。导致失败的原因并非数据集编码或数据集本身的偏移，而是模型将 MRI 扫描的内容信息在不同数据集中编码在不同的方向上。移除数据集信号的方法并未能提高序列迁移能力。只有 3DINO 和 SAM-Med3D 这类经过广泛预训练的模型能够相对稳定地编码序列信息并进行迁移，而 BrainIAC 和 BrainFM 等脑部专有模型表现不如未训练网络。

Conclusion: 脑部 MRI 基础模型在跨数据集迁移时表现不佳，主要归因于内容信息的编码方向在不同数据集间存在差异，而非模型编码了数据集来源信息。模型的预训练范围和其编码序列信息的一致性是影响其迁移能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Frozen features of brain MRI foundation models lose accuracy on new datasets, a failure usually blamed on the features encoding where each scan came from. We show that the failure lies elsewhere. Across 17 public datasets and eight frozen representations, every representation recognizes the MRI sequence within each dataset, yet most fail on an unseen one. The cause is neither dataset encoding nor a shift of each dataset: the content itself is encoded along different directions in each dataset. This explains which datasets fail, for the sequence and for sex, and why removing the dataset signal with INLP, LEACE, CORAL or adversarial training never improves sequence transfer. Only the broadly pretrained 3DINO and SAM-Med3D encode the sequence consistently enough to transfer; the brain-specific BrainIAC and BrainFM do no better than an untrained network. Code is available at https://github.com/BrainFM/brainfm-profiler/tree/main/representation_utility.

</details>


### [123] [GeoGR: Spatial-Semantic Token Compression and Group Routing for Efficient 3D VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13035)
*Yongxin Zheng,Hao Wu,Fang Li,Hu Fu,Peiran Yin,Xudong Wang,Haozhe Hu,Xinghao Chen,Yunpu Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoGR 是一种通过压缩场景表示和视觉计算来提高 3D 视觉语言模型 (VLM) 推理效率的框架，通过 voxel 令牌和分组跳过技术，在保持模型性能的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视图 3D VLM 由于视觉令牌序列过长而导致推理成本高昂，存在跨视图冗余和所有视觉令牌在 LLM 层中都被处理的问题。

Method: GeoGR 采用两阶段框架。第一阶段，GeoSemZip 在投影仪层面压缩场景表示，将跨视图观测合并为 3D voxel 令牌，并结合语义显著性和空间覆盖率。第二阶段，GroupRoute 在 LLM 层面通过分组跳过策略（晚进入、早退出、可恢复的组内跳过）来减少计算。此外，还通过压缩数据集进行后训练。

Result: GeoGR 在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 和 Video-3D LLM-7B 上分别保留了密集后训练模型 95.2% 和 96.8% 的性能，同时将 LLM 的预填充速度分别提高了 6.8 倍和 6.1 倍。

Conclusion: GeoGR 在不同类型的 3D VLM 上都有效，实现了出色的性能-效率权衡，显著降低了 3D VLM 的推理成本。

Abstract: Multi-view representations enable pretrained image and video vision-language models to understand 3D scenes, but their long visual token sequences make inference costly. Redundancy arises both from repeated observations of the same scene regions across views and from processing all visual tokens at every LLM layer, regardless of their relevance to the current computation. We propose GeoGR, a two-stage framework that jointly compresses scene representations and visual computation. At the projector level, GeoSemZip consolidates cross-view observations into 3D voxel tokens and combines semantic saliency with spatial coverage to construct a compact scene representation. Unselected voxel features are aggregated into contextual anchors to retain scene information that would otherwise be discarded. At the LLM level, we identify an effective visual computation window and observe that query-conditioned token relevance remains locally stable but changes over longer layer intervals. Guided by these findings, GroupRoute combines late entry and early exit with recoverable group-wise skipping. Low-relevance tokens bypass complete LLM layers while retaining their hidden states and can rejoin the active group at subsequent anchor layers selected under a computation budget. For compression-aware post-training, we construct a compact dataset of 67K samples by retaining 30% of the original training pool. With this protocol, GeoGR retains 95.2% and 96.8% of the dense post-trained models' performance on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Video-3D LLM-7B, respectively, while accelerating LLM prefill by $6.8\times$ and $6.1\times$. These results demonstrate the consistent effectiveness of GeoGR across 3D VLMs with and without explicit 3D spatial encoding, highlighting a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/GeoGR.

</details>


### [124] [Redirecting Teacher Supervision: Temporal-Response-Guided Latent Distillation for Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13045)
*Xinkai Lu,Junli Zha,Haolan Wei,Jiahui Wang,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Temporal-Response Guidance (TRG)的新型在线策略蒸馏方法，通过引导学生模型的时序表示对局部时序扰动的响应，来改进视频任务中的知识蒸馏效果，并在多个基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略蒸馏方法（如GKD, MiniLLM, Video-OPD）在知识迁移过程中，没有充分利用学生模型自身的时序响应来指导监督信号的方向，限制了时序学习的进一步提升。

Method: TRG方法通过以下步骤进行：1. 测量学生模型表示对局部时序扰动的响应；2. 将教师的校正投影回学生响应的范围内，并加回到原始校正中；3. 保持校正的原始范数；4. 使用正交切线投影器和分离的目标，在保持余弦损失值和对齐表示梯度范数的同时，改变监督方向。

Result: 在TempCompass, Video-MME, MVBench和Video-MMMU数据集上，TRG将4B学生模型的宏观准确率比输出仅OPD基线提高了2.86个百分点。

Conclusion: TRG是一种有效的在线策略蒸馏方法，通过引导学生模型对时序扰动的响应，可以显著提升视频多模态理解任务中学生模型的性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers capabilities through teacher predictions on student-generated sequences, as studied by GKD and MiniLLM for language models and Video-OPD for video tasks. We ask how internal supervision can further support temporal learning. Direct hidden-state alignment does not explicitly use the student's temporal responses to choose a supervision direction. We propose Temporal-Response Guidance (TRG), which measures representation responses to local temporal perturbations, adds the teacher correction's projection onto their span back to the original correction, and restores its original norm. The projection is not normalized separately, and correction components outside the response space are retained. With an orthogonal tangent projector and a detached target, TRG changes the supervision direction while preserving the cosine-loss value and aligned-representation gradient norm at target construction. On TempCompass, Video-MME, MVBench, and Video-MMMU, TRG improves the 4B student's macro accuracy by 2.86 percentage points over the reported output-only OPD baseline.

</details>


### [125] [NOFACE3D: NOise-scheduled Focused Anchoring and Controlled Enhancement for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13050)
*James Rose,Jingwen Liang,Wenchao Ma,Oren Jacob,Haomiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOFACE3D是一个无需训练的框架，通过在生成3D模型时放大和重新编码细节关键区域，来提升细微结构的几何和纹理细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像到3D模型在生成精细细节方面存在不足，特别是对于细长、丰富或高曲率的区域，这主要是由于固定分辨率的生成器在不同复杂度的区域上采用相同的空间采样密度。

Method: NOFACE3D接收生成的网格、输入图像和细化框，提取子网格，放大并重新编码到高分辨率潜在网格中。通过基于流的采样和空间变化的噪声调度来细化该区域，然后将其整合回原始资产，同时保持周围几何和外观。

Result: 在TRELLIS.2、PAniC-3D、TexVerse和BigDetail数据集上，NOFACE3D生成比基线模型更清晰的几何形状和更精细的纹理细节。

Conclusion: NOFACE3D能够有效地提升3D模型中细节关键区域的重建质量，而无需重新训练现有的生成器，并且在推理时表现出色。

Abstract: Recent image-to-3D models generate complete, high-quality assets from a single image, with global shape and appearance that are already convincing; what remains is the fine scale, where small, thin, elongated, or detail-rich regions are reconstructed softly and under-resolved. This gap can arise in generators with fixed-resolution spatial grids: the same spatial sampling density is used for a large, smooth, low-curvature surface and for a thin, high-curvature structure that carries most of the perceived detail. To this end, we introduce NOFACE3D, a training-free framework that refines these detail-critical regions while preserving the rest of the asset. Given a generated mesh, its input image, and a refinement box, NOFACE3D extracts the selected submesh, scales it up to fill the generator's input volume, and re-encodes it into the full-resolution latent grid. Conditioned on the image crop, it refines the region through flow-based sampling with a spatially varying noise schedule that anchors boundary latents. The refined region is then integrated back into the original asset, preserving the surrounding geometry and appearance. We implement NOFACE3D on TRELLIS.2 and its sparse voxel representation; all of it operates purely at inference time on the frozen pretrained generator. We evaluate NOFACE3D on PAniC-3D, TexVerse and BigDetail, a new benchmark of 339 assets with annotated detail-critical regions. On all three, it generates sharper geometry and finer texture detail than the baselines. Project page: https://idkcouscous.github.io/NoFACE3D/

</details>


### [126] [UniMergeTrack: Hierarchical Model Merging for General RGB-X Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13053)
*Shiao Wang,Xiao Wang,Yuhe Ding,Jiarui Xu,Zikang Yan,Bo Jiang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为UniMergeTrack的新模型融合范式，用于RGB-X跟踪，通过独立训练和分层模型融合，实现了跨任务知识整合和任务特定的优化，并在多个基准测试中取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RGB-X跟踪器面临知识孤立、模型冗余或跨任务优化干扰的问题，需要一种能保留任务特定优化同时促进跨任务知识整合的新方法。

Method: UniMergeTrack首先独立训练特定模态的跟踪器，然后进行分层模型融合。利用高通滤波融合合并RGB-X任务中的可迁移骨干网络，并结合轻量级特定任务的专家来保留模态特定线索。通过最优传输引导的通道对齐来合并每个任务内的跟踪头，以解决通道不匹配问题。最后，通过分布对齐微调进一步提高统一骨干网络和特定任务头之间的兼容性。

Result: UniMergeTrack在LasHeR、RGBT234、VisEvent、VOT-RGBD22、COESOT和CDTB等多个基准测试中，在RGB-Thermal、RGB-Event和RGB-Depth跟踪任务上均取得了最先进或具有竞争力的性能，并且在新数据集上表现出很强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniMergeTrack通过其新颖的模型融合范式，有效地解决了现有RGB-X跟踪方法的局限性，实现了优异的跟踪性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: RGB-X tracking leverages complementary modalities, such as thermal infrared, event, or depth, to improve robustness under challenging conditions. Existing general-purpose RGB-X trackers typically follow two paradigms: independent task-specific training and joint training across modality combinations. However, the former suffers from knowledge isolation and model redundancy, while the latter introduces optimization interference across heterogeneous tasks. In this work, we present UniMergeTrack, a new model merging paradigm that preserves task-specific optimization while enabling effective cross-task knowledge integration. UniMergeTrack first trains modality-specific trackers independently and then performs hierarchical model merging. Specifically, transferable backbones are merged across RGB-X tasks via high-pass-filtered merging, and complemented by lightweight task-specific experts that preserve modality-specific cues. Tracking heads are merged within each task using optimal transport-guided channel alignment to mitigate hidden channel mismatch. A subsequent distribution alignment fine-tuning stage further improves compatibility between the unified backbone and task-specific heads. Extensive experiments on the LasHeR, RGBT234, VisEvent, VOT-RGBD22, COESOT, and CDTB benchmarks demonstrate that UniMergeTrack achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance across RGB-Thermal, RGB-Event, and RGB-Depth tracking tasks, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen datasets. The source code will be released upon acceptance. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking.

</details>


### [127] [A4-MR: Abnormality-aware Anatomical Alignment for MR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13056)
*Noel D'souza,Zhijian Yang,Nishank Singla,Pranay Doshi,Krisztian Koos,Fei Tan,Bruno Nunes,Laszlo Rusko,Bernadett Kolozsvari,Eszter Sabo,Emanuele Valeriano,Vikash Gupta,Seyed Iman Zare Estakhraji,Marc Lebel,Farzin Haddadpour,Taha Kass-Hout,Erhan Bas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: A4-MR是一个3D医学影像基础模型，通过大规模预训练和解剖级别对齐，在解剖定位、病理识别和分割等任务上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像（特别是MRI）的视觉-语言模型在解剖特异性定位和病理感知表示方面存在局限性，尤其是在实际应用中。

Method: 训练了一个包含约50,000份MRI研究（491,000个序列）的大规模3D视觉-语言基础模型A4-MR，其中一半数据配有放射学报告。模型设计用于通过带噪声标签的大规模预训练学习解剖定位，在解剖层面联合图像和文本表示，并学习区分健康与异常解剖的表示。

Result: A4-MR在多种下游任务中优于现有基础模型，包括在异常检测任务上比MedSigLIP提高了+1.7 AUROC。使用冻结编码器时，A4-MR在膝关节分割任务上比端到端的MedNeXt模型提高了+1.4 Dice，并在腹部器官分割任务上与最佳模型相当。在没有解剖提示的情况下，A4-MR的3D解剖定位能力与使用真实边界框的MedSAM2相当。

Conclusion: 解剖级别的多模态对齐和病理感知表示学习能够使一个MRI基础模型同时支持定位、分割和与病理相关的任务。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale foundation models have enabled strong generalization across a wide range of clinical and research tasks in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). However, existing vision-language MRI models remain limited in anatomy-specific localization and pathology-aware representation, particularly in real-world settings. We introduce A4-MR, a 3D vision-language foundation model trained on approximately 50,000 MRI studies comprising 491,000 series, roughly half of which are paired with radiology reports. A4-MR is designed to: (1) learn anatomy localization through large-scale pretraining with noisy labels; (2) align image and text representations at the anatomical level; and (3) learn distinct representations of healthy and abnormal anatomy. Across a diverse set of downstream tasks, A4-MR outperforms existing foundation models, including a +1.7 AUROC improvement over MedSigLIP for abnormality detection. Using a frozen encoder, A4-MR also surpasses an end-to-end task-specific MedNeXt model for knee segmentation by +1.4 Dice and performs comparably to the best task-specific model for abdominal organ segmentation on AMOS, within 0.4 Dice. Finally, without anatomical prompts, A4-MR localizes 3D anatomy comparably to MedSAM2 provided with ground-truth bounding boxes. Together, these results demonstrate that anatomy-level multimodal alignment and abnormality-aware representation learning can enable a single MRI foundation model to support localization, segmentation, and pathology-related tasks.

</details>


### [128] [Evolving Creatives with Clicks: CTR-Driven Advertising image Editing via Attribute Disentanglement and Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13066)
*Xu Zhang,Haohan Wang,Zheng Zhang,Yingjie Xia,Wei Feng,Honghe Zhu,Jingjing Lv,Changping Peng,Xuheng Qin,Zehua Zhang,Zheng Luo,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为ECC（Evolving Creatives with Clicks）的新方法，用于优化电商广告创意。ECC通过精细化编辑而非从头生成来改进广告图片，能够选择性地优化影响点击率（CTR）的属性，同时保留有益于点击的属性，显著提高了广告图片的CTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电商广告创意优化方法会平等地惩罚图片中的所有属性，即使其中一些属性实际上是有益于点击的。这导致了效率低下，因此需要一种更精细化的方法。

Method: ECC首先通过ANOVA和随机森林回归识别28个与CTR显著相关的属性。然后，基于不同CTR图片之间的属性对比，对提示模型进行指令微调以生成编辑建议。接着，利用真实的点击偏好进一步优化该模型。最后，引入一致性偏好优化（CPO）策略，通过强制执行语义等价的打乱建议之间的一致性，来缓解DPO过拟合并提高偏好泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，ECC显著提高了广告图片的CTR，并且在性能上大幅超越了最先进的模型。

Conclusion: ECC通过精细化编辑广告创意，能够更有效地识别和优化对CTR有负面影响的属性，同时保留有益属性，从而在电商广告创意优化方面取得了显著的改进。

Abstract: The optimization objective for e-commerce advertising creatives has shifted from aesthetic quality to online business metrics like click-through rate (CTR). Recent methods generate appealing ad images from product images by constructing CTR-based reward signals and refining prompt generation via Reinforcement Learning. Such paradigms equally penalize all attributes within an underperforming image, even though some are actually beneficial to clicks. In this work, we propose Evolving Creatives with Clicks (ECC), which reformulates creative optimization as fine-grained editing rather than generation from scratch. ECC selectively refines attributes that hurt CTR while preserving those that contribute positively. Specifically, faced with a rich attribute space across massive images, ECC first identifies 28 attributes significantly correlated with CTR via ANOVA and Random Forest regression. Based on attribute contrasts between images with disparate CTRs, we instruction-tune a prompt model to generate editing suggestions, then refine it with real click preferences since reference-derived suggestions are not uniformly beneficial. Furthermore, we introduce Consistent Preference Optimization (CPO) as ECC's preference optimization strategy, which enforces consistency across semantically equivalent shuffled suggestions to mitigate DPO overfitting and improve preference generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ECC significantly improves the CTR of ad images, outperforming state-of-the-art models by a substantial margin. The complete data pipeline, including attribute extraction and analysis, the resulting dataset, and source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [129] [Volume Rendering with Metric--Phase Fields for Thin Structures and Sign-Ambiguous Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13088)
*Junkai Deng,Jiayi Kong,Xuhui Chen,Guanli Hou,Fei Hou,Junhui Hou,Wenping Wang,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PhaseSurf的新型神经体积渲染框架，利用度量-相位场（MPFs）来改善对薄结构和符号模糊几何的重建质量，克服了现有基于SDF和UDF方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于SDF的神经隐式表面方法在重建非水密几何（如薄结构和符号模糊区域）时存在不足，而基于UDF的方法优化难度大。研究动机是开发一种鲁棒且易于优化的新方法。

Method: 提出PhaseSurf框架，引入度量-相位场（MPFs）。MPFs将几何分解为非负度量场（捕捉无符号邻近性）和连续相位场（提供软符号线索），并通过残差相位注入耦合。该方法支持直接提取等值面，并采用MPF特有的训练损失来增强稳定性。

Result: PhaseSurf在具有薄结构和符号模糊几何的多视图数据集上进行了实验。结果表明，与基于UDF的方法和3DGS管道相比，PhaseSurf能够生成更稳定、更高质量的几何结构，并避免了基于SDF方法的常见伪影。

Conclusion: PhaseSurf通过度量-相位场（MPFs）成功地实现了对具有挑战性几何的鲁棒且高质量的神经体积渲染重建，克服了现有方法的局限性，并提供了优于SDF和UDF方法的性能。

Abstract: Neural implicit surface methods that couple volume rendering with signed distance functions (SDFs), such as VolSDF and NeuS, achieve high-quality multi-view reconstruction for watertight objects. However, these approaches rely on a globally consistent inside-outside partition of space. This assumption becomes fragile for thin structures (e.g., sheets, tubes, and thin walls) and can break down for sign-ambiguous geometry (e.g., surfaces with boundary, non-manifold junctions, and closely spaced surface components), often resulting in spurious closures or loss of fine structures. Unsigned distance field (UDF) formulations relax the global sign requirement and better support non-watertight geometry, but are substantially harder to optimize. We propose PhaseSurf, a neural volume rendering framework built on Metric-Phase Fields (MPFs). MPFs decompose geometry into a nonnegative metric field capturing unsigned proximity and a continuous phase field providing a soft sign cue. By coupling the two through residual phase injection, MPFs yield a signed composite implicit function with stable near-surface gradients while retaining UDF-like robustness in sign-ambiguous regions. This design enables direct iso-surface extraction using standard Marching Cubes and avoids the failure modes of both pure SDF- and UDF-based formulations. We further introduce MPF-specific training losses that enforce metric consistency, stabilize phase transitions near the interface, and suppress spurious geometry in free space. Experiments on multi-view datasets with thin structures and sign-ambiguous geometry show that PhaseSurf produces more stable reconstructions and higher-quality geometry than UDF-based methods and recent 3DGS pipelines, while avoiding artifacts typical of SDF-based approaches.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Vision-Language Navigation with Awareness of Instruction-Execution Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13104)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 VLN-AIEP 的新颖框架，用于解决视觉语言导航中的指令执行进度问题，通过将进度估计与导航决策耦合，无需显式进度监督即可提高导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的视觉语言导航方法通常将轨迹与整个指令进行匹配，导致已执行的指令内容干扰后续决策。现有的进度估计方法需要显式监督、复杂的学习阶段或昂贵的在线 LLM 推理。

Method: VLN-AIEP 框架将进度估计与导航决策相结合。它通过将访问过的轨迹与指令关联来抑制已执行指令内容的嵌入，然后将候选位置与待执行内容进行匹配。该方法构建了一种基于地标的轨迹表示，以编码地标的语义和几何信息，从而将访问轨迹与已执行指令内容相关联。

Result: 在 R2R、RxR、CVDN 和 R2R-CE 数据集上的实验表明，所提出的 VLN-AIEP 方法在多个指标上均取得了良好的性能。

Conclusion: VLN-AIEP 成功地将进度估计与导航决策耦合，使进度估计器能够直接从导航决策标签中学习，从而无需显式进度注释，并在多个 VLN 基准测试中实现了最先进或有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: In Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks involving multi-step instructions as guidance, an agent must associate its observations with the relevant instruction content at each decision point. Most graph-based methods commonly match candidate trajectories with the entire instruction, allowing already executed content to interfere with subsequent decisions. Although instruction-execution progress provides a direct cue for separating executed and pending content, existing progress estimation approaches often rely on explicit progress supervision, sophisticated progress-learning stages, or costly online reasoning with LLMs. In this paper, we propose a robust VLN framework with Awareness of Instruction-Execution Progress (VLN-AIEP), which couples progress estimation with navigation decision-making. VLN-AIEP associates the visited trajectory with the instruction to suppress embeddings corresponding to executed content, and then matches candidate locations with the pending content. This coupling makes correct navigation decisions depend on accurate progress estimation, allowing the progress estimator to learn directly from navigation decision labels without explicit progress annotations. To support progress estimation, we construct a landmark-anchored trajectory representation that encodes the semantic labels and viewpoint-relative geometry of instruction-referred landmarks. These landmarks serve as spatial references for associating the visited trajectory with executed instruction content. Extensive experiments on R2R, RxR, CVDN, and R2R-CE demonstrate that the proposed method achieves favorable performance across multiple metrics.

</details>


### [131] [What Do Visual-Head Discovery Scores Actually Find? Attention Source, Necessity, and Random-Control Pitfalls in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13111)
*Md Abdul Kadir,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究人员发现，在发现视觉语言模型（VLMs）中注意力头的过程中，选择注意力来源和查询类型比选择数据集对最终确定的注意力头影响更大。注意力头的“必要性”指标能有效区分不同的发现设置，而“可引导性”指标则不能。在此基础上，作者提出了一个用于报告视觉头发现的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）使用注意力头来指导、解释模型行为并减少幻觉，但如何选择这些注意力头以及如何验证其有效性尚不清楚。研究人员希望探究在注意力头发现过程中，不同选择（如查询类型、数据集、注意力来源）如何影响最终结果，以及哪种验证方法（可引导性或必要性）能更有效地识别有用的注意力头。

Method: 在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 模型上，通过 2x3x2 的设计（查询类型、数据集、注意力来源）来探索注意力头的发现过程。研究人员评估了所有 12 种头集的可引导性（CERS）和必要性（NCS），并与同层的随机头集进行比较。同时，在多个 VLM 上复现了“referring + query-span”设置的效果。

Result: 1. 注意力来源和查询类型对选定头的影响远大于数据集；2. 必要性指标能区分不同的注意力来源，从查询范围注意力中发现的头比从最后一个标记注意力中发现的头更具必要性；3. 可引导性指标对发现的头没有特异性，发现的头集与随机头集在可引导性上无显著差异；4. “referring + query-span”设置的效果在多个 VLM 上均可复现。

Conclusion: 注意力头的发现过程，尤其是注意力来源和查询类型的选择，对最终结果有重要影响。必要性指标比可引导性指标更能有效地区分不同的发现设置。研究人员基于这些发现，提出了一套用于报告视觉头发现的协议。

Abstract: Attention heads that attend to image regions relevant to a question are increasingly used to steer, ground and interpret vision-language models (VLMs) and to reduce their hallucinations. They are mostly found by ranking heads with an attention- pattern score and validated by steering or masking them. We ask how the choices inside such a discovery score shape the heads it returns, and which of the two validations identifies them. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct we vary the query type, the discovery corpus and the token positions whose attention is read (the attention source) in a full 2 x 3 x 2 design, and test all 12 head-sets for steerability (CERS: Contrastive Evidence-Region Steering score) and necessity (NCS: Necessity Con- trast Score) against layer-matched random head-sets. (1) The attention source and the query type reshape the selected heads far more than the corpus: changing the source keeps a median of 49 of the top-100 heads, the query 55 and the cor- pus 84, while two halves of the same data share 96-99. (2) Necessity follows the attention source: head-sets discovered from query-span attention are more nec- essary than last-token ones (difference 0.083, p=6 x 10^-10), and the two re- ferring + query-span sets lie 17-19 standard deviations above 250 layer-matched random sets (Holm-adjusted p=0.048). The referring + query-span effect re- plicates on several recent VLMs. (3) Steerability is not specific to the discovered heads: no discovered head-set steers better than its layer-matched random sets (rank p >= 0.14), and random sets drawn from the same or from any layer span the discovered sets' scores. Necessity therefore separates the discovery settings, while steering at this strength does not. We turn these findings into a protocol for reporting visual-head discoveries.

</details>


### [132] [Replaying the Movie in Your Mind Decoding Dynamic Visual Perception and Recall from EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13120)
*Zikai Fang,Tian-Yi Zhou,Xuan-Hao Liu,Yixi Li,Xiaoyu Xing,Yutong Pei,Bao-Liang Lu,Wei-Long Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个名为PAIR的EEG基准数据集，用于研究人在内部回放动态场景时，哪些神经表征被保留下来。通过对比观看视频和回放视频的EEG信号，研究发现内部回放的神经表征在一定程度上与视觉感知相似，但感知和回放的状态在EEG信号上是高度可分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG解码动态视觉内容的研究主要集中在视觉感知阶段，但对于人们在内部回放动态场景时，神经表征的哪些方面得以保留，仍然不清楚。

Method: 研究者收集了一个名为PAIR的EEG数据集，该数据集包含20名参与者，采用交错、片段式范式，将观看自然视频与立即进行视觉回放配对。研究者使用多个解码模型，在不同层级的语义分类（精细和粗略）、元特征解码（运动、颜色、物体数量、人脸、人类存在）以及状态解码（区分感知与回放）任务上进行了基准测试。

Result: 在被试内验证中，一些模型-任务组合的表现相似。在粗略语义分类任务上，感知任务的准确率略高于回放任务（经过多重比较校正）。在状态解码任务上，感知和回放的状态高度可分。即使在去除alpha频段后，状态解码的准确率仍然很高。

Conclusion: 尽管内部回放的神经表征在一定程度上与视觉感知相似，但目前的验证协议下，感知和回放的状态在EEG信号上是高度可分的。Alpha频段并非区分感知和回放状态的唯一因素。

Abstract: Decoding dynamic visual content from EEG has advanced rapidly for visual perception, yet it remains unclear which aspects of neural representations are preserved when people internally replay dynamic scenes. We introduce PAIR, an EEG benchmark collected from 20 participants using an interleaved, clip-wise paradigm that pairs naturalistic video viewing with immediate visual recall. This design enables a comparison between externally driven perception and internally generated recall for the same stimulus content within a fixed trial sequence. We benchmark multiple decoding models on hierarchical semantic classification at both fine-grained and coarse-grained levels, meta-feature decoding tasks covering motion, color, object number, face, and human presence, and a state-decoding task that discriminates perception from recall. Within-subject validation results show numerically similar performance for several model--task combinations, alongside higher perception accuracy in two coarse-grained comparisons after multiple-comparison correction. In contrast, perception and recall are highly separable at the state level under the present validation protocol. State accuracy remains high after alpha-band ablation, although this does not exclude ocular or other task-related contributions.

</details>


### [133] [GuidedNav: Shaping Vision-Language Navigation Representations through Pre-action Attention and Spatial Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13122)
*Jingyu Guo,Jiaxin Huang,Chengxing Lin,Xiaoyu Luo,Cheng Wen,Shanshan Ye,Tingjin Chu,Shaoli Huang,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GuidedNav的统一框架，通过在训练时引入子任务、地标和空间引导，来加强视觉语言导航（VLN）代理的表征学习能力，以应对长距离导航任务，并取得了比现有方法更好的性能和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端导航策略依赖于动作监督，容易过拟合训练数据中的相关性，而不是学习任务相关的定位和几何推理能力，这限制了其泛化能力。作者希望显式地指导策略如何将指令与动作联系起来，以提高导航的有效性和泛化能力。

Method: GuidedNav框架通过在训练时引入三种引导方式来塑造表征：1) 子任务和地标引导：指导预动作注意力机制关注当前活跃的指令片段和相关的视觉区域。2) 空间引导：使策略的内部表征与捕捉长距离上下文和局部空间结构的几何特征对齐。此外，作者还创建了Guided-R2R和Guided-RxR两个增强型数据集来支持可解释的VLN研究。

Result: GuidedNav在R2R-CE和RxR-CE的val-unseen数据集上分别取得了8.1%和13.1%的相对成功率提升，创下了单目RGB策略的最新性能纪录，并且推理速度更快。实验证明了该方法的有效性和在真实世界中的迁移能力。

Conclusion: GuidedNav通过在训练中显式地引导表征学习，有效解决了当前VLN方法在处理长距离导航任务时的局限性，通过增强对指令、视觉信息和空间结构的理解，显著提高了导航性能和泛化能力，同时避免了额外的推理开销。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to track progress through an extended instruction, identify landmarks relevant to its current objective, and understand the surrounding spatial structure to act. End-to-end navigation policies typically rely on action supervision to implicitly learn these capabilities. Without explicit guidance on the evidence underlying each decision, however, policies can overfit to correlations predictive of training actions rather than learn the task-relevant grounding and geometry needed for generalization. Our insight is to guide the representations that connect instructions to actions: identifying what remains to be done determines which visual evidence matters, while understanding the geometry around that evidence informs how to move. We introduce GuidedNav, a unified framework that explicitly shapes these representations through training-time guidance. Subtask and landmark guidance steer native pre-action attention toward the active instruction segment and relevant visual regions, grounding action prediction in the current objective. Spatial guidance aligns the policy's internal representations with geometric features that capture long-horizon context and local spatial structure. Together, these objectives strengthen navigation capabilities while avoiding the inference overhead of intermediate textual reasoning and online geometric encoding. We also introduce Guided-R2R and Guided-RxR, two annotation-enriched navigation datasets that support more interpretable VLN research. GuidedNav establishes state-of-the-art performance among the compared monocular RGB policies, improving success rates by 8.1% and 13.1% relative to the strongest prior results on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, respectively, while achieving faster inference. Extensive experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness and real-world transferability of our approach.

</details>


### [134] [Measurable Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting with Joint Region and Depth Supervision for Interactive Building Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13143)
*Yi Zhang,Tian Tan,Ray Kai Leung Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可测量的语义3D高斯溅射（3DGS）框架，通过区域和深度监督，结合掩码、多视图立体（MVS）点云和语义高斯场，实现了交互式建筑检测。开发了AEC-SplatTwin平台，用于实时可视化、语义分析、定量测量和多模态大语言模型（MLLM）辅助交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图像的3D重建方法在 photorealistic 重建方面表现良好，但在工程应用所需的几何可测量性、语义结构和交互式信息访问方面存在不足。

Method: 该研究提出了一种基于掩码区域监督和点级深度监督的3DGS框架。掩码用于去除背景，深度监督利用MVS点云引导高斯中心到物理表面。同时，通过语义高斯场将类别特征与高斯原语关联，实现语义渲染和组件级检测。最终开发了AEC-SplatTwin平台。

Result: 在QuakeCity和HKUPB数据集上，高斯中心深度MAE分别降低了58.1%和86.8%，语义场mIoU达到0.8529和0.8642。在HKUPB上，高度、宽度和面积测量的平均相对误差分别为0.33%、1.07%和0.60%。

Conclusion: 该框架将3DGS从纯视觉重建扩展到了可测量、语义结构化和交互式的工程表示，适用于建筑检测。

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction provides a scalable representation for building inspection and maintenance. However, photorealistic reconstruction alone does not fully provide the geometric measurability, semantic structure, and interactive information access required for engineering applications. This study proposes a measurable semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework with region and depth supervision for interactive building inspection from multi-view imagery. Mask-based region supervision suppresses irrelevant backgrounds and focuses optimization on target regions. Point-wise depth supervision guides Gaussian centers toward physical surfaces using reference geometry derived from multi-view stereo (MVS) point clouds, providing geometric anchors for measuring physical dimensions and areas. A semantic Gaussian field associates learnable class features with individual Gaussian primitives, enabling semantic rendering and component-level inspection. These capabilities are operationalized through AEC-SplatTwin, an interactive building inspection platform supporting real-time visualization, semantic analysis, quantitative measurement, and multimodal large language model (MLLM)-assisted interaction. On the synthetic UAV-inspection QuakeCity and smartphone-captured HKUPB datasets, the framework reduces Gaussian-center depth MAE by 58.1% and 86.8%, while its semantic field achieves mIoU values of 0.8529 and 0.8642. On HKUPB, the mean relative errors for height, width, and area measurements are 0.33%, 1.07%, and 0.60%. These results show that the proposed framework extends 3DGS beyond visual reconstruction into a measurable, semantically structured, and interactive engineering representation for building inspection.

</details>


### [135] [From Glance to Focus: Mitigating Recognition Laziness in MLLMs for Fine-Grained Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13154)
*Yutong Yang,Zhuqi Liu,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了名为LIVE（Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition）的训练无关框架，用于解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度视觉识别（FGVR）中存在的“FGVR懒惰”现象，通过优化候选类别列表和识别引导来发掘MLLMs的潜在FGVR能力，并在多项基准测试中证明了其有效性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度视觉识别（FGVR）方面表现不如预期，尽管它们具备视觉感知和广泛的类别知识基础。现有方法多采用领域特定的后训练，可能牺牲泛化能力。本研究旨在从经验和理论上深入理解MLLMs的FGVR机制，并发现“FGVR懒惰”现象。

Method: 研究提出了Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition (LIVE) 框架，这是一个训练无关的框架。LIVE通过查询自适应的候选类别推断来生成精简但可靠的候选列表。同时，LIVE通过挖掘类内特征和类间差异来构建类别特定的FGVR技能。最后，LIVE根据识别的不确定性和类别混淆情况，有选择地调用详细的技能引导，将FGVR从粗略观察转变为关注区分性视觉线索与类别概念的关联。

Result: 在13个基准测试（包括细粒度类别识别、动作识别和一般类别识别）上，与24种最先进的方法进行对比，LIVE证明了其对不同MLLM骨干模型的有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: FGVR懒惰是MLLMs在FGVR中表现不足的原因，该现象指的是在特定候选类别列表和识别引导下，视觉线索与类别概念的关联不足。LIVE框架通过优化候选列表和识别引导，成功缓解了FGVR懒惰，并显著提升了MLLMs在FGVR任务上的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) generally fall short of expectations in Fine-Grained Visual Recognition (FGVR), although they exhibit promising FGVR foundations, i.e., visual perception and broad category knowledge. Most existing studies seek to remedy this deficiency by adopting domain-specific post-training, at the risk of compromising generalization to unseen domains. Different from these efforts, in this paper, we revisit the FGVR mechanism underlying MLLMs from both the empirical and theoretical perspectives and uncover an inference-time phenomenon termed FGVR Laziness. In brief, FGVR Laziness refers to the insufficient association between visual cues and category concepts under certain candidate category lists and recognition guidance, leaving the FGVR foundations of MLLMs largely under-utilized. To mitigate FGVR Laziness, we propose Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition (LIVE), a training-free framework which jointly refines candidate lists and recognition guidance to elicit the latent FGVR capability of MLLMs. Specifically, LIVE first performs query-adaptive candidate inference to derive a compact yet reliable candidate list. Meanwhile, LIVE constructs category-specific FGVR skills by mining the intra-category characteristics and inter-category differences. Finally, LIVE selectively invokes detailed skill guidance according to recognition uncertainty and category confusion, shifting MLLM-based FGVR from a cursory glance to a focus on associating discriminative visual cues with category concepts. Extensive experiments on 13 benchmarks against 24 state-of-the-art methods spanning fine-grained category recognition, action recognition, and general category recognition, verify the effectiveness and generalizability of LIVE across different MLLM backbones. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [136] [PreCoG: Preview-Edit for Efficient Video Correction in Generation-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13174)
*Yilai Liu,Wenqi Pei,Zhifeng Lu,Shiyuan Zhang,Zongxi Liu,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PreCoG的迭代文本到视频生成框架，通过低成本预览诊断错误并对生成轨迹进行局部编辑，以在纠正语义错误的同时最小化对已有正确内容的干扰，从而提高生成效率和内容一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的迭代文本到视频生成方法在每次纠错时都需要完整的生成过程，导致计算冗余并可能破坏已正确生成的部分。研究的动机是开发一种更高效、更能保持内容一致性的迭代纠错方法。

Method: PreCoG框架包含两个主要部分：PreQA负责诊断低成本预览中的语义失败并生成纠正条件；PreEdit负责在这些条件下编辑缓存的生成轨迹，通过限制正交变化来减少对已正确内容的干扰。最终仅对选定的候选进行完整生成，并重用其缓存轨迹。

Result: PreCoG在VBench和T2V-CompBench基准上取得了最高分，在VS2得分上接近领先方法的性能但推理时间仅为其0.56倍。此外，在质量和对齐度指标上，PreCoG的性能优于GENMAC，推理时间为其0.86倍。

Conclusion: PreCoG是一种有效的迭代文本到视频生成框架，它通过局部编辑和重用缓存轨迹，显著提高了生成效率，同时保持了语义一致性，并能有效纠正语义错误，而不会对已正确生成的内容造成不必要的破坏。

Abstract: Iterative text-to-video generation relies on feedback to correct compositional and temporal failures. However, existing methods typically require another full generation run for each correction, repeating computation and potentially disrupting already correct content. We introduce PreCoG, an iterative framework for correcting semantic failures during video generation.
