PreQA diagnoses failures from low-cost previews and constructs corrective conditions, while PreEdit edits cached generation trajectories under these conditions, attenuating orthogonal changes to limit disruption of already correct content. PreCoG completes generation only for the selected candidate, reusing its cached trajectory to reduce redundant computation and help preserve semantic consistency between the preview and the final video. Experiments show that PreCoG achieves the highest VBench and T2V-CompBench scores and approaches VSR's leading VS2 score at $0.56\times$ its inference time. It also outperforms GENMAC on all three quality and alignment metrics at $0.86\times$ its inference time.

</details>


### [137] [Fix the Match, Not the Solver: STITCH -- a Self-Supervised Compatibility Measure for Eroded Visual Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13176)
*Nadav Alali,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为STITCH的自监督兼容性度量（CM）方法，用于解决真实世界拼图碎片边界缺失导致传统CM失效的问题。STITCH通过ViT内部块的交叉注意力机制进行匹配，无需人工标注，在多个拼图基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升，并证明了CM是拼图重构性能的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的拼图碎片边界常因侵蚀而丢失，这使得依赖边界信息的传统兼容性度量（CM）失效，成为拼图重构的性能瓶颈。研究人员希望找到一种新的CM方法，能够有效处理边界缺失的碎片，并提升整体拼图重构的性能。

Method: 研究提出了一种自监督的兼容性度量方法STITCH。该方法利用冻结的ViT（Vision Transformer）块内部的Token，通过一个轻量级的交叉注意力模块进行比较，从而避免了对缺失边界的依赖。STITCH在没有人工标注的情况下进行训练，并且可以作为一个即插即用的模块集成到现有的兼容性求解器中。

Result: STITCH在JPwLEG-5基准测试中取得了.992（绝对值）/.954（性能）的成绩，性能提升了.629，接近饱和。在新的ImageNet 7x7基准测试中，STITCH也优于包括基于生成模型的方法在内的所有其他方法。研究还发现，STITCH在不同程度的碎片侵蚀下表现出鲁棒性，并且无需重新训练即可泛化到超过40%碎片缺失的拼图。在GAP数据集上，STITCH也使得基于兼容性的求解器超越了最新的基于学习的求解器。

Conclusion: 兼容性度量（CM）是拼图重构性能的关键瓶颈，而非求解器本身。通过改进CM（如STITCH），即使是简单的求解器也能实现高性能的拼图重构。STITCH是一种有效的自监督CM，能够处理边界缺失的真实世界拼图碎片，并在多个基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升，证明了其在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Unlike the clean and pixel-aligned complete pieces of standard jigsaw benchmarks, real-world puzzle fragments lose their boundary regions to erosion, destroying the signal that classical compatibility measures (CMs) rely on. We first re-establish, via oracle experiments, that the CM, not the solver, is the performance bottleneck: with a perfect CM, even a simple greedy solver achieves perfect reconstruction. This motivates STITCH, a self-supervised CM that sidesteps missing boundaries by comparing frozen ViT patch tokens from piece interiors via a lightweight cross-attention head (~249K trainable parameters), trained without manual annotation. Plugged into unchanged compatibility-based solvers, STITCH reaches .992/.954 Abs/Perf on JPwLEG-5 (+.629 Perf over the best prior method), nearly saturating the benchmark. On a new ImageNet 7x7 benchmark, it outperforms all alternatives, including inpaint-then-match pipelines built on modern generative models. Importantly, performance remains robust across erosion levels and generalizes to puzzles with over 40% missing pieces without retraining. Trained on the recent GAP datasets of irregular, heavily eroded fragments, the same CM recipe enables compatibility-based solvers to surpass recent learning-based solvers on GAP-3 and GAP-5. Since this leap in performance is achieved by a pluggable CM module, the takeaway is clear: fix the match, not the solver.

</details>


### [138] [CompactSplat: Spatially Adaptive Gaussian Distribution for Feedforward Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13177)
*Bing He,Jingnan Gao,Yunuo Chen,Zhengxue Cheng,Rong Xie,Li Song,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CompactSplat是一种新的前馈3D高斯喷溅框架，通过内容感知的高斯分配来优化稀疏视图下的3D场景重建，在复杂区域密集分配高斯，在平坦区域扩大高斯，从而减少了冗余，提高了重建质量和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D高斯喷溅方法在稀疏视图下重建3D场景时，存在表示冗余（尤其是在同质区域）和未能利用平面结构先验的问题，导致几何结构建模不理想。

Method: CompactSplat通过集成纹理引导的空间划分和分层采样，实现了3D高斯的自适应分配：在复杂区域密集分布高斯，在平坦区域使用尺度调制的扩展高斯。该框架还支持推理时的动态稀疏控制，无需重新训练网络。

Result: CompactSplat在RealEstate10K、DL3DV和ScanNet数据集上进行了实验，结果显示其在标准指标和高分辨率渲染一致性方面均优于现有方法，用更少的高斯实例实现了高保真重建。

Conclusion: CompactSplat通过内容感知的高斯分配，有效解决了现有3D高斯喷溅方法的冗余和结构建模问题，实现了更高效、更高质量的3D场景重建，并且能够灵活控制表示的稀疏度。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from sparse views without per-scene optimization remains highly challenging, especially for recovering accurate geometry and fine textures. While recent feedforward approaches leverage generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for scene generation, they typically assign one or multiple Gaussians to each pixel. Such uniform allocation not only produces highly redundant representations by wasting primitives in homogeneous areas, but also fails to exploit the inherent geometric priors of planar surfaces, often resulting in sub-optimal structural modeling. To address these limitations, we present CompactSplat, a novel feedforward framework that enables a content-aware allocation of 3D Gaussians. By integrating texture-guided spatial partitioning with hierarchical sampling, CompactSplat adaptively distributes primitives - concentrating dense Gaussians in complex regions while tiling flat areas with expanded, scale-modulated primitives. Moreover, our framework inherently supports dynamic sparsity control at inference time, enabling seamless trade-offs between rendering fidelity and memory footprint without requiring network retraining. Extensive experiments on RealEstate10K, DL3DV, and ScanNet demonstrate that CompactSplat consistently outperforms prior methods on both standard metrics and high-resolution rendering consistency, achieving high-fidelity reconstructions with significantly fewer primitives.

</details>


### [139] [High-Throughput Generative Video Super-Resolution on a Single Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13179)
*Georgios Panaretou,Sotirios Chatzis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需重新训练即可在单个高端消费级GPU上实现高效高清生成式视频超分辨率（VSR）的零样本框架，通过解耦空间增强和时间稳定，并引入分辨率感知混合注意力机制来提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式VSR方法虽然能生成视觉上吸引人的细节，但其迭代采样和时空耦合的特性限制了其实际应用。研究人员希望在单个高端消费级GPU上实现实用的高清生成式VSR，且无需端到端重新训练。

Method: 该框架解耦了空间增强和时间稳定。空间上，使用时间蒸馏的单步扩散模型进行上采样。时间上，通过轻量级预训练的基于流的插值器生成中间帧以促进时间连贯性。引入了分辨率感知混合注意力机制，在零样本设置下，用邻域注意力替换选定的高分辨率全局注意力层，同时保留低分辨率关键层的全局注意力，以提高可扩展性和时间一致性。

Result: 该框架在720p分辨率下可达约32 FPS。在4K分辨率下，提出的注意力替换机制可带来高达44%的加速。在标准VSR基准测试中，该方法显著提高了运行时效率，在感知质量和时间一致性方面表现良好，尽管在处理复杂运动方面存在局限性，但可以通过运动门控进行部分缓解。

Conclusion: 提出的零样本框架有效地解决了生成式VSR的实际部署难题，通过解耦时空处理和引入高效的注意力机制，实现了在消费级硬件上的高性能高清视频超分辨率，并在速度和质量之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Generative video super-resolution (VSR) can recover visually compelling high-frequency detail by leveraging diffusion priors, but iterative sampling and heavy spatio-temporal coupling make practical deployments difficult. We study a different operating point: practical HD generative VSR on a single high-end consumer GPU without end-to-end retraining. To this end, we present a zero-shot framework built entirely from pretrained components.
  The proposed system decouples spatial enhancement from temporal stabilization. A time-distilled one-step diffusion backbone handles spatial upscaling, while temporal coherence is promoted by a lightweight pretrained flow-based interpolator that synthesizes intermediate frames between super-resolved keyframes. To further improve scalability, we introduce a resolution-aware hybrid attention scheme that, in a zero-shot manner, replaces selected high-resolution global-attention layers with neighborhood attention while preserving global attention in semantically critical low-resolution layers, while also improving temporal consistency.
  Experiments show that the proposed framework reaches up to ~32 FPS at 720p resolution under interleaved inference on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090. At 4K, the proposed attention substitution can yield up to a 44% speedup over the original attention implementation. Across standard VSR benchmarks, the method consistently shifts the speed-quality trade-off decisively toward runtime efficiency, trading reconstruction fidelity for strong no-reference perceptual quality, while remaining reasonably competitive against generative approaches in temporal consistency. It also exposes clear limitations in handling more challenging motion, which a simple motion gate partially mitigates. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [140] [MixTok: Adaptive Visual Tokenization with Global 1D Tokens and Sparse 2D Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13181)
*Chenge Li,Yefei He,Xinyu Liu,Feng Chen,Hong Zhou,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTok 是一种混合视觉标记器，通过结合紧凑的 1D 全局表示和稀疏的 2D 标记来实现高效的图像表示，同时保留精细细节。它使用内容自适应路由器来确定 2D 标记的数量和位置，以实现具有竞争力的图像重建和生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于网格的标记器在空间分配上不均匀，而紧凑的 1D 标记器可能无法保留局部细节。需要一种能够平衡表示紧凑性和保留精细图像内容的新型标记方法。

Method: MixTok 结合了紧凑的 1D 全局表示和稀疏的 2D 标记。内容自适应路由器根据细化优先级对候选位置进行排序，并根据目标平均标记预算确定每个图像的 2D 标记数量。最高分位置的 2D 标记特征替换了从 1D 表示恢复的空间特征，共享解码器从混合特征中重建图像。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256x256 数据集上，MixTok 在大约 128 和 131 个标记/图像的平均预算下，分别取得了 1.195 的 rFID 和 1.95 的 gFID，实现了极具竞争力的重建和生成性能。

Conclusion: MixTok 是一种有效的混合视觉标记方法，通过结合全局和局部信息，在保持表示紧凑性的同时，成功地保留了图像的精细内容，并在图像重建和生成任务上取得了优异的性能。

Abstract: Efficient visual tokenization requires balancing representation compactness with the preservation of fine-grained image content. Conventional grid-based tokenizers allocate tokens uniformly across spatial locations despite non-uniform visual complexity, whereas compact 1D tokenizers aggregate global information but can struggle to preserve local details. We introduce MixTok, a hybrid visual tokenizer that combines a compact 1D global representation with sparse 2D tokens for local refinement. A content-adaptive router ranks candidate locations by refinement priority and determines the number of 2D tokens for each image under a target average token budget. At the highest-scoring locations, 2D token features replace the spatial features recovered from the 1D representation, and a shared decoder reconstructs the image from the resulting mixed features. On ImageNet 256 x 256, MixTok achieves highly competitive reconstruction and generation performance, with an rFID of 1.195 and a gFID of 1.95 at respective average budgets of approximately 128 and 131 content tokens per image.

</details>


### [141] [Destination-Conditioned Adaptation with Semantic-Geometric Alignment for Generative Inbetweening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13187)
*Zhiyu Xu,Wei Shang,Yushen Xu,Wenwen Li,Zhiyan Xu,Pengfei Zhu,Dongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DSG-GI的框架，用于改进基于预训练扩散模型的视频插帧，特别是针对大位移和非刚性形变的场景，通过目标条件适应和语义几何对齐来增强目标帧的约束性和运动的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练图生视频扩散模型在大运动插帧方面虽然有优势，但并未专门针对目标帧进行优化，可能导致生成的帧偏离目标或运动不稳定。

Method: DSG-GI框架包含两个主要组件：1. 目标条件适应（Destination-Conditioned Adaptation），将目标帧视为序列的终点，提供沿生成轨迹的渐进式引导。2. 语义几何对齐（Semantic-Geometric Alignment），将特征的空时关系几何与冻结的视频基础编码器对齐，并对概率路径数据端点附近的样本施加流匹配感知的潜在流约束。

Result: 在标准和大型运动插帧基准测试中，DSG-GI在时间稳定性、目标帧保真度以及感知质量方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: DSG-GI框架通过目标条件适应和语义几何对齐，有效增强了视频插帧对目标帧的引导能力，并保持了连贯的语义几何运动结构，能够生成更稳定、更准确且质量更高的中间帧。

Abstract: Generative inbetweening aims to synthesize temporally coherent intermediate frames between a start frame and a target frame. Although pretrained image-to-video diffusion models provide strong priors for large-motion inbetweening, they are not explicitly designed to reach a specified target frame. They may therefore generate realistic individual frames while drifting from the target or producing unstable motion under large displacements and non-rigid dynamics. We propose DSG-GI, a framework that adapts pretrained latent video diffusion models through \textbf{D}estination-Conditioned Adaptation and \textbf{S}emantic-\textbf{G}eometric Alignment. Destination-Conditioned Adaptation treats the target frame as the destination of the sequence and provides temporally progressive guidance along the generated trajectory. Semantic-Geometric Alignment aligns the token-wise spatio-temporal relational geometry of backbone features with that of a frozen video foundation encoder and applies a flow-matching-aware latent flow constraint to samples near the data endpoint of the probability path. Together, these components strengthen destination guidance and preserve coherent semantic-geometric motion structure. Extensive experiments on standard and large-motion inbetweening benchmarks demonstrate improvements in temporal stability, target-frame faithfulness, and perceptual quality. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [142] [Golden Anchor for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13217)
*Jinyuan Xu,Shitong Shao,Lichen Bai,Zikai Zhou,Guoxi Huang,Nantheera Anantrasirichai,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，直接从文本生成图像模型（T2I）生成的图像作为视频生成模型（I2V）的锚点，其质量与最终视频质量相关性不高，甚至负相关。提出了一种名为Golden Anchor Learning（GAL）的框架，通过下游视频反馈来优化T2I模型，从而生成更适合I2V模型的锚点，显著提高了视频生成质量和运动流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要依赖图像到视频（I2V）生成，其中文本到图像（T2I）生成的图像作为锚点。但研究发现，T2I生成的图像质量与下游视频质量之间缺乏直接的正相关性，甚至存在负相关性。因此，需要一种新的方法来生成更适合I2V模型的锚点。

Method: 提出Golden Anchor Learning（GAL）框架，通过下游视频反馈来优化T2I生成器。GAL首先在一个固定的I2V模型下评估候选锚点生成的视频，然后将视频奖励转化为图像级别的偏好，用于通过差分偏好优化（DPO）来微调T2I生成器。

Result: GAL框架在所有测试的I2V模型上均显著提高了视频质量（VBench平均提升2.97%-8.46%），并提升了视频的运动流畅度（VBench动态度提升5.62%-38.24%）。即使GAL仅在HunyuanVideo-1.5上训练，其提升效果也能泛化到其他I2V模型。

Conclusion: 通过下游视频反馈优化T2I模型以学习“黄金锚点”是一种有效的方法，能够显著提升视频生成质量和运动表现，解决了现有T2I-I2V流程中锚点不匹配的问题。

Abstract: Video generation supports digital content creation, with applications in virtual avatars, AI-driven filmmaking, interactive game engines, and world models. State-of-the-art systems achieve controllability primarily through image-to-video (I2V) generation, in which an injected anchor image determines the identity, composition, and style of the resulting video. In modern pipelines, this anchor is synthesized by a text-to-image (T2I) generator. This raises a largely overlooked question: Are images obtained directly from the T2I model truly suitable for current video models? Our analysis shows that anchor aesthetic and quality scores are weakly correlated with downstream video quality. The structure-and-texture score is negatively correlated with I2V video quality in both the visual and motion dimensions. Fine-tuning the T2I model on an image reward accordingly yields anchors with visibly denser structural and character detail: they score higher on image metrics, yet lower the video reward on every I2V model we test and change downstream motion unpredictably. We therefore introduce Golden Anchor Learning (GAL), to our knowledge the first framework for learning golden anchors by optimizing a T2I generator with downstream video feedback. GAL evaluates candidate anchors through the videos they induce under a frozen I2V model and converts video rewards into image-level preferences for DPO fine-tuning of the T2I generator. On all four I2V models, GAL raises the VBench average by $2.97\%$--$8.46\%$ over the untuned generator. The rendered clips also score higher under VideoReward-Overall, and their extracted frames under HPSv3 and UniPercept. GAL yields consistent motion gains across all four I2V models, as measured by a $5.62\%$--$38.24\%$ increase in VBench dynamic degree. Although GAL is trained only on HunyuanVideo-1.5 renders, these gains extend to the other three I2V models.

</details>


### [143] [Complexity-Calibrated Reconstruction Discrepancy for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13219)
*Haocheng Wu,Xiangjie Sui,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需参考的3D点云质量评估（PCQA）方法，通过连续重建来分离和补偿由点云结构复杂性和信号退化引起的差异，并结合几何和投影视图信息得到最终的退化分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决表面重建在扭曲点云中引起的差异，该差异同时受点云结构复杂性和信号退化影响，并提出一种无需参考（reference-free）的PCQA策略来估计和补偿这种复杂性引起的成分。

Method: 采用连续重建策略。首先，对扭曲点云进行一次重建，得到输入到重建的差异（包含信号退化和内容复杂性）。然后，用更强的平滑度对输入点云进行再次重建，得到连续重建之间的差异，将其作为复杂性引起的成分的代理。最后，利用该代理来校准输入到重建的差异，并将校准后的几何和投影视图差异融合得到最终的退化分数。使用预训练的感知表示来测量差异，且无需PCQA特定训练。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法一致优于大多数通用基线方法，并达到了与一些全参考指标相当的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效地分离和补偿点云的结构复杂性和信号退化对重建差异的影响，提供了一种无需参考且性能优越的3D点云质量评估方案。

Abstract: The discrepancy induced by surface reconstruction in a distorted point cloud is jointly influenced by its intrinsic structural complexity and signal degradation. This observation motivates a referencefree point cloud quality assessment (PCQA) strategy that estimates and compensates for the complexity-induced component. We implement this strategy through successive reconstruction. Specifically, reconstructing the distorted point cloud produces an input-toreconstruction discrepancy that combines the effects of signal degradation and content complexity. Reapplying the reconstruction operator with stronger smoothing produces a discrepancy between successive reconstructions, which serves as a proxy for the complexityinduced component. This proxy is then used to calibrate the inputto-reconstruction discrepancy. We apply the same strategy to 3D geometry patches and their aligned 2D regions, enabling local comparison of the resulting discrepancies. The calibrated geometric and projected-view discrepancies are then fused into a final degradation score. For both branches, the discrepancies are measured using frozen pretrained perceptual representations. Our method does not require PCQA-specific training or fine-tuning. Experiments on three public databases show that the proposed method consistently outperforms most generic baselines and achieves performance comparable to several full-reference metrics.The code is available at https://github.com/HC-W-whc/CCRD-NR-PCQA.git.

</details>


### [144] [Multi-Modal Graph Neural Network-Inspired Vessel Identification and Tracking for UAV-Based Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13220)
*Chenjie Zhao,Jiangtian Nie,Jiawen Kang,Yuxu Lu,Ryan Wen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AMGT的无人机多模态图学习框架，用于UAV辅助的海事物联网中的船舶识别和跟踪，通过多模态数据融合、优化的检测和跟踪算法，以及运动感知坐标统一策略，提高了态势感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在海事监控中具有重要作用，但实际应用中面临异构传感、恶劣成像条件和无人机运动导致的空间不对齐等挑战，影响了态势感知能力的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 提出多参数视角变换模型，对可见光和红外图像进行对齐和融合。2. 使用插值和线性预测技术，对自动识别系统（AIS）消息进行时间同步。3. 开发了MAir-YOLOv8-OBB检测器，用于检测小型和定向船舶。4. 采用MAir-SORT算法进行稳定的船舶跟踪。5. 设计了图学习驱动的关联网络，以建模船舶之间以及船舶与无人机之间的时间动态和空间依赖关系。6. 引入了运动感知坐标统一（MCU）策略，以减少无人机运动引起的非线性不对齐。

Result: 在MAP-M2UAV50数据集上进行实验，AMGT框架在准确性和效率方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: AMGT框架有效解决了无人机海事监控中的关键挑战，展现了其在船舶监控、交通态势感知和智能海事交通服务等UAV辅助海事物联网应用中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: The unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based maritime surveillance is becoming an important part of maritime Internet of Things (IoT) and intelligent transportation systems. It provides a flexible and cost-effective way to large-scale maritime traffic monitoring. However, achieving robust situational awareness remains challenging in practical maritime environments. The main challenges arise from heterogeneous sensing modalities, adverse imaging conditions, and spatial misalignment caused by UAV motion. To address these problems, this paper proposes an airborne multi-modal graph learning-driven framework, termed AMGT, for vessel identification and tracking in UAV-assisted maritime IoT. Specifically, visible and infrared images are first aligned and fused through a multi-parameter perspective transformation model. This improves visual perception under low visibility and complex illumination conditions. Automatic identification system (AIS) messages are then temporally synchronized using interpolation and linear prediction. To detect small and oriented vessels, we develop a mixed-modality MAir-YOLOv8-OBB detector, followed by the MAir-SORT algorithm for stable vessel tracking. We further design a graph learning-driven association network to model temporal dynamics and spatial dependencies among vessels and between vessels and UAVs. A motion-aware coordinate unification (MCU) strategy is also introduced to reduce nonlinear misalignment caused by UAV motion. Experiments on the MAP-M2UAV50 dataset show that AMGT achieves high accuracy and efficiency. The results demonstrate its potential for UAV-assisted maritime IoT applications, including vessel monitoring, traffic situational awareness, and intelligent maritime transportation services. The dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/MAPGroup/AMGT.

</details>


### [145] [Automated Detection and Encoding of Cartographic Signs in Historical Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13224)
*Remi Petitpierre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于检测历史地图中的地图符号并生成包含语义和风格信息的符号嵌入的两阶段框架，并介绍了相应的 CaSiDD 数据集。该方法利用 YOLOv10-X 和改进的 RADIOv4 主干，实现了自动符号检测和可解释的符号表示，为地图符号的规模化提取和比较研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史地图中的符号和图标是历史地理学的重要证据来源，但手动提取和分析效率低下。研究旨在开发自动化方法来解决这一问题，以便进行更大规模的地图符号比较研究。

Method: 该研究提出了一个两阶段框架：1. 使用 YOLOv10-X 检测历史地图中的地图符号。2. 生成包含语义和风格变化的、可解释的符号嵌入。通过系统地设计嵌入策略，最大化表达能力并抑制数字化和背景材料的干扰。最终方法基于适应性强的 RADIOv4 主干。

Result: YOLOv10-X 在历史地图上的自动符号检测是可行的。所提出的方法能够有效地编码符号的形式和语义，并生成可解释的表示，同时考虑了风格变化。

Conclusion: 这项工作为规模化提取地图符号证据，支持地图文化和地理历史研究中的比较分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Symbols and icons in archival maps represent an expansive source of evidence for historical geography. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework for (1) detecting cartographic signs in historical maps and (2) generating interpretable sign embeddings that capture both semantics and stylistic variations. We also introduce CaSiDD, an open benchmark dataset spanning multiple sign categories and diverse cartographic styles, designed for generic sign detection. Using YOLOv10-X, we demonstrate the practicality of automated sign detection on historical maps. We then present strategies for creating contrasted, informative embeddings of cartographic signs. We approach embedding design systematically, maximizing expressiveness while suppressing digitization or background material confounders. Our final method, based on an adapted RADIOv4 backbone, effectively encodes both sign form and semantics, while producing interpretable representations. This work lays a foundation for scalable evidence extraction for the comparative study of map signs within cartographic culture and geohistorical research.

</details>


### [146] [FPD-Drive: Future Policy Distillation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13232)
*Li-Heng Chen,Qifeng Chen,Chengwei Wu,Yingjie Zhang,Sheng Yang,Hongbo Fu,Shaoqing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 FPD-Drive 的端到端自动驾驶模型，通过从具有未来知识的教师模型中蒸馏多目标未来轨迹，并采用安全分数校正来改进训练，从而在多个基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶模型主要依赖真实世界日志训练，监督信号有限，难以学习多样化的安全驾驶行为。现有的多目标监督方法在安全候选轨迹选择方面面临挑战。

Method: 1. 未来特权多目标蒸馏 (Future-Privileged Multi-Target Distillation)：将教师模型的未来信息（包括多样的高质量轨迹目标）转移给学生模型，以扩展监督范围。2. 安全感知分数校正 (Safety-Aware Score Correction)：纠正安全和不安全候选轨迹之间的排名误差，提高安全轨迹的评分。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 navtest 上，基本模型达到 94.9 PDMS，FPD-Drive-Pro 达到 95.5 PDMS。在 NAVSIM v2 navhard 上，基本模型达到 55.9 EPDMS，FPD-Drive-Pro 达到 58.1 EPDMS，均达到 SOTA 性能。在 HUGSIM 上的零样本闭环评估中，HD-Score 为 45.1，RC 为 55.3。

Conclusion: FPD-Drive 模型通过利用未来特权知识和改进的安全候选轨迹选择方法，能够有效地提升端到端自动驾驶模型的训练效果和性能，并在多个基准测试中取得了最先进的成果。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on real-world driving logs, which record only the trajectory actually executed in each scene. This provides limited supervision for learning alternative valid behaviors. Multi-target supervision can mitigate this limitation, but broader trajectory coverage makes safe candidate selection more challenging. We propose FPD-Drive, an end-to-end planner that distills privileged future knowledge into a visual driving policy to improve both multi-trajectory supervision and safety selection. Specifically, we introduce Future-Privileged Multi-Target Distillation to transfer diverse, high-quality trajectory targets from a teacher with access to recorded future observations to the student. These targets extend supervision beyond the single recorded human trajectory. For candidate selection, we further introduce Safety-Aware Score Correction to correct ranking errors between nearby safe and unsafe candidates and raise underestimated safety scores of safe candidates. At deployment, the student requires neither future observations nor teacher inference. Our base model achieves 94.9 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest and 55.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navhard. The scaled FPD-Drive-Pro improves these scores to 95.5 PDMS and 58.1 EPDMS, respectively, achieving SOTA performance among the compared methods on both benchmarks. Zero-shot closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM further yields an HD-Score of 45.1 and RC of 55.3.

</details>


### [147] [Jag: Direct Box Prediction for Efficient Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13239)
*Xiuyuan Zhu,Ke Lu,Hao Wu,Zijin Du,Dongming Zhang,Jian Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Jag的新模型，它通过一个轻量级的回归头直接预测边界框坐标，而不是像传统方法那样将其表示为坐标序列。这种方法能够直接利用预训练的多模态模型进行视觉定位，并在不增加额外计算的情况下提高准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归多模态模型在视觉定位任务中通常将边界框表示为坐标序列，这增加了计算负担。作者希望设计一种直接预测边界框几何形状的方法，以提高效率和准确性。

Method: Jag模型采用了一个预训练的紧凑型多模态模型，并增加了一个轻量级的回归头。该回归头直接从输入多模态信息的最后有效状态预测所有四个边界框坐标。通过联合几何监督来训练语言骨干、视觉融合器和回归头，使定位损失能够同时塑造多模态表示和坐标映射。

Result: Jag在多个基准测试中，在各种目标尺寸和表达方式下，都一致地提高了其基础模型的视觉定位准确性。与单独训练回归头相比，联合训练表示和回归头能够显著提高准确性。与现有的专用定位模型相比，Jag在保持竞争性准确率的同时，降低了单请求延迟和GPU内存使用量。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对几何答案量身定制的监督方法可以使紧凑型预训练多模态模型专注于准确的视觉定位和高效的直接预测。Jag模型在提高视觉定位准确性和效率方面取得了成功，并展示了直接几何预测的潜力。

Abstract: Visual grounding combines image--language understanding with geometric prediction, yet autoregressive multimodal models often express a box as a coordinate sequence. Sequential decoding adds computation to the multimodal processing needed for localization. Adapting these representations to box geometry offers a route to direct prediction. We present Jag, which adapts a compact pretrained multimodal model for direct continuous box prediction. A lightweight regression head reads the last valid input-token state, conditioned on the image and referring expression, and predicts all four box coordinates together. Geometric supervision jointly adapts the language backbone, visual merger, and regression head, allowing localization losses to shape both the multimodal representations and the coordinate mapping. Inference retains image--language processing and returns the box in one forward pass, without coordinate generation or text parsing. Across benchmarks, Jag consistently improves grounding accuracy over its base model across the observed target sizes and expression groups. Component ablation show that jointly adapting the representations and head substantially improves accuracy over training the head alone. Against existing specialized grounding models, it achieves competitive accuracy with lower single-request latency and GPU memory use. These results show how supervision tailored to the geometric answer can specialize a compact pretrained multimodal model for accurate grounding and efficient direct prediction. Project page: https://xyzzzh.github.io/Jag/

</details>


### [148] [Deep Learning Approach for RealTime Contraction Detection in Laboring Dairy Cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13242)
*Sebastian Puchala,Dominik Gronkiewicz,Marcin Gołębiewski,Grzegorz Grodkowski,Grzegorz Gwardys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于计算机视觉和深度学习（CNNs、ViTs）的方法，用于自动识别奶牛的分娩宫缩，准确率超过95%，可用于提升奶牛产业的生产力和动物福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物生产领域需要自动化的技术来监测动物的行为和健康；现有技术在自动识别奶牛分娩宫缩方面存在不足。

Method: 收集了100例奶牛分娩的视频，提取了8500个宫缩动作和约25000个非宫缩序列作为数据集。利用图像处理技术、卷积神经网络（CNNs）和视觉变换器（ViTs）从视频帧中提取特征，以检测和分类宫缩。

Result: 该方法在检测奶牛分娩宫缩方面取得了超过95%的高准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化系统能够准确检测奶牛的劳动宫缩，有望通过实时监测和促进及时干预难产，从而提高奶牛产业的生产力和动物福利。

Abstract: Currently, the animal production sector is undergoing rapid development of techniques that allow automatic monitoring of animal behaviour and health. This paper presents a computer vision-based approach for the automatic recognition of labor contractions in cows. During the course of the study, videos depicting 100 cow births were collected. From the obtained videos, 8500 cow contraction actions and about 25 000 non-contraction sequences were extracted which constituted the dataset. Leveraging image processing techniques and deep learning algorithms like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs), our methodology extracts relevant features from video frames to detect and classify contractions. Our study demonstrates a high accuracy rate of over 95% in detecting labor contractions. This automated system has the potential to enhance productivity and animal welfare in the dairy industry by providing real-time monitoring and facilitating timely intervention during difficult calvings. The video demonstration of the system can be found at the following URL: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8fTSG5D7dZ0

</details>


### [149] [Vision Transformers Learn Word Meaning from Images Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13252)
*Anders Gjølbye,Sebastian Ray Mason,Stella Frank,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究表明，仅通过图像自监督学习的 DINOv3 视觉模型能够组织图像中的文字，并捕捉其语义关系，而无需任何文本监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望探索纯粹通过图像自监督学习，视觉模型是否能理解文字的含义，以及文字的语义结构。

Method: 研究者从 DINOv3 模型中提取渲染文字的词嵌入，通过比较文字图像与匹配对照图像的嵌入差异来获得词表示。然后，他们测试语义相关的词是否在嵌入空间中彼此靠近，并控制拼写相似性。同时，还将 DINOv3 的词表示与文本和语音编码器的表示进行对比，并进行了对比性对齐实验。

Result: DINOv3 模型（DINOv3-H 和 DINOv3-7B）能够将同一行业的品牌名称和同一语义领域的名词聚类在一起。国家-首都对表现出一致的向量偏移。此外，模型的词表示与它们所命名的视觉对象类别表示一致。对比性对齐实验能增强 DINOv3-H 的词级语义结构。

Conclusion: 仅使用图像的自监督训练，可以使视觉模型不仅学会识别文字的外观，还能理解文字之间的语义关系，表明视觉模型可以从像素中学习到词汇的含义。

Abstract: Can a vision model learn what words mean from pixels alone? We show that the two largest DINOv3 vision transformers organize words rendered in images by word meaning, despite being pretrained using self-supervision on images only, without paired captions, class labels, or other language supervision. We derive word representations from embedding differences between word images and matched controls, then test whether semantically related words are neighbors within each model's embedding space, while controlling for spelling similarity. We find that brand names in the same industry group together, as do nouns from the same semantic domain. Country-capital pairs exhibit consistent vector offsets, similarly to word embeddings trained on text. These word representations also align with the representations of the visual object categories they name. DINOv3-H and DINOv3-7B are, to our knowledge, the first self-supervised vision models to show substantial semantic structure for rendered words. Comparisons with text and speech encoders reveal shared structure among representations of the same words. Contrastive alignment with either encoder further strengthened DINOv3-H's word-level semantic structure, while leaving the other finetuned vision models largely unchanged. Image-only training can therefore teach vision models not just what words look like, but also how their meanings relate.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond a Single Crop: Contextualized Embeddings for Player Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13259)
*Ge Luo,Changhuo Yang,Weisi Fan,Rui Yang,Shaokai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ContextReID的框架，通过联合考虑同一帧内的其他球员以及跨帧的观测信息，来学习球员的身份特征。该方法在多个多目标跟踪基准测试中均优于传统的单裁剪图像嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的球员重识别方法主要依赖于单个球员图像的学习，忽略了体育赛事视频中固有的上下文信息（如同一球队球员的相似外观、不同视角和环境下的重复观测），而这些信息对于区分外观相似的球员至关重要。

Method: 提出ContextReID框架，利用视觉-语言模型，输入球员裁剪图像集合，生成考虑了局部球员和跨帧观测的上下文感知嵌入。通过结合上下文学习和检索目标，模型能够捕捉跨帧的身份关系，并保持区分度高的嵌入。

Result: 在三个多目标跟踪基准测试上，ContextReID在Rank-1和mAP指标上均显著优于传统的单裁剪嵌入方法。

Conclusion: 空间和时间上下文对于广播体育视频中可靠的球员身份识别非常重要，ContextReID框架有效地利用了这些上下文信息来提高球员检索性能。

Abstract: Person re-identification is typically addressed by learning identity-discriminative embeddings from individual person crops. However, player re-identification in broadcast sports videos presents a unique challenge: players from the same team often share similar appearances, while video sequences provide repeated observations of the same players under different viewpoints and surrounding contexts. Existing crop-based approaches largely ignore this contextual information, which can be critical for resolving visually similar identities.
  Here, we propose ContextReID, a contextualized embedding framework that represents a player by jointly considering other players in the same frame and observations from different frames of the broadcast sequence. By incorporating both co-occurring players and cross-frame observations, the model learns identity features relative to the surrounding visual context rather than relying only on isolated appearance cues. We implement this framework with a vision-language model that takes sets of player crops as input and generates context-aware embeddings for individual players. A combined contextual learning and retrieval objective encourages the model to capture cross-frame identity relationships while maintaining discriminative embeddings for player matching.
  Experiments across three multi-object tracking benchmarks show that ContextReID improves player retrieval over conventional single-crop embedding approaches in both Rank-1 and mAP. These results highlight the utility of spatial and temporal context for reliable player identification in broadcast sports videos.

</details>


### [151] [SheafPrune: Sheaf-Guided Graph Propagation for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13263)
*Xiaoyang Ma,Chi Wang,Hong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SheafPrune 是一种新的视觉标记选择方法，通过基于图的传播和度校正来减少多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的预填充成本，同时保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的MLLMs在处理完整视觉标记序列时，预填充成本高昂，即使大部分标记与指令无关。现有的标记级显著性方法无法充分捕捉标记间的依赖关系。

Method: SheafPrune 采用基于图的选择方法，模拟了形层（sheaf geometry）的思路。它使用特定于模态的映射和稀疏的文本-视觉图来传递相关性，然后在独立的视觉图上进行分数传播，并进行度校正。最终在冻结的语言模型之前保留一个硬性的 top-k 子集。

Result: SheafPrune 可以在各种骨干模型、基准测试和修剪比率下，在大多数设置中实现最佳的保留率，并显著提高预填充速度（在 LLaVA-NeXT 上为 5.15 倍）和降低 FLOPs（减少 84.0%）。

Conclusion: SheafPrune 支持基于图的约束重要性传播和度校正作为视觉标记选择的设计，提高了准确率保留与推理成本之间的权衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models process the complete visual token sequence, increasing prefill cost even when many tokens are irrelevant to the instruction. Token-wise saliency captures limited inter-token dependence; graph-based selection instead depends on how relations govern importance propagation and ranking. We study support-constrained propagation with node-degree correction. Inspired by sheaf geometry, SheafPrune uses modality-specific maps and a sparse text--visual graph to transfer relevance, then propagates scores on a separate visual graph and corrects them for degree. It retains a hard top-$k$ subset once before the frozen language model. The selector has about $4.5$M parameters and is trained end-to-end through the frozen LLaVA-NeXT-8B backbone using only the language-modeling objective. Explicit retention-ratio conditioning lets one checkpoint serve every budget. Across four backbones, ten benchmarks and seven pruning ratios, with all baselines re-implemented under one protocol, SheafPrune achieves the best retention at $22$ of $28$ backbone--budget settings, with monotonically growing margins in the moderate-to-aggressive regime. On LLaVA-NeXT, retaining $10\%$ of visual tokens yields a $5.15\times$ net prefill speedup and an $84.0\%$ end-to-end FLOPs reduction. Together with component ablations, these results support graph-constrained importance propagation and degree correction as a design for visual token selection across the evaluated backbones and tokenization schemes, improving the trade-off between accuracy retention and inference cost. code:https://github.com/Bob0817912/SheafPrune_ICLR_2027

</details>


### [152] [CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13264)
*Haowei Wang,Hao Lin,Qi Zhu,Jiayi Ji,Deyi Ji,Liujuan Cao,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，现有基于教师-学生模型的视觉语言模型（VLM）蒸馏方法（OPD）存在让模型过度依赖答案提示而忽略视觉线索的问题，即使在视觉区域被遮挡或打乱时也能正确回答。为解决此问题，提出了一种对比视觉在线蒸馏（CV-OPD）方法，通过引入负样本对比和差异感知门控机制，强制模型关注视觉证据，显著提升了VLM的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）的在线蒸馏（OPD）方法在实际应用中存在一个异常现象：即使相关的视觉区域被遮挡或替换为噪声，蒸馏后的VLM仍能准确回答问题，表明模型未能真正依赖视觉证据。这促使研究人员探索改进蒸馏方法，使其能够训练学生模型真正理解并利用视觉信息。

Method: 提出对比视觉在线蒸馏（CV-OPD）方法。该方法通过对比由正负样本（图像的不同区域）产生的教师模型输出分布，并以学生模型的同一前缀为条件，来纠正学生模型利用视觉区域的方式。具体而言，引入与答案无关的负样本教师，设计对比散度损失（CDL）来约束学生模型的特征分布。此外，引入差异感知门控（DAG），利用token和样本级别的差异来选择视觉相关的token进行优化，引导学生模型关注视觉内容。

Result: CV-OPD方法在广泛的细粒度视觉基准测试中进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法显著缓解了模型依赖答案提示的捷径问题，并持续提升了现有VLM的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: CV-OPD是一种有效改进OPD方法的对比学习框架，它通过引入负样本蒸馏和差异感知门控，能够有效地训练VLM真正依赖视觉证据进行推理，从而克服了现有方法的局限性，并提高了模型在视觉任务上的性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise. Building on this observation, our quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that OPD, which uses the answer as a hint, has failed to teach students to genuinely seek out and rely on visual evidence. Our theoretical analysis suggests that this failure arises from a shortcut enabled by the lack of visual constraints during distillation. To address the issue, we introduce Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD), which compares teacher distributions induced by positive and negative crops while conditioning on the same student prefix and augments optimization with a margin-based contrastive constraint, which is modulated with discrepancy gating across token and sample level. Specifically, CV-OPD first introduces a negative teacher unrelated to the answer and designs a Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL) that constrains the feature distribution of student by contrasting the positive and negative teachers, correcting how the student exploits visual regions. The CV-OPD introduces a Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG) that uses token-level and sample-level discrepancies to select visually relevant tokens for optimization, directing the student's attention toward visual content. Extensive experiments across a broad range of fine-grained visual benchmarks demonstrate that CV-OPD substantially alleviates shortcut and consistently improves the visual reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs.

</details>


### [153] [ConClick: Element-Wise Contrastive Learning Improves GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13268)
*Ruotian Zhang,Yuhui Zhang,Junzhe Huang,Yu Xin,Hehe Fan,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ConClick的对比学习框架，用于提高GUI元素的定位准确性，并引入了ReClick推理时策略进一步提升性能。研究发现现有模型在定位目标元素时常误选相似的干扰元素，ConClick通过区分目标与干扰元素来解决此问题，并构建了专门的数据集ConClick-Data。实验证明ConClick和ReClick能显著提升现有模型的GUI定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding模型错误率高，常导致下游任务失败。研究者发现，模型错误定位的模式是倾向于选择非目标但相似的界面元素（干扰项），而非背景。受此启发，作者希望训练模型区分目标元素和这些干扰项，从而提高定位的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 系统性评估了10个现有的GUI grounding模型在5个数据集上的表现，识别出模型常误选干扰元素的问题。2. 提出ConClick对比学习框架，训练模型区分目标元素和潜在的干扰元素。3. 构建了ConClick-Data数据集，包含4292个具有挑战性的目标-干扰项对。4. 使用互补的listwise ranking和pairwise margin目标进行模型训练。5. 引入ReClick推理时策略，通过重新审视和比较多个候选区域来优化最终预测。

Result: ConClick框架显著提高了Qwen3-VL-8B模型在ScreenSpot-Pro（从54.71%提升到66.03%）、MMBench-GUI L2（从83.72%提升到86.28%）和OSWorld-G（从63.33%提升到70.20%）上的GUI grounding准确率，优于使用相同或相似规模骨干网络的先前方法。ReClick策略在三个基准测试上平均额外提高了2.96个百分点的准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统分析GUI grounding中的错误模式，提出了有效的ConClick对比学习框架和ConClick-Data数据集，以及ReClick推理时策略，共同为构建更可靠的GUI代理提供了实证见解和实用工具。这些方法能够有效提升GUI元素的定位准确性，减少因误定位导致的下游任务失败。

Abstract: GUI grounding, the task of locating the interface element specified by a user instruction, is a fundamental capability of GUI agents. However, existing grounding models remain error-prone, often causing downstream task failures. We systematically evaluate 10 grounding models across five datasets and identify a major error pattern: most incorrect clicks hit non-target interface elements rather than the background, with different models repeatedly selecting the same distractors. Motivated by these findings, we propose ConClick, a contrastive learning framework that teaches models to distinguish the target element from plausible distractors. We construct ConClick-Data, a dataset containing 4,292 challenging target-distractor pairs, and train the model using complementary listwise ranking and pairwise margin objectives. ConClick improves the grounding accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B from 54.71% to 66.03% on ScreenSpot-Pro, from 83.72% to 86.28% on MMBench-GUI L2, and from 63.33% to 70.20% on OSWorld-G, outperforming prior methods built on the same or similarly sized backbones. We further introduce ReClick, an inference-time strategy that revisits promising regions and compares predictions across competing candidates, yielding an additional 2.96 percentage-point improvement in average accuracy across the three benchmarks. Together, our analysis, dataset, and methods provide both empirical insights and practical tools for building more reliable GUI agents. Data and code are available at https://github.com/zhangruotian/ConClick.

</details>


### [154] [OWLET: On-Policy Weighted Learning via Evidence Transfer for Video Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13272)
*Qitan Lv,Ke Fan,Hong Wang,Wen Wu,Yilin Wang,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为OWLET的新型视频大语言模型（Video-LLM）后训练方法，通过利用更丰富的“教师视角”来增强模型对视觉证据的利用能力，从而提高视频推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型后训练方法（如强化学习RL和on-policy蒸馏OPD）未能充分利用视觉证据。RL的奖励信号过于粗粒度，而OPD缺乏对生成输出的时空表示的直接监督。研究发现，结合全局时间上下文和问题相关细节的视频视图能提高模型的推理能力。

Method: OWLET是一种“证据优先”的强化学习方法。它构建了一个“教师视角”，结合了全局上下文和问题条件化的局部窗口，为模型提供丰富的视觉证据。具体来说，OWLET引入了两种机制：1. 证据感知信用再分配（evidence-aware credit redistribution），根据不同token的证据支持程度重新分配奖励；2. 时空状态对齐（spatiotemporal state alignment），对基于验证器接受的响应的两个视角（教师视角和学生视角）的隐藏状态进行对齐。

Result: 研究在大量实验中发现，结合全局时间上下文和问题相关细节的视频视图能够提高模型的推理准确性。通过比较同一学生生成响应在不同视图下的token似然和内部表示，OWLET能够产生有效的训练信号，指导模型更好地利用证据。

Conclusion: OWLET通过利用更丰富的“教师视角”和引入证据感知信用再分配与时空状态对齐机制，有效地增强了视频大语言模型在后训练阶段对视觉证据的利用能力，从而提升了其视频推理的准确性，且无需更大的模型。

Abstract: Post-training has become a key stage for eliciting reasoning capabilities in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing post-training methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, they do not fully exploit visual evidence for effective training. RL supervises the policy with a single trajectory-level reward, which is too coarse-grained for video reasoning: the same response-level advantage is applied to every generated token, disregarding that the tokens of a video response are grounded in the visual stream to very different degrees. OPD provides token-level supervision through output-distribution matching, but lacks direct supervision of the spatiotemporal representations that produce those outputs. We observe across extensive experiments that the same model reasons more accurately when its video view combines global temporal context with question-relevant details. Comparing the same student-generated response under this view and uniform sampling reveals changes in token likelihoods and internal representations, providing a training signal for evidence use. Building on this observation, we propose OWLET, an evidence-privileged RL method that derives training signals from a richer teacher view. This view couples uniform global context with question-conditioned local windows, providing visual evidence without requiring a larger model. To better exploit this privileged visual evidence, OWLET introduces (i) \textbf{evidence-aware credit redistribution}, which compares token likelihoods across the two views and redistributes positive response-level advantages according to their relative evidence support; and (ii) \textbf{spatiotemporal state alignment}, which aligns the two views' hidden states on verifier-accepted responses.

</details>


### [155] [QCLR: A Plug-in Robustification Module via Quantization for Contrastive Learning in Self-Supervised Skeleton-Based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13281)
*Nanzhu Chen,Feixiang He,Yunfeng Diao,Duygu Sarikaya,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为QCLR的即插即用模块，通过量化增强了自监督骨骼动作识别的对比学习方法的鲁棒性，使其更能抵抗对抗性攻击，同时保持了分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督骨骼动作识别方法（特别是基于对比学习的方法）虽然性能优越，但容易受到对抗性攻击，鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为QCLR的模块，采用双层量化方案来中断梯度传播并吸收小的对抗性扰动。为了减轻量化误差的影响，设计了一种注意力引导的多方面量化策略来维持表示质量。

Result: 将QCLR集成到主流对比学习模型中，可以显著提高其在白盒和黑盒对抗性攻击下的鲁棒性，同时保持具有竞争力的分类准确率。

Conclusion: QCLR是一种有效的即插即用模块，可以通过量化显著提高自监督骨骼动作识别方法的鲁棒性，同时保持其表示能力和分类性能。

Abstract: Skeleton-based human action recognition has gained increasing attention due to the stability of skeleton data across diverse environments and advances in motion capture technologies. Following the success of supervised learning approaches, self-supervised learning approaches, particularly contrastive learning ones, have achieved impressive performance. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. In this work, we propose a plug-in robustification module via quantization for contrastive learning in self-supervised skeleton-based human action recognition (QCLR), which enhances the robustness of representations against adversarial attacks while preserving their representational capability. Specifically, we employ a dual-level quantization scheme across the encoding pipeline, which breaks the gradient propagation and absorbs small adversarial perturbations within the quantization bins. To mitigate the impact of inevitable quantization error, we further design an attention-guided multi-faceted quantization strategy to maintain the representation quality. Experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that integrating QCLR into a progressive lineage of mainstream contrastive learning models substantially improves robustness against both gradient-based white-box and gradient-free black-box adversarial attacks, while preserving highly competitive classification accuracy.

</details>


### [156] [Sonic-AMG: Streaming Human Motion Generation from Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13300)
*Jingcheng Ni,Zekun Li,Zhiyang Dou,Xiaoyan Cong,Ziyao Zeng,Hongyang Du,Randall Balestriero,Taku Komura,Srinath Sridhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Sonic-AMG的框架，用于从空间音频流式生成人类运动，解决了离线生成和训练-推理不匹配的问题，并在SAM数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类对空间音频反应的研究通常是离线进行的，依赖于完整的声学和空间输入序列。然而，人类的反应是实时发生的，并且无法预知未来的声音。因此，需要一个能够实时处理流式空间音频并生成相应人类运动的框架。

Method: Sonic-AMG框架包含以下关键技术：1. 编码器将声学内容和身体相对声源几何信息编码为独立的token。2. 因果Transformer结合流模型生成连续的运动latent表示。3. 因果解码器将latent表示重构为运动片段。4. 引入“rehearsal training”来解决训练-推理不匹配问题，结合了全序列监督和短自回归预测。5. 提出“spatial feedback”机制，在训练和推理时根据生成的姿势重新计算声源位置，以保持空间条件的一致性。

Result: 在SAM数据集上，Sonic-AMG在FID和R-precision指标上达到了最先进的水平。此外，该框架能够实时生成连贯的、对不同声事件和声源位置做出反应的人类运动。

Conclusion: Sonic-AMG是首个能够从流式空间音频生成人类运动的框架，有效地解决了现有方法的局限性，并实现了高质量的实时运动生成。

Abstract: Modeling human reactions to spatial audio requires understanding both what is heard and where it comes from. Prior work generates motion offline from complete acoustic and spatial input sequences. Humans, however, react to sounds without knowing what they will hear next. We present Sonic-AMG, to our knowledge the first framework for streaming human motion generation from spatial audio. The model encodes acoustic content and body-relative source geometry as separate tokens. Conditioned on these tokens, a causal transformer with a flow head generates continuous motion latents, from which a causal decoder reconstructs motion segments. Standard teacher-forced training derives motion histories and body-relative source locations from ground-truth motion, whereas streaming inference relies on generated motion for both, resulting in a training-inference mismatch. To reduce this history discrepancy, we introduce rehearsal training, which combines full-sequence teacher-forced supervision with supervised prediction along short autoregressive self-rollouts. To keep spatial conditioning consistent with generated motion, we propose spatial feedback, which recomputes body-relative source locations from generated root poses during both rehearsal training and inference. On SAM, Sonic-AMG achieves state-of-the-art FID and R-precision. It further supports real-time online generation of coherent reactions across sound events and source locations. Project page: https://jingchengni.com/sonic-amg/.

</details>


### [157] [Decoding Register Tokens Preserves Global Scene Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13299)
*David Jacob Drexlin,Julius Hense,Jonas Dippel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究表明，保留预训练 Vision Transformer 中的非空间寄存器令牌（register tokens）可以提高图像重建和生成任务的性能，因为这些令牌编码了重要的全局场景属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Vision Transformer 在解码时忽略了寄存器令牌中编码的全局场景信息（如光照、模糊、风格），迫使解码器仅从图像块中恢复外观。研究者希望探索保留这些信息是否能提升解码性能。

Method: 研究者构建了带有冻结寄存器令牌的预训练 Vision Transformer 的表示自编码器（RAEs）。解码器通过联合自注意力机制同时处理寄存器令牌和图像块令牌。研究还采用了多头注意力池化（MHAP）预测器来从图像块恢复寄存器特征，以用于生成任务。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 数据集上，使用 DINOv2 和 DINOv3 的 RAEs 在图像重建任务上的 FID 分数分别降低了 9.5% 和 5.2%。在图像生成任务上，DINOv2 模型在相同的采样图像块下，FID 分数降低了 9.8%。

Conclusion: 保留 Vision Transformer 中的非空间寄存器令牌，并将其纳入解码过程，可以显著提高图像重建和生成任务的质量。这些令牌捕获了对图像外观至关重要的全局场景属性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision transformers learn structured image representations spanning spatial patch tokens and non-spatial tokens. Recent analyses suggest that non-spatial register tokens encode global scene properties such as illumination, blur, and style. Yet patch-based image decoders discard these outputs, leaving the decoder to recover image appearance from patches alone. We investigate whether retaining register features improves decoding when the complete spatial patch representation is already available. We study this question using representation autoencoders (RAEs) with frozen register-equipped encoders. Our decoder processes four register tokens together with all patch tokens through joint self-attention. Appearance probes, paired reconstruction-error analysis, and fixed-decoder register substitutions examine which global image properties are accessible from registers and how their values affect reconstructed pixels. For generation, we train a multi-head attention pooling (MHAP) predictor to recover register features from sampled patches, keeping the pretrained diffusion model and its samples unchanged. On ImageNet-1K, our ViT-B decoders achieve mean reconstruction FIDs of 0.472 with DINOv2 and 0.442 with DINOv3, reductions of 9.5% and 5.2% relative to their respective patch-only baselines. The corresponding PSNRs are 19.73 and 20.54 dB. For generation, our DINOv2 models achieve a mean FID of 1.641, 9.8% below the patch-only baseline on identical sampled patches.

</details>


### [158] [NovaSplat: Model-Free 6D Object Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting Onboarding and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13309)
*Georgios Floros,Evangelos Sartinas,Orestis Vangelis,Maria Pateraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为NovaSplat的模型无关的6D姿态估计方法，它使用智能手机视频训练3D高斯溅射模型，并结合光度和几何损失进行姿态优化，在多个基准测试中取得了优于现有方法（包括模型相关方法）的结果，并提出了一个更快的推理版本NovaSplat-Lite。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型无关的6D姿态估计方法在需要从头构建对象表示时仍然是一个挑战，并且性能落后于基于模型的方法。研究的动机是弥合这一差距，开发一种高性能的模型无关方法。

Method: 该方法首先从一个短的智能手机视频中训练一个3D高斯溅射（3DGS）模型。这个3DGS模型既用作检测和粗略姿态估计的模板源，也用作姿态精炼的可微骨干。姿态精炼器通过结合光度损失和几何损失，在无梯度奇异性的连续旋转参数化下，优化对象到相机的姿态。

Result: 在家庭用品基准测试中，NovaSplat的表现优于所有基于模型的方法。在一个具有无纹理和镜面表面的工业基准测试中，它超越了所有模型无关的竞争对手，并大幅缩小了与基于模型方法的差距。此外，提出的NovaSplat-Lite版本在精度损失可忽略的情况下，推理速度提高了3.1倍。

Conclusion: NovaSplat成功地通过使用3D高斯溅射作为统一表示，实现了高性能的模型无关6D姿态估计，并且在各种挑战性场景中取得了最先进的结果。NovaSplat-Lite的提出进一步提高了其实用性。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of novel objects without CAD models remains an open challenge: model-free methods must build the object representation from scratch during onboarding and have long trailed their model-based counterparts. We present NovaSplat, a model-free pipeline that closes this gap. From a short smartphone video captured at onboarding, we train a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model that serves both as a template source for detection and coarse pose estimation and as a differentiable backbone for pose refinement. Our refiner optimizes the object-to-camera pose with combined photometric and geometric losses under a continuous rotation parametrization free of gradient singularities. On a household-object benchmark, NovaSplat surpasses all model-based methods. On a challenging industrial benchmark with textureless and specular surfaces, it outperforms all model-free competitors while substantially narrowing the gap to model-based ones. We further introduce NovaSplat-Lite, an inference variant that is $3.1\times$ faster at negligible accuracy cost. Code will be made available at \href{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}.

</details>


### [159] [Exploring Attention Sinks in Text-to-Image Transformers from Diffusion and Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13337)
*Binglei Li,Mengping Yang,Zhiyu Tan,Wenxu Qian,Junping Zhang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次探索了文本到图像（T2I）Transformer中的“注意力汇聚点”（attention sinks）现象，发现在双向扩散模型和因果自回归模型中都存在，但模式不同。研究通过注意力分析和干预证实了注意力汇聚点对生成过程的影响，并提出通过引入“寄存器令牌”（register tokens）来重分布注意力汇聚点，以期改善生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力汇聚点在大型语言模型（LLM）中已被广泛研究，但其在文本到图像（T2I）Transformer中的行为和功能相关性却鲜为人知。本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入理解T2I Transformer如何分配和路由注意力。

Method: 研究者系统性地分析了双向扩散模型和因果自回归模型中的注意力模式，识别了注意力汇聚点的存在、位置和影响。此外，通过对注意力汇聚点相关的头和令牌位置进行干预，并引入“寄存器令牌”来重分布注意力汇聚点，以评估其功能相关性。

Result: 研究发现，注意力汇聚点在双向扩散模型和自回归模型中均存在，但模式不同：自回归模型倾向于将注意力集中在首个和全局令牌上；双向模型则在模态边界、文本区域和空间角落附近出现汇聚点。这些模式随生成阶段变化，且局限于稀疏的层和头。干预汇聚点相关的头比非汇聚点头产生更大的平均变化，而某些汇聚点位置的干预对双向模型得分影响有限。引入寄存器令牌重分布注意力汇聚点，在某些配置下能保持或略微提高生成性能。

Conclusion: 注意力汇聚点是依赖于模型架构、推理阶段和组件的现象。本研究为T2I Transformer的机制分析和原则性注意力设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Attention sinks are tokens or positions that consistently receive disproportionate attention mass, often beyond what their semantic content would suggest. Despite extensive study in large language models, their behavior and functional relevance in text-to-image (T2I) Transformers remain largely unexplored. To fill this gap and better understand how text-to-image Transformers allocate and route attention, we explore this phenomenon of text-to-image transformers across bidirectional diffusion models and causal autoregressive models. Specifically, we explore whether attention sinks exist, where they emerge, and how they functionally influence generation. Through systematic attention analysis and targeted interventions, we identify that attention sinks emerge in both bidirectional diffusion and autoregressive models but exhibit distinct patterns. The autoregressive model concentrates attention on the first and global-scale tokens, whereas the bidirectional models develop sinks near modality boundaries, within text regions, and at spatial corners. Moreover, these patterns vary across generation stages and are localized to a sparse subset of layers and heads. To assess their functional relevance, we perturb sink-related heads and token positions. It turns out that perturbing sink-ranked heads produces larger average changes than non-sink comparisons, while some sink-position perturbations have limited effects on bidirectional model scores. We further introduce register tokens to redistribute attention sinks and find that several configurations preserve or modestly improve generation performance. Overall, attention sinks are architecture-, inference-stage-, and component-dependent phenomena, providing a basis for mechanistic analysis and principled attention design in text-to-image transformers.

</details>


### [160] [S-Space: Exploring Spatial Workspace in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13527)
*Zhiyuan Shi,Xiang Li,Xingzhuo Guo,Shangchen Miao,Weiliang Chen,Qiyu Dai,Jinshan Ren,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu,Mingsheng Long,Jialong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态语言模型（MLLMs）中存在一个名为 S-Space 的内部线性表示子空间，该子空间能够编码物体在空间中的连续坐标，并充当模型进行空间推理的工作空间。该空间在理解物体位置、支持空间判断、进行视角变换以及连接物理方向与抽象意义方面发挥作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对多模态语言模型（MLLMs）在空间推理方面的内部工作机制了解不足。

Method: 通过区分空间答案分数与物体-token 激活来识别 S-Space 的三个轴（水平、垂直、距离）。通过因果干预来验证 S-Space 的功能，并将其用作诊断工具。

Result: S-Space 作为一个内部空间工作空间，能够编码物体位置、支持空间判断、进行视角变换，并将物理方向与抽象语义联系起来。研究还发现了 S-Space 在参考系耦合以及空间变换能力方面的局限性。

Conclusion: S-Space 是 MLLMs 内部进行空间推理的关键机制，它能够编码物体位置并支持多种空间操作。然而，模型在跨视角变换的推理方面存在瓶颈，这限制了其空间推理的可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal language models (MLLMs) exhibit promising spatial reasoning capabilities, yet the internal mechanisms supporting them remain poorly understood. We uncover S-Space, a linear representation subspace in which object-token representations encode continuous coordinates along horizontal, vertical, and distance axes. Although we identify these axes by differentiating spatial-answer scores with respect to object-token activations, S-Space is intrinsic rather than induced by specific data or prompts, remaining readable under non-spatial questions. We show that S-Space functions as an internal spatial workspace of MLLMs. Perception encodes object locations into it, forming spatial representations that can be directly read out to support accurate spatial judgments. Internal reasoning transforms it for changing viewpoints. Verbal responses selectively access it, evidenced by causal interventions that alter spatial judgments while largely preserving non-spatial responses. Case studies further show that S-Space assigns locations to unseen entities and the observer, and connects physical directions with abstract linguistic meanings, suggesting both the capabilities and limitations of large-scale language training. Finally, we use S-Space as a diagnostic tool for spatial reasoning failures. Direction-word readouts reveal systematic coupling between cardinal and image-relative reference frames. More importantly, a perception-computation decomposition exposes a key bottleneck: MLLMs can form capable spatial representations, yet fall short in the spatial transformations required to reason reliably across viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [161] [LAPT-Scale: Scaling Embodied Policies with Latent Action Pre-Training on Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13732)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Peidong Liu,Mingyang Li,Jiawei Li,Wenhow Li,Wenbo Ding,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAPT-Scale 是一种通过利用大量人类 egocentric 视频进行预训练的方法，旨在克服机器人策略训练数据收集成本高昂的问题。该方法学习连续的潜在动作，并将其用作人类行为的监督信号，从而显著提高了机器人在现实任务中的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集用于训练通用机器人策略的大量具身交互数据成本高昂。虽然大规模 egocentric 人类视频包含丰富信息，但缺乏机器人动作标签，现有标注方法在真实世界视觉复杂性和多样化操作动态下噪声较大。

Method: LAPT-Scale 包含两个组件：LAPT-Scale-LAM 通过解释视频帧间视觉变化来学习连续的潜在动作，并作为人类监督信号；LAPT-Scale-Policy 利用人类视频的潜在动作预训练，结合机器人具身对齐和任务特定后训练来训练一个 VLA 策略。该方法使用连续潜在空间而非向量量化，并结合了感知、语义和光流对齐信号来改进过渡级别监督。

Result: 在 23.3K 小时 egocentric 视频上训练的 LAPT-Scale-LAM，为策略预训练提供了隐式动作监督。当人类视频规模从 1K 增加到 5K 小时，机器人的实际成功率从 63.3% 提高到 85.0%，并实现了持续的规模化收益。学习到的策略还能泛化到未见的视觉条件。

Conclusion: LAPT-Scale 是一种有效的利用大规模人类视频进行机器人策略预训练的方法，能够克服数据收集限制，提高机器人性能，并具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. We introduce LAPT-Scale, a scalable latent action pre-training paradigm with two components: LAPT-Scale-LAM learns continuous latent actions by explaining frame-to-frame visual transitions in human videos and uses them as human-derived supervision, while LAPT-Scale-Policy trains a VLA policy through human-video latent-action pre-training, target-robot embodiment alignment, and task-specific post-training. Unlike prior vector-quantized LAMs, LAPT-Scale-LAM uses a continuous latent space to preserve fine-grained motion and interaction dynamics. We further combine perceptual, semantic, and optical-flow alignment signals to improve transition-level supervision beyond pixel reconstruction. Trained on 23.3K hours of egocentric video, LAPT-Scale-LAM provides implicit action supervision for policy pre-training, yielding consistent scaling gains and improving real-robot success from 63.3% to 85.0% as annotated human video scales from 1K to 5K hours. The learned policy also generalizes to unseen visual conditions.

</details>


### [162] [PAGE: Parts-Aware Guidance for Co-Speech Gesture Portrait Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13301)
*Yabiao Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yicheng Xu,Teng Hu,Shengzhe Zhou,Yanxiao Sun,Jiangning Zhang,Ying Tai,Yong Liu,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAGE是一个通用的、不依赖特定骨干网络的优化框架，用于提升基于扩散模型的说话同步手势视频生成，通过 parts-specific reweighting (PSR) 增强训练时的局部细节，通过 consistency classifier guidance (CCG) 和 differential guidance (DG) 改进推理时的音视频对齐和局部细节。同时，作者构建了包含大量中文内容和多样性的 CAS 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的说话同步手势视频生成方法在视觉细节和音视频对齐方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出了 PAGE 框架，包含训练阶段的 Parts-Specific Reweighting (PSR) 机制（增强唇部、眼睛、手部等关键区域的监督）和推理阶段的 Consistency Classifier Guidance (CCG)（纠正音视频不一致）以及 Differential Guidance (DG)（分别优化面部和身体运动）。此外，构建了 Chinese Announcer Speech (CAS) 数据集。

Result: PAGE 在 PATS 和 CAS 数据集上均取得了领先的感知质量、手势多样性和音视频对齐效果。

Conclusion: PAGE 框架能够有效地提升基于扩散模型的说话同步手势视频生成质量，特别是在局部细节和音视频一致性方面，并且 CAS 数据集为该领域的研究提供了重要的基准。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture video generation aims to synthesize realistic, audio-driven portrait videos from a single reference image and a speech signal. Despite advances in visual quality, diffusion-based approaches still suffer from degraded local details and imperfect audio-visual alignment. We present PAGE (Parts-Aware GuidancE), a general, backbone-agnostic optimization framework for diffusion-based generation that enhances local spatial fidelity and global audio-visual consistency at both the training-objective and denoising-trajectory levels. During training, we propose Parts-Specific Reweighting (PSR), which adaptively increases supervision in semantically important regions such as lips, eyes, and hands, improving visual fidelity in perceptually critical areas. During inference, we introduce Consistency Classifier Guidance (CCG), an audio-conditioned latent classifier that identifies and corrects audio-visual inconsistencies in the generated video. We further design Differential Guidance (DG), an efficient inference strategy that refines facial and body motion separately while avoiding cross-region interference. To enable large-scale evaluation, we construct the Chinese Announcer Speech (CAS) dataset, the first comprehensive Chinese benchmark for co-speech gesture portrait video generation. Compared with the widely used PATS dataset, CAS includes substantially more identities, video clips, and scene diversity. Extensive experiments on both PATS and CAS demonstrate that PAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance in perceptual quality, gesture diversity, and audio-visual alignment. Project page: https://aigc-explorer.github.io/PAGE/.

</details>


### [163] [ThinkDrag: Semantic Drag-Based Image Editing with Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13316)
*Armando Fortes,Tianyi Wei,Chindanai Trakantannarong,Shangchen Zhou,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ThinkDrag的统一多模态框架，用于基于拖拽的图像编辑，通过引入视觉推理来理解图像内容和语义变换，从而超越仅基于几何位移的传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拖拽式图像编辑方法主要将用户指令解释为几何位移，难以处理依赖于图像内容理解和语义推断的编辑任务。本文旨在解决这一局限性，实现更智能、更具解释性的拖拽编辑。

Method: ThinkDrag是一个统一的多模态框架，通过将点约束与有意义的对象和场景级变换相关联进行训练。它还可以生成一个显式的推理轨迹，用于解释编辑意图。框架引入了“拖拽终点标记”以提供明确的对应信号。为支持该框架，作者构建了一个包含语义拖拽变换和推理轨迹的监督数据集，并推出了DragBench++基准测试。

Result: ThinkDrag在生成质量和合理性方面取得了最先进的性能，同时保持了有竞争力的点跟随精度。推理轨迹显著提高了对模糊拖拽指令的可解释性。

Conclusion: ThinkDrag通过整合视觉推理，成功提升了拖拽式图像编辑的能力，使其能够理解并执行更复杂的语义变换，并提供了更好的可解释性。

Abstract: Drag-based image editing provides an intuitive interface for spatially controlled image manipulation by allowing users to specify handle and target points. Existing methods have made substantial progress through optimization-based, one-step, and feed-forward formulations, but they often interpret drag instructions as geometric displacements. This limits their effectiveness when the desired edit depends on interpreting image content and inferring a plausible semantic transformation from sparse point constraints. We introduce ThinkDrag, a unified multimodal framework for drag-based image editing with visual reasoning. ThinkDrag is trained to associate point constraints with meaningful object- and scene-level transformations, and can optionally produce an explicit reasoning trace that interprets the intended edit before image generation. This reasoning trace improves interpretability, particularly for ambiguous drags. Drag Endpoint Markers, motivated by an analysis of cross-image attention, provide an explicit correspondence signal that grounds each drag in image space. To support this framework, we construct a supervised dataset of semantic drag transformations paired with reasoning traces and introduce DragBench++, a benchmark targeting challenging drag-based editing scenarios with reference edit solutions. Experiments show that ThinkDrag achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving generation quality and plausibility while maintaining competitive point-following precision.

</details>


### [164] [Tracking the Invisible: Self-Supervised Wheel Track Detection with Thermal-RGB Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13872)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种使用RGB-I（RGB + 长波红外）图像的低成本、自监督方法，用于检测积雪路面上的车辙，以提高自动驾驶的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行驶区域检测方法在积雪路面上不足，而跟随车辙可以提供更好的抓地力、可预测的车辆动力学和稳定的路径。因此，需要一种专门针对积雪路面车辙的检测方法。

Method: 使用同步的RGB-I图像，通过将GNSS记录的未来车辙轨迹投影来自动生成训练标签。设计了一个神经网络来处理4通道的RGB-I图像，以识别车辙。提出并使用了名为ThermalTrack的新数据集。

Result: 提出的方法在视觉上单调和缺乏特征的恶劣环境下有效，能够鲁棒地检测车辙。

Conclusion: 该方法为冬季自动驾驶提供了一种鲁棒且经济高效的解决方案，能够有效地检测积雪路面上的关键车辙。

Abstract: Accurate detection of drivable area is essential for autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. While state-of-the-art methods focus on general traversability estimation, they prove inadequate for snow-covered roads where following existing wheel tracks offers crucial advantages: improved traction, predictable vehicle dynamics, and verified path stability. To address this, we propose a low-cost, self-supervised approach detecting safety-critical wheel tracks using synchronized RGB-I (RGB + Long-Wave Infrared) imagery. Our method automatically generates training labels by projecting future wheel trajectories from recorded GNSS data. A neural network is designed to identify wheel tracks with the 4-channel RGB-I images from challenging, white-out road surfaces. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in visually monotonous and featureless adverse environment, offering a robust and cost-effective alternative for winter autonomy. The newly introduced ThermalTrack dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/thermaltrack/.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond the Snowflakes: Disentangling Weather Effects from Confounding Meta-Factors in LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13891)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管降雪会产生噪声，但其增加的物体点云数量（由雪的积累和反射率变化引起）能显著提升 LiDAR 检测的准确性，这与现有研究认为雪会降低性能的观点相反。因此，在评估 LiDAR 系统时，需要区分大气噪声和物体表面积累效应，否则可能得出误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为 LiDAR 在恶劣天气（尤其是降雪）下性能会下降，但作者通过对 WADS-3D 和 CADC 数据集的详细评估，发现了与此相反的现象，即雪的强度增加反而提高了检测精度。因此，研究的动机是解释和理解这一反直觉的现象。

Method: 研究采用了对 WADS-3D 和 CADC 数据集的定量评估，通过消融实验（ablation study）和元信息分析来探究雪对 LiDAR 检测性能影响的根本原因。作者试图将降雪的影响分解为两个主要因素：大气噪声和物体点云数量的变化。

Result: 研究结果表明，尽管降雪本身会引入大气噪声，但其带来的物体点云数量的显著增加（由雪的积累增加了物体表面积以及从镜面反射转向漫反射引起）是主导因素，从而提升了 LiDAR 的检测准确性。这一发现与以往将降雪视为纯粹噪声的观点相悖。

Conclusion: 在评估 LiDAR 等感知系统时，不能简单地将天气严重程度进行分类，而应考虑物体层面的相关数据。简单地将降雪视为噪声会得出误导性的结论。准确的评估需要将大气噪声与表面积累效应分离开来，并对相关的物理模型进行验证。

Abstract: Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) is critical for autonomous perception, yet its performance is known to degrade in adverse weather. While existing literature models snow as atmospheric noise that degrades performance, our granular evaluation on the WADS-3D and CADC datasets reveals a counter-intuitive phenomenon: detection accuracy consistently improves as snow intensity increases. Through ablation study and meta-information analysis, we reconcile this by confirming that while falling snow acts as noise, its negative impact is outweighed by a dominant, concurrent factor: significantly increased object point count. We hypothesize that this object point count boost acts as a geometric enhancement, driven by snow accumulation increasing vehicle surface area and a shift from specular to diffuse reflectance. Our findings demonstrate that evaluating perception systems using broad weather-severity bins without accounting for correlated object-level data can lead to misleading conclusions about detector robustness. We conclude that accurate evaluation requires decoupling atmospheric noise from surface accumulation effects and outline future research to validate these physical models. The WADS-3D dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/wads-3d/.

</details>


### [166] [CoVA: Composing VAE Representations for Generative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13322)
*Zhaowei Chen,Yikai Ge,Linze Li,Yao Tang,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CoVA的轻量级框架，通过在线组合VAE特征并进行阶段性引导，显著提升了Transformer扩散模型的训练效率和生成质量，同时解决了软标签条件设置中的隐藏混淆问题，并在ImageNet数据集上取得了优异的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于预训练视觉编码器的表示对齐方法在训练扩散Transformer时效果显著，但需要额外的编码器和训练开销。而利用生成模型自身VAE的特征虽然无需额外编码器，但其重建导向的特征并不适用于生成学习所需的语义判别和空间结构。因此，需要一种更高效、更有效的表示学习方法来增强扩散模型的生成能力。

Method: CoVA框架通过一个小型在线潜在作曲家（latent composer）来组合VAE特征，并采用分阶段的引导策略进行流匹配（flow-matching）训练。该作曲家通过类别监督、掩码潜在重构和水平翻转一致性进行联合训练，以生成语义判别性强、空间结构化密集的特征以及样本相关的类别后验。在训练初期，CoVA将SiT（Swin Transformer）的中间特征与组合表示进行对齐；随着训练的进行，逐渐去除这种对齐，并利用解耦的作曲家后验进行样本相关的软标签条件设置。此外，CoVA还纠正了软标签条件设置中的混淆问题，即平均类别嵌入会削弱其幅度，从而将语义插值与条件强度耦合。通过恢复硬标签幅度，CoVA隔离了依赖于后验的定向语义。

Result: CoVA框架仅增加了850万个（占SiT-XL/2的1.3%）仅用于训练的辅助参数，并且在推理时没有额外的开销。在ImageNet 256x256数据集上，CoVA在400K步的训练中将SiT-XL/2的FID从17.2降低到7.4（降低了57%）。结合无分类器引导，CoVA在仅400个epoch的训练后即可达到2.03的FID，而原始SiT-XL/2在1400个epoch后才达到2.06。

Conclusion: CoVA是一种高效且有效的框架，通过精心设计的特征组合和阶段性训练策略，能够显著提升扩散Transformer在图像生成任务上的性能，尤其是在FID指标上取得了大幅度的改进。该方法还深入分析并解决了软标签条件设置中的一个重要混淆问题，进一步巩固了其在生成模型训练领域的价值。

Abstract: Representation alignment with pretrained visual encoders improves diffusion transformer training but requires auxiliary encoders and extra training overhead. Recent approaches instead exploit representations from the generative model's own variational autoencoder (VAE). However, reconstruction-oriented VAE features are not optimized for the semantic discrimination and spatial structure needed for generative learning. We introduce \textbf{CoVA} (\textbf{Co}mposed \textbf{VA}E Representations), a lightweight framework that composes VAE features online into stage-wise guidance for flow-matching training. A small latent composer is jointly trained with class supervision, masked latent reconstruction, and horizontal-flip consistency, yielding semantically discriminative, spatially structured dense features and sample-dependent class posteriors. CoVA aligns intermediate SiT features with the composed representations early in training, then removes the increasingly detrimental alignment and reuses detached composer posteriors for sample-dependent soft label conditioning. We further uncover a hidden confound in soft label conditioning: averaging class embeddings attenuates their magnitude, coupling semantic interpolation with conditioning strength. This attenuation alone can substantially improve FID while degrading Recall, revealing a non-semantic source of the apparent gain. CoVA corrects this confound by restoring the hard-label magnitude, isolating posterior-dependent directional semantics. CoVA adds only 8.5M training-only auxiliary parameters (1.3\% of SiT-XL/2) and introduces no inference-time overhead. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, CoVA reduces SiT-XL/2 FID from 17.2 to 7.4 (\textbf{57\%}) in 400K steps. With classifier-free guidance, it achieves an FID of \textbf{2.03} in only 400 epochs, versus 2.06 after 1,400 epochs for vanilla SiT-XL/2.

</details>


### [167] [LETHE: Defending against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Generation via Value Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13333)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为 LETHE 的新颖图像到视频（I2V）模型保护方法，通过干扰参考图像的“值向量扩散”机制，有效阻止恶意生成不忠实或视觉上损坏的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 I2V 扩散模型易被滥用，可以将个人照片制作成虚假视频。尽管已有保护方法，但在常见的扰动预算下效果不佳，原因是对 I2V 模型如何处理后续帧对参考图像的读取机制理解不足。

Method: 通过对 I2V 模型读取机制进行凸几何分析，发现“参考值向量的扩散”是关键因素。LETHE 方法通过两个互补的目标来直接干扰这一过程：1）值塌缩目标：使参考值向量趋于均匀；2）注意力吸引目标：吸引后续帧的注意力到这些塌缩的 token 上，以放大破坏效果。

Result: LETHE 在新构建的两个以人为中心的 I2V 保护基准上进行了广泛实验，证明了其在多种现代 I2V 模型上的最先进的保护性能和通用性。即使在轻微的扰动下，也能显著降低生成视频的质量。

Conclusion: LETHE 成功地揭示并利用了 I2V 模型读取参考图像的底层机制，提供了一种高效且通用的方法来抵御 I2V 模型的滥用，显著优于现有技术。

Abstract: Image-to-video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single reference image into high-quality, subject-consistent videos, posing threats of misuse as anyone can turn personal photos into fabricated videos without consent. A promising solution is to proactively add an adversarial perturbation to the image before release, such that videos generated from it would be unfaithful or visually corrupted. However, due to an insufficient understanding of what governs how subsequent frames read the reference image in modern I2V models, existing protections struggle to offer strong protection under common perturbation budgets. In this paper, we approach the problem by exploring this underlying reading mechanism. Through a convex geometric analysis, we theoretically identify the spread of the reference value vectors as a key factor governing this reading process, and empirically find that even a mild contraction of this spread can severely degrade the generated video, making it an efficient lever for protection. Guided by this insight, we propose LETHE, a novel protection method that directly disrupts this reading process. LETHE pairs two complementary objectives, where a value collapse objective drives the reference value vectors toward uniformity, while an attention attraction objective draws subsequent frames toward these collapsed tokens to amplify corruption. We further construct two human-centric I2V protection benchmarks and conduct extensive experiments with multiple modern I2V models, which demonstrate the state-of-the-art protection performance and versatility of LETHE.

</details>


### [168] [VideoAWR: Reinforcing Video Flow Models with Decoupled-Timestep Advantage-Weighted Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13338)
*Zheming Fu,Wenbin Ning,Ruizhe He,Wei Shang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Lianrui Mu,Chang Liu,Lei Wang,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 VideoAWR 的新方法，用于解决视频生成中基于回归的强化学习（RL）模型面临的冷启动和指导膨胀问题，通过解耦采样和优化目标，实现了更稳定、更高质量的视频生成，并且推理时可以选择无分类器自由引导（CFG）或保留 CFG 语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于回归的 RL 方法在视频生成中存在冷启动问题（无指导采样导致低保真度）和指导膨胀问题（有指导采样导致优化目标不准确）。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: VideoAWR 通过解耦采样和优化目标来解决上述问题。具体来说，使用 EMA 参考策略生成有指导的采样，以恢复奖励的可分离性；而训练策略则根据无指导的条件锚点进行校正，从而将指导和优化锚点分开。此外，还引入了“解耦时间步校正”，独立采样每个展开的时间步长进行监督，以提高样本效率。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-1.3B 和 14B 数据集上的实验表明，VideoAWR 实现了稳定的优化，并在无 CFG 和有 CFG 的情况下都取得了优越的视觉和运动质量。

Conclusion: VideoAWR 成功解决了视频生成中基于回归的 RL 方法的冷启动和指导膨胀问题，通过解耦采样和优化目标，实现了更稳定、更高质量的视频生成，并且推理时可以选择无 CFG 或有 CFG 模式。

Abstract: Regression-based RL for flow models mandates sampling \emph{without} classifier-free guidance (CFG) to align the rectification target with the reference marginal velocity. Extending Advantage-Weighted Rectification (AWR), which dynamically scales this regression target based on reward, to video generation encounters a cold-start pathology: unguided rollouts collapse into low-fidelity regions where intra-group reward gaps remain wide but no longer track perceptual quality, rendering the optimization target arbitrary. Conversely, utilizing guided rollouts to restore fidelity, while keeping the regression target tied to the guided sampling distribution, provably triggers a geometric guidance blow-up under exponential-moving-average (EMA) updates. We propose \textbf{VideoAWR}, which resolves this tension by untying sampling from the optimization anchor. An EMA reference policy generates \emph{guided} rollouts to restore reward separability, while the trainable policy is rectified against the \emph{unguided} conditional anchor. We prove this decoupled objective is mathematically exact: the centered advantage cancels the anchor in expectation, filtering out the reward-neutral guidance offset and retaining only the reward-correlated covariance increment. Consequently, inference runs single-branch and CFG-free, yet inference-time CFG preserves its original semantics. Furthermore, we introduce \emph{Decoupled Timestep Rectification}, supervising each rollout at an independently sampled timestep without bias to improve sample efficiency. Experiments on Wan2.1-1.3B and 14B demonstrate stable optimization and superior visual and motion quality in \emph{both} CFG-free and CFG-enabled regimes. Code is available at \pdfstartlink attr{/Border [0 0 0]} user{/Subtype /Link /A << /S /URI /URI (https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR) >>}% \textcolor{pearDark}{VideoAWR}\pdfendlink.

</details>


### [169] [Why DiT Models Underperform as Representation Learners without Long Skip Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13344)
*Benyuan Meng,Yiliang Zhang,Jin-Wen Wu,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang,Longtao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究发现，在Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 模型中引入长跳跃连接 (LSCs) 可以显著提升其特征质量，从而改善生成与理解任务的统一性。研究揭示了LSCs通过将扩散噪声直接传递到所需位置，减少了噪声对图像信号的干扰，从而提高了特征的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于DiT模型在生成能力上优于其前代模型，但在特征提取质量上表现较弱，这阻碍了生成与理解任务的统一性。作者希望通过研究DiT模型的结构来改善其特征质量。

Method: 作者通过从头开始训练DiT模型，并引入长跳跃连接（LSCs），来评估LSCs对特征质量的影响。通过详细分析模型内部信号的传递过程，揭示LSCs改善特征质量的机制。

Result: 在DiT模型中引入LSCs可以产生更优的特征。LSCs通过直接将扩散噪声传递到模型所需的特定层，避免了噪声信号在主干网络中“污染”图像信号，从而提高了特征的质量。

Conclusion: 长跳跃连接（LSCs）不仅仅是一个简单的技巧，它与扩散模型的生成目标密切相关。在DiT模型中加入LSCs能够有效提升特征质量，为生成与理解任务的统一性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion feature, i.e., intermediate activations extracted from diffusion model backbones, has emerged as a promising approach for unifying generation and vision understanding (discrimination). Despite the advancement of diffusion backbones into Diffusion Transformers (DiT), it has been found in previous literature that DiT models tend to yield weaker features despite their stronger generation capability. This poses a threat to good generation-understanding unification. In this work, we show via controlled from-scratch training that long skip connections (LSCs) affect feature quality: a DiT with LSCs can produce better features. Furthermore, through detailed diagnosis, we reveal the mechanism causing this effect. Specifically, the input to a diffusion backbone contains both the latent image and the diffusion noise, where the diffusion noise, though appearing in the form of noises, actually reflects the information of the diffusion trajectory. To make the final prediction of a noisy variable ($v$ or $ε$), the diffusion backbone needs both signals. However, carrying both signals through the main backbone effectively "pollutes" the image signal with noises, leading to poor feature quality. In comparison, LSCs can shortcut diffusion noises directly to where they are needed, leaving much weaker noise signals in the main backbone, thus improving feature quality. To name the broader impact, this reveals that LSCs are not a mere trick but closely related to the diffusion objective. The code is at https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-lsc.

</details>


### [170] [OccludeBench: Can VLMs Find What's Missing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13790)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Ryo Mitsuhashi,Linrong Cai,Xingyu Fu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV
