This combination - an exact output budget, end-to-end differentiability, and linear cost - is precisely what previous reducers trade off against one another. The budget can be changed without changing operator parameters, and the learned mass doubles as a token-level map of where the budget is allocated. Across vision and vision-language tasks, LapMerge matches or improves on strong token reducers while being up to two orders of magnitude faster at large sequence lengths; it is particularly effective when token order reflects spatial or temporal locality, and works both as a drop-in reduction module and as a trainable architectural component.

</details>


### [204] [Event-based Action Recognition by Pre-Training on Multi-Frame Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13856)
*Emma Norrby,Tomas Wilkinson,Per Nyblom,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于Transformer的多帧事件表示方法，通过稀疏感知掩码和多掩码令牌来提高长时序复杂事件的识别性能，并在THU-EACT-50 CHL和DailyDVS-200数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单帧事件的动作识别方法在处理长时序和复杂事件序列时，区分能力会显著下降。为了解决这个问题，研究者们提出了多帧表示方法。

Method: 研究者们采用掩码自编码框架预训练Vision Transformer，结合稀疏感知掩码算法和令牌选择机制来提升下游任务性能并控制计算复杂度。此外，还引入了多掩码令牌和位置感知注意力机制以进一步提高性能。

Result: 所提出的多帧表示方法在处理具有挑战性的长时序和复杂事件序列方面表现出色，并在THU-EACT-50 CHL和DailyDVS-200数据集上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种新颖的多帧事件表示方法，显著提高了事件相机在长时序复杂动作识别任务上的性能，为该领域的研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Action recognition is a crucial task in domains like robotics, patient monitoring, or surveillance. Recently, event-based action recognition has attracted much attention due to the event camera's ability to capture fast motions and its improved privacy over conventional vision cameras. While frame-based event representations work well for short event sequences, their discriminative ability declines sharply with longer and more complex event sequences. In this work, we introduce a multi-frame representation that greatly improves recognition performance over its single-frame counterpart. We adopt a masked auto-encoding framework to pre-train a Vision Transformer using a sparsity-aware masking algorithm and token selection mechanism to improve downstream performance and control computational complexity. We also introduce multiple mask tokens for the decoder with a position-aware attention mechanism, leading to further improved performance. Our proposed method shows impressive performance for challenging long-duration and complex event sequences, yielding state-of-the-art performance on THU-EACT-50 CHL and DailyDVS-200.

</details>


### [205] [Make It Flow: Animating Water in 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13860)
*Kjell Keune,Florian Hahlbohm,Jannis Möller,Susana Castillo,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种在静态3D高斯泼溅场景中动画化水流的方法，通过估计2D光流并将其提升为3D运动场，然后利用受电影图启发算法变形高斯图元，实现无缝循环动画，同时保持实时渲染性能和自由视点导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法主要用于静态场景，无法处理水流等高频动态现象。由于无法进行密集多视角、高速采集，因此需要一种无需动态采集或物理模拟的方法来为静态3D高斯泼溅场景添加逼真的水流动画。

Method: 1. 从参考图像或模型渲染中估计2D光流。2. 将2D光流提升为一致的3D欧拉运动场。3. 采用受电影图启发的算法，将该运动场应用于高斯图元的变形，生成时间上无缝循环的动画。

Result: 该方法能够为静态3D高斯泼溅场景添加逼真的水流动画，在保持实时渲染性能和自由视点导航能力的同时，显著提高了感官真实性。在新的野外瀑布场景数据集中进行了评估，与现有动态高斯泼溅和3D电影图生成方法相比，在视觉质量和计算效率方面均有明显提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功实现了在静态3D高斯泼溅场景中动画化水流，为处理真实世界中普遍存在的动态现象提供了一种有效且高效的解决方案，且无需复杂的动态采集或物理模拟。

Abstract: Explicit 3D scene representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently enabled high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis. However, these methods assume static scenes, limiting realism in the presence of dynamic phenomena ubiquitous in real-world environments. While coherent motion (e.g., humans or vehicles) can be captured and reconstructed, high-frequency effects such as flowing water remain intractable under casual capture conditions, where dense multi-view, high-speed acquisition is not feasible. We present a method to animate water in static 3DGS scenes, enabling visually plausible motion without requiring dynamic capture or physical simulation. Our approach estimates 2D optical flow from reference images or model renderings and lifts it into a consistent 3D Eulerian motion field. We propose a cinemagraph-inspired algorithm that applies this motion field to deform Gaussian primitives, producing temporally seamless looping animations. The resulting representation retains real-time rendering performance, significantly enhances perceptual realism, and preserves free-viewpoint navigation within the scene. We evaluate our method on a newly captured dataset of in-the-wild waterfall scenes and demonstrate clear improvements over prior work in dynamic Gaussian splatting and 3D cinemagraph generation in visual quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [206] [Can machines reason non-verbally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13925)
*Shaheer U. Saeed,Zion Tse,Gregory Slabaugh,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GenVerOpt的通用框架，通过生成、验证和优化相结合的流程，提升机器在视觉非语言推理方面的能力，使其超越现有系统，甚至在某些任务上超越人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进的AI系统在处理需要视觉非语言推理的任务时仍存在局限性，而这类推理过程的中间步骤难以明确验证，使得建模和提升机器在该方面的能力成为一个挑战。

Method: GenVerOpt框架整合了生成、验证和优化三个阶段。它首先生成潜在的解决方案，然后验证这些解决方案的正确性，最后通过迭代优化来改进它们，实现了逐样本优化。

Result: GenVerOpt在多个领域展现出卓越性能，包括超越人类在抽象智力测试题、媲美临床医生在前列腺癌分割、优于专家在肝脏血管重建，以及在肿瘤边界对齐方面超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GenVerOpt证明了机器推理能力可以超越语言，并将视觉非语言推理确立为实现更灵活、更通用智能的基础。

Abstract: Modern machines can solve equations, follow logic and reason through language, yet they still struggle with problems that require visual non-verbal reasoning. This is evident even in advanced systems such as DeepSeek, ChatGPT and Gemini, which remain limited on visual tasks such as completing abstract patterns or delineating ambiguous regions-of-interest. Unlike explicit intermediate steps in verbal reasoning which can be verified through logic or automated tests, visual reasoning involves implicit intermediate steps with no clear methods for verification. Non-verbal reasoning is therefore challenging to model and, as a result, remains a fundamental limitation of current machine intelligence. Here we introduce GenVerOpt, a general framework that unifies generation, verification and optimisation into a reasoning pipeline for machines. By generating candidate solutions, verifying their correctness, and iteratively refining them, GenVerOpt enables per-sample optimisation rather than relying solely on dataset-level trends captured during model training. This process mirrors human problem-solving where multiple possibilities are imagined, evaluated, and refined until a correct solution emerges. Across diverse domains, GenVerOpt demonstrates capabilities beyond existing systems: it surpasses humans on abstract IQ puzzles, matches clinicians in prostate cancer segmentation, exceeds experts in liver vessel reconstruction and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in tumour boundary alignment. These results show that machine reasoning can extend beyond language, establishing visual non-verbal reasoning as a foundation for more flexible and generalisable intelligence.

</details>


### [207] [Where to Look Matters: Rethinking Sub-Volume Sampling in 3D Medical Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13915)
*Junkai Liu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on 3D medical images commonly relies on random sub-volume sampling during pretraining. However, random cropping implicitly treats all spatial regions as equally worth observing, despite anatomical priors and spatial redundancy that make regions inherently unequal in their value for representation learning. To this end, we first show through controlled crop-quality manipulations that better sub-volumes lead to better downstream transfer, revealing the observation policy as an overlooked bottleneck in 3D medical SSL. Motivated by this finding, we revisit sub-volume sampling as a \emph{where-to-look} problem. We propose \textbf{\method}, a plug-and-play framework that replaces random cropping with an offline coarse-to-fine probe bank guided by \textit{anatomy-awareness}, \textit{informativeness}, and \textit{diversity}. We instantiate \method as a lightweight \lite variant using geometric and image-statistical cues, and a diagnostic \feat variant using frozen visual features, to examine whether lightweight volume-intrinsic priors suffice or richer semantic cues reliably provide additional value. Extensive experiments across multiple 3D medical SSL backbones and downstream tasks demonstrate that \method-\lite consistently improves transfer performance over random sampling. Mechanistic analyses further attribute these gains to more anatomically plausible and informative observations, reduced spatial redundancy, more efficient learning dynamics, and more structured representations.

</details>


### [208] [InstantFusion: A Shared Latent Interface for Heterogeneous Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13944)
*Xuanlang Dai,Jiazi Bu,Yujie Zhou,Yuhong Liu,Beichen Zhang,Penghui Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 InstantFusion 的轻量级共享潜在接口，旨在连接不同图像生成模型的去噪轨迹，实现了跨模型的状态转换，并在加速、多偏好组合、跨架构蒸馏和跨模型组合等任务中展现了通用性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散/流模型在图像生成方面取得了显著进展，但不同模型之间潜在表征的跨模型对齐以及去噪动态的一致性仍未得到充分研究。作者观察到独立训练的生成器表现出相似的潜在空间组织，并且直接传递中间状态可以保留粗略的视觉内容，这促使了本研究。

Method: InstantFusion 引入了模型特定的潜在自编码器 (LAEs)，用于将不同生成器（在训练后冻结）的中间状态投影到一个共享潜在空间，并将其解码到目标生成器的潜在空间。该共享空间通过两个互补目标进行约束：对齐来自同一图像在匹配噪声水平下的中间状态，以及同步它们各自的局部去噪速度。

Result: InstantFusion 能够实现不同图像生成模型之间的无缝去噪状态转换，无需为每对模型进行专门的映射。在加速、多偏好组合、跨架构 on-policy 蒸馏和跨模型组合等多种应用场景中，InstantFusion 取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: InstantFusion 提供了一种通用且轻量级的方法，可以连接和利用异构图像生成模型的去噪动态，从而实现更灵活和高效的图像生成应用。

Abstract: Diffusion/flow models have emerged as the de facto paradigms for image generation, giving rise to a diverse family of modern generators with increasingly heterogeneous architectures and representations. Despite their remarkable generation capabilities, the cross-model alignment of latent representations and the consistency of their underlying denoising dynamics remain underexplored. In this work, we uncover an intriguing cross-model compatibility: independently trained generators exhibit similar latent spatial organization, while directly handing off intermediate states between unaligned models can still preserve coarse visual content. Motivated by these observations, we propose InstantFusion, a lightweight shared latent interface that connects the denoising trajectories of heterogeneous image generators. Specifically, to enable flexible cross-model state translation, model-specific Latent AutoEncoders (LAEs) are introduced to project intermediate states from different generators into a shared latent space and decode them into the latent space of a target generator. This shared space is constrained by two complementary objectives: aligning intermediate states derived from the same image at matched noise levels and synchronizing their corresponding local denoising velocities. Once trained, InstantFusion allows denoising to seamlessly transition across frozen generators without requiring a dedicated mapping for every model pair. The versatility of InstantFusion is demonstrated across acceleration, multi-preference composition, cross-architecture on-policy distillation, and cross-model composition, achieving competitive performance across these diverse settings.

</details>


### [209] [Towards Physically Grounded Articulated 3D Assets: A Multi-Agent Pipeline and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13947)
*Xudong Cai,Ruijie Zhu,Haoliang Shang,Ruining Li,Marc Pollefeys,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Artisan系统，通过多智能体流水线和物理模拟反馈，实现了更具物理接地性的3D资产生成，超越了单纯的几何和外观生成，关注运动和功能性。同时构建了一个包含空间保真度、物理合理性、下游任务性能和整体质量的统一基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D资产生成方法主要关注几何和外观的复现，而忽略了物体的运动和功能性。研究动机是为了实现更具物理接地性、可操作性的3D资产生成。

Method: 提出Artisan系统，该系统扩展了Articraft方法，引入了一个多智能体流水线，并结合了物理模拟反馈。该系统能够生成具有碰撞规避、重力稳定性、准确质量和材料属性的3D资产。同时，构建了一个包含100个参考资产、50个稳定性提示和50个ABO对象的统一基准。

Result: Artisan系统在空间保真度方面具有竞争力，并在碰撞率和稳定性率方面优于其他代表性方法。在包含10个代表性模型的评估中，Artisan取得了最低的碰撞率和最高的稳定性率。

Conclusion: Artisan系统和新构建的基准推动了3D生成朝着物理上合理、功能上可用的资产发展，为未来研究提供了新的方向和评估标准。

Abstract: In this paper, we study physically grounded articulated 3D asset generation---moving the goal from reproducing how objects look to capturing how they move and function. Inspired by the literature on procedural and agentic generation, we present Artisan, a system that extends the latest method Articraft by introducing a multi-agent pipeline with physical simulation feedback, enabling comprehensive attributes beyond geometry, appearance, and physically plausible articulation, such as collision-free motion, stability under gravity, and accurate mass and material properties. Furthermore, we construct a unified benchmark spanning four key aspects: spatial fidelity, physical plausibility, downstream task performance, and overall quality. The benchmark encompasses 100 reference assets from ArtVIP and LightWheel, 50 stability prompts, and 50 ABO objects with reference masses, with protocols evaluating various metrics. Through extensive evaluation of more than 10 representative models, Artisan achieves competitive spatial fidelity while attaining the lowest collision rates and highest stability rates among the evaluated methods. Together, our system and benchmark establish insights for advancing 3D generation towards physically plausible, functionally usable assets.

</details>


### [210] [Images Talk Back: Guiding MMDiTs by Suppressing Image-to-Text Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13941)
*Yucheng Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Shusheng Yang,Jiakang Chen,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，多模态扩散 Transformer (MMDiT) 中的图像到文本（I2T）反馈在生成过程中会保留图像中已有的错误，并提出了语义反馈引导（SFG）及其就地变体（iSFG）来抑制这种负面反馈，从而提高了图像和视频生成的对齐度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMDiT 的内部文本表示通过联合注意力机制由演进中的图像更新，这引发了一个问题：这种反馈是帮助纠正提示偏差还是保留它们？研究者发现 I2T 反馈会保留图像中已有的对象和属性（包括错误），这促使他们去寻找一种方法来控制和优化这种反馈。

Method: 研究者提出了语义反馈引导（SFG），通过引导一个加强 I2T 反馈并削弱文本到图像（T2I）条件约束的辅助分支来抑制 I2T 反馈的影响。此外，还提出了就地变体 iSFG，直接抑制 I2T 反馈并加强 T2I 条件约束，无需额外的模型评估。还探索了将 SFG 应用于生成过程的早期阶段。

Result: 在三种图像生成模型上的实验显示，SFG 和 iSFG 能够提高对齐度和用户偏好分数。例如，在 FLUX 上，SFG 将 GenEval 分数从 0.6661 提高到 0.7479；在 SD3.5M 上，iSFG 在零额外 NFE 的情况下，HPSv2.1 分数比 CFG 提高了 1.33。这种控制方法也成功应用于文本到视频和图像到视频生成，提高了 VBench 分数。

Conclusion: I2T 反馈在多模态扩散模型中既可能是有益的，也可能是有害的，它会保留生成过程中的错误。通过语义反馈引导（SFG）和其就地变体（iSFG）可以有效抑制 I2T 反馈的负面影响，从而显著提高图像、视频生成任务的质量和用户满意度。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs) allow the evolving image to update internal text representations through joint attention. These representations then guide subsequent image updates, raising a question: does this feedback help correct deviations from the prompt, or preserve them? We find that image-to-text (I2T) feedback can preserve the objects and attributes already represented in the evolving image, including errors. Transplanting I2T messages can rescue failures or disrupt successes. At matched intervention strengths, suppressing the influence of I2T feedback improves generation, whereas opposite controls degrade it. These findings motivate Semantic Feedback Guidance (SFG), which suppresses the influence of I2T feedback by guiding away from an auxiliary branch that strengthens this feedback and weakens text-to-image conditioning. The in-place variant, iSFG, directly suppresses I2T feedback and strengthens text-to-image conditioning, requiring no extra model evaluations. Temporal interventions motivate running the SFG branch during the first 20% of image sampling steps, requiring about $1.2\times$ the base NFE without classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments across three image-generation backbones show gains in alignment and preference-model scores. On FLUX, adding SFG to CFG raises GenEval from $0.6661$ to $0.7479$; on SD3.5M, iSFG adds $+1.33$ HPSv2.1 over CFG at zero extra NFE. The control also transfers to text-to-video and image-to-video generation, improving VBench scores. Code is available at https://github.com/yyc1215lll-creator/SFG.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [AutoIndexer: Flexible-Order Decoding by Training Causal Models on Chains of Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12545)
*Shu Ishida,Aliasghar Khani,Tianyu Zhang,The Cong Luong,James Seale Smith,Adam Gaier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AutoIndexer的生成模型，通过模拟“编辑链”的训练和解码机制，克服了传统自回归和扩散模型在处理非因果依赖数据时的局限性，实现了对已生成文本的灵活后验修改（插入、替换、删除），并能处理带噪声的上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自回归模型在处理需要后验修正的任务（如交叉引用、未来依赖）时存在不足，因为其输出无法进行事后修改。虽然扩散模型有所改善，但仍无法灵活地插入或删除任意数量的token。人类的推理过程则可以灵活地修改之前的输出。

Method: AutoIndexer采用一种新颖的训练和解码机制，模拟“编辑链”。模型学习预测用于打开和关闭编辑的标记符，并同步移动光标到现有token序列中的位置，类似于文本编辑器。训练数据通过对真实标签应用逆向的链式编辑操作来生成，模型通过“撤销”这些逆向操作来学习编辑。

Result: AutoIndexer展示了其在生成后对上下文进行编辑的能力，这是大多数自回归和扩散模型所不具备的，同时在标准LLM基准测试上没有性能损失。该方法对错误的上下文具有鲁棒性，并能适应代码修正等编辑任务。

Conclusion: AutoIndexer是一种能够灵活修改已生成文本的生成模型，通过创新的“编辑链”机制，有效解决了传统模型在处理非因果依赖数据时的不足，并在保持标准任务性能的同时，提供了强大的后验编辑能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding is less suited to non-causally ordered data, e.g., cross-references or future dependencies, since their outputs cannot be revised post hoc, unlike iterative human reasoning. Diffusion LLMs partially mitigate this limitation but still cannot flexibly insert or remove arbitrary numbers of tokens from a pre-generated context. We design AutoIndexer as a generative model with a novel training and decoding mechanism that simulates a Chain of Edits, allowing the model to flexibly modify its prior outputs in hindsight via token insertions, substitutions, and deletions. The model learns to predict marker tokens for opening and closing edits, simultaneously moving a cursor to positions in the existing token sequence, analogous to a text editor. Training sequences are obtained by perturbing ground-truth labels with inverse chained edit operations, and the model learns to edit by undoing those inverses. The perturbation training allows AutoIndexer to be robust to erroneous context and adaptable to editing tasks such as code correction. We demonstrate that our method exhibits post-generation context-editing capabilities that most autoregressive and diffusion models lack without compromising performance on standard LLM benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/AutodeskAILab/autoindexer.

</details>


### [212] [Contextual Correction of Parallel Draftsfor Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12570)
*Roy Eisenstadt,Harel Lidar,Tal Gonen,Omer Luxembourg,Itamar Zimerman,Lior Wolf,Eli David,Ilan Naiman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 DCOCO 的轻量级机制，用于增强投机解码（speculative decoding）的效率，特别是在使用基于扩散模型的草稿器时。DCOCO 通过在并行生成过程中考虑相邻 token 的上下文依赖性，提高了 token 选择的准确性，从而在不显著增加计算成本的情况下，显著提升了生成速度和吞吐量，特别是在高并发服务场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投机解码方法，尤其是基于扩散模型的草稿器，虽然通过并行草稿提高了效率，但由于缺乏对已生成 token 的感知，忽略了 token 间的上下文依赖性，影响了生成质量和最终吞吐量。因此，需要一种机制来恢复这种并行生成中的上下文依赖性。

Method: DCOCO 是一种轻量级机制，它在并行草稿阶段保留多个候选 token，并并行评估它们在相邻草稿位置的上下文兼容性。这种方法能够在低额外成本下，实现上下文感知的 token 选择，从而恢复了被忽略的 token 间依赖性。

Result: 在四种验证器骨干模型和十个基准测试中，DCOCO 在所有评估配置下均取得了最高的平均吞吐量，与标准解码相比，速度提升高达 6.6 倍。在并发服务场景下，DCOCO 的表现优于最强的竞争方法 6-23%，而其他顺序校正器在相同负载下表现不如未经 DCOCO 优化的 DFlash。

Conclusion: 通过并行上下文评分恢复块内依赖性，可以为不同解码模式和服务负载提供一致的加速。DCOCO 是一种有效的机制，能够显著提升基于扩散模型的投机解码的吞吐量，并在实际应用中具有很高的价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position's top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.

</details>


### [213] [SWE-Duel: Towards a Self-Scaling Arena for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12569)
*Wei Minn,Zhensu Sun,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SWE-Duel的自适应编码基准测试方法，其中两个AI代理通过相互评估和修复对方引入的bug来竞争。该方法通过在评估时动态生成任务，避免了训练数据泄露，并根据代理的能力动态调整难度。实验结果表明，SWE-Duel能够有效地评估和区分不同AI代理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态编码基准测试容易因任务泄露而饱和，并且难度固定，无法适应AI代理能力的快速发展。因此，需要一种能够动态调整难度、避免数据泄露的自适应基准测试。

Method: SWE-Duel通过以下方式实现自适应性：1. 动态生成任务：在评估时创建任务和Oracle，避免了预先存在的数据泄露。2. 红蓝回合制：两个AI代理轮流扮演“红方”（创建任务并引入bug）和“蓝方”（修复bug）。3. 严格的PR审查：只有在满足特定测试条件（通过现有测试，失败bug测试，且能被同一代理修复）的情况下，Pull Request才被接受。4. 盲修bug：蓝方代理在不知道bug存在的情况下进行修复。5. 胜利条件：蓝方代理成功修复bug并通过所有测试后获胜。

Result: 在15个代码库、5种语言的1500回合比赛中，GPT-5.5（Codex CLI）成为最强的修复者（98.3%的修复率），Claude Opus 4.8（mini-swe-agent）成为最强的任务创造者（27.3%的红方胜率）。同一模型家族的新版本编写的任务比旧版本更能击败固定参考解算器（42.9% vs 4.0%），初步表明任务难度随任务编写能力而扩展。

Conclusion: SWE-Duel成功地构建了一个自适应的编码基准测试环境，能够动态评估AI编码代理的能力，并为未来开发更具挑战性和适应性的AI基准测试提供了方向。实验结果表明，该方法能够有效地区分不同AI代理的求解和任务生成能力，并初步证实了任务难度与任务编写能力同步增长的假设。

Abstract: Static coding benchmarks are fixed at release. Over time their tasks leak into training data, and stronger agents eventually saturate them. We argue that a benchmark should instead aim to be self-scaling: its tasks and oracles are created at evaluation time, so no content exists in advance to leak, and its difficulty is set by the participating agents rather than fixed at release. We introduce SWE-Duel, an arena in which two coding agents evaluate each other over mirrored Red-Blue turns. In each turn, the Red agent works on a real repository across three context-isolated sessions. It proposes and implements a new feature with feature tests, then embeds a subtle bug in that feature along with sealed bug-triggering tests. The resulting pull request is admitted only if (1) it passes the feature tests and the repository's existing tests while failing exactly the bug tests, and (2) a fresh session of the same agent can repair the bug blind, which rules out unsolvable tasks. The Blue agent then reviews the pull request without being told a bug exists. It wins only if its revision passes all three test suites, and then the two agents swap roles. We run a tournament among 11 model-harness participants on 15 repositories in five languages for 1,500 turns. The leaders win in different ways. GPT-5.5 with Codex CLI ranks first as the strongest solver, repairing 98.3% of the defects it reviews. Claude Opus 4.8 with mini-swe-agent ranks second as the strongest task author, with a 27.3% Red win rate. Finally, within one model family, tasks written by the newest version defeat a fixed-reference solver far more often than those of older versions (42.9% vs. 4.0%), giving preliminary evidence that task difficulty scales with task authoring capability.

</details>


### [214] [CLUE: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Unsupervised Event Type Induction and Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12580)
*Pratik Maitra,Lauren Kwon,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLUE框架，通过联合嵌入表示、对比损失引导聚类以及事件命名和细化，实现了事件类型的自动归纳、命名和标注，解决了传统方法资源密集和易受偏见影响的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的事件本体创建过程需要领域专家参与，成本高昂且易受人类偏见影响。

Method: 设计了联合嵌入表示（结合动词中心视图和语义视图），使用对比损失引导的聚类方法进行事件类型归纳，并进行事件命名和细化。

Result: 实验证明了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CLUE框架能够有效地进行事件类型归纳、命名和标注，克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Event ontology is the foundational framework for defining events in a dataset. Event type induction generally involves domain experts who define guidelines for creating event types and their names based on the dataset's domain and scope. However, this process is resource-intensive in terms of annotation costs and is prone to human biases. To alleviate the need for manual event ontology creation, we propose the CLUE framework for event type induction, naming, and annotation. Our main contributions in this work are: 1) designing a novel joint embedding representation for expressing both the verb-centric view and the semantic view of events; 2) event type induction using contrastive loss guided clustering; 3) event naming and refinement. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Our code is available at https://github.com/PratikMaitra/CLUE.

</details>


### [215] [One Model to Loop Them All: Elastic Latent Recurrence for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12582)
*Faisal Ladhak,Shantanu Acharya,Santiago Akle Serano,Simeng Sun,Boris Ginsburg,Krishna C Puvvada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR) 的训练方法，用于解决循环语言模型在单循环（即与非循环模型相同计算量）时性能较低的问题。ELR 通过三种关键技术（门控状态更新、混合循环计数、条件化）使得单循环 ELR 模型在计算量和准确性上都能与同等规模的非循环模型媲美，并且在增加循环次数时能有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环语言模型虽然可以通过增加循环次数来提升准确性，但在单循环计算量下，其性能往往远不如同等规模的非循环模型。这导致在对延迟敏感的应用场景下，循环模型不具备优势。因此，需要一种方法来消除这种“单循环税”，使得循环模型在较低计算量下也能达到与非循环模型相当的性能。

Method: ELR 训练方法包含三个核心组成部分：1. 采用门控状态更新和学习到的步长，以保证单循环时的准确性。2. 在优化器更新时混合不同的循环计数，这有助于在训练过程中保持单循环性能。3. 通过条件化（考虑循环索引和计算预算）来指导每一层的计算，从而在增加循环次数时降低损失。此外，研究还探讨了在预训练后期引入混合深度、在 MoE 模型中复用专家路由以及在推理时优化 KV 缓存等优化策略。

Result: ELR 模型在单循环时，其计算成本与同等规模的非循环模型相当，准确性也接近。随着循环次数的增加，准确性得到显著提升。在 4B 稠密和 30B MoE 模型上的实验表明，ELR 在单循环时性能略低于基线，但在四循环时性能大幅领先。ELR 训练出的 Koi-4B 模型在 AAI 指数上，单循环时已能媲美 Qwen3.5-4B，四循环时更是达到 29 的高分。

Conclusion: ELR 是一种有效的训练循环语言模型的方法，能够消除单循环时的性能劣势，并为模型提供了在计算量和准确性之间进行灵活权衡的能力。这种方法在提升模型性能和效率方面显示出巨大的潜力，尤其是在部署时能够根据实际需求进行计算资源的分配。

Abstract: A latent recurrent language model applies a shared block of layers repeatedly, so one checkpoint can trade inference compute for accuracy by changing the loop count. At one loop, however, such models typically fall well short of a non-recurrent model of the same size, a one-loop tax that makes a separate non-recurrent model the better choice whenever latency matters. We present Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR), a recipe for training looped language models without this tax. At one loop, an ELR model costs the same to serve as a non-recurrent model of the same size and, trained on the same data, reaches similar accuracy. Each additional loop then buys more accuracy for more inference compute. Three choices make this work: a gated state update with a learned step size, loop counts mixed within each optimizer update, and conditioning each pass on its loop index and budget. The first two preserve one-loop accuracy, and the third lowers loss at every additional loop. Mixed depths can be introduced late in pretraining to limit the added training cost, and with zero-initialized conditioning added at the switch, this late schedule reaches lower loss than mixed-depth training from scratch. In MoE models, reusing first-loop expert routes on later loops cuts mixed-depth update time by 11.7% with no measurable change in accuracy. At inference, keeping only the last loop's attention KV cache holds attention KV memory at its one-loop level while retaining most of the gain from additional loops. On 4B dense and 30B-A3B MoE hybrid models, ELR at one loop is 0.52 points below and 1.04 points above its matched baseline on a ten-task average, and four loops add 7.25 and 4.75 points. Carried through post-training, the recipe yields Koi-4B, a single checkpoint that scores an estimated 18 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at one loop, matching Qwen3.5-4B, and 29 at four loops.

</details>


### [216] [Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12601)
*Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为GEPA的反射式提示进化方法，用于优化大型语言模型代理，解决了提示优化可能导致过拟合（记忆训练数据而非泛化能力）的问题，并在事实性任务中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示优化方法在处理事实性任务时存在一个潜在的失败模式，即优化器可能会将训练数据的答案直接写入提示中，导致模型出现过拟合（记忆）而非真正的行为泛化。为了解决这一问题，研究者希望找到一种更有效的提示优化方法。

Method: 研究者提出了反射式提示进化（GEPA）方法，并将其应用于多智能体深度研究代理的两个系统提示上。实验设置了严格的、主题不重叠的训练-验证-测试分割，以评估模型的泛化能力。研究还进行了消融实验，分离了不同组成部分（如逐维度反馈）对模型性能的影响，并使用了不同来源的评估者（Judge）来验证结果的鲁棒性。

Result: GEPA方法在主题不重叠的测试集上，引用奖励（citation reward）从0.702提升到0.781（提升0.079）。尽管获胜提示中包含大量“记忆”的答案，但消融实验表明这些内容本身并不能带来奖励。研究发现，逐维度的反馈信息是实现泛化能力的关键，将其简化为单一标量奖励会损害泛化能力。手写版本的提示也能在很大程度上复现实验结果。

Conclusion: GEPA是一种有效的提示优化方法，能够克服提示优化中的过拟合问题，显著提升语言模型代理在事实性任务上的泛化能力。研究强调，对提示优化器进行可信评估需要使用主题分离的数据集、多随机种子以及细致的评估者消融分析。逐维度的反馈机制是实现泛化增益的关键因素。

Abstract: Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.

</details>


### [217] [Self-Preservation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12602)
*Dongkyu Cho,Rumi Chunara,Ali Behrouz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在持续学习中，模型是否能通过生成和选择重放数据来更好地保护自身能力。结果表明，模型生成的重放数据能减少在未纳入任务集中的遗忘，并且在某些情况下，模型还能辅助选择重放目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法通常只保留部分先前知识，而遗忘可能超出这些范围。语言模型可以通过生成重放数据来缓解遗忘，但如何让模型自身参与选择更有效的重放目标以实现更广泛的自我保护，是本研究的核心问题。

Method: 研究者构建了任务描述及其示例组成的“任务银行”，并利用冻结的模型通过指导重放来生成数据。通过对Qwen模型进行持续指令微调，并分析不同模型大小下的性能，研究了新任务、学习率、任务银行选择策略以及重放数据对遗忘的影响。

Result: 1. 新任务和学习率会影响先前能力的退化，而跟踪先前正确答案的损失比平均准确率更能揭示遗忘。2. 生成的重放数据能减少任务银行之外的任务的遗忘，组合部分任务银行能进一步增强保护效果。3. 在特定模型规模下（4B），模型能通过选择重放目标来提高遗忘前的保留率。4. 在固定示例预算下，用重放数据替换新任务示例可以提高保留率，但会牺牲新任务的性能增益。

Conclusion: 模型可以通过生成重放数据以及在某些情况下选择重放目标来支持更广泛的自我保护，从而在持续学习中更好地保留自身能力。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks to adapt pretrained models while preserving prior capabilities. Yet the tasks supplied for preservation cover only part of what a model knows, while forgetting can extend beyond them. Recent work shows that language models can supplement this limited evidence by generating useful replay without access to their original training data. This raises a question about self-preservation: can a model help choose preservation targets that protect its own capabilities more broadly? To study this question, we organize task descriptions and their examples into Task Banks, whose entries can be selected and combined to guide replay generation by a frozen model. Continual instruction tuning with Qwen models of varying sizes yields three findings. First, the new task and learning rate shape which prior capabilities degrade, while tracking previously correct answers reveals losses that average accuracy can conceal. Second, generated replay reduces forgetting on tasks outside the bank. Combining partial banks strengthens this protection, improving mean retention of previously correct answers over either bank alone across the tested sizes. Third, the model can also help choose replay targets: selection informed by the new task improves mean held-out retention over random selection at 4B, with similar new-task gains, although smaller models show no corresponding advantage. Under a fixed example budget, however, replacing new-task examples with replay improves preservation at the cost of smaller new-task gains. Together, these findings show that models can support broader self-preservation by generating replay data and, in some settings, choosing which tasks to replay.

</details>


### [218] [GeopoliticalQA: Benchmarking Retrieval and Generation over Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12617)
*Veronika Solopova,Ivan Havlytskyi,Viktoria Skorik,Ostap Vykhopen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究者构建了一个包含415份地缘政治和战略情报报告的数据集，用于评估检索增强生成（RAG）模型在处理复杂文本上的表现，发现当前模型在回答推断性和风险型问题上仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地缘政治和战略情报报告包含大量专家知识，但在当前政治环境下，由于其异质性和分析复杂性，给检索增强问答带来了挑战。

Method: 构建了一个包含415份开源地缘政治和战略情报报告的数据集，生成了5,525个问题-答案对。提出了一种文档感知生成流程，并使用专家标注和LLM-as-judge协议验证数据质量。在此基准上，评估了15个检索系统和6个开源LLM。

Result: 在文档级检索方面，模型表现相似；在更难的块级检索方面，嵌入模型的大小并非关键因素，一个中等规模的模型（bge-m3）表现可与规模大得多的模型媲美，并优于商业嵌入API。然而，即使提供了黄金证据，答案生成仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在处理推断性和风险型问题时。

Conclusion: 尽管在检索技术方面取得了一些进展，但利用复杂的地缘政治和战略情报报告来准确回答需要推理和风险评估的问题，仍然是检索增强生成模型面临的关键挑战。

Abstract: Geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports constitute a rich source of expert knowledge and have increasing value in the current political climate, but their heterogeneity and analytical complexity pose challenges for retrieval-augmented question answering. We introduce a benchmark for grounded question answering over 415 open-source geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports, comprising 5,525 document-grounded question-answer pairs spanning factual, inferential, comparative, and risk-oriented questions. We propose a document-aware generation pipeline that profiles reports before generating questions and validate dataset quality through expert annotation and an LLM-as-judge protocol. Using the benchmark, we evaluate 15 retrieval systems and six open-weight LLMs for RAG. We find that models perform similarly on document-level retrieval, while at the harder chunk level retrieval quality is not driven by embedding-model scale: bge-m3, a mid-size open encoder matches an order of magnitude larger ones and outperforms commercial embedding APIs. In contrast, answer generation remains challenging even given the gold evidence, particularly for inferential and risk-oriented questions.

</details>


### [219] [perturber: A Stateless Service for Semantics-Preserving Text Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12667)
*Khoi-Nguyen Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 perturber 的工具，它是一个无状态的服务和 Python 库，用于对输入文本应用单个、语义保持的扰动，旨在更可靠地评估大型语言模型（LLM）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型（LLM）在干净基准上的评估结果高估了模型的鲁棒性，因为实际输入经常是经过扰动的，而这些保持语义的变体却常常导致模型输出错误。需要一种系统化的方法来研究这一点，并且现有的工具将扰动与基准测试栈耦合，而不是作为可重用的转换层。

Method: 开发了一个名为 perturber 的无状态服务和 Python 库。该工具每次调用应用一个语义保持的扰动，并从包含 11 个类别共 68 种扰动的目录中抽取。perturber 基于一个单一的基本操作，通过调用链组合扰动，确保服务和库产生相同输出。对 perturber 工具本身的正确性、可复现性和成本进行了评估，并通过测量扰动对语义的影响来评估其有效性。

Result: perturber 工具能够独立地、可复现地应用单个语义保持的扰动。其评估表明了工具的正确性、可复现性和成本效益。此外，该工具还提供了交互式目录和一键式可复现评估功能。

Conclusion: perturber 工具通过提供一个可重用的、精确控制的扰动层，解决了现有 LLM 鲁棒性评估方法中存在的不足，能够更准确、更系统地评估 LLM 在真实世界输入下的表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluations on clean benchmarks overstate model robustness: real inputs arrive perturbed, and these meaning-preserving variants routinely move model answers. Studying this systematically requires applying perturbations one at a time, reproducibly, and identically wherever they run, yet existing tools couple them to a benchmarking stack rather than packaging them as a reusable transform layer. We present perturber, a stateless service and Python library that applies exactly one semantics-preserving perturbation per call, drawn from a first-party catalog of 68 perturbations across 11 families. It turns on a single primitive, with composition left to the caller chaining calls, so the service and the library produce identical output by construction. We evaluate the tool itself, its correctness, reproducibility, and cost, rather than any model's robustness, measuring meaning-preservation per perturbation rather than assuming it. The demonstration adds a live playground, an introspectable catalog for benchmark selection, and a one-command reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [220] [GeoFitBench-LLM: A Benchmark for Assessing Large Language Models' Capabilities in Geospatial Data Requirements Elicitation for Geo-Analytic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12669)
*Amirhossein Vahdat,Thierry Badard,Luc Lamontagne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GeoFitBench-LLM 是一个新基准，用于评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在地理空间人工智能（GeoAI）系统中识别地理空间数据需求的能力，重点关注数据集、支持资源和约束的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在 GeoAI 系统中的应用日益广泛，但其评估很少关注地理空间数据需求（如数据集、资源和约束）的提取，而这是可靠进行地理分析任务的关键。

Method: 引入 GeoFitBench-LLM 基准，围绕时空关系和语义接地两个推理维度构建。该基准使用独立于提供商的固定模式编码地面真相需求，以进行可重现的资源识别和属性级别规范评估。

Result: 在七种模型上进行实验，结果表明 LLMs 通常能够识别所需资源，其中 GPT-5.4 Thinking 在恢复完整资源集方面表现最佳（66.67%）。然而，随着推理需求的增加，准确的适用性规范仍然更具挑战性。

Conclusion: GeoFitBench-LLM 基准证明了评估 LLMs 在 GeoAI 中提取地理空间数据需求的能力。虽然识别所需资源是可行的，但更复杂的适用性规范仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Large language models are becoming central to GeoAI systems, yet their evaluation rarely considers the elicitation of geospatial data requirements. This omission is critical as retrieval oriented geo-analytic tasks cannot be supported reliably without first identifying the necessary datasets and constraints. GeoFitBench-LLM is introduced as a benchmark for assessing whether LLMs can infer the datasets, the supporting resources and the constraints required by geo-analytic requests in natural language. The benchmark is structured around two reasoning dimensions, Spatio-Temporal Relational and Semantic Grounding, reflecting long-standing challenges in geospatial information retrieval and related natural language tasks. Ground truth requirements are encoded in a fixed, provider-independent schema for reproducible evaluation of resource identification and attribute-level specification. Experiments on seven models under minimal prompting indicate that identifying the necessary resources is often achievable, with GPT-5.4 Thinking achieving the best result by recovering the complete set of resources in 66.67% of benchmark requests. Results further indicate that accurate fitness-for-use specification remains more difficult as inferential demands increase.

</details>


### [221] [Activating the Annotator: Operationalising Lived Experience in Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12701)
*Sheza Munir,Ramaravind Kommiya Mothilal,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了AXLE，一个在标注前通过情景叙事引导对话生成第一人称微叙事，以激活标注者经验的方法，旨在解决传统人口统计学信息作为静态协变量的局限性。实验结果表明，AXLE能提高标注者身份共鸣和对伤害的敏感度，但同时也增加了标签熵和降低了标注者间一致性，突显了经验的多样性而非制造共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然认识到标注者立场对主观任务标签的影响，但缺乏将其操作化的方法。传统做法是将标注者招募时填写的人口统计信息作为静态协变量，这假设了这些信息所代表的知识在标注时是活跃的，而本研究将此假设视为一个设计问题，并探索是否可以通过任务本身来激活标注者的生活经验。

Method: 研究提出了AXLE（Annotation Experience-Linking Engine）方法，一个预标注阶段。在该阶段，通过一个情景短片（scenario vignette）引发一段简短的、基于轴向引导的对话，然后将对话合成为一个第一人称的微叙事，并在标注开始前返回给标注者。研究在一个2x2的被试间研究中（104名标注者，30个条目，1040个标注）对模糊的仇恨言论（涉及移民、宗教和性别）进行了测试。

Result: AXLE方法被证实与更高的身份共鸣（identity resonance）和更高的伤害敏感度（sensitivity to harm）相关。同时，它也增加了标签熵（label entropy）并降低了标注者间一致性（inter-annotator agreement）。

Conclusion: AXLE方法能够有效激活标注者的生活经验，使其在进行标注时能更好地体现其个人立场和对潜在伤害的敏感度，从而表面化经验的多样性，而非试图制造标注上的共识。这与标注者培训的目标（即收敛）形成了鲜明对比。

Abstract: Perspectivist NLP has established that annotator positionality shapes labels for subjective tasks, but the field still lacks methods for operationalising it. Positionality is typically operationalised as a demographic form completed at recruitment: a static attribute recorded once and treated as a covariate, on the assumption that the knowledge it indexes is active when the annotator reads an item. This paper treats that assumption as a design problem and asks whether lived experience can instead be primed by the task itself. We introduce AXLE, a pre-annotation stage in which a scenario vignette anchors a short, axis-guided conversation that is synthesised into a first-person micro-narrative and returned to the annotator before labelling begins. In a 2x2 between-subjects study (104 annotators, 30 items, 1,040 annotations) on ambiguous hate speech spanning immigration, religion and gender, elicitation was associated with higher identity resonance and higher sensitivity to harm. It also increased label entropy and lowered inter-annotator agreement: the stage appears to surface plurality rather than manufacture consensus, which distinguishes it from annotator training, whose success criterion is convergence.

</details>


### [222] [Unsupervised Multimodal Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12707)
*Yueying Li,Jingxuan Fan,Kianté Brantley,Sham M. Kakade,Hanlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人工偏好标注的视觉-语言奖励模型训练方法，通过在冻结的Qwen3-VL骨干网络的中间层训练一个线性头部，并使用扰动样本进行对比学习，显著降低了训练成本，并在多个基准测试中取得了与GPT-4o相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言奖励模型的训练成本高昂（需要大量标注偏好数据），且标注数据可能存在不一致性，评估基准也存在局限。

Method: 1. 使用冻结的Qwen3-VL作为骨干网络。
2. 在中间层添加一个线性头部进行训练。
3. 训练目标是让模型能够区分真实的网页标题与其扰动版本，无需人工标注的偏好对。
4. 通过人工标注数据仅用于选择最优的中间层。
5. 使用“同图扰动”作为训练数据。
6. 采用“验证器驱动的自举”来减少模型幻觉。

Result: 1. 所提出的方法在32B模型规模下，在三个基准测试中性能优于或持平GPT-4o。
2. 在VL-RewardBench基准上，性能领先GPT-4o 6.6分。
3. 使用“同图扰动”训练的冻结头部比精心挑选的RLAIF-V数据能提高3-5分。
4. 重新训练头部以减少幻觉，可将CHAIR得分提高5.1分，并将图像生成通过率提高5.4%。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种无需人工标注、成本极低且由训练数据本身定义的视觉-语言奖励模型构建方法，通过中间层探测、同图扰动和验证器驱动的自举，可以有效地替代昂贵的微调和人工标注方法，并在多个任务上展现出强大的竞争力。

Abstract: Vision-language reward models are trained on annotated preference pairs, which are expensive to collect, can disagree, and are ranked by benchmark accuracy. We build one with no human preference label: a linear head on an intermediate layer of a frozen Qwen3-VL backbone, trained to score a web caption above a perturbed version of the same caption; labeled data enters only to select the layer. The layer is the decisive choice, worth 5.8 points over the last layer, and one recipe transferred without re-tuning beats or at least matches GPT-4o on three benchmarks at 32B, 6.6 points ahead on VL-RewardBench, from a few thousand parameters at about a thousandth of fine-tuning compute. At an equal pair budget, perturbations teach a frozen head 3 to 5 points more than curated RLAIF-V pairs. Because the backbone is frozen, the objective is convex, and the perturbation sampler specifies the reward, which the benchmark does not identify: two heads trained on disjoint halves of the same perturbations tie on the benchmark and differ by 6.4 points in hallucination judging. Retraining the head on the policy's own verifier-labeled candidates cuts caption hallucination by 5.1 CHAIR points and lifts image-generation pass rate by 5.4, while scoring lower on every reward benchmark.
  Together, an intermediate-layer probe, free same-image perturbations, and a verifier-driven bootstrap turn give a recipe for multimodal reward modeling that needs no annotator, costs a fraction of fine-tuning, and is specified by the comparisons it is trained on rather than by a benchmark rank.

</details>


### [223] [U4RASD at KnowledgeGraphEval-2026: Ontology-Constrained Encoder and LLM Systems for Arabic Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12742)
*Ahmad Abbas,Hussein Mazeh,Chadi Abou Chakra,Aya Jouni,Mohammad Ballout,Fadi A. Zaraket*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于编码器和大型语言模型（LLM）的阿拉伯语定向关系抽取方法，并在 KnowledgeGraphEval 2026 子任务 2 中取得了第一名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 KnowledgeGraphEval 2026 的阿拉伯语定向关系抽取子任务开发有效的模型。

Method: 研究人员探索了两种方法：1）基于编码器的系统，使用类型化实体标记和分离的关系存在/分类头；2）大型语言模型（LLM）系统，通过 QLoRA 微调 Yehia 和 Qwen3.5 模型，并结合本体约束预测。最终的获奖系统是 Yehia-RE、Qwen-RE 和 TypedPair 的集成。

Result: 集成系统达到了 94.06 的微 F1 分数，排名第一。其中，Yehia-RE（全词汇）取得了 92.98 的分数，最佳编码器系统和 Qwen-RE 分别取得了 92.69 和 91.94 的分数。

Conclusion: 约束预测和互补建模方法对于阿拉伯语关系抽取至关重要，LLM 在此任务中表现出强大的潜力。

Abstract: This paper details our submission to KnowledgeGraphEval 2026 Subtask 2 on directed Arabic relation extraction. We explored encoder-based and large language model approaches for predicting one of 40 relations or no_relation from an Arabic sentence and a subject-object pair. Our encoder systems use typed entity markers and separate relation-existence and relation-classification heads, while our LLM systems fine-tune Yehia and Qwen3.5 with QLoRA and ontology-constrained prediction. Our best system, an ensemble of Yehia-RE, Qwen-RE, and TypedPair, achieved a micro-F1 score of 94.06 and ranked 1st in Subtask 2. Yehia-RE (full-vocabulary) achieved 92.98, while the best encoder and Qwen-RE achieved 92.69 and 91.94 respectively. Our results highlight the importance of constrained prediction and complementary modeling approaches for Arabic relation extraction.

</details>


### [224] [MADS: Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation for Robust Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12757)
*Arkajyoti Chakraborty,Adam Zernik,Ishika Kulkarni,Mudit Arora,Hugo Lin,Justin Chiu,Alessandro Di Bari,Neha Gupta,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了MADS（多智能体对话模拟）框架，用于生成包含对抗性对话轨迹的合成数据集，并构建了MADS-Bench基准测试集，旨在提升任务导向型对话代理在用户非合作行为下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能调用基准测试未能充分体现对抗性对话场景，导致用于训练鲁棒对话代理的资源不足。

Method: 使用MADS框架生成结构化、多样化的对话轨迹，注入对抗性对话以捕获不理想的对话路径。构建了包含六个领域、55个角色和1095个对话的MADS-Bench基准测试集。采用监督微调（SFT）和可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的两阶段训练方法来评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: MADS-Bench包含了约66%的易失败对话场景。通过两阶段训练方法，可以评估和提升模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MADS框架和MADS-Bench基准测试集为开发和评估更鲁棒的任务导向型对话代理提供了有效的解决方案，特别是在处理用户非合作行为方面。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under realistic conditions, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present MADS (Multi-Agent Dialog Simulation), a synthetic data generation pipeline that produces structured, diverse dialogue trajectories. We inject adversarial conversation, producing validated conversation trajectories that capture realistic, unhappy paths. Using MADS, we construct MADS-Bench: a benchmark spanning six domains, 55 personas, and 1095 conversations, with approximately 66% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We evaluate the robustness of the trained models using a two-stage training approach that combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR).

</details>


### [225] [DistressBench: Evaluating Multi-Dimensional Crisis Support in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12758)
*Drew Rein,Patrick Oathout,Vishal Kumar,Udari Madhushani Sehwag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个名为DistressBench的新基准，用于评估语言模型在处理用户自杀和自残（SSH）危机时的支持能力，而非仅仅评估其拒绝回答的能力。结果表明，即使是顶尖模型也未能完全满足危机支持的需求，尤其是在行动响应和资源导向方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估语言模型风险的方法，如拒绝率，未能有效衡量模型在处理用户披露自杀和自残危机时的支持能力，因为在这种情况下，模型需要提供实质性的支持而非拒绝。因此，需要一个能够评估模型危机支持质量的基准。

Method: 研究者构建了一个包含718个英文对话的DistressBench数据集，其中包含临床医生撰写的用户回合，涵盖23个SSH子类别。每个对话都配有详细的、加权的原子标准评分细则，覆盖危机支持的七个维度，以及一个由临床医生提供的参考回应。模型的得分是其回应满足的标准所占的加权平均比例。

Result: 在25个前沿模型中，最佳模型的得分达到88.4%，中位数模型为71.4%，而临床医生参考回应的得分为98.7%。在35.3%识别出危机的对话中，模型未能将用户导向人工帮助。模型在需要采取行动（而非回避）的维度上的表现较差。多轮对话的表现（68.5%）低于单轮对话（74.2%）。

Conclusion: 研究者认为，评估模型危机安全性的重点应放在其提供的支持质量上，而非其拒绝回答的能力。同时，模型的去升级（de-escalation）和资源路由能力应被视为部署要求。

Abstract: Language models have been adopted faster than evaluation practices that reliably assess their risks, and a growing share of use is affective rather than informational. Within that traffic users disclose suicide and self-harm (SSH) crises. Existing safety benchmarks score models primarily on refusal, but refusal is not the desired behavior here; the conversation requires a substantive supportive response. We introduce DistressBench, 718 English conversations with clinician-authored user turns spanning 23 SSH subcategories, each paired with a task-specific rubric of weighted atomic criteria across seven dimensions of crisis support and a clinician reference response. A model's score is the weighted average over the criteria its reply satisfies, reading directly as the fraction of required care delivered. The best of 25 frontier models satisfies 88.4% of weighted criteria and the median model 71.4%, against 98.7% for clinician references scored on the same criteria. Pooled across all 25 models, in 35.3% of conversations where a model satisfied every criterion for recognizing the crisis, it failed to route the user toward human help. The shortfall concentrates in the dimensions requiring the model to act rather than withhold. Contrasting each dimension's median model against its best separates capability limits from choices: de-escalation reaches 74.6% in the strongest model against a median of 46.0%, and safety disclaimers 84.6% against 8.3%. Multi-turn conversations score below single-turn ones (68.5% against 74.2%). We release the corpus, rubrics, judge calibration labels, and a companion annex of 20 long-horizon red-team transcripts. These results argue for evaluating crisis safety on the care a model delivers rather than on what it declines to say, and for treating de-escalation and resource routing as deployment requirements.

</details>


### [226] [People Making Conversation: Evaluating How Naturally Speech Models Converse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12802)
*Arya Bastani,Iman Modarressi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估了六种实时对话语音系统在暂停和回合结束时的决策能力，发现全双工模型（PersonaPlex, Moshi）在保持沉默和中断方面表现更佳，而托管API模型（GPT Realtime, Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, Grok Voice）则倾向于立即回应。记忆能力方面，托管API模型在文本输入时表现稍好，但在音频输入时全双工模型表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话语音系统评估方法未能有效衡量系统在暂停时是否应该说话的决策能力，这项研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究人员在176次无脚本对话中的427个暂停和482个回合结束时部署了六种实时语音系统。系统接收到之前的对话历史，然后根据其生成内容与人类行为进行比较，评估指标包括：是否以及何时说话、延迟、长度、措辞，以及回答预设问题的能力。通过文本和音频两种方式输入对话历史。

Result: 托管API模型几乎在每个暂停时都会做出回应，其响应时间受限于端点检测（静默检测约1-4秒，语义回合检测约8秒）。全双工模型是唯一能定期保持沉默并能在人类反应时间内打断的系统。托管API模型的回应长度比人类更长。在记忆力方面，托管API模型在文本输入时回忆事实的比例为41-60%（由Claude Opus 5.5判断），音频输入时全双工模型回忆事实的比例为17-22%。

Conclusion: 全双工对话语音系统在模拟人类对话的暂停、沉默和中断行为方面表现优于托管API模型。当前托管API模型的局限性在于其无法有效模拟人类在暂停时的决策，倾向于立即回应。同时，记忆能力在不同输入模式下也存在差异，文本输入时托管API模型稍占优势，而音频输入时全双工模型表现更佳。研究发布了数据集、评估指标和所有系统输出，以促进未来的研究。

Abstract: A conversational speech model has to decide, at every pause, whether to speak. Existing evaluations test boundary detection, policy adherence, or aggregate statistics, not this decision. We place real-time speech systems at 427 pauses and 482 turn ends from 176 unscripted conversations in Seamless Interaction, give each the conversation so far, and score what it generates against what the person did: whether and how soon it speaks at a pause; its latency, length, and wording at a turn end; and whether it can answer a question about the preceding 30 to 120 seconds. Across six systems, the hosted APIs (GPT Realtime, Gemini 3.1 Flash Live, Grok Voice) respond at almost every pause whichever endpointer they use; only their latency, about 1 to 4 s with silence-based endpointers and about 8 s with semantic turn detection, decides whether the words land inside the pause. The full-duplex models (PersonaPlex, Moshi) are the only systems that regularly stay silent and the only ones that interrupt within a human reaction time. Hosted systems reply longer than the person. Given the history as text, hosted systems recall 41 to 60% of conversation facts under a Claude Opus 5.5 judge and 19 to 26% under a strict word matcher; hearing it as audio, full-duplex models recall 17 to 22% and 12 to 15%. We release the labels, points, harness, and every output.

</details>


### [227] [When Charts Deceive Web Agents: A Paired Benchmark for Misleading Visualization-Grounded Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12823)
*Yisheng Li,Dongming Han,Hongkun Pan,Bingcheng Mao,Zijian Wang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 MisVisAgentBench，一个用于评估误导性可视化如何影响 Web Agent 执行的基准测试。结果表明，误导性图表会显著降低 Web Agent 的任务成功率，并系统性地诱导其做出错误决策，凸显了可视化鲁棒性对于 Web Agent 安全性和可靠性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 Web Agent 在处理视觉信息时，尤其是图表，可能受到误导性可视化的影响，导致错误的下游操作。现有研究未能充分解决这一可靠性风险。

Method: 提出了 MisVisAgentBench，一个包含 280 个任务实例的配对基准测试，每个实例都有干净和误导性图表的配对。此外，还提供了一个诊断框架，用于区分误导性图表引起的失败和基线任务难度，并捕获执行层面的影响。

Result: 在 13 个视觉-语言 Web Agent 上进行测试，用误导性图表替换干净图表后，平均任务成功率下降了超过 34 个百分点。这些失败主要遵循了被误导的行动分支，表明欺骗性图表会系统性地重定向而非随机扰乱 Agent 的决策。

Conclusion: 可视化鲁棒性是 Web Agent 安全性和可靠性中一个关键但被低估的维度。MisVisAgentBench 证明了误导性可视化对 Web Agent 执行的负面影响，并强调了在 Agent 设计中解决此问题的必要性。

Abstract: Web agents increasingly browse, interpret, and act on web content on behalf of users. In many realistic workflows, however, the visual evidence they consume is not neutral: charts and dashboards can be misleading, shaping judgments without issuing any explicit malicious instruction. Unlike chart question answering, Web Agents treat charts as intermediate evidence whose misinterpretation can propagate into concrete downstream actions --- a reliability risk that remains underexplored. We introduce MisVisAgentBench, the first paired executable benchmark for evaluating how misleading visualizations affect Web Agent execution. It comprises 280 task instances in matched clean--misleading pairs that differ only in chart evidence. We further provide a diagnostic framework that isolates misleading-induced failures from baseline task difficulty (Zhu et al., 2025) and captures execution-level effects such as extra browsing steps and decision hesitation. Across thirteen vision-language Web Agents, replacing clean charts with misleading counterparts reduces the average task success rate by more than 34 percentage points. Moreover, these failures predominantly follow the targeted misleading action branch, suggesting that deceptive charts systematically redirect rather than randomly disrupt agent decisions. These results reveal that visualization robustness is a critical yet understudied dimension of Web Agent safety and reliability.

</details>


### [228] [COFFEQA: Counterfactual-Guided Few-Shot Distillation for LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12837)
*Kavinda Kehelella,Sanghamitra Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为COFFEQA的新型少样本知识蒸馏方法，通过引入反事实样本来增强模型在开放式问答任务中的表现，尤其是在处理接近决策边界的样本时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本知识蒸馏方法在选择训练样本时性能波动较大，特别是当选择的样本包含教师模型决策边界附近的“不确定”样本时，会导致蒸馏性能下降。作者希望通过一种更优的样本选择策略来解决这一问题。

Method: COFFEQA方法的核心是利用反事实样本。反事实样本是指通过对原始上下文进行微小修改，使得教师模型能够产生不同于原始答案的替代答案。文章提供了反事实样本如何约束师生模型决策边界偏差的几何解释，并引入了“上下文遵循难度”（CFD）度量来预验证反事实样本的有效性，确保其与预期替代答案的一致性以及对上下文的依赖性。最后，使用CFD验证过的反事实-事实样本对进行少样本蒸馏。

Result: 在五个同域和三个异域的问答基准测试中，COFFEQA在相同的少样本预算下，相比仅使用事实样本进行蒸馏的方法，性能有所提升。例如，在TriviaQA数据集上，使用32个少样本对时，T5模型使用COFFEQA比仅使用事实样本蒸馏平均EM得分提高了8.77，平均F1得分提高了9.98。

Conclusion: COFFEQA通过反事实样本的引入，为少样本知识蒸馏提供了一种有效的额外监督信号，尤其能够提升模型在处理具有挑战性的事实样本时的表现。该方法在多个问答任务和模型系列上均表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to train a smaller student model to mimic a larger teacher model for resource-constrained environments. However, the process of distillation is typically data-intensive and computationally expensive, particularly for generative tasks. Few-shot distillation offers an alternative by transferring teacher knowledge using limited training samples. However, we observe that few-shot distillation performance is heavily dependent on the choice of these training samples. In fact, we demonstrate that using more uncertain samples near the teacher's decision boundary can significantly deteriorate distillation performance. Motivated by this observation, we propose Counterfactual-guided Few-shot Distillation in Open-book Question Answering (COFFEQA), a novel strategy that aptly uses counterfactuals to provide additional supervision around challenging factual samples. In the generative QA setting, we define a counterfactual as a minimally modified context that induces the teacher to change its response to an alternative answer. We provide geometric insights on how factual-counterfactual pairs can constrain the deviation between the teacher's and student's decision boundaries. We further introduce a new metric, Context Following Difficulty (CFD) that further validates counterfactuals prior to distillation based on agreement with the intended alternative answer and dependence on the modified context. COFFEQA uses CFD-validated factual-counterfactual pairs for few-shot distillation. Experiments across five in-domain and three out-of-domain QA benchmarks using T5 and Qwen2.5 families show that COFFEQA outperforms factual-only distillation baselines under the same few-shot budget. For instance, on TriviaQA, COFFEQA improves over the factual-only baseline by 8.77 average EM points and 9.98 average F1 points under the T5 setting with 32 few-shot pairs.

</details>


### [229] [Fear Is Not Always Fear: Variation in Emotion Label Equivalence Across Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12841)
*Emily Öhman,Anna Koufakou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 跨数据集的情感分类模型在面对相同标签（如“恐惧”或“惊喜”）时，结果并不一致，表明训练数据对模型性能有显著影响，且简单的校准不足以解决所有问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者注意到，情感分类模型在训练数据之外的应用假设“恐惧”或“惊喜”等标签在不同资源中含义相同，但缺乏实证检验。因此，本文旨在验证这一假设，并分析导致不一致性的原因。

Method: 研究者比较了在GoEmotions, XED, 和 BRIGHTER三个数据集上的模型表现，使用了相同的输入数据，并进行了跨数据集迁移学习。通过分析模型在相同文本上的预测差异、标签流行度的影响以及采用拟合截距进行校准的方法，量化了模型性能的损失和不一致性。

Result: 在相同文本上，不同数据集训练的“恐惧”分类器预测结果差异巨大（71% vs 8%），但排名一致性较好。标签流行度解释了部分尺度不一致性，拟合截距可大部分消除尺度不一致。然而，跨数据集迁移模型会损失55%的判别力。对于“惊喜”标签，不同数据集甚至在哪些文本表达了该情感上存在分歧。在真实推断任务上，BRIGHTER和XED训练的模型在“惊喜”判断上得出相反结论。

Conclusion: 共享的情感标签名称并不能保证测量目标的等价性。训练数据的选择会显著影响情感分类模型的结论，跨数据集迁移会带来性能损失，且需要更深入的研究来解决标签含义在不同资源间的差异问题。

Abstract: Emotion classifiers are routinely applied beyond their training data, on the assumption that same-named labels such as fear or surprise mean the same thing across resources. We test this assumption for five labels shared by GoEmotions, XED, and BRIGHTER, comparing models on identical inputs and under cross-corpus transfer. On the same texts, one fear classifier flags 71% as fearful and another 8%, yet the two largely agree on how the texts rank. Across 30 resource-label comparisons, training-label prevalence explains about half of this kind of scale mismatch, and a fitted intercept removes most of it. What recalibration cannot repair is larger: transferred models lose 55% of native discrimination in aggregate, agreement in ranking between classifiers is only weakly related to that loss, and for surprise the resources disagree about which texts express the emotion. The choice of training resource can therefore change conclusions: on two external tweet collections, BRIGHTER- and XED-trained classifiers give opposite answers about which shows more surprise, even after rank normalization, with intervals excluding zero in both directions. A shared emotion label name does not establish an interchangeable measurement target.

</details>


### [230] [Unreliable Narrator Framing as a Cost-effective Mechanism for Satire Explanation of Australian and American Satirical News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12863)
*Kanay Gupta,David Y. Liu,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个利用不可靠叙述者理论来解释讽刺新闻（特别是来自澳大利亚和美国的）的新方法，并构建了一个新的数据集和评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讽刺新闻是一种独特的错误信息形式，其幽默意图可能被误解，而对其进行解释需要整合时事和地方背景信息，这具有挑战性。

Method: 该研究采用了一种基于不可靠叙述者理论的方法，并在开放权重模型上进行实验，以生成讽刺新闻文章的解释，并将其与来自美国和澳大利亚的例子进行比较。

Result: 研究表明，在开放权重模型上应用不可靠叙述者理论可以作为一种可行且经济高效的替代方法。模型识别美国讽刺新闻来源的讽刺手法比澳大利亚来源更准确。

Conclusion: 基于不可靠叙述者理论的方法在解释讽刺新闻方面是可行的，并且为讽刺解释生成创建了一个新的数据集和评估基准。此外，还发现了模型在处理不同国家/地区的讽刺内容时存在准确性差异。

Abstract: Satirical news is a unique form of misinformation: it is intended to be humorous by the original publication source but can be misinterpreted. Explanation of satirical news articles is challenging because recent and local context needs to be incorporated. This is a pilot study that explores explanation generation for satirical news articles from Australia and compares them alongside those from USA. The paper demonstrates that framing explanations on unreliable narrator theory on open-weights models can be a feasible, cost-effective alternative to frontier models. We find that models identify the satirical tropes more accurately on the American source than the Australian one. This work results in a novel dataset and an evaluation benchmark for satirical explanation.

</details>


### [231] [Time Delay System for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12864)
*Youshan Zhang,Jialu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非线性时延系统的新型序列模型框架，通过动态估计语义相似性来实现内容感知的时间记忆，并在此基础上构建了一种可解释的模糊语义调制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型（如 LSTM、Transformer、Mamba）在内容感知的时间记忆方面存在局限性，要么丢失历史信息，要么计算成本高昂，或缺乏明确的可寻址记忆。

Method: 引入了一种基于非线性时延系统的新型框架，包含：1. 可微分的语义滞后估计器，通过软注意力计算历史输入的加权上下文和相似度；2. 具有闭式累积和解的对角状态空间，实现 O(1) 的每步复杂度；3. 可解释的模糊语义调制机制，学习上下文相关的规则来平衡即时处理和历史回忆。

Result: 在文本分类和生成任务上取得了优于现有最先进方法的实验结果。

Conclusion: 该新型框架通过内容感知的时间记忆和可解释的模糊语义调制，有效克服了现有序列模型的局限性，并在多项任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Existing sequence models share a critical limitation: they lack an explicit, interpretable mechanism for content-aware temporal recall. LSTMs compress everything into a single hidden state, thereby losing information about specific past events. Transformers attend to all tokens indiscriminately at quadratic cost, while state-space models like Mamba compress history into a fixed-dimensional state without explicit content-addressable memory. To address this gap, we introduce a novel framework grounded in nonlinear time-delay systems, in which the effective delay is dynamically estimated based on the semantic similarity between current and historical inputs. Our approach features three key innovations: (i) a differentiable semantic lag estimator that computes content-aware soft attention weights over history, producing a weighted historical context and similarity score; (ii) a diagonal state space with closed-form cumulative sum solution, achieving O(1) per-step complexity with no sequential loops; and (iii) an interpretable fuzzy semantic modulation mechanism that learns context-dependent rules for balancing instantaneous processing versus historical recall, where rule centers correspond to prototypical semantic contexts. Experimental results on text classification and generation outperform state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [232] [Temporal Evidence Forests for Long-Horizon Multi-Party Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12874)
*Junyou Guo,Chongyang Tao,Yan He,Yong Wang,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了T-Mem，一种基于时间结构的长时记忆框架，通过时间证据发现和时间记忆构建来组织信息，并在多方长期交互任务中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆框架在处理多方长期交互时，未能有效利用信息的时序结构来恢复局部证据和重建事件进程，导致信息处理效率低下。

Method: T-Mem 框架包含三个主要机制：1. 时间证据发现（Temporal Evidence Discovery）利用信息局部聚集性发现相关证据；2. 时间记忆构建（Temporal Memory Structuring）建模状态转移和支持关系，压缩低信息段落；3. 证据森林序列化（Evidence Forest Linearization）将结构化证据序列化以生成答案。

Result: 在两个多方长期记忆基准测试中，T-Mem 在不同的大型语言模型、参数设置、领域和查询类型下，均一致优于竞争性记忆框架。

Conclusion: T-Mem 框架通过有效利用交互的时序结构，显著提升了在多方长期交互场景下长时记忆的性能，为LLM智能体提供了更强的记忆和推理能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory has been widely adopted in LLM-based agents to support persistent interaction and personalized assistance. Long-term multi-party interactions, however, accumulate substantially more detailed information, with decisions repeatedly updated and states evolving across participants and interaction stages. Temporal structure is therefore crucial for tracing such memories: relevant information often appears densely within short intervals, while transitions among different states are anchored by a strict temporal order. Existing temporally aware memory frameworks primarily store time as metadata or use temporal signals to guide retrieval over preconstructed memory structures, without using temporal structure to both recover locally concentrated evidence and reconstruct its complete event progression. To address these limitations, we propose T-Mem, a temporal-structure-based memory framework that preserves interactions and organizes query-relevant evidence through two temporal mechanisms. Temporal Evidence Discovery exploits the local concentration of related messages to recover overlooked evidence, while Temporal Memory Structuring models state transitions and supporting relations over time and compresses low-information spans within the resulting forest. Evidence Forest Linearization then serializes the structured evidence for answer generation. Experiments on two long-term multi-party memory benchmarks demonstrate that T-Mem consistently outperforms competitive memory frameworks across LLMs, parameter settings, domains, and query types.

</details>


### [233] [Rethinking the Roles of Diffusion and Decoding: Predictive-State Flows for Parallel Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12898)
*Jinjin He,Xinsong Feng,Lucheng Fu,Sinan Wang,Bo Zhu,Jiayun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为预测状态流 (PSF) 的新方法，用于改进连续扩散语言模型 (DLM)。PSF 将生成任务分解为两个阶段：扩散先验生成高层次的预测状态，解码器负责在每个块内解决具体的 token 选择。实验表明，PSF 在生成性能（困惑度）和效率（FLOPs）上均优于现有方法，并强调了在 DLM 中划分生成角色和设计潜在表征的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续扩散语言模型在生成文本时，扩散先验和解码器之间的角色划分不明确，导致在连续空间中建模离散的 token 选择变得困难，影响了模型性能。作者认为应该将生成任务分解，让扩散先验专注于高层次预测信息，解码器负责低层次的 token 选择。

Method: 提出预测状态流 (PSF) 模型，该模型将序列划分为块，并为每个块计算一个仅依赖于先前上下文的预测状态。一个修正流先验生成预测状态序列，然后一个块因果解码器并行生成所有块，仅在每个块内部进行自回归。这减少了扩散过程的状态数量和序列深度。

Result: 在 OpenWebText 数据集上，PSF 模型（1.97 亿参数，K=8）实现了 22.8 的生成困惑度和 5.16 的熵，仅需 0.31 TFLOPs/1024 token。相比之下，ELF-B（32 采样步）困惑度为 24.0，需要 7.2 TFLOPs。PSF 的 MAUVE@512 达到 0.93，而 ELF-B 为 0.73。额外的实验表明，包含自身块信息的“内容状态”比“预测状态”能提高 8-16% 的困惑度，并且使用共享均值替换采样轨迹会降低 MAUVE@512。

Conclusion: PSF 模型通过有效划分扩散先验和解码器的生成角色，显著提高了连续扩散语言模型的生成性能和效率。研究结果表明，在设计连续 DLM 的潜在表征时，不仅要考虑文本的紧凑表示，还要关注生成角色的合理划分。

Abstract: In continuous diffusion language models (DLMs), latents generated by the diffusion prior are mapped almost deterministically to tokens, either by a decoder or through a token embedding matrix. Unlike in image generation, where visual details can vary continuously, the diffusion prior must capture fine-grained discrete token choices in continuous space, which can make the joint latent distribution difficult to model. We argue that generative roles should instead be divided: the diffusion prior should generate high-level predictive information, while the decoder should resolve the remaining token-level choices. Based on this view, we introduce Predictive-State Flows (PSF), which partitions a sequence into blocks and associates each block with a predictive state computed solely from the preceding context. A rectified-flow prior generates the sequence of predictive states, after which a block-causal decoder generates all blocks in parallel, autoregressively only within each block. This design reduces diffusion from L token-level states to K << L predictive states and reduces token-level sequential depth to L/K. On unconditional OpenWebText generation, a 197M-parameter model with K=8 achieves a generative perplexity of 22.8 at entropy 5.16 using 0.31 TFLOPs per 1024-token document, versus 24.0 and 7.2 TFLOPs for ELF-B with 32 sampling steps. The corresponding measured operating points yield MAUVE@512 of 0.93 and 0.73, respectively. Controlled comparisons further show that content states, which additionally encode their own blocks, yield 8-16% higher generative perplexity than predictive states, and that replacing sampled predictive-state trajectories with a shared mean lowers MAUVE@512 by 0.11. These results suggest that latent design for continuous DLMs should consider not only how compactly text is represented, but also how generative roles are divided between the prior and the decoder.

</details>


### [234] [SEEDS: Preview-Guided Evidence Admission for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12932)
*Jiajin Liu,Chenxi Xu,Dongzhe Fan,Yuanfu Sun,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SEEDS框架，用于解决现有搜索代理中检索证据与消费证据混淆的问题。SEEDS通过轻量级预览和联合决策，使代理能够根据当前推理状态选择性地摄取检索到的证据，从而提高搜索效率并减少不必要的证据消费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理在检索证据和消费证据之间存在混淆，通常采用固定的或基于排名的策略来处理检索到的文档。然而，证据的效用与代理当前的推理状态有关，这可能导致低排名但关键的证据被忽略，或高排名但冗余/无关/误导性的证据被采纳。因此，需要一种机制来选择性地控制哪些检索到的证据应该进入代理的推理过程。

Method: 本文提出SEEDS（Selective Evidence Entry During Search）框架，一个预览引导的证据接纳框架。SEEDS首先为检索到的候选证据提供轻量级预览，然后让代理根据其当前的推理状态，联合决定哪些候选证据以及多少数量的证据应该被完全检查。

Result: 在九个知识密集型QA基准测试中，无论是在本地语料库还是实时网络搜索下，SEEDS都持续提高了搜索的有效性，同时显著减少了对全部证据的检查。实验分析表明，SEEDS比传统的检索前缀策略更能有效地选择有用的候选证据。

Conclusion: 证据接纳是代理搜索的一个重要补充维度，它将信息检索与最终用于指导推理的信息区分开来。SEEDS框架能够根据代理的推理状态动态选择性地摄取证据，从而提升代理的搜索效率和决策质量。

Abstract: Search agents extend large reasoning models with multi-round access to external knowledge, yet most existing systems largely conflate retrieving evidence with consuming it. Retrieved documents are commonly incorporated according to fixed or rank-based policies, despite the fact that evidence utility is conditioned on the agent's current reasoning state. Consequently, a lower-ranked candidate may be critical for the current step, while a higher-ranked candidate may provide redundant, irrelevant, or misleading information. We formulate this overlooked problem as evidence admission, which asks, at each search step, which retrieved candidates should be allowed to enter the agent's evolving reasoning context. To enable such selective control, we introduce SEEDS (Selective Evidence Entry During Search), a preview-guided evidence admission framework for search agents. SEEDS first exposes lightweight previews of retrieved candidates and then lets the agent jointly determine which candidates and how many should be fully inspected, conditioned on its current reasoning state. Unlike fixed or adaptive prefix policies, SEEDS can select arbitrary subsets with flexible cardinality, and it can be instantiated directly with an existing reasoning agent or further optimized through learning. Across nine knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks under both local corpus and live web search, SEEDS consistently improves search effectiveness while substantially reducing full-evidence consumption. Experimental analyses show that SEEDS more effectively selects useful candidates beyond the corresponding retrieval prefix. These results establish evidence admission as a complementary dimension of agentic search, separating what information is retrieved from what information is ultimately allowed to shape reasoning.

</details>


### [235] [Surfing the Activation Wave: Analyzing the Information and Geometry of LLM Hidden States for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12939)
*Raghavv Goel,Sudhanshu Agrawal,Mukul Gagrani,Risheek Garrepalli,Mingu Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息量和多样性原则的策略，用于在推测解码中选择大型语言模型（LLM）的多个隐藏状态层。通过熵和CKA分析，该方法比固定策略更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码方法通常依赖于固定的、基于位置的启发式方法来选择目标模型层，这可能无法捕捉到不同层中互补的信息。最优层选择是一个组合问题，难以穷尽搜索。

Method: 提出了一种基于信息量（使用矩阵熵分析）和多样性（使用居中核对齐 CKA）的原则性框架来选择多层隐藏状态。研究了层级香农熵、隐藏状态连接下的聚合熵以及跨深度的成对 CKA 动态。引入了“token-entropy filtering”来提高层选择统计数据的质量。

Result: 所选的隐藏状态层集在 EAGLE3 推测解码中优于固定的基线选择（包括默认的 [2, N//2, N-3] 规则）。实验结果揭示了 LLM 解码器表示中的几何和信息分隔。

Conclusion: 该研究为推测解码的多层隐藏状态选择提供了一个实用的方法，并加深了对 LLM 解码器状态几何和信息结构的理解。该框架也可用于相关应用。

Abstract: Decoder hidden states from different layers of large language models (LLMs) often encode complementary information. However, existing speculative decoding methods typically rely on a small set of target-model layers chosen using fixed, position-based heuristics. Selecting an optimal subset of layers is inherently a combinatorial problem, making exhaustive evaluation infeasible even for moderately deep models. In this work, we present a principled framework for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding based on two complementary criteria: informativeness, measured through matrix-entropy analysis, and diversity, quantified using centered kernel alignment (CKA). We investigate layer-wise Shannon entropy, aggregate entropy under hidden-state concatenation, and pairwise CKA dynamics across depth, revealing distinct geometric and informational compartments within decoder representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose entropy and CKA-based layer selection algorithms that favor hidden-state sets that are both highly informative and minimally redundant. As part of this framework, we introduce token-entropy filtering, which removes hidden states associated with low predictive-entropy tokens, thereby improving the quality of layer-selection statistics. In EAGLE3-style speculative decoding, our selected sets outperform fixed baseline choices, including the default $[2, N//2, N-3]$ rule, while providing a clearer explanation of why some defaults can remain competitive. Our results provide a practical recipe for multi-layer hidden-state selection for speculative decoding, insights into the geometry and information in LLM decoder states, and a foundation for extending similar analysis to related applications.

</details>


### [236] [Forecasting Corpus-Sampling Disagreement in Masked Text Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12987)
*Hare Krishna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个用于预测文本重建模型在不同语料库上性能变化的数学公式，并验证了其有效性，发现语料库规模对预测误差的影响比理论预测的要小，且存在校准误差，可以通过拔刀法等技术进行修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者想要量化由于数据采样偏差（即从同一人群中收集的两个独立文本语料库）导致文本重建预测器输出的变化程度，并了解这种变化是如何随语料库大小而变化的。

Method: 研究者开发了一个基于相邻标记对计数的平滑预测器，并推导了一个一阶公式来估计拟合到两个独立语料库的预测器之间的预期不一致性。该公式考虑了文档长度、随机上下文计数和跨标记偏移的相关性。他们还在 TinyStories、WikiText-103 和 CNN/DailyMail 数据集上进行了实验，并使用源拆分拔刀法和蒙特卡洛模拟来评估和校准预测。

Result: 在 TinyStories、WikiText-103 和 CNN/DailyMail 数据集上，将语料库大小从 512 增加到 2,048 个 64 标记的块，不一致性仅降低了 5-40%，远低于理论预测的 75%。研究发现，预测不一致性的变化主要归因于罕见上下文的平滑处理。校准误差可以通过源拆分拔刀法减少 57-59%。在校准后，当参考语料库小于目标语料库时，简单的平滑多项式近似最为准确；当参考语料库大于目标语料库时，非线性源自举法最为准确。基于计数的预测在一定程度上可以识别对语料库最敏感的输入，但无法捕捉神经网络模型不一致性下降的速度。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的公式能够有效预测文本重建模型在不同语料库上的性能变化，并揭示了语料库规模对预测误差的影响不如理论预测的那样显著，这主要归因于上下文的平滑处理。此外，通过拔刀法等校准技术可以显著提高预测的准确性，并且不同规模的参考语料库会影响最优预测方法的选择。

Abstract: If a second text corpus were collected from the same population, how much would a predictor's outputs change? We study this question for masked-text reconstruction using a smoothed predictor built from nearby token-pair counts and evaluated over the full 50,257-token GPT-2 vocabulary. Treating each source document as one observation, we derive a first-order formula for the expected disagreement between predictors fitted to two independent corpora. The formula accounts for variable document length, random context counts, and correlations across token offsets, and it can be evaluated from a separate reference corpus without access to either target corpus. On TinyStories, WikiText-103, and CNN/DailyMail, growing each corpus from 512 to 2,048 chunks of 64 tokens lowers disagreement by only 5--40\%, far short of the 75\% implied by $1/n$ scaling. The forecast tracks this slow decline, which we trace to smoothing of rarely seen contexts. Forecast accuracy depends less on how these correlations are modeled than on the size of the reference corpus: small references systematically underpredict disagreement. A source-split jackknife that uses only reference data reduces this calibration error by 57--59\%. After this correction, a simpler smoothed-multinomial approximation is most accurate when the reference corpus is smaller than the target corpus, and a nonlinear source bootstrap is most accurate when it is larger. Finally, small masked diffusion language models trained on the same corpora show that the count-based forecasts partly identify which inputs are most sensitive to the corpus, but miss the neural models' much faster decline in disagreement.

</details>


### [237] [Language Models Have Interpretable Narrative Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12989)
*David Yiyang Liu,Aditya Joshi,Paul Dawson,Andrew Piper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现 GPT-2, Gemma-2, Llama-3.1 等语言模型内部组件（如注意力头、MLP 神经元、稀疏自编码器特征）与文本的叙事性相关。移除这些组件会影响模型对高叙事性文本的预测，并降低其生成的故事叙事性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型内部是否存在专门处理叙事性的组件。

Method: 通过分析注意力头、MLP 神经元和稀疏自编码器特征的激活与文本叙事性的相关性，并进行消融实验，观察移除特定组件对模型预测损失和生成内容的影响，同时在下游任务（故事补全、叙事问答、混淆、幻觉）上进行评估。

Result: 识别出与叙事性相关的模型内部组件，并且消融这些组件会增加高叙事性文本的预测损失，降低生成故事的叙事性。在故事补全和叙事问答任务上效果最显著。

Conclusion: 现有语言模型中的部分组件确实对叙事性有贡献，但这些组件的内部组织、与叙事理论的关系以及更广泛的功能仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate whether language models contain internal components that contribute to representing narrativity, the degree to which a text exhibits narrative properties. Across GPT-2-Small, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B, we identify attention heads, MLP neurons, and sparse autoencoder features whose activations correlate with narrativity. Ablating selected neuron and feature sets increases next-token prediction loss more for high-narrativity than for low-narrativity texts, and reduces the narrativity of open-ended generations. On downstream benchmarks, the largest effects occur in story completion and narrative question answering, although they vary across models and languages; results on confabulation and hallucination benchmarks are mixed. These findings provide causal evidence that selected sets of narrative-associated components contribute to model behaviour, while their internal organisation, relation to narrative theory, and broader functions remain open questions.

</details>


### [238] [What Should SFT Learn? Conditional Information Gain for Generalizable Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12995)
*Yuxuan Gu,Kequan Niu,Xiaocheng Feng,Yun Li,Yiqiao Yang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 CIG-SFT 的新框架，通过估计演示数据中每个 token 的信息量来改进大型语言模型的监督微调，以提高模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督微调（SFT）方法可能因为强迫模型模仿外部演示而干扰预训练的效果，限制了模型的泛化能力。虽然动态 SFT 方法通过重新加权 token 级别监督来缓解这个问题，但模型兼容性并不等同于学习价值，可能误判需要学习的关键信息。

Method: CIG-SFT 是一种信息论框架，它通过比较给定任务输入时和不给定任务输入时参考模型对演示 token 的似然度来估计任务条件信息增益（CIG）。该方法将信息量转化为当前策略的 KL 正则化投影，并推导出一个等效的标量加权 SFT 目标。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，CIG-SFT 的性能优于标准 SFT 和其他自适应微调方法，尤其在面对具有挑战性的评估分布时，泛化能力得到了显著提升，且无需额外训练数据或在线采样。

Conclusion: CIG-SFT 是一种有效的信息论框架，能够通过量化演示数据中的信息量来优化监督微调过程，从而提高大型语言模型的泛化能力，特别是在处理需要学习特定任务决策的场景时。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) remains a widely used approach for post-training large language models, because fixed demonstrations can directly introduce desired reasoning strategies and behavioral patterns. Yet forcing a model to imitate externally provided trajectories may also interfere with behaviors already supported by pretraining, limiting generalization beyond the training distribution.
