TL;DR: 研究提出了一种多任务预训练方法，通过结合句子预测、知识图谱引导聚类和知识图谱感知段落分类，来增强法律文本理解中的结构化推理能力，并在德国交通法领域取得了显著的零样本推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律语言模型仅依赖于掩码语言模型，将法规文本视为扁平的词序列，忽略了其固有的层级结构，导致在法律文本理解和推理方面存在不足。

Method: 提出了一种多任务预训练方法，结合了三种互补的目标：句子预测（NSP）、知识图谱（KG）引导聚类（CLU）和知识图谱（KG）感知段落分类（CLS），旨在将结构化法律知识直接编码到模型权重中。

Result: 在德国交通法问答任务上，相对于基线模型，该方法取得了10.3%到155.0%的相对性能提升。在七个法律基准测试上，优于先前的LexGLUE基线模型。在奥地利驾驶法规上，实现了3.4%到15.8%的零样本迁移增益，表明该方法具有跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多任务预训练方法能够有效地将结构化法律知识整合到语言模型中，显著提升了法律文本的零样本推理能力，并且在不同法律领域之间表现出良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Legal language understanding requires reasoning over semi-structured regulatory documents, yet standard pre-training objectives do not explicitly target this structural capability. Because current legal language models rely solely on masked language modeling, they treat statutory text as flat token sequences and ignore the underlying hierarchical constraints. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-task pre-training approach for German traffic law (StVO). Our approach combines three complementary objectives: Next Sentence Prediction (NSP), Knowledge Graph (KG) Guided Clustering (CLU), and Knowledge Graph (KG) Aware Paragraph Classification (CLS). This approach explicitly encodes structured legal knowledge directly into the model weights. This encoding enables zero-shot legal reasoning at inference time and removes the need for discrete graph traversal. We evaluate our approach across nine Transformer-based encoders and 324 experimental runs. On synthetic StVO question answering, our models demonstrate consistent relative improvements between 10.3% and 155.0% over baselines. Furthermore, relative to the established LexGLUE baselines (Chalkidis et al., 2022), our models improve over prior results on four of seven legal benchmarks. It also achieves consistent zero-shot transfer gains between 3.4% and 15.8% on Austrian driving regulations. These experimental results indicate that our training approach generalizes successfully beyond the initial training legal domain.

</details>


### [275] [Not Just an Evaluation: A Case Study of Discourse Particle Sensitivity in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13794)
*William Sheffield,Jiahui Chen,Amy Zhang,Alex Warstadt,Junyi Jessy Li,Kanishka Misra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了大型语言模型（LLMs）对英语副词“just”和“only”在自然对话中的敏感性，发现LLMs在某种程度上模仿人类的偏好，并且指令微调能增强这种敏感性。通过分析模型内部，研究揭示了“just”和“only”的表示存在不对称的重叠，暗示LLM的词汇表示按意义组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于“just”和“only”这类语篇助词对语篇流动产生微妙影响且具有多功能性，常常与意义未被触及的语境和说话人态度相关，其在自然语言中频率很高但研究却很少。因此，本研究旨在调查LLMs对这些助词的敏感性。

Method: 本研究结合行为分析和机制分析，通过两个实验探究LLMs对“just”和“only”的敏感性。实验一比较了LLMs和人类在对话语境中对这些助词存在的偏好。实验二通过比较带有和不带这些助词的模型采样，评估LLMs对助词的敏感性。此外，还评估了指令微调对敏感性的影响，并通过定位模型内部对助词敏感的单元来分析其表示。

Result: 1. LLMs和人类在语境中都更倾向于出现“just”和“only”这两种助词，且两者都对“just”比对“only”更敏感。2. LLMs生成的文本在包含这些助词时，从其源语境来看，被认为更可能出现。3. 指令微调在两个实验中都提高了LLMs对这些助词的敏感性。4. 模型内部的分析显示，“only”的敏感单元可以转移到“just”的排他性用法，而“just”的所有敏感单元都能等同地转移到“only”的各种用法，表明LLM的词汇表示按意义进行了部分组织。

Conclusion: LLMs对“just”和“only”等语篇助词表现出一定的敏感性，并且这种敏感性可以通过指令微调得到增强。模型内部的分析揭示了“just”和“only”的表示存在不对称的重叠，说明LLMs的词汇层面表示可能受到意义的组织。这些发现有助于理解LLMs如何处理微妙的语言现象，并为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Discourse particles subtly shape the flow of discourse by reflecting conversational context and interlocutor attitudes, often leaving the truth-conditional meaning untouched. They are also often polyfunctional, with distinct senses which partially overlap with other particles; for example, the exclusive use of English "just" and "only" ("John only/just drinks water"). Despite the frequency of these words in natural language, they have gone largely unstudied due to the subtle nature of their effects. We investigate LLM sensitivity to "just" and "only" in natural conversations using behavioral and mechanistic analyses. First, we find that LLMs and humans similarly prefer the presence of these particles in context, and both are more sensitive to "just" compared to "only". Second, when sampling from LLMs with and without these particles, we find LLMs consistently rate generations as more likely, given their source context, demonstrating their particle sensitivity. In both experiments, instruction tuning increases sensitivity. Finally, we localize particle-sensitive units within LLMs and find the overlap between "just" and "only" is asymmetric: "only" units transfer selectively to exclusive "just", while all "just" units transfer to "only" equally well regardless of sense, suggesting LLM lexical-level particle representations are partially organized by sense. We release our code, sense-labeled dialogue stimuli, and human preference annotations at https://github.com/JiahuiKChen/Not_Just_an_Evaluation.

</details>


### [276] [REFLECT: Role-Aware Detection of Sentiment, Emotion, Politeness, and Formality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13797)
*Sangpil Youm,Annabel Stocks Natalias,Hayden Heninger,Leah Jones,Xiaoyang Jin,Xiaoya Zhang,Bonnie Dorr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为“角色感知Transformer”（Role-aware Transformer）的新型模型，该模型通过将语义角色标签（SRL）整合到Transformer的自注意力机制中，来提升对对话中情感、情绪、礼貌和正式度（SEPF）等交流意义的检测能力。实验表明，该模型在情感、礼貌和正式度方面优于现有方法，特别是在处理低频复杂情感和高度礼貌表达时效果显著，而对情感的提升效果不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话意义检测方法主要依赖上下文语言表示，忽略了参与者角色结构在传达情感、情绪、礼貌和正式度等交流意义方面的重要性。本研究旨在通过显式地建模这些角色结构来改进交流意义的检测。

Method: 提出了一种角色感知Transformer架构，将SEPF维度映射到SRL配置，并将其整合到Transformer的自注意力机制中。其中，角色感知注意力用于建模内部状态（情感、情绪），而定向角色感知注意力用于捕捉外部线索（礼貌、正式度）的参与者关系。通过一个学习到的门控机制将角色感知表示与上下文嵌入融合。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，该框架在情感、礼貌和正式度检测方面取得了统计学上的可靠提升，尤其在理论预测的场景下（如低频复杂情感、高度礼貌表达）表现更佳。对情感的提升效果不明显，表明情感更多通过词汇选择而非角色结构传达。在真实世界数据上的评估也显示，该框架提高了预测性能和模型的可追溯性。

Conclusion: 语言学理论指导下对Transformer自注意力机制的修改可以有效提升交流意义的检测能力，并且泛化能力强，能够从基准数据集扩展到真实世界数据，同时还能提供可追溯的、基于角色的解释，从而提升模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Detecting communicative meaning in dialogue requires modeling not only lexical content but also the participant-role structures through which sentiment, emotion, politeness, and formality (SEPF) are expressed. Existing approaches rely on contextual language representations while leaving semantic roles implicit. We introduce a role-aware transformer architecture that maps the four SEPF dimensions to theory-grounded Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) configurations, incorporating these configurations directly into the transformer's self-attention mechanism. Role-aware attention models non-directional internal states (sentiment and emotion), while directional role-aware attention captures initiator-to-recipient relations for external cues (politeness and formality). The resulting role-aware representations are fused with contextual embeddings through a learned gate that adaptively weights how much role information each input contributes, rather than appending semantic roles as an auxiliary feature vector. Across four benchmark datasets, the framework yields statistically reliable gains over strong BERT-based baselines for emotion, politeness, and formality, with gains concentrated in theoretically predicted cases such as low-frequency, complex emotions and highly polite expressions; sentiment improvements are marginal and non-significant, suggesting that sentiment is conveyed more through lexical choice than participant-role structure. Additional evaluation on real-world data demonstrates that the framework improves both predictive performance and model traceability. These results demonstrate that linguistically grounded modifications to transformer self-attention improve the detection of communicative meaning, generalize beyond benchmark datasets to real-world mental-health data, and yield traceable, role-grounded explanations that extend beyond predictive performance to interpretability.

</details>


### [277] [Error Rate is Not Enough: Costs of Augmentation in Low-Resource ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13807)
*Katsumi Ibaraki,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，针对低资源语音识别，结合音频增强（速度扰动、SpecAugment）和文本增强（文本转语音）可以产生互补的错误修正效果，尤其是在低资源场景下，并且这种互补性可以通过训练数据中的暴露度和声学特性来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据增强方法在低资源语音识别中的效益通常只以总体错误率报告，未能解释其具体作用机制和适用场景。研究旨在深入分析不同增强方法的作用方式，以及它们如何相互补充以提升识别性能。

Method: 在四种低资源语言和英语的五种不同数据规模下，研究人员将两种音频增强方法（速度扰动和SpecAugment）与两种基于文本转语音的增强方法进行组合实验。通过分析音素级别的错误，比较不同增强方法的互补性及其对特定错误类型（如声调和腭化）的影响。

Result: SpecAugment 在总体错误率上表现最稳定，但会增加声调和腭化错误。文本增强方法则会引入辅音插入错误。然而，当两种方法结合使用时，它们能够纠正彼此引入的错误，从而获得比单独使用时更显著的性能提升。这种互补性在训练数据量小于 100 小时时尤为明显。

Conclusion: 对于需要关注特定错误类型的低资源语音识别任务，建议根据训练数据暴露度和声学特性来选择和组合数据增强方法。这种基于可测量特征的策略可以在模型训练之前进行，以最大化增强效果。

Abstract: Data augmentation is standard practice for low-resource automatic speech recognition (ASR), but its benefits are almost always reported as aggregate error rates, leaving open why and when a method helps. On four low-resource languages and English at five data sizes, we cross two augmentations that modify existing audio (speed perturbation and SpecAugment) with two that modify text transcriptions and synthesize audio from it. We analyze errors at the phoneme level and find that they help for complementary reasons. SpecAugment has the most reliable aggregate improvements, but increases tone and palatalization errors, whereas text-based methods introduce consonant insertions; in combination, each reduces the errors the other introduces. This complementarity is predictable. Tone and palatalization are each signaled by a single acoustic property and occur least frequently in training (lowest training exposure), and text-based methods that flatten word frequency raise their frequency by 37--38%, reversing those errors while further improving aggregate error. The improvement from combining the methods exceeds the sum of their individual improvements; the superadditivity vanishes by 100 hours, limiting the complementarity to the low-resource regime. Where specific error types matter, we recommend selecting augmentation by training exposure and acoustic properties, both measurable before any training.

</details>


### [278] [Can Language Models Learn When to Abstain? Policy Learning and Calibrated Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13821)
*Juan Terven,Diana-Margarita Córdova-Esparza,Ruben Gomez,Carlos Miranda Rocha,Julio-Alejandro Romero-González,Edgar Chavez-Urbiola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，通过训练一个独立的答案选择器组件，可以提高语言模型在回答事实性问题时何时回答、何时表示“不知道”的能力，尤其是在TriviaQA数据集上，显著提高了覆盖率并降低了错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型需要知道何时回答问题，何时表示“不知道”。研究的动机是探索如何通过训练模型本身或一个独立的检查组件来改进这个决策过程。

Method: 研究人员在Qwen3-14B模型上，针对事实性问题，测试了两种方法：1. 训练模型本身；2. 训练一个独立的答案选择器组件来检查答案。他们使用一个奖励正确答案、惩罚错误答案并允许弃权的评分机制。在TriviaQA和NQ-Open数据集上进行了实验，并对结果进行了量化分析，包括在不同置信度阈值下的效用、覆盖率和错误率。还进行了部分人工审核。

Result: 在TriviaQA数据集上，训练出的答案选择器比原始模型的置信度过滤方法平均提高了效用，平均覆盖率从20.2%提高到30.9%，错误率从23.0%降低到14.5%。在NQ-Open数据集上，选择器虽然提高了效用，但仍不如总是弃权。强化学习的改变未能带来一致的提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持在特定场景下学习选择答案的方法，但表明在不同数据集上实现可靠的弃权机制仍是一个未解决的问题。

Abstract: A useful language model should know when to answer and when to say "I don't know." Can we improve that decision by training the model itself or by training a separate component to check its answers? We study both approaches on Qwen3-14B for factual questions, using a score that rewards correct answers, penalizes mistakes, and allows abstention. Across three training seeds and two held-out confidence thresholds, a learned answer selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the original model's calibrated confidence filter. On TriviaQA, mean coverage increases from 20.2% to 30.9%, while the pooled error rate among scored answers decreases from 23.0% to 14.5% under exact-match grading. The tested reinforcement-learning changes do not establish consistent gains. On NQ-Open, the selector improves threshold-averaged utility over the filter but still performs worse than always abstaining. A partial human-reviewed audit preserves the TriviaQA finding. Our results support learning to select answers in this setting while showing that reliable abstention across datasets remains unresolved.

</details>


### [279] [Task Context Shapes Component Roles in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13895)
*Sovesh Mohapatra,Dani S Bassett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过比较不同任务下大型语言模型（LLM）中共享注意力头的激活和影响，发现任务上下文会改变共享组件响应的图事实以及它们如何影响后续计算，为理解LLM如何利用共享参数执行不同计算提供了网络视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现LLM在不同任务间会复用相同的注意力头，但并未解释这些注意力头在不同任务中的具体作用差异。受大脑中任务依赖性脑区交互的启发，作者希望从网络计算的角度来理解LLM的行为。

Method: 研究者使用相同的图事实（graph facts）来比较不同任务下固定模型组件（主要是注意力头）的表现。他们通过干预（interventions）来测量注意力头对激活状态（influence）和最终答案（importance）的影响。为了量化任务变化的影响，他们构建了线性响应图（linear response maps），用于预测某个早层注意力头的变化如何影响晚层注意力头的响应。他们还对比了干预后预测误差的减少程度。

Result: 在五种不同模型家族（参数量1.5B-14B）上，任务切换会重新分配注意力头的“影响”。在Gemma-2-9B和Qwen3-14B模型中，激活编辑改变了内部激活向量，并导致模型读取的图事实发生变化，但书面指令保持不变。相同的注意力头会响应不同的图事实，并对后续组件产生不同的影响。线性响应图能够有效预测晚层头部的响应，在Gemma和Qwen模型中，针对特定事实干预的响应图能显著降低预测误差。

Conclusion: 研究表明，任务上下文不仅改变了共享组件响应的图事实，还改变了它们输出对后续计算的影响方式。一个网络化的解释框架有助于理解LLM如何通过共享参数实现多样化的计算。此外，对任务依赖性影响的量化测量也可为定向干预模型行为提供指导。

Abstract: Large language models reuse the same attention heads across tasks, but identifying these shared heads does not explain what they do differently in each task. A natural source of inspiration is task-dependent interactions among shared brain regions, which motivate a network view of language-model computation. Here, we compare fixed model components across tasks using identical graph facts, statements specifying which directed connections are present. We measure how interventions affect attention heads and the activation state in later layers (influence), as well as the answer (importance). Task changes redistribute the influence in five open-weight models spanning four families and 1.5-14 billion parameters. In Gemma-2-9B and Qwen3-14B, an activation edit changes an early internal activation vector and switches which fact the model reads while the written instruction stays fixed. The same heads then respond to different graph facts and influence later components differently. For each prompt, we construct two linear response maps, one before the edit and one after it. Each map predicts a later head's response from a measured change in an earlier head. For a separate factual intervention, the map for the corresponding condition reduces mean squared prediction error by 53.6% in Gemma and 5.0% in Qwen compared with the average of the two maps. These findings show that task context changes both which graph facts the shared components respond to and how their outputs affect later computation. A network account may help explain how models use shared parameters to perform different computations. Measurements of task-dependent influence could also guide interventions intended to alter one behavior while preserving others.

</details>


### [280] [Structural Automata for Neural Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13896)
*Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为接受路径解码（APD）的新方法，用于解决神经语义解析器在生成过程中可能出现的结构不一致问题，该方法在DRS和AMR数据集上均取得了显著的性能提升，并且在解码、后处理和训练三个阶段都引入了结构自动化，以提高输出的有效性和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经语义解析器在生成过程中容易产生结构无效的输出，并且在有限的输出长度限制下，即使是局部有效的序列也可能无法成功完成。研究的动机是提高生成结构良好且可完全解析的语义表示的鲁棒性。

Method: 研究在DRS解析的三个阶段（解码、后处理和训练）引入了结构自动化。核心方法是解码阶段的接受路径解码（APD），它通过维护一个在剩余长度限制内可完成的已验证前缀来 Guarantees an accepted output without retracting tokens。此外，还评估了在AMR上的解码，并探讨了后处理和训练阶段的结构自动化应用。

Result: 在DRS数据集上，APD在严格的长度限制下，Smatch++比局部掩码提高了5.1和2.4个点。在AMR数据集上，APD提高了6.4和3.2个点，性能与回滚方法相当。在后处理阶段，过滤无效输出的方法在训练数据有限时，能显著减少无有效输出的输入比例，且性能接近APD。在训练阶段，通过惩罚自动化拒绝的token，可以提高开发集上的Smatch++分数，但这种优势会随着训练的进行而减弱。

Conclusion: 结构自动化在神经语义解析的多个阶段都能有效提高生成结构的有效性和完整性。接受路径解码（APD）是一种有效的解码策略，能在不回溯的情况下Guarantees an accepted output。通过在训练早期引入结构信息，可以显著提升模型性能，并且模型本身也能逐渐学习到避免生成无效结构。

Abstract: Neural semantic parsers generate meaning representations token by token, yet well-formedness depends on references, scope, and graph relations that unfold during generation. Constrained decoding prevents locally invalid tokens but does not guarantee that a valid prefix can be completed within a fixed output-length limit. We investigate structural automata at three stages of DRS parsing (decoding, post hoc processing, and training) and additionally evaluate decoding on AMR. For decoding, we introduce accepting-path decoding (APD), which maintains a verified completion within the remaining length limit and follows the model's preferred token whenever that token can also be completed. APD guarantees an accepted output without retracting tokens. Under tight length limits, it improves Smatch++ over local masking by 5.1 and 2.4 points on DRS and by 6.4 and 3.2 points on AMR, comparable to rollback under the same limit, which retracts tokens. For post hoc processing, invalid outputs concentrate on the same inputs: eight unconstrained samples yield no valid output for 10.2% of inputs with limited training data, compared with 0.4% with full data, where filtering comes within 0.2 Smatch++ points of APD. For training, penalizing automaton-rejected tokens improves development Smatch++ by 3.6-11.0 points after 10% of training, but this advantage diminishes as cross-entropy alone reduces the probability assigned to rejected tokens from 14% to below 2% within the first sixth of training with limited data.

</details>


### [281] [When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13910)
*Shubh Garg,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为EICS的度量标准和CASP-VL基准，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）内部表征对空间关系的编码忠实度。研究发现，VLMs在空间关系上的表征不如词汇信息忠实，即使在组合性基准测试中表现良好。这种关系信息在输入时存在，但在模型聚合为全局表示时会丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）在分析和推理图像方面取得了进展，但其内部表征是否能真正编码结构化关系以支持推理仍不清楚。研究者希望量化和揭示VLMs在处理空间关系方面的不足。

Method: 研究者提出了EICS（Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability）度量标准，用于测试表征在受控的单概念干预下是否一致地编码概念。同时，他们创建了CASP-VL（Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models）基准，该基准包含源自Visual Genome场景图的因果控制的空间扰动。

Result: 研究发现，与词汇信息相比，VLMs在空间关系上的表征忠实度显著较低。即使模型在组合性基准测试中得分高于随机水平，这种差异依然存在。进一步分析表明，关系信息在其谓词自身标记处是可识别的，但在通过（EOS）标记或平均池化聚合为全局表示时会丢失。

Conclusion: VLMs在其下游使用的表征中，并不能很好地保留输入中存在的空间关系信息。这种系统性的失败是基于输出的评估所无法察觉的，揭示了模型在理解和表征图像结构化关系方面的深层局限性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate's own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.

</details>


### [282] [RSI-Anything: Unlocking Recursive Self-Improvement with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13912)
*Taoyong Cui,Pinxian Zeng,Wanghan Xu,Hao Xiao,Shuhan Xue,Qiang Gao,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RSI-Anything 是一个领域通用的框架，利用预测性世界模型指导行动和学习数据选择，通过观察到的反馈和组件兼容性检查实现持续改进，在代码修复、GUI评估、实验室规划和反应优化等领域均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的递归自我改进（RSI）方法面临外部反馈成本高昂以及随着策略演变世界模型预测可能变得不可靠的挑战。

Method: RSI-Anything 框架基于 JEPA-Anything，利用预测性世界模型来指导行动和学习数据选择。通过观察到的反馈来改进策略或工作流，并条件性地调整世界模型。组件和兼容性检查用于管理更新后的策略-模型对的保留。

Result: 在代码修复中，成功率从静态验证器的 26.3% 提高到 29.2%（通过预测选择）和 31.2%（通过世界模型适应）。GUI 评估显示任务完成度更高，平均动作减少 39.4%。实验室规划得分翻倍，从 21.80% 提高到 46.01%。在反应优化中，RSI-Anything 在相同预算下达到 96.15% 的平均最佳观测产量，优于贝叶斯优化的 82.16%。

Conclusion: 预测性世界模型和保留的系统更新能够实现跨不同环境的递归改进，RSI-Anything 展示了其在通用计算和科学领域的有效性。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to transform interaction experience into persistent improvements, yet external feedback is costly and world-model predictions may become unreliable as policies evolve. We introduce RSI-Anything, a domain-general framework built on JEPA-Anything that uses predictive world models to guide action and learning-data selection. Observed feedback drives policy or workflow improvement and conditional world-model adaptation, while component and compatibility checks govern the retention of updated policy-model pairs. Across diverse computational and scientific domains, RSI-Anything demonstrates substantial gains. In code repair, with the coder fixed, success increases from 26.3% with a static verifier to 29.2% through predictive selection and 31.2% after world-model adaptation, at comparable test usage. Graphical user interface (GUI) evaluations showed higher task completion, while average actions decrease by 39.4%. Across five successive policy versions, laboratory-planning scores more than double from 21.80% to 46.01%. In reaction optimization, RSI-Anything achieves 96.15% mean best observed yield versus 82.16% for Bayesian optimization under the same 40-experiment budget. An end-to-end immune-science application further connects workflow evolution and predictive modeling with therapeutic hypothesis generation. These results demonstrate how predictive world models and retained system updates enable recursive improvement across diverse environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [Fly-by-Code: Embodied Coding Agents for Aerial Manipulation with Active Visual and Physical Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12549)
*Jaewoo Lee,Jeongyeon Seo,Sihyun Cho,Gyeongrak Choe,Yutong Wang,Bavin Saravanan,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Sebastian Scherer,Guanya Shi,H. Jin Kim,Seungjae Lee,Dongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为“主动反馈”（Active Feedback, AF）的框架，用于解决在空中操作任务中，由于部分可观测性和有限的交互鲁棒性导致的代码代理（coding agent）在程序精炼时面临的证据不足问题。AF通过在模拟环境中运行诊断程序，主动获取未观测到的结果或测试失败假设，从而提高任务的成功率和完成度评估的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码代理在通过执行反馈精炼程序时，假设反馈能够提供充分的信息来判断任务成功与否以及指导程序修正。然而，在空中操作任务中，由于载荷限制导致的相机视角不足（部分可观测性）和动态耦合强、控制裕度有限导致的物理交互脆弱性（有限交互鲁棒性），执行反馈往往不足以解决失败原因。这阻碍了代码代理的有效学习和精炼。

Method: 本文提出了主动反馈（AF）框架，它包含一个“观察和探测”（View and Probe）机制。当执行反馈存在未解决的问题时，代理会在模拟环境中合成一个诊断程序。该诊断程序可以通过“观察”机制从不同的视角检查未观测到的结果，或者通过“探测”机制进行有针对性的物理交互来测试关于失败原因的假设，从而获取缺失的证据。

Result: 在六个模拟的空中操作任务中，AF框架将错误完成率从25%降低到5%，并将任务最终成功率从31.7%提高到75%，显著优于仅依赖终端反馈的方法。此外，在模拟环境中用AF精炼的任务程序，无需修改源代码，即可在真实的空中操作机器人上成功完成多阶段操作任务。

Conclusion: 可执行诊断（executable diagnosis）可以弥补实体代码代理在空中操作等复杂场景下面临的执行反馈信息不足的问题，从而提升其完成度评估的准确性和程序精炼的能力。AF框架为解决此类问题提供了一种有效的方法，并成功验证了其在模拟和真实环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied coding agents construct robot policies from natural-language instructions by generating executable programs and refining them from execution feedback. This refinement assumes that execution feedback reveals whether the task succeeded and, after a failure, provides sufficient evidence to guide program revision. Aerial manipulation violates this assumption in two ways. Payload constraints restrict onboard camera coverage and leave task-critical outcomes outside the robot's view (partial observability), while strong dynamic coupling and limited control margins make physical interactions fragile (limited interaction robustness) and leave failure causes unresolved. Re-examining the same feedback cannot recover observations that were never collected or separate failure causes that the available evidence does not distinguish. To acquire this missing evidence, we introduce Active Feedback (AF), an active "View and Probe" framework. When execution feedback leaves unresolved questions, the agent synthesizes a separate diagnostic program that runs in simulation to inspect unseen outcomes from informative viewpoints (View) or test hypotheses about failure causes through targeted physical interactions (Probe). Across six simulated aerial manipulation tasks with 60 trials per condition, AF reduces false completion declarations from 25 to 5 and raises final task success from 31.7% to 75% compared with terminal feedback under the same coding-agent configuration and task-execution limit. Using shared robot APIs, task programs refined with AF in simulation run on a physical aerial manipulator without task-program source-code modification and complete multi-stage manipulation tasks in the real world. These results show that executable diagnosis fills gaps in execution feedback for embodied coding agents, improving both completion assessment and program refinement in aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [284] [PanTact: Teaching Whole-Body Dexterity with a Wearable Contact Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12558)
*Xiaomeng Xu,Han Zhang,Zhian Ruan,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Ruihan Yang,Carmelo Sferrazza,Shuran Song,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PanTact是一个新系统，它通过学习人类无机器人演示来赋予人形机器人全身的灵巧性，使其能够利用身体的任何部分进行操纵，而不仅仅是手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动操纵研究主要将操纵限制在手上，并避免身体与其他环境的接触。作者希望扩展操纵能力，使其超越手部。

Method: PanTact采用了一种由三个主要部分组成的系统：1) 一个穿戴式的、无机器人的数据收集接口，用于记录身体接触的位置和状态；2) 一个视觉-动作策略，用于弥合视角观察的差距并预测全身接触目标；3) 一个考虑接触的全身体控制公式，利用合适的机器人表面来实现主动接触目标。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上部署PanTact，无需额外的身体接触传感器，成功展示了包括身体支撑、推、扫和负重分担在内的全身灵巧性。

Conclusion: PanTact成功地实现了人形机器人的全身灵巧性，使其能够利用身体的任何部分与环境进行交互和操纵，并且这种能力可以直接从人类的演示中学习。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have many degrees of freedom and contact surfaces, yet most loco-manipulation research restricts manipulation to the hands and avoids other body contacts with the environment. To extend manipulation beyond the hands, we present PanTact, a system that learns humanoid whole-body dexterity, the capability to manipulate with any suitable body surface, directly from robot-free human demonstrations. Our key idea is to use contact as manipulation-centric information that transfers across embodiments. Contact locations and timing specify the interactions needed to accomplish a task, while the robot chooses how to coordinate its body to realize them within its own physical constraints. Specifically, PanTact captures, learns, and executes whole-body contact via: 1) a wearable, robot-free data collection interface that augments egocentric vision and bimanual UMI grippers with whole-body modular contact patches to record body contact locations and states; 2) a visuomotor policy that mitigates the egocentric observation gap and predicts whole-body contact goals; and 3) contact-aware whole-body control formulations that realize active contact goals using suitable robot surfaces. We deploy PanTact on a Unitree G1 humanoid without additional body contact sensors, demonstrating whole-body dexterity involving body support, pushing, sweeping, and load-sharing. Website: https://pantact.github.io

</details>


### [285] [What Should Embodied Agents Remember? Benchmarking and Evaluating Memory Modality for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12561)
*Junxiang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Yi Wu,Guofei Chen,Nir Pechuk,Tao Chen,Zackory Erickson,Yuzhe Qin,Tianyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MeMoREval基准，用于评估具身智能体在机器人任务中利用不同视觉记忆模式（如帧、描述、场景图）的能力，并分析了成本效益，同时展示了其在模拟和物理机器人上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）代理需要记忆来处理过去的经验。对于控制机器人的具身智能体，视觉和空间观察带来了更丰富的记忆模态选择，这会影响信息可用性和模型推理成本。因此，需要一个评估这种视觉记忆策略的基准。

Method: 提出了MeMoREval基准，该基准包含一个受控的经验-请求协议。代理首先在不知道后续任务的情况下经历一系列事件，然后利用记忆来响应信息和动作请求，而无需新的观察。基准包含53个任务，跨越4个模拟室内环境。评估了任务成功率以及记忆构建和使用的推理成本。还进行了物理机器人演示。

Result: 存储帧比存储生成的描述在主要模型较低的写入时间推理成本下表现更好。存储帧并结合场景图实现了所有测试模型中最高的总体任务成功率。研究还展示了积累经验、选择性帧保留和视觉输入到记忆写入器等扩展如何支持进一步的内存设计研究。

Conclusion: MeMoREval基准提供了一个评估具身智能体视觉记忆策略的框架，揭示了不同记忆存储方式的成本效益权衡，并证明了其在模拟和真实机器人应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Memory allows large language model (LLM) agents to use past experience, often stored as textual records, when responding to requests. For embodied agents that control robots, visual and spatial observations introduce a broader choice of memory modalities, affecting both the information available for subsequent tasks and the model inference costs of constructing and using memory. We introduce MeMoREval, a benchmark for studying this problem through a controlled experience-request protocol. Agents first experience a sequence of events without knowing the subsequent tasks, and subsequently they use memory to respond to information and action requests without receiving new observations. The benchmark comprises 53 tasks across four simulated indoor environments and measures task success alongside the inference costs of memory construction and use. Our reference evaluations reveal that storing frames outperforms storing generated descriptions at lower write-time inference cost for the primary model, while storing frames with scene graphs achieves the highest aggregate task success for every tested model. Extensions to accumulated experience, selective frame retention, and visual input to memory writers demonstrate how the benchmark accommodates further memory-design investigations. Two physical-robot demonstrations establish the feasibility of applying its protocol beyond simulation.

</details>


### [286] [CASTER: Formalizing Zero-Shot Manipulation from Synthetic Videos as Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12567)
*Yinlong Dai,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从合成视频生成机器人真实轨迹的优化方法，用于零样本操纵任务，并通过实验证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖真实人类演示，而视频生成模型提供了合成视频的新范式。尽管合成视频可以针对机器人环境生成，但缺乏物理一致性和机器人基础，无法直接用于机器人轨迹生成。

Method: 将问题形式化为约束优化问题。利用从合成视频提取的目标轨迹作为初始猜测，在数字孪生中进行优化，以确保物理可行性和语义一致性。分析了视频生成模型成功的必要属性，并提出了一种统一的数学框架。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法消除了现有方法的简化，更接近底层优化，从而提高了从合成视频生成零样本轨迹的鲁棒性，平均任务成功率提高了34.1%。

Conclusion: 提出的基于优化的方法能够有效地将合成视频转化为机器人可执行的真实世界轨迹，并通过在数字孪生中强制执行物理和语义约束来改进轨迹。该方法为视频生成模型在机器人任务中的应用提供了新的视角和解决方案。

Abstract: Prior research on learning from humans often requires real human demonstrations. But advances in video generation models offer a new paradigm: we can now prompt these generators to make synthetic videos of the task. On the plus side, these synthetic videos take place in the robot's current environment, and do not require actual human teaching. However, while the generated videos appear to show plausible motions, they lack both physical consistency and robot grounding, and thus the robot cannot directly roll out the trajectory shown by the video. In this paper we seek to formalize the process of going from synthetic video generation to real-world robot trajectories for zero-shot manipulation tasks. We recognize that this problem is an instance of constrained optimization. The object trajectories extracted from the synthetic video provide an initial guess at the correct motion, and the robot must refine these trajectories (for instance, within a digital twin) while enforcing physical feasibility and semantic consistency. Our optimization-based formalism provides insights into the video generation properties necessary for success. Specifically, we show that the video generation must probabilistically seed the search within a semantic basin of attraction where the trajectory can be locally modified without losing its task alignment. Our analysis further reveals that state-of-the-art approaches can be viewed as instances of our unified formalism with key simplifications. In our experiments we remove these simplifications to more closely match the underlying optimization, leading to more robust zero-shot trajectories from synthetic videos and improving average task success by 34.1% compared to the best-performing baseline. See videos at our project website: https://collab.me.vt.edu/CASTER

</details>


### [287] [Image-Based Path-Planning for Navigation of Soft-Growing Robots in the Spinal Sub-Arachnoid Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12631)
*George Dimas,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Panagiotis Vartholomeos,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图像的A*算法扩展，用于在脊髓蛛网膜下腔（SSS）中规划软体机器人的安全路径，并创建锚点以辅助导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在微创手术和药物输送方面具有潜力，需要专门的路径规划算法来安全有效地在人体内导航，特别是在脊髓蛛网膜下腔这样脆弱的环境中。

Method: 提出了一种基于图像的3D路径规划算法，该算法是对A*算法的扩展。该算法能够估算到达目标位置的安全路径，并同时确定锚点以辅助软体机器人的转向。

Result: 使用高精度的脊髓蛛网膜下腔模型对该算法进行了评估，结果表明该算法能够满足机器人运动要求和路径效率。

Conclusion: 该图像驱动的A*扩展算法能够为软体机器人在脊髓蛛网膜下腔内的导航提供安全有效的路径规划，并通过创建锚点解决了软体机器人控制的挑战。

Abstract: Recently, soft robotic procedures are being developed aiming at achieving targeted minimally invasive operations or drug administration in specific locations in the human body. Accordingly, specialized path-planning algorithms need to be investigated for the estimation of efficient paths that would protect the delicate tissue structures and simultaneously satisfy a set of requirements for the efficient navigation of the robot. In this context, we propose an image-based 3D pathplanning algorithm, which is an extension of the A* algorithm, for the navigation of a soft-growing robot inside the spinal subarachnoid space (SSS). The proposed algorithm is capable of estimating a safe pathway towards the goal location, while ensuring the establishment of anchor points that facilitate the efficient steering of the soft robot. The algorithm is evaluated using a highly detailed model of the SSS with respect to its capacity to satisfy the robot movement requirements and path efficiency.

</details>


### [288] [Graph-Based Heuristics for Minimum Constraint Removal in Cluttered Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12609)
*Antony Thomas,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Marco Baglietto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于图的启发式方法来解决机器人避障问题中的最小约束移除（MCR）问题，该问题在实践中计算成本高昂。所提出的方法通过结合双向可行集生成和缩减图搜索，在效率和解的质量之间取得了良好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类居住的复杂环境中，机器人规划运动时可能会遇到由于移动障碍物导致路径规划不可行的情况。最小约束移除（MCR）问题旨在找到最少需要移除的障碍物以实现无碰撞路径，但该问题是NP难的，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 该研究提出了一种基于图的启发式方法，包括：1. 双向可行集生成；2. 在缩减的MCR图上进行固定长度搜索；3. 扩展的可行集生成器，以应对第一种生成器未能找到最优解集的情况。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，所提出的管道在三个案例中找到了最优解。在其他案例中，其选择的障碍物数量比最优解多出最多31.25%，但计算速度比精确搜索快3.4到33.7倍。在一个涉及操纵任务的基准测试中，通过移除两个障碍物而不是七个，将总规划时间从84.72秒减少到30.59秒。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法为在移动障碍物环境中进行运动规划提供了一种实用的折衷方案，在计算效率和解的质量之间取得了良好的平衡，使得机器人能够更有效地在复杂环境中导航和执行任务。

Abstract: Humanoid and service robots operating in clut- tered human environments may encounter motion planning problems that are infeasible when all surrounding objects are treated as fixed obstacles. The Minimum Constraint Removal problem, which we refer to as MCR, addresses this situation by identifying the minimum number of constraints, such as movable objects, whose removal enables a collision-free path. However, computing an optimal MCR solution is NP-hard. In this work, we propose a graph-based heuristic pipeline that combines bidirectional feasible-set generation with fixed- length searches over reduced MCR graphs. We further derive an extended feasible-set generator for instances in which the candidates produced by the first generator do not contain a complete optimal removal set. Across six benchmarks, the evaluated pipeline matches the exact solution in three cases and, in the remaining cases, selects at most 31.25% more obstacles than the optimum, while being between 3.4 and 33.7 times faster than exact search. In one of the benchmarks, involving a manipulation task amidst clutter, selecting two rather than seven obstructing objects reduces the combined MCR, inverse- kinematics, and motion planning time from 84.72 s to 30.59 s. These results indicate that the proposed approach provides a practical compromise between computational efficiency and solution quality for motion planning among movable obstacles.

</details>


### [289] [Selectively Actuated Fiberbots for High-precision Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12636)
*Jinshi Zhao,Libaihe Tian,Alex Ranne,Qindong Zheng,Yue Li,Xiaotong Guo,Ziqi Yang,Ali Anil Demircali,Mohamed E. M. K. Abdelaziz,Bruno Gil Rosa,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena,Burak Temelkuran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电热驱动的光纤机器人，直径1.9毫米，长1.2米，可用于微创手术，实现低于100微米的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长而细的器械对于微创手术至关重要，但当驱动力沿整个器械分布时，实现精确的远端操控变得越来越困难。

Method: 该机器人使用热拉伸工艺制造，将焦耳加热集中在远端活动段，并通过嵌入的金属丝进行热驱动。机器人的光纤直径为1.9毫米，长度为1.2米。

Result: 该光纤机器人能够精确操控手术激光光纤，定位精度低于100微米。其紧凑的尺寸使其能够通过柔性内窥镜的工作通道，并与内窥镜实现机械解耦。

Conclusion: 该光纤机器人实现了精确的远端运动，并有潜力通过提高微创手术的精度来改善临床结果。

Abstract: Long, slender instruments are essential for minimally invasive surgery (MIS), but achieving precise distal manipulation becomes increasingly difficult when actuation extends along the entire instrument. Here, we present an electrothermally actuated fiber-based robot designed for MIS procedures, in which Joule heating is concentrated in the distal active segment. The fiber is fabricated using a thermal drawing process and uses embedded metallic wires for thermal actuation. This robotic fiber, 1.9 mm in diameter and 1.2 m in length, can precisely manipulate surgical laser fibers with positional precision below 100 $μ$m. The compact size of the fiber robot allows it to pass through the working channel of a flexible endoscope, while keeping both devices mechanically uncoupled to allow independent motion. A phantom demonstration combining the robotic fiber with a da Vinci instrument conceptually demonstrated its compatibility with existing endoscopic systems for clinical applications. Our robotic fiber enables precise distal motion and holds the potential to improve clinical outcomes by enhancing precision in MIS procedures.

</details>


### [290] [Distributed Harmonic-Covariance Feedback for Hole Closure in Planar Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12637)
*Jitian Liu,Subhrajit Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谐波协方差反馈的算法，利用谐波边信号定位和闭合多智能体系统中随时间变化的Čech复形的洞，并通过固定度多项式实现了分布式评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在多智能体系统中如何有效地闭合由其感知区域形成的Čech复形中的洞，以实现全局一致性控制。

Method: 提出一种谐波协方差反馈机制，利用谐波边信号定位洞，并将其转换为智能体间的力。通过固定度多项式和正则化Hodge拉普拉斯算子进行分布式评估，该多项式可以通过局部算子应用来计算。

Result: 闭环系统从所有安全初始状态出发，具有唯一的全局轨迹。在给定假设下，可以离线计算出累积洞面积的下界，且这些下界收敛于实际累积面积。智能体可以通过时间平均意义上闭合所有洞。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效地闭合多智能体系统中的洞，实现全局轨迹控制，并能计算出累积洞面积的下界。数值仿真结果表明该方法在实际应用中是有效的。

Abstract: We consider hole closure in a planar multi-agent system whose sensing disks form a time-varying Čech complex. We propose a harmonic-covariance feedback that uses harmonic edge signals to locate holes and converts those signals into agent forces. For distributed evaluation, we use a fixed-degree polynomial of the regularized Hodge Laplacian. This polynomial is evaluated through local operator applications. The closed loop has a unique global trajectory from every safe initial state. The model data and initial state are supplied with procedures for approximation to any requested accuracy with explicit error bounds. We assume that hole area is measurable and bounded, holes evolve continuously without splitting or merging, and only finitely many temporary new holes appear. Under these conditions, offline finite calculations produce rational lower bounds for the cumulative hole area on the time horizon without querying trajectory data. These bounds converge to that cumulative area, whether finite or infinite. If it is finite, holes close in the time-averaged sense: the average normalized hole area over every fixed-length time window tends to zero as time tends to infinity. In a 15-second numerical demo, all holes close by 9.9s and remain closed through the end.

</details>


### [291] [PriMoRetarget: Humanoid Motion Transfer Through Prioritized Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12642)
*Nicholas Ioannidis,Dun Yang,Max Smith,Tue Vu,Ziyu Zhang,Shayegan Omidshafiei,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 PriMoRetarget 的优先级运动重定向框架，通过引入优先级层次结构来解决多角色和多对象交互中的运动重定向问题，确保关键的运动特征（如脚部放置和交互接触）得到优先保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法在处理包含多个角色和对象的复杂交互场景时，难以同时保留所有重要的运动特征，因为它们通常基于加权多目标优化，需要仔细调整权重且可能妥协关键运动特性。

Method: PriMoRetarget 框架引入了显式的优先级层次结构，取代了相对目标权重。低优先级更新受到约束，以保留高优先级目标，从而确保脚部放置和交互接触等关键特征优先于姿态等低优先级目标。

Result: 在包含单角色运动、角色-对象操作和角色-角色交互的四个数据集上进行了评估。结果表明，PriMoRetarget 生成的运动能更好地保留任务相关的交互，并且提供的参考轨迹比现有流行方法生成的轨迹更易于跟踪。

Conclusion: PriMoRetarget 是一种有效的运动重定向框架，通过优先级层次结构能够更好地处理复杂的多角色和多对象交互场景，优先保留关键运动特征，从而生成更优的重定向运动。

Abstract: Motion retargeting is a critical preprocessing step for transferring human motion data to humanoid robots. While existing methods are effective for single-character motions, interactions involving multiple characters and objects introduce additional contacts and spatial relationships that must be preserved. Retargeting is commonly formulated as a weighted multi-objective optimization problem, where conflicting objectives can require careful tuning of relative weights and can still compromise important motion characteristics. We introduce PriMoRetarget, a prioritized motion-retargeting framework that replaces relative objective weighting with an explicit priority hierarchy. Lower-priority updates are constrained to preserve higher-priority objectives, allowing critical characteristics such as foot placement and interaction contacts to take precedence over lower-priority pose objectives. The method is evaluated on four datasets spanning single-character motion, character-object manipulation, and character-character interactions. The resulting motions better preserve task-relevant interactions and provide reference trajectories that can be tracked more reliably than those produced by widely used retargeting methods.

</details>


### [292] [Robust and Scalable UAV-UGV Planning for Aerial Monitoring under Energy and Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12670)
*Cahit Ikbal Er,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对多UAV-UGV协同进行空中监测任务的规划算法，该算法考虑了监测点的访问顺序和UAV的续航能力，并允许车辆动态调整速度以满足约束，最终目标是最小化任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在处理UAV-UGV协同任务时，通常忽略了监测点访问顺序的严格要求以及UAV能量限制，并且缺乏动态调整策略。因此，需要一种能够同时考虑这些因素并优化任务完成时间的规划方法。

Method: 开发了一种多UAV-UGV团队协同规划算法，该算法能够联合确定车辆路径和时间安排。算法允许车辆根据需要减速以满足访问顺序约束，同时考虑UAV的续航能力和有限的行程时间扰动。算法具有多项式时间复杂度，并提供了可行性保证。

Result: 该算法能够生成满足UAV能量限制和监测点访问顺序要求的有效解决方案，并成功最小化了任务完成时间。数值比较结果表明了算法的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地开发了一种高效的规划算法，用于解决具有顺序约束和UAV能量限制的多UAV-UGV空中监测任务。该算法能够联合优化路径和时间，并提供可行性保证，为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider planning for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) teams in aerial monitoring missions with ordering requirements on the monitoring points to be visited by the UAV. The system consists of multiple UAV-UGV teams, each comprising one UAV and one UGV. UAV energy limitations are modeled through endurance, defined as the maximum flight time on a full battery. The UGVs serve as mobile charging stations and transport the UAVs between consecutive aerial tours. Given a set of monitoring points subject to ordering constraints and the teams' initial and terminal positions, the objective is to minimize mission completion time while satisfying UAV energy limits and the required visit order under bounded travel-time disturbances that may arise during execution. Unlike prior work, the plan jointly determines vehicle routes and their timing, allowing teams to slow down as needed to satisfy the ordering constraints. We develop a polynomial-time algorithm that generates efficient solutions to the underlying NP-hard planning problem. We establish feasibility guarantees, characterize the algorithm's computational complexity, and evaluate its performance via numerical comparisons.

</details>


### [293] [Non-Prehensile Multi-Object Placement with Mobile Robots via Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12684)
*Lance Pharand,Megnath Ramesh,Mark Chiu,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于移动机器人多目标推物体（multi-object pushing）的扩散规划器（diffusion planner），在Push-T基准测试和仓库场景模拟中均取得了优于现有方法的成功率，并实现了更快的重规划速度和更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非抓取式操作研究主要集中在粗略放置，而对于移动机器人来说，高精度放置（如Push-T基准）的纠错需要复杂的重新定位。本文旨在解决移动机器人高精度多目标推物体的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于因果分解的扩散规划器，将机器人和物体的去噪步骤解耦，允许在一个机器人规划下采样多个物体状态，从而捕捉推物体过程的多模态性。设计了一套针对移动机器人的推物体特定成本函数，用于指导规划和进行最佳N采样。方法的核心是扩散模型，通过迭代去噪生成机器人和物体的轨迹。

Result: 在Push-T基准上，该规划器取得了最高的成功率和最低的不可信物体运动率。在模拟仓库场景中，相比无指导基线，放置箱子的比例提高了21.5个百分点，重规划速度快1.8倍。在真实机器人部署中，对于训练数据中未出现过的物体形状，成功率达到85.0%，远高于Diffusion Policy的37.5%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的扩散规划器能够有效地解决移动机器人的高精度多目标推物体问题，在各种场景下均表现出优越的性能，并且在真实机器人上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation with mobile robots has primarily targeted placement in coarse goal regions. Precision placement has been studied with manipulators on benchmarks such as Push-T, where an omnidirectional end effector can readily re-approach an object from any side. For a mobile robot, correcting the same placement errors may require wide repositioning maneuvers around the object. We formalize multi-object pushing with a mobile robot as a multi-target assignment problem at tight tolerances. We propose a diffusion planner with a causal factorization: each robot denoising step is followed by an object step conditioned on the current clean robot estimate. This factorization allows us to sample multiple object outcomes for a single robot plan, capturing the multi-modality of pushing. We also design a set of pushing-specific costs for mobile robots, used for both guidance and best-of-$N$ sampling at inference. On Push-T, our planner attains the highest success rate of the methods we compare, while having the lowest rate of implausible object motion. Across three multi-box pushing environments resembling warehouse scenarios, it raises the fraction of boxes placed by $21.5$ percentage points over an unguided baseline, and replans $1.8\times$ faster than a planner that denoises the two trajectories together. Lastly, we deploy the guided planner on a TurtleBot 3 Burger with object shapes absent from the training data, where it places $85.0\%$ of objects within tolerance against Diffusion Policy's $37.5\%$.

</details>


### [294] [Spa-Bench: A Real-Robot Benchmark for Spatial Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12696)
*Justin Tien-Smith,Matthew Pidden,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了Spa-Bench基准测试，用于评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在真实机器人上理解和执行空间指令的能力。结果表明，现有VLA模型在空间推理方面存在不足，即使在熟悉的对象和动作上，当遇到新的空间配置时，其性能也会显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估主要关注整体操作成功率，未能清晰展示模型在物理场景中准确理解空间描述的能力。因此，研究者希望更精确地评估VLA模型将空间描述转化为实际机器人行为的可靠性。

Method: 研究者引入了Spa-Bench基准测试，包含六种桌面任务（对象状态、相对大小、指代描述、相对位置、序数位置、计数）。在真实机器人（SO-101）上收集了4092个实验数据，包括1200个演示和2892个来自五个先进VLA策略的评估。通过比较“分布内”（ID）和“分布外”（OOD）的空间配置，并设置了四种诊断条件，以探究空间推理、语言敏感性、对象-任务关联以及无需动作的识别能力。

Result: 在三个完全评估的VLA策略中，模型在ID空间配置下的成功率从67.5%-73.3%下降到OOD空间配置下的40.7%-52.0%。相匹配的直接指令可以将成功率提高28.3-40.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 评估的VLA模型通常能够执行所需的操作，但在空间推理方面缺乏鲁棒性。在新的空间配置下，模型的性能会显著下降，表明其空间接地能力有待提高。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) offer a promising route toward general-purpose robotics by transferring semantic knowledge from large-scale vision-language pretraining into language-conditioned control. However, most VLA evaluations emphasize aggregate manipulation success under changes in objects, instructions, or environments, leaving unclear how reliably these policies ground spatial descriptions in the physical scenes where they act. We therefore ask a sharper question: how reliably can current VLAs translate spatial descriptions into appropriate real-robot behavior? To investigate this, we introduce Spa-Bench, a real-robot benchmark that evaluates spatial grounding across six tabletop tasks covering Object State, Relative Size, Referential Description, Relational Placement, Ordinal Position, and Counting. Our primary evaluation compares in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) spatial configurations while retaining training support for the relevant objects, concepts, and actions, reducing object and motor unfamiliarity as alternative explanations for failure. Four diagnostic conditions further probe physical execution, sensitivity to wording, object-task associations, and recognition that no action is required. We release 4,092 episodes collected on the SO-101 robot arm: 1,200 human-teleoperated demonstrations and 2,892 real-robot evaluation rollouts from five state-of-the-art VLA policies. Across the three fully evaluated policies, success falls from 67.5-73.3% on ID spatial configurations to 40.7-52.0% on OOD spatial configurations, while matched direct instructions improve success by 28.3-40.0 percentage points. These results show that the evaluated VLAs can often execute the required manipulations but lack robust spatial grounding. Project materials, including videos, code, and benchmark data, are available at https://justintiensmith.github.io/Spa-Bench/.

</details>


### [295] [FallGown: A Soft Pneumatic Wearable Device for Fall Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12706)
*Megan C. Coram,Haoran Lei,Steven H. Collins,Steve B. Cousins,Michelle Baldonado,Cynthia Sung,Allison M. Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为FallGown的软质可穿戴支撑设备，旨在通过气动囊袋提供全身支撑来防止老年人跌倒，同时保持便携性和易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多老年人因担心跌倒和受伤而避免进行有益健康的锻炼。现有的辅助设备存在笨重、僵硬、不易使用或带有污名化等局限性。

Method: FallGown是一种由织物外壳内的充气气囊组成的软质可穿戴设备。研究人员评估了其便携性、穿脱时间和对用户的阻力。在单用户跌倒预防测试中，评估了不同配置（气囊数量、地板直径、是否使用滚动底座）下的稳定性和防跌倒效果。此外，还探索了利用内置压力传感器进行运动监测和自适应支撑的可能性。

Result: FallGown具有良好的便携性（6.9公斤，0.051立方米）、穿脱便利性（平均穿戴<2.5分钟，脱卸<0.5分钟）。在跌倒预防测试中，一个包含12个气囊、1.4米直径地板和滚动底座的配置成功防止了所有跌倒，除了一次机械故障。用户也发现其拖拽力在可接受范围内（7.24-20.8牛）。

Conclusion: FallGown是一种有前景的软质可穿戴防跌倒设备，它能够在不限制用户活动的情况下提供显著支撑。该设备的设计考虑了便携性、易用性和有效性，并且通过集成传感器提供了进一步的监测和自适应能力，有望解决老年人因担心跌倒而避免锻炼的问题。

Abstract: Concerns about falling and potential resulting injuries prevent many older adults from participating in forms of health-improving exercise such as walking, dance, and tai chi. Existing passive devices like walkers require use of the arms for support, and active devices like exoskeletons and robotic frames are often rigid, bulky, and stigmatized, which limits their use. The FallGown is a soft wearable support structure consisting of pressurized pneumatic pouches within a fabric shell; it can support the full weight of a user's body and aims to prevent falls. It is portable (6.9 kg and 0.051 m3 when stored), minimally burdensome (7.24-20.8 N drag force depending on floor type and whether an optional rolling base frame is used), and convenient (less than 2.5 minutes on average to don and less than 0.5 minutes on average to doff). The optional rolling base lowers friction with the floor, but reduces portability and skirt deformation (which can be useful to fit through doors and other tight spaces). Fall prevention testing with a single user revealed a tradeoff between device size and stability against tipping; a skirt with 12 pneumatic pouches and a floor diameter of 1.4 meters with a base frame prevented all falls except in one case with a mechanical failure. On-board pressure sensors can sense the user's weight distribution, providing an opportunity for monitored exercise and adaptive support.

</details>


### [296] [REMM: Revisable Embodied Memory for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12710)
*Revanth Krishna Senthilkumaran,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Alessandro Oltramari,Jonathan Francis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 REMM 的可修改具身记忆系统，用于增强机器人完成长周期操纵任务的能力，即使在遇到干扰时也能恢复和纠正。REMM 能够区分不可变的证据历史和可修改的任务状态，并将派生出的结论与其支持事实联系起来，从而在事实发生变化时自动撤销过时的结论并安排修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期机器人操纵任务需要机器人能够记住过去的事件并根据新的信息修正之前的判断。现有的方法在面对干扰导致证据变化时，难以有效地撤销不再适用的已完成任务条件，也难以确定需要修复哪些已承诺的任务。

Method: REMM 作为一个监督记忆层，集成到一个固定的 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 执行器之上。它将只追加的证据历史与可修改的任务状态分离开来，并明确地将派生的关系和已完成的条件与其支持事实关联起来。当证据发生变化影响到支持事实时，REMM 会撤销依赖于该事实的结论，并在验证过的技能边界处安排有针对性的恢复任务，而无需重新训练执行器。

Result: 在 51 次物理实验中，REMM 显著提高了任务进度。在干预恢复方面，平均任务进度从基线的 57% 提高到 91%；在序列回忆方面，从 43% 提高到 70%。此外，视频诊断显示 REMM 能够更好地将保留的历史信息与下一条指令联系起来，优于与 π0.5 兼容的高级规划器。

Conclusion: 可修改的记忆是实现机器人持续操纵而无需重新训练策略的一种实用接口。REMM 系统通过分离证据历史和任务状态，并建立事实与结论之间的联系，有效地解决了长周期操纵中的恢复和修正问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to retain task-relevant history while revising conclusions no longer supported by physical evidence. Retaining observations alone does not specify which completed conditions should be withdrawn after a disturbance or which obligations require repair. We introduce REMM, Revisable Embodied Memory for Manipulation, a supervisory memory layer for a frozen G0.5 vision-language-action executor. REMM separates an append-only evidence history from revisable task state and explicitly links derived relations and completed conditions to their supporting facts. When accepted evidence changes a supporting fact, it withdraws dependent conclusions and schedules targeted recovery at verified skill boundaries without updating the executor. Across 51 physical trials, including operator-assisted runs, REMM improves mean recorded task progress over the strongest baseline from 57% to 91% in intervention recovery and from 43% to 70% in sequence recall. Complementary video diagnostics show improved grounding of retained history into the next instruction, including comparisons with $π_{0.5}$-compatible high-level planner adaptations. These results support revisable memory as a practical interface for continued manipulation without policy retraining. Project: https://revanthsenthil.github.io/remm/.

</details>


### [297] [Visual-Wi-Fi Navigation to Signal-Emitting Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12733)
*Lihao Wang,Tianjian Xu,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种利用 Wi-Fi 信号和机器人自身运动进行室内导航的新方法，无需预先绘制地图或连接到目标设备，并在模拟器和真实机器人上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内导航，尤其是在目标较小、被遮挡或不明显时，仍然是一个挑战。同时，智能设备产生的 Wi-Fi 信号是一种普遍存在的空间线索，但尚未得到充分利用。

Method: 该研究提出了 WiFiSpaces 模拟器，它增加了光线追踪的 Wi-Fi 感知功能，并在此基础上构建了 ViFiNav 导航框架。ViFiNav 包含一个在线源定位算法，该算法利用机器人运动来处理多径传播带来的信号干扰，并将来自多个视角的测量值聚合成对源位置的置信度估计。一个学习到的策略则融合了这一估计、深度观测，并根据估计的精化进行导航。

Result: 实验表明，利用机器人运动对于准确估计 Wi-Fi 源位置至关重要。在 WiFiSpaces 中使用光线追踪 Wi-Fi 观测进行微调，能够显著提高 ViFiNav 的成功率。该策略无需额外训练即可在新场景数据上表现良好，并且在真实机器人上部署时也取得了成功。

Conclusion: 研究证明了结合视觉信息和利用机器人运动进行 Wi-Fi 源定位是实现鲁棒室内导航的有效途径。该方法具有良好的泛化能力，并能在无额外训练的情况下应用于真实世界场景。

Abstract: Indoor navigation to task-relevant targets remains difficult when the target is small, occluded, or visually indistinct. Meanwhile, smart appliances, mobile devices, and IoT tags emit Wi-Fi signals during everyday communication, providing a pervasive but underused spatial cue. We study visual-Wi-Fi navigation, where a robot reaches a signal-emitting device in an unmapped environment using onboard visual observations and Wi-Fi packets overheard from the device, without connecting to the device or modifying infrastructure. Realizing this requires pose-dependent Wi-Fi observations during closed-loop policy learning and reliable spatial cues from bearing measurements corrupted by indoor multipath propagation on a moving robot. We present WiFiSpaces, which extends the Habitat embodied simulator with online ray-traced Wi-Fi sensing, rendering Wi-Fi observations for a robot-mounted antenna array at continuous poses during policy rollouts. On top of it, we build ViFiNav, a localization-aware navigation framework: an online source-localization algorithm exploits robot motion to disambiguate and robustly aggregate bearings from multiple viewpoints into a confidence-aware source estimate, and a learned policy fuses this evolving estimate with depth observations and learns to act on it as it is refined. Experiments show that exploiting robot motion is key to accurate source estimates, and that fine-tuning on ray-traced Wi-Fi observations in WiFiSpaces improves the success rate of ViFiNav. The policy transfers zero-shot to unseen scene datasets, and we deploy it on a real robot with a Wi-Fi sensor built from a commodity router, without any real-world training.

</details>


### [298] [StereoEngine: Scaling Stereo Bimanual Robot Data without Stereo Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12734)
*Jason Chen,I-Chun Arthur Liu,Gaurav Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StereoEngine，一种用于机器人操作任务的立体视觉数据合成方法，通过单目视频到立体图像的转换和模拟渲染生成逼真的立体数据，有效解决了现有数据稀缺的问题，并提升了双臂操作的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中立体视觉应用日益广泛，但缺乏大规模真实世界立体数据集限制了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的扩展。现有的单目到立体方法不适用于机器人操作场景，而模拟数据在真实场景中效果有限。

Method: StereoEngine 包含两种方法：1. 基于估计深度将单目（左）视频扭曲到右视图，并使用视频扩散模型修复遮挡区域，从而无需真实立体数据即可将单目演示转换为立体对。2. 在模拟器中渲染立体对，并联合生成逼真的左右视图，无需真实视频作为输入。

Result: StereoEngine 生成的立体数据质量优于现有方法。使用其合成数据进行策略模型的中期训练或特定任务微调，可显著提高下游双臂操作任务的性能。

Conclusion: StereoEngine 成功生成了高质量的机器人操作立体数据集，克服了真实数据稀缺的瓶颈，并证明了合成数据在提升机器人操作能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Stereo vision is increasingly used for robotic manipulation, with a growing body of work incorporating it into Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, the scarcity of large-scale, real-world stereo datasets remains a fundamental bottleneck for scaling such policies. Recent generative monocular-to-stereo methods target settings such as VR content and autonomous driving. No prior work produces realistic synthetic stereo data for robotic manipulation, where stereo supervision has come predominantly from simulation. We propose StereoEngine, which introduces two complementary methods to synthesize stereo data for robotic manipulation. In the first, we warp a monocular (left) video into the right view using estimated depth and inpaint the disoccluded regions with a video diffusion model, lifting monocular demonstrations into stereo pairs without training on real stereo. In the second, we render a stereo pair in simulation and jointly generate both photorealistic views, producing consistent left-right data without any real video as input. StereoEngine outperforms existing baselines in generated stereo quality, and using its synthetic data for policy mid-training or task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves downstream bimanual manipulation. Project website: https://stereoengine.github.io

</details>


### [299] [Verifier-Mediated Multi-LLM Coordination for Multi-Robot Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12761)
*Vivek Khatana,Zijian Song,Naira Hovakimyan,Petros Voulgaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种通过几何验证器协调多机器人导航的LLM规划方法，通过反馈机制解决独立规划造成的冲突，并与集中式LLM规划进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多机器人任务规划的LLM独立生成的符号计划，在几何约束下可能导致联合不可行，需要一种能够处理空间和时间约束的执行感知协调方法。

Method: 提出了一种验证器介导的去中心化多LLM架构，其中每个机器人独立规划，几何验证器检测并反馈冲突，促进迭代协调。并与集中式LLM规划架构进行比较。

Result: 在CARLA和Crazyflie飞行器上进行了实验，评估了在不同任务复杂度下，集中式、去中心化验证器介导协调以及物理可执行性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 验证器介导的多LLM架构能够有效地将语义任务推理与执行层面的可行性分离开来，实现迭代协调，并为多机器人导航规划提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used for high-level multi-robot task planning, yet independently generated symbolic plans can become jointly infeasible after geometric grounding because of shared spatial and temporal constraints. We study execution-aware coordination of large-language-model (LLM) planners for multi-robot navigation. We compare a centralized architecture, in which one LLM jointly reasons over the robot team, with a decentralized multi-LLM architecture in which each robot independently proposes a task plan. A geometric verifier grounds the candidate plans into robot trajectories, detects predicted inter-robot conflicts, and converts conflict information into structured feedback for targeted replanning. This verifier-mediated architecture separates semantic task reasoning from execution-level feasibility while enabling iterative coordination among LLM planners. Experiments in CARLA and on Crazyflie aerial robots evaluate coordination performance as task complexity increases and characterize the trade-offs between centralized reasoning, decentralized verifier-mediated coordination, and physical executability.

</details>


### [300] [Trajectory Prediction for Autonomous Driving in Road Construction Zones Using Human-in-the-Loop Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12747)
*Shuo Zhang,Aman Sunesh,Semiha Ergan,Kaan Ozbay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个针对城市道路施工区域的自动驾驶车辆轨迹预测模型WZDrive，并发布了一个包含1183个施工区域驾驶片段的多模态数据集WZDrive-Data。模型在预测精度上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市道路施工区域的车辆轨迹预测是一个被忽视的领域，缺乏专门的数据集和系统的评估。施工区域的道路布局和连通性变化复杂，增加了预测的难度。

Method: 研究者收集了WZDrive-Data数据集，包含33名驾驶员在模拟器中驾驶的1183个施工区域片段。利用包含真人交互和VR控制工人的驾驶模拟器，记录了驾驶员的控制和注视信号，以及车辆轨迹和场景信息。在此基础上，提出了一个多模态的ego-轨迹预测器WZDrive。

Result: WZDrive模型在预测精度方面表现出色，取得了minADE (K=6) 1.09m 和 minFDE (K=6) 2.12m 的结果。相比于最先进的基线方法，WZDrive在minADE (K=6)上降低了7.18%，在minFDE (K=6)上降低了13.18%。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了自动驾驶在城市施工区域研究的空白，通过结合真人交互式数据收集和多模态学习（结合人类驾驶信号和施工区域上下文），有效地提高了施工区域车辆轨迹预测的准确性。

Abstract: Ego-trajectory prediction in urban roadway work zones remains an underexplored topic in autonomous-driving research, with limited dedicated data and systematic evaluation. Roadway work zones involve temporary changes in the lane layouts and connectivity of the roads, complicating interactions with surrounding vehicles and workers. These changes make future vehicle trajectories difficult to predict. To address these challenges, we collect WZDrive-Data, a multimodal dataset comprising 1,183 work-zone driving segments from 33 drivers, using a human-in-the-loop driving simulator with prerecorded worker animations and VR-controlled workers. Driver control and gaze signals are recorded from human participants, while vehicle trajectories and scene information are obtained from the simulator. Our proposed multimodal ego-trajectory predictor, WZDrive, achieves a mean minADE (K=6) of 1.09 m and minFDE (K=6) of 2.12 m. Compared with the best-performing state-of-the-art baseline for each metric in our benchmark, WZDrive reduces minADE (K=6) by 7.18% and minFDE (K=6) by 13.18%. This research addresses a gap in autonomous-driving research on urban work zones by combining human-in-the-loop data collection with multimodal learning from human driving signals and in the context of work zones.

</details>


### [301] [Reflex: Visually Grounded Reactive Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12772)
*Taoyang Jia,Weikai Huang,Linxin Song,Jared Darlington,Jieyu Zhao,Yue Wang,Jiafei Duan,Zhongzheng Ren,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Reflex的三阶段框架，用于解决人形机器人全身运动控制以接住抛掷物体的挑战。该框架通过强化学习和视觉编码器，逐步学习全身协调、实时动态推断和从单目RGB-D传感器感知。在模拟和真实机器人实验中，Reflex展示了高效的接物能力，并且表明视觉历史信息对成功至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人在实时全身运动控制方面面临挑战，尤其是在需要预测和响应动态目标（如接住抛掷物）的情况下。现有方法往往只能解决部分问题，需要额外的外部传感器或标记物体，难以实现真正自主和实时的全身反应。

Method: Reflex是一个三阶段的学习框架：1. 状态策略学习全身接物，利用强化学习。2. 策略学习从延迟和不完整观测中推断移动物体的动力学。3. 编码器从单目RGB-D图像中直接学习动力学表示，同时固定全身控制器。这种分解方式使各能力拥有独立的训练信号，提高了视觉学习的可扩展性。

Result: 在模拟环境中，Reflex从单目RGB-D传感器达到了85.7%的接物成功率。移除时间视觉历史信息后，成功率显著下降。在真实的Unitree G1机器人上，Reflex在2.5米和5米距离上分别实现了65%和40%的接物成功率，接近人类的成功率。

Conclusion: Reflex框架能够有效地使人形机器人实现全身运动控制以接住抛掷物，并且能够从单目RGB-D视觉信息中学习动力学表示。时间视觉历史对于成功接物至关重要。该方法为解决更广泛的实时全身控制问题提供了一个有前景的途径。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly react to the world; they adeptly return a tennis shot, catch a falling cup, step aside from a passing cart, or pass a box tossed during truck unloading. These everyday actions require anticipating a moving target and reflexively responding with the whole body within about a second. Such visual reactive whole-body control remains challenging for humanoids, which must (1) coordinate whole-body control, (2) react to object dynamics in real time, and (3) ground perception in egocentric vision. We study catching thrown boxes as a representative task that demands all three capabilities; existing systems cover only part of it, relying on end-effectors, motion capture, or marked objects. We present Reflex, a three-stage framework that learns these capabilities stage by stage while connecting them through a compact representation of object dynamics. First, a state-based policy learns whole-body catching with reinforcement learning. Second, the policy learns to infer the dynamics of a moving box from delayed and incomplete observations. Third, an egocentric RGB-D encoder learns to recover this dynamics representation directly from visual observations while keeping the whole-body controller fixed. This decomposition provides each capability with a direct training signal and makes visual learning substantially more scalable. In simulation, Reflex achieves 85.7% catch success from egocentric RGB-D, compared with 87.9% when given privileged box observations. Temporal visual history is critical: removing it reduces success by 35.6 and 39.8 points on centered and laterally offset throws. On a real Unitree G1, Reflex catches human-thrown boxes with 65% and 40% success at 2.5 and 5 m, reaching 76% and 59% of human catchers' success at the same distances.

</details>


### [302] [Multimodal Image-based Robotic Assistance and Guidance Environment (MIRAGE): A Novel Unified Framework for Surgical Navigation and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12780)
*Ehsan Nasiri,Yi Zheng,Michael A. Kokko,Xiaoyao Fan,William R. Warner,Keith D. Paulsen,Ryan J. Halter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为MIRAGE的多模态图像引导机器人手术框架，该框架集成了立体视觉、增强现实、抬头显示和术中超声，用于实时可视化和导航。该框架在达芬奇手术机器人平台上进行了临床前验证，并在不同型号上都取得了良好的三维重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人手术系统在实时可视化和导航方面存在不足，需要一种能够整合多模态信息并提供精确导航的框架，以提高手术的准确性和响应性。

Method: MIRAGE框架集成了立体视觉场景重建、增强现实视频叠加、抬头显示导航和术中超声，并通过C++后端进行视频输入输出和渲染，Python GUI进行协调。该框架还包括了光学/电磁跟踪器配准、立体标定、带RMSE反馈的刚性配准和三维点云重建等模块。框架通过零拷贝绑定层实现模块间的同步和交互。预手术标定和术中校准均采用优化方法。

Result: 在幻影实验中，MIRAGE框架在达芬奇S、Si、Xi和SP平台上进行了验证。在临床相关的操作距离内，平均三维重投影误差为0.6mm，在Xi平台上100mm处达到最佳的0.1mm RMSE。

Conclusion: MIRAGE框架为多模态手术引导提供了一个准确、响应迅速且可扩展的临床前技术验证平台，有望提升机器人手术的导航精度和用户体验。

Abstract: This paper presents the computational architecture and quantitative preclinical evaluation of MIRAGE, a multimodal image-guided robotic surgery framework for real-time visualization and navigation. It integrates stereovision scene reconstruction, augmented-reality (AR) video overlay, quantified heads-up display (HUD) navigation, and intraoperative ultrasound within the da Vinci console's TilePro display (Intuitive Surgical). Additional modules include optical/electromagnetic tracker registration, stereo calibration, fiducial rigid registration with RMSE feedback, and 3D point-cloud reconstruction. Video I/O and rendering run in a C++ backend, while a Python GUI coordinates calibration, registration, reconstruction, visualization, and user interaction through an in-process zero-copy binding layer. The framework formalizes spatial transforms, module states, and synchronization of heterogeneous video and tracking streams. Preoperative calibration uses synchronized checkerboard image pairs and optimization, with estimated intrinsics, extrinsics, and rectification reused intraoperatively. MIRAGE was validated in phantom experiments across da Vinci S, Si, Xi, and SP platforms. Across clinically relevant working distances, mean 3D reprojection error was 0.6mm, with best 0.1mm RMSE at 100mm on Xi. Results support MIRAGE as a preclinical technical-validation platform for accurate, responsive, and extensible multimodal surgical guidance.

</details>


### [303] [Tactile Sensing-based Secure and Gentle Grasping of Unknown Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12790)
*Feng Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的、数据驱动的在线预测控制框架，用于安全地动态抓取未知物体，无需先验知识或离线训练。该方法利用紧凑的单通道触觉传感模块来实时表征夹持器-物体动力学交互，并通过有限状态机机制确定初始抓握力。使用高斯过程（GP）模型在线学习动力学，并利用模型不确定性来形成鲁棒的抓握约束，最终嵌入到模型预测控制（MPC）方案中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在抓取未知物体时，要在保持牢固抓握的同时避免接触滑动和结构损坏（即“安全且温和”的抓握）是一个挑战。

Method: 1. 采用一对紧凑的单通道触觉传感模块实时表征夹持器-物体动力学交互。2. 开发了一个基于有限状态机的抓握力探测机制来确定初始抓握力和抓握力约束。3. 通过在线动力学学习，使用高斯过程（GP）模型来捕捉夹持器-物体交互动力学。4. 利用GP模型的不确定性来形成鲁棒的抓握约束，并将其嵌入到模型预测控制（MPC）方案中。

Result: 所提出的控制框架能够以100 Hz的速率实时运行，能够动态地操纵物体，而不仅仅是静态保持。在不同接触表面拓扑和多种物体上的大量实验评估表明，与基线策略相比，该方法具有优越的性能和效率。该方法能够操纵从柔软的生豆腐、棉花糖到粗糙的石头等各种物体。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动的在线预测控制框架能够安全有效地处理未知物体，满足了安全且温和抓握的要求，并且无需先验知识或离线训练，在动态操作任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Reliable robotic manipulation requires maintaining a firm grasp while strictly avoiding both contact slip and structural damage to the object. Satisfying these inherent \textit{secure-and-gentle} grasping constraints is challenging for unknown objects. We present a novel online data-driven predictive control framework for safe dynamic robotic grasping of unknown objects. The proposed system features a pair of compact, single-channel tactile-sensing modules that characterize the gripper-object dynamic interaction in real time, entirely bypassing the need for prior knowledge or offline training. A finite-state-machine-based grasping force probing mechanism is developed to establish the initial grasping force and explicitly identify grasping force constraints. Through online dynamics learning, a Gaussian Process (GP) model captures gripper-object interaction dynamics. The model uncertainties are further used to form robust grasping constraints, which are embedded into a model predictive control scheme. We demonstrate error convergence in a probabilistic sense. The proposed control framework runs at 100~Hz in real time to dynamically manipulate and handle objects rather than static holding. Extensive experimental evaluations across diverse contact surface topologies and various types of objects validate superior performance and efficiency compared to baseline strategies. The proposed method is able to manipulate objects ranging from delicate items, such as raw tofu and marshmallows, to rough objects, such as rocks.

</details>


### [304] [Gamma: Grounded Multi-Agent Memory with Mechanical Audit for Memory-Intensive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12793)
*Yudong Liu,Yueqian Lin,Zijia Tang,Yuxi Zheng,Yuan Li,Shuai Zhang,Yi Li,Taotao Jing,Shuangjun Liu,Dashan Gao,Ning Bi,Jingwei Sun,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GAMMA的多智能体记忆系统，用于解决预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在记忆任务上的不足。GAMMA通过协作的 detector 和 writer 代理，将记忆压缩为文本，并利用 reasoner 代理和提议-验证机制来生成基于视觉证据的子目标，显著提高了在记忆操纵任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预训练VLA策略在依赖长期记忆的任务上表现不佳，因为其训练数据主要是马尔可夫性的，且将情节记忆直接映射到动作块很困难。尽管通过一次微调可以大幅提升性能，但关键在于如何将记忆映射到具体、符号化的子目标，而现有的单VLM方法在这一环节存在不足，如信息丢失、推理上下文过长、幻觉等问题。

Method: GAMMA系统包含两个协作代理：一个用于空间定位的检测器（SAM-3），一个用于将每个重规划窗口提炼成一行文本的VLM writer。它还包括一个只追加的文本记忆库和一个VLM reasoner，用于读取记忆库并按照oracle的节奏输出下一个子目标。整个系统被包装在一个提议-验证框架中，该框架在训练数据构建和部署时都会根据机械视觉证据，对每个代理的声明进行接受、推迟、纠正或拒绝。

Result: 在16个任务的记忆操纵测试套件上，GAMMA在固定策略的情况下，恢复了很大一部分oracle的性能上限，并在多个任务上达到了oracle的水平。此外，GAMMA在第二个基准测试中也实现了零样本迁移，且协议不变。

Conclusion: GAMMA通过引入一个协作性的多智能体记忆系统，有效解决了现有VLA策略在处理依赖长期记忆的任务时遇到的挑战，将记忆映射到接地符号化子目标的能力得到了显著提升，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies fail on memory tasks, behaviour that depends on events observed long before the current frame, because their training data is overwhelmingly Markov and mapping episodic memory directly to action chunks is hard. Yet the same policy, finetuned once to follow a privileged oracle's grounded-subgoal text, nearly solves these tasks: the difficulty lies entirely in mapping memory to a grounded symbolic subgoal. Existing work entrusts this stage to a single VLM that selects keyframes and reasons over raw frames, and falls far short of the oracle: key identities and dynamics are buried in noisy pixels, the reasoning spans hundreds of frames in an unbounded context, and hallucination corrupts the memory itself. We instead present GAMMA (Grounded multi-Agent Memory with Mechanical Audit), which builds the stage as a collaborating two-agent memory system: a detector tool (SAM-3) for spatial grounding, a VLM writer that distils each replan window into one grounded text line, an append-only text memory bank, and a VLM reasoner that reads the bank and emits the next grounded subgoal at the oracle's cadence, wrapped in a propose-verify harness that admits, defers, corrects, or rejects every agent claim against mechanical visual evidence, at training-corpus construction and at deployment alike. On a sixteen-task memory-manipulation suite GAMMA recovers a large fraction of the oracle ceiling with the policy held fixed, reaching the ceiling on several tasks, and transfers with unchanged contracts to a second benchmark.

</details>


### [305] [LeWAM: End-to-End World and Action Modeling with JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12818)
*Minghao Fu,Tavis Siebert,Eryk Halicki,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeWAM 是一种端到端的联合嵌入预测架构 (JEPA)，用于机器人控制，它通过联合训练目标和各向同性高斯约束，实现了比现有 JEPA 方法更高的成功率和更快的规划速度，并在行为克隆任务中表现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 JEPA 方法通常将视觉表征、动作条件动力学和控制策略分开训练，效率不高。作者希望开发一种端到端的 JEPA，可以直接用于控制，并提高规划效率和成功率。

Method: LeWAM 采用了联合训练目标，该目标包括预测下一个潜在状态和匹配动作流。同时，它还引入了对潜在状态的各向同性高斯约束，这使得单个模型能够直接执行策略并进行高效规划。

Result: 在接触密集型任务中，LeWAM 的成功率从 28.6% 提高到 89.7%；在长时任务中，成功率从 10.9% 提高到 46.2%。规划速度提高了 32.3 倍。在模拟和物理机器人上的行为克隆任务中，LeWAM 也与现有方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LeWAM 是一种有效的端到端 JEPA 方法，能够学习到与控制相关的表征和动作分布，从而显著提高机器人控制任务的性能，并在规划效率和成功率方面超越了先前的 JEPA 方法。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models without pixel reconstruction. Existing JEPA pipelines typically train visual representations and action-conditioned dynamics before adding a control policy or solver. We introduce LeWAM, an end-to-end JEPA for control with: (1) a joint training objective over next latent state prediction and action flow matching, and (2) an isotropic Gaussian constraint on the latent state, enabling a single model to support direct policy execution and efficient planning by evaluating sampled actions with its learned latent dynamics. Compared with prior JEPAs in visual planning, LeWAM improves success rate from 28.6% to 89.7% on contact-rich tasks and from 10.9% to 46.2% on long-horizon tasks, and plans 32.3 times faster. On behavior cloning tasks in simulation and physical robots, LeWAM is also competitive with established methods. Interpretability analyses show that LeWAM learns control-relevant representations and action distributions effectively guiding test-time planning and control.

</details>


### [306] [Cubic Spline Interpolation for Smooth Trajectory Generation in Autonomous Motion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12826)
*Aarush Mandayam,Sriram Aditya Gundla,Elliott Tammaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动构建和求解三次样条插值问题的程序，用于生成位置-时间航点序列的轨迹，该轨迹在位置、速度和加速度上都连续，从而避免了分段线性航点跟随中固有的无限加速度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分段线性航点跟随方法存在瞬时加速度变化的问题，导致轨迹不连续，这在机器人和无人机轨迹生成等应用中是不可取的。因此，需要一种能够生成位置、速度和加速度都连续的平滑轨迹的方法。

Method: 该程序将插值和连续性条件组装成一个单一的线性系统，并直接求解样条系数。这避免了为每组新的航点手动推导每段方程的需要。

Result: 所提出的方法在自由曲线绘制和签名数字化等任务上得到了验证，表明其能够生成平滑且连续的轨迹。这些任务与机器人和无人机轨迹生成所依赖的底层机制相同。

Conclusion: 该程序提供了一种自动、高效的方法来生成平滑、连续的位置-时间轨迹，适用于各种应用，包括机器人和无人机导航。未来的工作将致力于扩展该实现以支持多轴轨迹、显式的速度和加速度约束，并利用更高效的三对角线求解方法来处理更大的航点集。

Abstract: We presented a program that automates the construction and solution of the cubic spline interpolation problem for a sequence of position-time waypoints, producing trajectories that are continuous in position, velocity, and acceleration and therefore avoid the infinite-acceleration problem inherent to piecewise-linear waypoint following. By assembling the interpolation and continuity conditions into a single linear system and solving directly for the spline coefficients, the program removes the need to manually derive per-segment equations for each new waypoint set. We illustrated the approach on free-form curve drawing and signature digitization, two tasks that use the same underlying machinery required for smooth robot and drone trajectory generation. Future work includes extending the implementation to multi-axis trajectories with explicit velocity and acceleration bounds, adopting the more computationally efficient tridiagonal solution method for larger waypoint sets, and evaluating the approach quantitatively against alternative trajectory generation methods.

</details>


### [307] [VLMs are Native Action Models: Unleashing the Manipulation Capabilities of VLMs with Explicit Action-Space Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12832)
*Jingkun Yi,Shujie Zhang,Zirui Zhou,Haoxiao Wang,Haiyang Sun,Yuhang Dong,Xudong Xu,Xing Shen,Tao Zhang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为EASG的显式动作空间接地框架，它通过将机器人动作表示为VLM的文本生成接口，使预训练的VLM能够直接生成可执行的机器人动作，实现了零样本操作和泛化能力，并支持后续微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练的视觉语言模型（VLMs）转化为通用的机器人操纵策略面临挑战，需要一个合适的动作接口来暴露其能力。现有的方法要么引入需要额外学习的动作组件，要么依赖于外部规划和控制框架，无法实现端到端的统一训练。

Method: EASG框架通过将以工具中心点（TCP）为中心的动作帧投影到腕部相机观测中，并将动作表达为VLM的文本生成接口，使得连续机器人动作空间在VLM的感知上下文中可直接观察。这使得预训练的VLM能够利用其现有的视觉空间推理能力直接生成可执行的机器人动作，无需机器人-动作预训练或额外的动作模块。

Result: EASG实现了零样本操作和跨物体位置、类别及机器人执行器的泛化能力。同时，该框架支持在同一连续动作空间内使用机器人轨迹进行微调，从而不断提升模型性能。

Conclusion: EASG框架证明了，通过提供合适的动作接口，预训练的VLMs本身可以作为机器人操纵的原生动作模型，实现了零样本操作、泛化和有效的微调。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already exhibit strong visual, spatial and reasoning capabilities relevant to robotic manipulation. The key challenge in turning pretrained VLMs into generalist manipulation policies is to expose these capabilities through an appropriate action interface. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified trainable policy, but typically introduce learned action tokens, action heads, or action experts whose semantics must be learned from robot data pretraining. In contrast, modular foundation-model-based manipulation methods can exploit pretrained VLM capabilities for zero-shot manipulation, but rely on external planning and control scaffolds that are not end-to-end trainable as a unified continuous-action policy. In this work, we introduce EASG, an Explicit Action-Space Grounding framework that bridges these two paradigms. By projecting a tool center point (TCP)-centered action frame into the wrist camera observation and expressing actions in the form of VLM's native textual generation interface, EASG makes the semantics of a continuous robot action space directly observable in the VLM's perceptual context. This allows pretrained VLMs to directly generate executable robot actions using their existing visual-spatial reasoning capabilities, without robot-action pretraining or additional action modules. Besides, the same action interface supports direct fine-tuning with robot trajectories, allowing the models to continuously improve beyond their manipulation capabilities unleashed by EASG. Overall, EASG enables zero-shot manipulation and generalization across object positions, categories, and robot embodiments, while supporting effective fine-tuning in the same continuous action space. These results demonstrate that, given an appropriate action interface, pretrained VLMs can themselves serve as native action models for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [308] [Evidence-Guided Counterfactual Learning for Long-Tailed Robotic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12861)
*Weiwei Chen,Chenghao Xu,Meiling Yu,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为“证据引导的反事实学习”的框架，用于解决机器人模仿学习数据集中任务长尾分布导致的性能下降问题，通过识别和移除关键视觉证据来训练模型，使其更好地泛化到数据稀少的尾部任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范数据集存在长尾分布问题，即头部任务数据丰富，尾部任务数据稀少。这导致共享策略倾向于头部任务的行为模式，牺牲了对尾部任务的性能。特别是当尾部任务所需的视觉证据不足时，问题更加严重。

Method: 提出“证据引导的反事实学习”框架。该方法通过指令和动作信号识别与决策相关的视觉证据，然后构建移除该证据的反事实观察。利用反事实预测来加强指令、视觉证据和动作之间的联系，并根据任务演示数量自适应调整监督。该框架无需额外数据或标注，部署时无额外计算开销。

Result: 实验证明，所提出的框架能够稳健且一致地改善长尾机器人策略的学习，特别是对尾部任务的性能提升明显，同时保持头部任务的性能。

Conclusion: 证据引导的反事实学习是一种有效解决机器人策略长尾分布问题的训练框架，通过反事实数据增强来提升模型在数据稀疏任务上的表现，且无需额外资源。

Abstract: In the practical deployment of vision--language--action policies, handling long-tailed task distributions is a common challenge due to the highly imbalanced composition of robot demonstration datasets. To investigate this issue, we study the head-task action bias in shared robot policies, particularly focusing on its relationship with task-specific visual evidence.
