Our experimental analysis shows that, when the visual evidence required by a tail task is insufficient, the policy increasingly falls back on action patterns reinforced by data-rich head tasks, resulting in degraded performance on under-represented tasks. Motivated by this observation, we propose Evidence-Guided Counterfactual Learning, a training framework for long-tailed robot policies. Our method identifies decision-relevant visual evidence using instruction-related and action-related signals, and constructs counterfactual observations by removing the selected evidence. The resulting counterfactual predictions are used to strengthen the association among task instructions, visual evidence, and actions, with the supervision adaptively adjusted according to the number of demonstrations available for each task. The proposed framework requires neither additional training data nor region-level annotations and incurs no additional computation during deployment. Extensive experiments across simulated benchmarks, different VLA architectures, and real-world robot platforms demonstrate that our method robustly and consistently improves long-tailed robot policy learning, particularly for tail tasks, while maintaining reliable performance on head tasks.

</details>


### [309] [TAME-Nav: Temporal Asymmetric Mixture-of-Experts Distillation for Agile and Versatile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12877)
*Lingjie Zhang,Jun Xue,Shanze Wang,Haoyu Xi,Xinming Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 TAME-Nav 的教师-学生（Teacher-Student）导航框架，利用软混合专家（Soft-MoE）来处理复杂和动态的环境。该框架在模拟和真实机器人实验中均取得了优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的导航策略难以应对复杂且多变的场景，需要一种能够适应不同决策需求的框架。

Method: TAME-Nav 采用非对称教师-学生架构，教师利用干净的观测和动态障碍物轨迹构建特权表示，学生则通过损坏的观测历史、时间编码、软专家路由和潜在蒸馏来学习互补的导航特征。研究还采用了程序化场景生成、并行任务级仿真以及部署导向的非对称域随机化。

Result: 在分布内和未见的组合场景中，TAME-Nav 实现了更高的成功率、更低的碰撞率和更高的导航分数。在真实四足机器人上，该方法成功完成了在局部陷阱逃逸和狭窄通道导航中的所有测试，仅发生一次轻微接触。

Conclusion: TAME-Nav 框架能够有效地解决复杂和动态环境下的机器人导航问题，并在模拟和真实世界应用中展现出优越的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Agile and reliable navigation is a core capability for embodied robots navigating complex spatial constraints and dynamic interactions. However, a single policy often struggles to accommodate the distinct decision-making demands of diverse scenarios. We present TAME-Nav, an asymmetric Teacher-Student navigation framework built around a temporal soft mixture of experts (Soft-MoE). The Teacher combines clean observations with causally maintained dynamic-obstacle tracks to construct privileged representations, while the Student learns complementary navigation features from corrupted observation histories through temporal encoding, soft expert routing, and latent distillation. Procedural scenario generation and parallel task-level simulation broaden training coverage, while deployment-informed asymmetric domain randomization supports sim-to-real transfer. Across in-distribution and unseen compositional scenarios, TAME-Nav achieves higher success rates, lower collision rates, and higher navigation scores than representative baselines. On a real quadruped robot, it completes all twenty trials across local-trap escape and narrow-passage navigation with sudden dynamic obstacles, with only one minor contact.

</details>


### [310] [LeapBot-JAC: Joint Action Chains for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12886)
*Chenhao Zhu,Baibing Liu,Pei Liu,Rui Yang,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 LeapBot-JAC 的新方法，通过在共享动作专家中分配稀疏的未来控制参考和密集的执行目标，优化了机器人在固定动作令牌预算下的执行效率，并在多个基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在一个固定的动作令牌预算下，有效地组织预测的未来控制指令以支持实际执行，是一个关键问题。现有的方法在执行前会进行重新规划，这会影响效率。

Method: LeapBot-JAC 在一个共享的动作专家中同时分配稀疏的未来控制参考和密集的执行目标。通过定向注意力机制，执行令牌可以读取参考令牌，同时考虑主体的可见性约束。JAC 不会增加额外的动作令牌或参数，并保留了主体原有的世界建模过程。推理时，两个链条都参与，但只有执行链驱动机器人。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，JAC 显著提高了 FastWAM 和 LeapBot-WA 的成功率，分别从 97.0% 提高到 98.3%，从 97.3% 提高到 98.3%。在零样本 LIBERO-Plus 测试中，成功率分别从 53.5% 提高到 70.4%，从 73.1% 提高到 77.3%。此外，在 RoboTwin 和两个真实世界任务上也进行了部署评估。

Conclusion: LeapBot-JAC 是一种有效的策略，可以在固定动作令牌预算下优化机器人执行。其动作角色组织方式是一个实用的设计选择，能够有效提升机器人的性能，尤其是在复杂或零样本场景下。

Abstract: Action-chunking policies predict future controls but execute only a prefix before replanning. How should a fixed action-token budget be organized to support execution? We propose LeapBot-JAC, which allocates sparse future-control references and dense execution targets within one shared action expert. Both chains are supervised by demonstrated controls and jointly denoised at aligned physical-time positions. Directed attention allows execution tokens to read reference tokens subject to the host's visibility constraints. JAC adds neither action tokens nor parameters and retains the host's world-modeling process; both chains participate in inference, while only the execution chain drives the robot. On LIBERO, JAC reaches 98.3% success with FastWAM and LeapBot-WA, up from 97.0% and 97.3%. On zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, their success rates improve from 53.5% to 70.4% and from 73.1% to 77.3%, respectively. RoboTwin and two real-world tasks provide additional deployment evaluations. Single-training-seed comparisons and FastWAM ablations support action-role organization as a practical design choice under a fixed action-token budget.

</details>


### [311] [Sampling-based Kinodynamic Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12910)
*Yitian Gao,Hanlan Yang,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的动态规划方法，将采样搜索与视觉世界模型相结合，以解决长时域任务中的规划瓶颈，并在模拟和真实机器人实验中取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉世界模型的规划方法在处理需要中间运动（例如绕过障碍物）的长时域任务时效果不佳，因为这些运动本身不直接奖励。这促使研究人员探索新的规划策略。

Method: 该研究将运动规划中的采样技术（如RRT和KPIECE）应用于视觉世界模型的潜在空间，利用学习到的模型作为树搜索的传播器。作者提出了两种在潜在空间中探索的策略：最近邻引导的RRT和基于投影的KPIECE，并利用GPU进行并行计算加速。

Result: 在模拟任务中，作者的方法在规划时间上比CEM和MPPI快13.8倍，同时成功率相当或更高。在真实世界的积木推动任务中，成功率提高了20%。

Conclusion: 将传统的采样基运动规划技术扩展到视觉世界模型的潜在空间，可以有效地解决长时域任务中的规划问题，并且比基于优化的方法更高效。

Abstract: Visual world models predict the consequences of actions from images, but planning with them is typically done by optimizers such as CEM and MPPI. However, these optimizers stall on long-horizon tasks where progress requires motion the objective does not reward, such as moving around a block before pushing it. This paper revisits kinodynamic sampling-based motion planning for world models, using the learned model as the propagator of a tree search in latent space. Our key insight is that the sampling strategies devised for high-dimensional search in configuration space apply directly to the latent space of a world model, where they rapidly expand through intermediate states that myopic optimizers never sample. We present two exploration strategies for the high-dimensional latent space, nearest-neighbor-guided RRT and projection-based KPIECE, with GPU-native batched expansion for efficient device utilization. We evaluate with an off-the-shelf DINO-WM on four simulated domains and a real-world block-pushing task. Our approaches match or exceed the success rate of CEM and MPPI at up to 13.8x lower planning time in simulation, and improve the success rate by 20% on the real robot.

</details>


### [312] [Evo-World: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model with Future Motion Prediction via Optical-Flow Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12936)
*Zile Zhong,Yuxin Du,Tao Lin,Haoxiao Wang,Wei Dai,Binhua Zuo,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Evo-World 是一个轻量级的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型，通过光学流监督预测未来运动，以增强机器人控制能力，在模拟和现实世界任务中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型缺乏对未来场景变化的预测能力，这限制了其机器人控制的性能。单纯的动作监督无法显式地鼓励这种预测，而未来图像预测则会将任务相关的运动与静态外观混淆。

Method: 提出 Evo-World 模型，通过光学流监督来预测未来运动，为策略学习提供任务相关的预测信号。设计了一个保留预测表示的训练过程，以解耦表示学习和动作优化。

Result: 在 Meta-World MT50 基准测试中，Evo-World 达到了 91.1% 的平均成功率，比非预测性基线提高了 13.0%。在真实世界的 AgileX 机器人上，Evo-World 在单臂和双臂操作任务中优于基线，同时参数量更少，GPU 内存占用和推理效率更高。

Conclusion: 通过光学流监督进行未来运动预测，是增强 VLA 模型预测能力的一种有效且轻量级的方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for visual understanding, language grounding, and robot control, yet anticipating how the scene will evolve beyond the current observation can provide additional information for control. Action supervision alone does not explicitly encourage such anticipation, while future-image prediction entangles task-relevant motion with largely static appearance. To address these limitations, we introduce Evo-World, a lightweight VLA model that augments policy learning with future motion prediction through optical-flow supervision, thereby providing a task-relevant predictive signal focused on motion rather than appearance. We further design the training procedure to preserve these predictive representations during policy learning, regulating representation diversity while decoupling representation learning from action optimization. Across simulation benchmarks, Evo-World demonstrates strong performance, achieving a tier-averaged success rate of 91.1% on Meta-World MT50 , with a 13.0% relative improvement over the non-predictive baseline. On real-world AgileX robots, Evo-World outperforms the evaluated baselines across single-arm and bimanual manipulation tasks while using only 0.87B parameters and delivering lower GPU memory usage and higher inference efficiency. These results demonstrate that future motion prediction through optical-flow supervision provides an effective and lightweight approach to enhancing the predictive capabilities of VLA models.

</details>


### [313] [JoyInside-VLM-Agent: Product-Conditioned Multimodal Policy Learning for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12957)
*Gangyi Li,LuYao Wang,Jingfu Sun,Jingyi Yuan,Hang Liu,Hao Zong,Yujia Hu,Tingting Gao,Jianyong Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 JoyInside-VLM-Agent 的多模态智能体，它能够处理复杂的产品约束、目标变化和执行反馈，并在机器人和智能硬件上执行任务。该智能体通过一种名为 DPER 的新颖方法进行训练，该方法结合了产品约束轨迹生成、分阶段策略学习和双源特权自蒸馏技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的具身智能体需要将多模态观察和用户请求转化为可执行动作，同时适应产品约束、目标变化和执行反馈。现有的方法在处理这些复杂性和动态性方面存在不足。

Method: JoyInside-VLM-Agent 采用 ReAct 风格的多模态智能体框架，通过产品条件轨迹构建和分阶段策略学习进行开发。训练过程包括使用封闭式沙箱生成多模态交互轨迹（包括目标修订和失败恢复），并通过监督微调、多教师策略内蒸馏和 DPER（双源特权策略内自蒸馏，带令牌级证据路由）进行强化学习。DPER 实现了特权教师和学生之间的令牌级证据路由，并调节奖励导出的令牌优势。训练奖励结合了生成过程评估、多裁判排名和任务队列一致性。

Result: JoyInside-VLM-Agent 在 BFCL v4 上取得了 77.44 的总体分数，优于基线。在 JoyInside-Devices 和 JoyInside-Robotics 上，该系统在所有指标上均领先于基线。在两个真实的四足机器人案例研究中，证明了其在视觉基础搜索、执行失败后的重新规划和明确的失败报告方面的能力。

Conclusion: JoyInside-VLM-Agent 是一种有效的多模态具身智能体，能够在复杂和动态的环境中执行任务。其提出的 DPER 训练方法和轨迹生成策略在各种基准测试和实际应用中均表现出色。

Abstract: Embodied agents must translate multimodal observations and user requests into executable actions while adapting to product constraints, changing goals, and execution feedback. We present JoyInside-VLM-Agent, a ReAct-style multimodal agent developed through product-conditioned trajectory construction and staged policy learning for robotic platforms and smart hardware. A closed-loop sandbox generates multimodal interaction trajectories covering goal revisions and failure recovery, which are checked against execution outcomes and semantic criteria. Supervised fine-tuning and multi-teacher on-policy distillation precede reinforcement learning with DPER (Dual-Source Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Token-Level Evidence Routing). DPER routes token-level evidence between a frozen privileged teacher and a privileged student sharing the rollout policy's parameters, modulating reward-derived token advantages while preserving their signs. Training rewards combine generative process evaluation, multi-judge rankings, and task-queue consistency. JoyInside-VLM-Agent achieves a BFCL v4 overall score of 77.44, exceeding the evaluated baselines; results on the other public benchmarks are mixed. On JoyInside-Devices and JoyInside-Robotics, the evaluated system, which includes domain-specific skill refinement, leads the baselines on all reported metrics. Two real-world quadruped case studies illustrate visually grounded search, replanning after execution failure, and explicit failure reporting.

</details>


### [314] [Adaptive Whole-Body MPC with Optimal Posture Planning for Humanoid Robots under Unknown Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12972)
*Manh Cuong Nguyen,Duc Cuong Vu,Viet Anh Vu,Duc Tuan Pham,Toan Nguyen,Minh Hiep Phung,Ngo Anh Vien,Minh Nhat Vu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应全身控制框架，用于解决人形机器人在处理未知载荷时维持平衡的问题，该框架结合了MPC、自适应模糊平衡控制器和最优姿态规划器，通过实验证明其提高了平衡鲁棒性并减少了姿态转换和提升任务中的跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在执行提放和物料搬运任务时，需要应对未知和变化的载荷，而载荷的不确定性会对全身动力学产生显著影响，可能导致传统控制器不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种自适应全身控制架构，包括：1) 一个质心模型预测控制器（MPC），生成动力学上可行的参考运动；2) 一个自适应模糊平衡控制器，通过产生分布在支撑腿关节上的校正扭矩来补偿载荷引起模型失配；3) 一个基于三积分模型的路径点最优姿态规划器，生成满足质心（CoM）稳定性约束、关节限制和全身可行性要求的平滑坐站和站坐轨迹。

Result: 该框架能够实现稳定地下降、抓取、提升、搬运和放置物体，即使在载荷不确定性很大的情况下。仿真和硬件实验表明，与非自适应基线相比，该框架提高了平衡鲁棒性，并减少了姿态转换和提升任务中的跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应全身控制框架能够有效地解决人形机器人在处理未知载荷时的平衡问题，显著提高了机器人在执行复杂操作任务时的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: Humanoid robots performing lifting and material-handling tasks must maintain balance despite unknown and varying payloads. However, payload uncertainty significantly affects whole-body dynamics and may destabilize conventional controllers. We propose an adaptive whole-body control architecture that integrates three components: (i) a centroidal model predictive controller (MPC) that generates dynamically feasible reference motions, (ii) an adaptive fuzzy balance controller that compensates for payload-induced model mismatch by generating corrective torques distributed across the supporting-leg joints, and (iii) a waypoint-based optimal posture planner built on a triple-integrator model that produces smooth sit-to-stand and stand-to-sit trajectories satisfying center-of-mass (CoM) stability constraints, joint limits, and whole-body feasibility requirements. Together, these components enable stable lowering, grasping, lifting, carrying, and placing behaviors under significant payload uncertainty. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework improves balance robustness and reduces tracking degradation during posture transitions and lifting tasks compared to non-adaptive baselines.

</details>


### [315] [EgoReveal: Learning Active Vision and Object Manipulation under Partial Occlusions from Egocentric Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12999)
*Hiromu Kurihara,Kazutoshi Tanaka,Masashi Hatano,Tadashi Kozuno,Hideo Saito,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoReveal 是一个从人类示范中学习机器人操控框架，通过学习重新定位视角来解决部分遮挡问题，从而提高任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于以自己为中心的示范学习方法在处理日常环境中常见的物体部分遮挡问题时存在不足。

Method: EgoReveal 框架包含两个模仿策略：一个根据当前视角和摄像头-手臂姿态预测未来的摄像头-手臂姿态序列，另一个根据物体关键点和控制命令历史预测未来的控制命令。该框架根据目标可见性在两个策略之间切换。

Result: 在物体初始部分被遮挡的实验中，EgoReveal 通过重新定位摄像头以获得可见性，并比固定摄像头的基线方法更可靠地完成了任务。

Conclusion: EgoReveal 能够有效地从以自己为中心的示范中学习视角重新定位和操控动作，从而克服部分遮挡带来的挑战，提高机器人操控任务的可靠性。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstrations (LfD) enables robots to learn object manipulation directly from human demonstrations. In contrast to resource-intensive and labor-intensive teleoperation, egocentric demonstrations are easy to collect, enabling scalable data collection. Manipulated objects are often partially occluded in everyday environments, yet prior egocentric demonstration-based methods that require no robots or specialized teaching devices do not explicitly address occlusions. To fill this gap, we propose EgoReveal, a framework that learns viewpoint-repositioning together with manipulation actions from egocentric human demonstrations to reposition the viewpoint and obtain target visibility under partial occlusions. EgoReveal instantiates this as two imitation policies: one predicts the future camera-arm pose sequence from the current view and camera-arm pose, and the other predicts the future control commands from object keypoints and a history of control commands. EgoReveal switches between the two based on target visibility. In experiments where the manipulated object is partially occluded initially, EgoReveal repositions the camera to obtain visibility and completes the task more reliably than fixed-camera baselines.

</details>


### [316] [CeDR-Nav: Cross-Embodiment Mapless Navigation via Offline Demonstration Recasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13007)
*Xianghui Wang,Xinming Zhang,Yiwei Qian,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CeDR-Nav的框架，无需在线收集数据，即可利用源域机器人收集的演示数据来训练目标域机器人。该框架通过几何一致缩放和每演示有效性评估来转换源域演示，并使用特定于目标域的高斯混合模型策略来生成可行的避障策略。实验证明了该方法的有效性，显著提高了跨域导航的成功率，并且训练速度极快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于学习的机器人导航方法在跨不同实体（embodiment）的导航时性能下降，现有方法需要耗时的在线训练，并且效果不如目标实体专门训练的策略。然而，源实体收集的演示数据包含了可重用的导航经验，但由于实体不匹配，无法直接用于监督目标实体的策略训练。

Method: CeDR-Nav框架通过以下步骤实现跨实体演示重塑：1. 几何一致缩放（Geometry-consistent scaling）和每演示有效性评估（per-demonstration validity assessment）将源演示转换为目标训练数据。2. 训练一个目标实体特定的高斯混合模型策略（target-specific Gaussian mixture policy）来生成可行的避障策略。

Result: 在多机器人平台的定量模拟和真实世界部署中，CeDR-Nav的平均成功率比DRL-DCLP在跨实体模拟中提高了13.8个百分点。目标实体特定策略的训练可以在几秒钟内完成。

Conclusion: CeDR-Nav框架能够有效地利用现有源域演示数据，为目标域机器人训练出高效的导航策略，无需在线数据收集，大大提高了训练效率和跨实体导航的性能。

Abstract: Learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance in mapless robot navigation. However, a learned policy remains restricted to the embodiment on which it was trained. Many cross-embodiment navigation methods require time-consuming online training and can still underperform target-specific policies. In fact, demonstrations collected on a source embodiment already contain navigation experience that is potentially reusable for deriving a policy for each target embodiment, but the embodiment mismatch prevents these demonstrations from directly serving as valid supervision for the target. In this article, we present CeDR-Nav, a cross-embodiment demonstration recasting framework that trains a dedicated navigation policy for each target embodiment from existing source demonstrations without online data collection. Geometry-consistent scaling with per-demonstration validity assessment converts the source demonstrations into target training data, and a target-specific Gaussian mixture policy generates feasible avoidance maneuvers for each target embodiment. Extensive quantitative simulations and real-world deployments across multiple robot platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of CeDR-Nav, with an average success-rate gain of 13.8 percentage points over DRL-DCLP in cross-embodiment simulations and target-specific policy training completed within seconds.

</details>


### [317] [Failure-Resilient Multi-Robot Coordination with $k$-Connectivity Constrained Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13049)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Hojune Kim,Ellen Novoseller,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 解析错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析错误

Method: 解析错误

Result: 解析错误

Conclusion: 解析错误

Abstract: Multi-robot coordination is essential in applications such as coverage, target tracking, and information gathering, where robots must jointly select actions to maximize team performance. Submodular maximization provides an effective framework for such coordination, and resilient variants further account for robot failures or adversarial removals. However, existing methods typically overlook the impact of robot loss on the communication network, which may disconnect the surviving team and prevent continued coordination. To address this issue, we introduce Resilient $k$-Connected Submodular Maximization, which jointly considers task performance, resilience, and communication connectivity. The resulting feasible family is not a matroid, preventing the direct application of classical curvature-greedy guarantees. We overcome this challenge through a Menger-based connectivity reduction, a connectivity-aware greedy algorithm with a feasibility-preserving fallback, and a new curvature-based analysis that captures the cost of enforcing connectivity through a single factor $ρ$. Our method achieves a worst-case post-failure objective within a factor of $[\max(1-ν_f(\mathcal{I}),h(N,β))]^2 ρ\frac{1}{κ_f}(1-e^{-κ_f})$ of the optimal connectivity-respecting resilient assignment, while recovering the classical unconstrained guarantee in the appropriate limits. Experiments on multi-robot target tracking validate the proposed method and characterize the practical impact of connectivity constraints.

</details>


### [318] [Towards Safer Cities: A Design Process for Perception Datasets in Urban Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13077)
*Alexander Kroth,Cristian Martinez,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于用户生成地图数据的城市自动驾驶数据集创建方法，重点关注卡车等大型车辆的特殊场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集在城市复杂场景和非乘用车（如卡车、公交车）的数据采集方面存在不足，而这些车辆在城市环境中面临独特的挑战。

Method: 通过分析现有数据集并结合用户生成地图数据，提出了一种指导性的数据集创建方法，并识别和举例说明了卡车特有的应用场景。

Result: 该方法能够支持针对卡车等大型车辆在城市特定场景下的数据采集规划。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法可以有效提升城市复杂环境中，特别是针对大型车辆的自动驾驶感知系统数据集的质量和代表性。

Abstract: Urban environments present significant challenges for the perception systems of automated driving due to their high complexity, characterized by dense traffic, diverse obstacles, or a wide range of possible scenarios. While numerous automotive datasets exist, few focus on urban settings, and the majority are acquired exclusively on passenger cars and overlook other transport modalities such as heavy-duty trucks, busses and trams. In contrast to passenger vehicles, these platforms operate under different geographical and operational constraints; including traversing pedestrian zones or platform-specific tasks such as movement of trailer or winter service. In this work a method is proposed for a guided dataset creation based on an analysis of existing datasets using user-generated map data. Furthermore, representative truck-specific use cases are identified and exemplified to demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methodology and to support targeted acquisition planning.

</details>


### [319] [VICAR: Video Imitation with Contact-Aware Retargeting for Humanoid Interactive Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13079)
*Dvij Kalaria,Bike Zhang,S. Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICAR 提出了一种从第三方人类视频中学习人形机器人交互技能的流水线，重点在于在身体不匹配和运行时场景变化的情况下保留接触姿态、运动速度和碰撞清除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频到人形系统在处理接触丰富的模仿方面存在不足，因为任务通常由世界帧接触定义，而这些接触必须能够适应身体不匹配和运行时场景变化。

Method: VICAR 流水线首先恢复人类运动，从单目视频中重建与任务相关的障碍物，然后将运动重新定向到人形机器人，同时保留全局接触姿态、运动速度和碰撞清除。它将用户指定的接触姿态和碰撞清除表示为高优先级拉格朗日惩罚项，并优化整个运动以在接触丰富的过渡中保持平滑。然后，学习到的运动用于训练接触条件的运动生成器，该生成器允许在运行时适应新的接触位置。

Result: 该框架在十项不同的第三方视频技能上得到了验证，包括乒乓球发球、物体拾取和爬梯，并在硬件上进行了部署。结果表明，接触保持重定向能够成功地将人类学习到的交互行为转移到人形机器人上。

Conclusion: 接触保持重定向是实现人形机器人通过观察人类来学习有用交互行为的一个实用步骤。

Abstract: Third-person human videos offer a scalable alternative to task-specific reward engineering, teleoperation, and manually scripted demonstrations for humanoid loco-manipulation. Recent video-to-humanoid systems have shown that noisy human motion, object trajectories, and contacts can be extracted from unstructured videos and refined into deployable skills. However, a key gap remains for contact-rich imitation: the task is often defined by world-frame contacts whose poses, timing, and speed must survive embodiment mismatch and runtime scene changes. Towards this, we present VICAR (Video Imitation with Contact-Aware Retargeting), a pipeline for learning humanoid interactive skills from third-person human videos. The system first recovers human motion, reconstructs task-relevant obstacles from monocular video, and then retargets the motion to a humanoid while preserving global contact poses, motion speed, and collision clearance. Unlike retargeting methods that only remove penetration or preserve local interaction geometry, our formulation represents user-specified contact poses and collision clearance as high-priority Lagrangian penalty terms and optimizes the full motion to remain smooth through contact-rich transitions. The resulting motions are used to train a contact-conditioned motion generator whose inputs include movable contact points enabling runtime adaptation to new contact positions. We demonstrate the framework on ten diverse third-person video skills, including six table-tennis serves, three object-pickup tasks, and ladder climbing, and deploy the learned skills on hardware. These results suggest that contact-preserving retargeting is a practical step toward humanoids that learn useful interactive behavior by watching humans.

</details>


### [320] [Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion: Object-Centric Flow Replanning for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13087)
*Chen Yu,Sudarshan S Harithas,Juan Borbon,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BOPI的框架，通过在线估计、基于流的规划和全身控制的闭环，使得人形机器人能够通过小推车探索未知质量和轮子刚度的推车。BOPI通过根据信念的变化调整规划，在模拟和硬件实验中都取得了比固定深度重规划更好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在与未知物体交互时，通常通过小幅度的探索性推本来学习其运动规律，并据此调整后续动作。受此启发，研究者希望开发出能够模仿这种学习和适应能力的人形机器人。

Method: 提出了一种面向对象的框架，将在线估计、基于流的规划和全身控制整合到一个闭环系统中。当对物体（推车）的信念发生变化时，该框架会进行规划的更新。具体方法是“信念重叠部分逆转”（Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion, BOPI），它通过只在信念不一致的范围内进行调整，从而在信念更新的同时保证了动作的平滑性。

Result: 在18个模拟目标下，BOPI将最终位置和航向误差平均降低了约33%和47%，优于固定的重规划方法。在硬件上，该方法成功地控制了非完整性推车的移动和转向。

Conclusion: BOPI框架有效地实现了机器人对未知物体（推车）的在线估计、规划和控制，克服了传统重规划方法在动态环境中存在的弊端，并成功在模拟和硬件上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: People often learn how an unfamiliar object moves through a small exploratory push, using its response to guide their next motion. Inspired by this behavior, we study humanoids pushing carts whose mass and caster stiffness are initially unknown. Our object-centric framework closes online estimation, flow-based planning, and whole-body control into a loop, creating a tension: replacing the plan whenever the belief shifts disrupts the maneuver under way, while keeping it strands the robot on a path its evidence has ruled out. Belief-Overlap Partial Inversion (BOPI) scales revision to agreement, inverting the flow under the old belief only as far as the beliefs disagree before reconstructing under the new one. Across 18 simulated goals it lowers pooled final position and yaw error by roughly 33% and 47% against fixed-depth replanning, and on hardware it translates and turns a non-holonomic cart.

</details>


### [321] [ReAT: Residual Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13093)
*Dijia Zhan,Zhuo Li,Xuemiao Xu,Sun Zheng,Jinyi Li,Wenjun Wang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReAT（Residual Action Tokenization）的新型动作分词器，它通过使每个离散令牌显式地修正动作重建误差，从而提升了自回归机器人策略的学习效果，并在多项模拟和真实世界任务中取得了优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分词器在将连续动作转化为离散序列时，未能明确每个令牌对最终动作重建的贡献，限制了自回归策略的精细化学习能力。

Method: ReAT 采用迭代的方式，利用先前解码动作的残差作为下一令牌生成的输入，从而使每个令牌都能直接修正当前动作的重建误差，形成一个编码-解码的闭环。

Result: 在19个模拟和真实世界的操纵任务中，8个令牌的ReAT策略在任务成功率上优于基线方法。在LIBERO基准测试中，仅用4个令牌的ReAT策略就能超越使用8个令牌的基线方法。

Conclusion: ReAT 通过赋予每个令牌明确的误差修正作用，促进了自校正策略的学习，使得自回归策略能够逐步优化其动作规划，从而在机器人控制任务中取得更好的表现。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot policies rely on action tokenizers to convert continuous action chunks into compact sequences of discrete prediction targets. Existing tokenizers can reconstruct action chunks from discrete codes, but often leave implicit what each individual token contributes to the reconstructed action. We introduce Residual Action Tokenization (ReAT), which gives each token an explicit role in refining the current action reconstruction. It uses the decoded reconstruction as feedback to construct the next token from the remaining action error. This closes the loop between encoding and decoding, making each new token responsive to the errors left by the actual decoded prefix. Across 19 manipulation tasks spanning three simulation benchmarks and real-world settings, 8-token ReAT policies outperform the evaluated baselines in task success; on LIBERO, even 4-token ReAT surpasses the evaluated 8-token action-tokenization baselines at half the token budget. These results suggest a path toward action tokenization that supports self-corrective policy learning, helping autoregressive policies learn to progressively refine their own action plans.

</details>


### [322] [Forward Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13117)
*Zipei Ma,Junzhe Jiang,Jiahui Zhang,Juntong Zhang,Chun Gu,Bozhou Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为前向动力学模型（FDM）的机器人动作模型，它通过将动作和未来世界状态的预测解耦，避免了现有方法的弊端，并在仿真和真实机器人实验中取得了显著的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型缺乏对未来世界状态的显式监督，而世界动作模型（WAM）在动作中引入了额外的推理成本，并且视觉预测误差会传播到动作中。作者旨在解决这些问题，提出一种更有效、计算成本更低的机器人动作模型。

Method: FDM 采用“动作到未来”的范式，将动作和未来世界状态的预测耦合起来。未来预测以动作特征为条件，而不是反之。这种端到端训练允许真实的未来状态通过动作特征来监督策略，避免了预测误差向动作传播。FDM 在推理时跳过了未来生成步骤，从而降低了计算成本。该模型支持多种骨干网络，并分别使用了视觉-语言模型（VLM）和视频生成模型（VGM）进行了实例化。

Result: 在 LIBERO 数据集上，FDM 取得了 99.4% 的平均成功率，达到了当前最佳水平。在真实机器人任务上，FDM 相较于 VLA 和 WAM 方法，成功率提升了高达 13%。

Conclusion: FDM 是一种有效的前向动力学模型，能够通过解耦动作和未来状态的预测来提高机器人策略学习的性能和效率。其“动作到未来”的范式避免了现有方法的固有缺陷，并在各种实验设置下均表现出色。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) have advanced robotic policy learning by building on pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) and video generative models (VGMs), respectively. Yet VLAs learn without explicit supervision from future world states, while WAMs that condition actions on predicted futures incur extra inference cost and let visual prediction errors propagate into actions. We present Forward Dynamics Models (FDM), a family of robotic action models that couples action and future world-state prediction through an action-to-future paradigm. Future prediction is conditioned on action features, never the reverse: end-to-end training lets ground-truth future states supervise the policy through these features, while prediction errors cannot propagate into actions. At inference, FDM skips future generation entirely, avoiding its computational cost. The paradigm is backbone-agnostic, and we instantiate it with both VLM and VGM backbones. Experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate the effectiveness of FDM across both backbone families, achieving a state-of-the-art 99.4% average success rate on LIBERO and absolute gains of up to 13% over VLA and WAM methods on real-world tasks.

</details>


### [323] [The value of information and the emergence of communication in evolved e-puck robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13118)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究了自主机器人之间通信的出现以及内在动作选择架构的作用，发现通信的出现与信息价值成正比，而固有的隔离式代理架构会阻碍社会性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自主机器人之间通信产生的机制，以及固有的动作选择架构在通信中的作用。

Method: 使用 PyBullet 模拟 e-puck 机器人，通过 GRU 循环网络实现控制，并引入一个嵌入式动作选择架构（EASA）进行对比。评估指标为机器人停留在食物区域的时间，并改变了是否有食物指示器的环境和场地大小。

Result: 在没有食物指示器的情况下，小场地中机器人发展出多种策略（参考信号、永久信标、无信号）。随着场地增大，参考信号策略泛化能力更强。EASA 中的固有反射会限制网络并降低性能，但随着优先级演化，这些反射会被削弱。

Conclusion: 通信的出现与信息传递的价值成正比；为隔离代理设计的固有脚手架在社会性任务中可能成为障碍。

Abstract: We study when communication emerges between autonomous agents and what role an innate action selection architecture plays. Two e-puck robots, reconstructed in PyBullet from their Webots model and validated against it, must find a feeding zone that can only be detected when a robot passes over it. Each robot can emit a one-bit signal whose direction is detected by the receiver. Control is provided by a GRU recurrent network with a residual path, either operating alone or as a channel within an Embedded Action Selection Architecture (EASA). Fitness measures only the time the robots spend over the food. When the robots receive a compass pointing to the food, use of the signal disappears completely. Without a compass, in a small arena, three independent runs achieved similar performance using different strategies: a referential signal, a permanent beacon, or no use of the signal. As the arena expands, the strategy based on a referential signal generalizes best. In contrast, evolving directly in the large arena does not increase the frequency of communication, but it does separate the strategies, and the referential signal clearly outperforms solitary searching. Within EASA, innate reflexes constrain the network and reduce performance. As its priorities evolve, evolution consistently weakens those reflexes. The results suggest that communication emerges in proportion to the value of the information it transmits, and that an innate scaffolding designed for isolated agents can be an impediment in a social task.

</details>


### [324] [Human2Dex: Dexterous Robot Learning from Wrist-Centric Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13119)
*Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human2Dex，一种以手腕为中心的人类演示接口，通过分离通用人类观察和动作语义与特定执行，实现不同灵巧手之间的演示数据复用，无需为每种机械手单独收集演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每种机械手收集灵巧操作演示数据需要重复进行任务特定的数据采集，耗时耗力。研究动机是探索是否能利用一份共享的、自然的、由人类进行的演示数据集，作为不同灵巧手的可重用数据接口，从而避免为每种目标机械手收集演示。

Method: Human2Dex 采用一种以手腕为中心的人类演示接口。它通过局部手部外观适应和共享的21点手部表示来对齐观察，确保了跨人类和机器人观察的一致结构语义。通过将融合后的人类手部运动重新定向到目标手部来构建可执行的监督信号。引入了抓握-口袋交互表示（GPIR）来表达相对于以手为中心的函数抓握口袋的追踪对象。然后，该语料库被转换为Linker O6和Wuji的独立训练集，尽管动作标签、归一化、训练和扩散策略检查点仍然是手部特定的。

Result: 在六项真实世界任务中，Human2Dex 在 Linker O6 上实现了 109/120 (90.8%) 的成功率，在 Wuji 上实现了 97/120 (80.8%) 的成功率。相比之下，仅使用人类原始演示（Human-Raw）在两台机械手上总共只实现了 63/240 (26.3%) 的成功率，而完整配置的 Human2Dex 则达到了 206/240 (85.8%) 的成功率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，以手腕为中心的人类演示可以作为一种可重用的接口，用于校准过的灵巧机械手之间的数据共享，同时保留了特定执行的策略学习。这有效地减少了数据收集成本，并提高了跨不同机械手的操作性能。

Abstract: Collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations for each robot hand requires repeated task-specific data acquisition. We ask whether one shared corpus of natural human demonstrations can instead serve as a reusable data interface for different dexterous hands, without collecting task demonstrations on each target robot. We present Human2Dex, a wrist-centric human demonstration interface that separates shared human observation and motion semantics from embodiment-specific execution. It aligns observations through local hand-appearance adaptation and a shared 21-point hand representation with consistent structural semantics across human and robot observations. It constructs executable supervision by retargeting fused human hand motion to each target hand, and introduces the Grasp-Pocket Interaction Representation (GPIR) to express tracked objects relative to a hand-centered Functional Grasp Pocket. The corpus is converted into separate training sets for Linker O6 and Wuji, while action labels, normalization, training, and diffusion-policy checkpoints remain hand-specific. Across six real-world tasks, Human2Dex achieves 109/120 (90.8%) successes on Linker O6 and 97/120 (80.8%) on Wuji. Across both hands, Human-Raw achieves 63/240 (26.3%) successes, whereas the complete Human2Dex configuration achieves 206/240 (85.8%). These results support the use of wrist-centric human demonstrations as a reusable interface across calibrated dexterous hands while retaining embodiment-specific policy learning. The project website is publicly available at https://human2dex.pages.dev/.

</details>


### [325] [Low-Latency Mapless ESDF Queries for MAV Reactive Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13158)
*Leonard Freißmuth,Cedric Le Gentil,Helen Oleynikova,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需预处理地图的、基于 GPU 加速的 ESDF 查询方法，可以直接处理深度图像，实现了快速、高精度的碰撞检测，并在实际森林飞行测试中显著降低了碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知、杂乱的环境中，机器人需要快速（毫秒级）地感知障碍物并进行运动纠正。现有的基于地图的方法需要预处理，这对于查询次数较少的近场、短视界反应式规划来说成本过高。

Method: 提出了一种无需地图、GPU 加速的 ESDF 查询方法，直接处理带姿态的深度图像。通过图像池化而非体素大小来设置分辨率，实现了极少的预处理。将此查询方法集成到模型预测控制器 (MPC) 中。

Result: 该方法在嵌入式 GPU 上，在同等分辨率下，比所有测量的体素网格配置在精度和总延迟方面都更优，且内存占用低 1-2 个数量级。在模拟森林环境中，该方法集成的 MPC 控制器可以将碰撞率从 5.9% 降低到 0.4%。

Conclusion: 提出的地图无关、GPU 加速的 ESDF 查询方法能够以更低的延迟和内存占用实现高精度的碰撞检测，适用于需要快速反应的机器人应用，并在实际森林飞行测试中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Reactive collision avoidance in unknown, cluttered environments must perceive obstacles and correct motion within milliseconds. Existing methods rely on maps for high-rate collision queries, but their acceleration structures require pre- processing. That cost is amortized in long-horizon planning, where a map serves millions of queries; reactive planning, however, is a near-field, short-horizon problem with potentially only hundreds of queries per measurement, where building a map may not pay off. We propose a mapless, Graphics Processing Unit (GPU)-accelerated Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF) query with negligible pre-processing that operates directly on posed depth images, with resolution set by pooling the image rather than by a voxel size. On an embedded GPU it is simultaneously more accurate and lower in total latency than every voxel-grid configuration we measured between 2 cm and 20 cm, at one to two orders of magnitude less memory at matched resolution. With our query integrated into a Model Predictive Controller (MPC), simulated runs in laser-scanned forests at up to 9 m/s collide in 0.4 % through dense branches, against 5.9 % with the best voxel grid and 5.5 % with the best mapless baseline at our resolution, whose failures we attribute to voxel resolution and total latency respectively. We deploy the system onboard at two forest sites and fly at commanded speeds of up to 4 m/s.

</details>


### [326] [Grounded-Dreamer: Robot Learning from World Model Synthetic Data with Physical Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13164)
*Feng Jiang,Shu Chen,Yuanfei Wang,Yuan Li,Zhenhao Shen,Jiaqi Liang,Yang Chen,Lei Wei,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Grounded-Dreamer是一个新的框架，它利用视频世界模型和模拟器物理引擎自动生成用于机器人操作任务的、具有物理依据的合成数据，无需人工演示，从而有效促进了机器人策略的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界中收集用于训练机器人操作策略的大量、多样化且带有动作标签的数据成本高昂且难以规模化。现有方法要么依赖于人类遥操作，要么依赖于视觉真实性有限的模拟，要么使用缺乏具身特定标注的人类视频。

Method: Grounded-Dreamer从少量真实图像开始，自动重建任务相关的物体和相机几何，在模拟器中对齐它们，并收集物理上有效的轨迹。然后，这些轨迹被用来后训练视频世界模型和逆动力学模型，从而能够从真实图像生成视觉上逼真的机器人操作视频和相应的机器人动作。为了过滤掉不符合物理规律的模拟结果，该框架会在重建的模拟环境中回放生成的轨迹，只保留成功完成任务的轨迹。策略学习完全基于这些合成轨迹。

Result: 该方法能够合成具有物理依据的数据，并在真实机器人操作任务中极大地促进了策略的学习，而无需人工遥操作。

Conclusion: Grounded-Dreamer成功地解决了机器人操作数据收集的挑战，通过结合生成视频模型和模拟器物理引擎，实现了高效、无需人工演示的数据合成，并显著提升了策略学习的效果。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation policies requires large amounts of diverse, action-labeled data, yet collecting such data in the real world remains expensive and difficult to scale. Existing methods either depend on human teleoperation, rely on simulation with limited visual realism, or use human videos that lack embodiment-specific annotations. In this paper, we introduce Grounded-Dreamer, a framework for synthesizing physically grounded robot manipulation data with video world models while avoiding human teleoperation demonstrations. Grounded-Dreamer combines the visual realism of generative video models with simulator physics engines. Starting from a small set of real images depicting robot task initial states, our method automatically reconstructs task-relevant objects and camera geometry, aligns them in simulation, and collects physically valid trajectories. These trajectories are used to post-train both a video world model and an inverse dynamics model, enabling the generation of visually realistic robot manipulation videos and corresponding robot actions from real images. To filter physically implausible rollouts, Grounded-Dreamer replays generated trajectories in the reconstructed simulation and retains only those that successfully complete the tasks. The real-world robot policies are trained solely on the grounded synthetic trajectories without any human demonstrations. Empirical results in real-world tasks demonstrate that Grounded-Dreamer can synthesize physically grounded data and substantially facilitate policy learning without requiring human teleoperation effort.

</details>


### [327] [Prediction-Informed Landing Window Planning for UAV Landing on USV in High Sea States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13190)
*Kaizhen Li,Xi Zhou,Dan Zhang,Xiaowei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测的 Uav 自主降落 Usv 的分层控制框架，通过显式预测 Usv 的姿态来改进降落窗口选择和着陆时机规划，以应对高海况下的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Uav 在高海况下自主降落 Usv 的方法，因其隐式推断 Usv 未来运动的局限性，难以适应严峻且快速变化的海况。

Method: 开发一个预训练模型来显式预测 Usv 在高层降落决策过程中的姿态。同时，使用传统的低层飞行控制器来维持 Uav 稳定性和跟踪运动指令。构建了一个真实的仿真验证平台，将真实海况下的 Usv 运动数据传输到 MuJoCo Usv 中进行实验。

Result: 实验证明，通过显式姿态预测，可以改进降落窗口的选择，并支持在快速变化海洋环境中进行时机感知的 Uav 降落规划。

Conclusion: 显式姿态预测是一种有效的方法，可以提高 Uav 在恶劣海况下自主降落 Usv 的性能，特别是在窗口选择和时机规划方面。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) on unmanned surface vehicle (USV) is difficult in high sea states. Existing hierarchical landing methods often infer future USV motion only implicitly from short observation histories, which limits their ability to adapt to severe and rapidly changing sea conditions. This paper presents a prediction-informed hierarchical control framework. A pretrained model is developed to explicitly predict the USV attitude during high-level landing decisions. The prediction helps identify suitable landing windows and choose the right touchdown time, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains UAV stability and tracks motion commands. To evaluate the framework under realistic disturbances, a real-sim validation setup is constructed in which USV-motion data collected in real sea conditions are transferred to a MuJoCo USV. The experiments demonstrate the feasibility of using explicit attitude prediction to improve landing-window selection and support timing-aware UAV landing planning in rapidly varying marine environments.

</details>


### [328] [PATACON: GPU-Parallel Tangent-Bundle RRT-Connect for Constrained Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13195)
*Gahyun Oh,Junho An,Suhyun Jeon,Suhan Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PATACON的GPU并行双向规划器，该规划器通过在切空间中进行探索来利用约束函数诱导的局部流形几何，从而提高了受约束的全身体运动规划的效率，特别是在高维和高约束问题上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的受约束运动规划方法计算成本高，尤其是在高维和高约束问题上，因为需要反复进行约束投影。尽管GPU并行规划器有所改进，但它们仍在整个配置空间中进行搜索，这导致了大量的开销。

Method: 提出了一种名为PATACON的GPU并行双向规划器。PATACON在切空间中进行探索，以利用由已知约束函数引起的局部流形几何，从而减少了约束投影的开销。

Result: 在双臂封闭链和人形全身约束规划任务上的实验表明，PATACON在配置空间维度和约束余维度增加时，性能优于现有方法，最高可达11.25倍的加速。

Conclusion: GPU并行切空间探索使得受约束的全身体运动规划更接近实时性能。

Abstract: Fast whole-body motion planning enables continuous replanning for humanoid robots in dynamic environments while satisfying collision avoidance and kinematic constraints. However, constrained motion planning incurs substantial computational cost due to repeated constraint projection in addition to collision checking and nearest-neighbor search. Recently, GPU-based massively parallel planners have been proposed to reduce computational cost. Nevertheless, these planners still steer in the ambient configuration space, so projecting candidates that deviate from the manifold remains a significant source of overhead, particularly in highly constrained, high-dimensional problems. In this work, we introduce PATACON, a GPU-parallel bidirectional planner that explores tangent space to exploit the local manifold geometry induced by known constraint functions. Experiments on dual-arm closed-chain and humanoid whole-body constrained planning tasks show that PATACON outperforms the prior work as configuration-space dimension and constraint codimension grow, achieving up to an 11.25 times speedup. These results suggest that GPU-parallel tangent-space exploration brings constrained whole-body motion planning closer to real-time performance.

</details>


### [329] [Beyond Actions: Learning Future Operation Targets for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13212)
*Min Lin,Bokai Zhou,Jianqi Lin,Kaiyuan Yang,Yicheng Chang,Junjie Yu,Xiaofan Liu,Jianhua Han,Rui Li,Shen Zhao,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AFCE（Action--Future Coupled Effect Learning）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，它将预测目标从动作块转变为未来物理效果，并通过离线分词器和流匹配VLA实现，在DexJoCo基准测试中显著提高了成功率，特别是在双臂协调任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略主要通过预测动作块来学习，但这种方法没有明确描述动作所能达到的目标。研究人员希望通过预测动作的未来物理效果来改进VLA策略，从而更好地理解和执行任务。

Method: AFCE策略主要预测未来物理效果。首先，使用离线分词器从演示动作、机器人响应和稀疏的视觉转换中构建按时间顺序排列的效果（Effect）标记。然后，使用重构目标使表征保留可执行细节和观察到的物理变化。最后，一个基于流匹配的VLA模型从当前图像、指令和机器人状态预测效果，而一个状态条件解码器将效果转换为动作块。该方法还通过具身特定的接口支持单臂和双臂任务。

Result: 在DexJoCo基准测试中，AFCE实现了50.97%的总体成功率，比π0.5和UVT分别提高了11.82和15.21个百分点。AFCE在所有五个双臂任务上均取得了领先，证明了效果监督对于协调的灵巧操作的价值。

Conclusion: AFCE通过将预测目标从动作块转向未来的物理效果，并利用效果标记和流匹配模型，显著提升了VLA策略的性能，尤其是在复杂的双臂协调任务中，证明了效果导向学习的有效性。

Abstract: Actions specify what a robot should execute, but do not describe what those commands will achieve. Vision-language-action (VLA) policies nevertheless learn primarily by predicting action chunks from images, language, and state. We introduce Action--Future Coupled Effect Learning (AFCE), which makes a future-grounded physical Effect the policy's primary prediction target. An offline tokenizer constructs temporally ordered Effect tokens from demonstrated actions, realized robot responses, and sparse visual transitions. Reconstruction objectives encourage the representation to retain executable detail and observed physical change. A flow-matching VLA predicts Effects from current images, instruction, and robot state, while a state-conditioned decoder converts them into action chunks. Embodiment-specific interfaces allow single- and dual-arm tasks to share the Effect model. On DexJoCo, AFCE achieves $50.97\%$ overall success, improving over $π_{0.5}$ and UVT by 11.82 and 15.21 percentage points, respectively. It leads on all five bimanual tasks, highlighting the value of Effect supervision for coordinated dexterous manipulation. The project page is available at https://afceproject.github.io/AFCE-Web/.

</details>


### [330] [Dual-Stream Memory for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13262)
*Yang Wang,Ping Liu,Ruiyu Wang,Xuhua Chen,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种双流记忆机制，用于视觉-语言-动作策略，通过任务无关的门控 DeltaNet 压缩历史信息，并通过任务条件检索器保留相关帧，从而提高机器人在需要记忆的任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够跟踪执行状态并恢复不再可见的视觉证据的策略，以实现记忆依赖的任务操作。

Method: 提出了一种双流记忆机制，包括：1. 任务无关的 Gated DeltaNet，通过进度预测和动作条件特征重构进行监督，将历史压缩成一个时间标记。2. 任务条件检索器，保留相关性高于固定阈值的帧；线性多尺度融合将每帧编码成十六个空间标记。在检索和策略学习过程中，时间压缩器被冻结。

Result: 在 RoboMME 的十六个任务中，该模型实现了 49.22% 的平均成功率，优于现有方法 FrameSamp+Modul（44.51%），并提高了十四个任务和所有四个套件的平均值。在四个真实机器人任务中，平均成功率达到 62.5%，显著优于 37.5%，尤其是在需要执行阶段判别的重复动作任务中。

Conclusion: 将执行上下文与选择性视觉访问相结合是有效的，尤其是在需要区分执行阶段的任务中。然而，在杯子交换等任务中出现的失败表明，身份跟踪仍然是一个未解决的挑战。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires a policy to track execution state and recover visual evidence that is no longer observable. We propose dual-stream memory for vision-language-action policies, assigning these requirements to complementary representations. A task-agnostic Gated DeltaNet compresses history into one temporal token, supervised by progress prediction and action-conditioned feature reconstruction. A task-conditioned retriever retains frames above a fixed relevance threshold; linear multi-scale fusion encodes each frame into sixteen spatial tokens. We then freeze the temporal compressor and jointly optimize retrieval and policy learning. Across sixteen RoboMME tasks, the complete model achieves 49.22% mean success versus 44.51% for the published FrameSamp+Modul reference, improving fourteen tasks and all four suite means. On four real-robot tasks, mean success reaches 62.5% versus 37.5%, with the largest gains on repeated actions requiring execution-stage discrimination. These results support combining execution context with selective visual access, while failures under cup swaps expose persistent identity tracking as an unresolved requirement.

</details>


### [331] [Evo-RL: Iterative Robot Reinforcement Learning from Selective Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13345)
*Kai Ye,Yifan Jin,Qianhang Luo,Hongru Zhang,Jiedong Zhang,Shuyuan Hu,Song-Yuan Wu,Jihai Xu,Jie Zhao,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Evo-RL 提出了一种结合人类干预和优势引导经验选择的迭代强化学习方法，通过选择性地重用有价值的经验来改进机器人策略，并在高精度机器人操作任务中取得了显著的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真实世界强化学习方法在策略改进中，经验的价值参差不齐，即使是成功的轨迹也可能包含低效或冗余的行为，因此需要更具选择性的经验重用。

Method: Evo-RL 是一种迭代强化学习方法，它结合了人类干预和来自成功轨迹的具有正估计优势的经验。通过人类纠正和高优势经验来迭代地改进策略。

Result: 在真实世界的高精度操作任务中，Evo-RL 相较于 RECAP 将尝试成功率提高了 30.46%。

Conclusion: Evo-RL 通过优势引导的经验选择，能够更有效地利用不同来源的经验（人类干预和自主学习），从而在真实世界的机器人策略学习中取得更好的性能。

Abstract: Existing real-world reinforcement learning methods improve robot policies by combining human corrections with autonomous rollouts. However, accumulated experience varies in its value for policy improvement, and even successful trajectories can contain inefficient or redundant behavior, motivating more selective experience reuse. To this end, we propose Evo-RL, an iterative reinforcement learning method that combines human interventions with advantage-guided experience selection. Evo-RL uses human corrections and experience with positive estimated advantages from successful trajectories to iteratively refine the policy. To support learning from these different experience sources, we introduce RW-RL-Dataset, which spans over 1,000 hours across four robot embodiments. On a real-world high-precision manipulation task, Evo-RL improves attempt success rate over RECAP by 30.46\%. Our code, model checkpoints, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [332] [PartiGen: Part-Aware Representations for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13373)
*Chuxuan Huang,Zhanxiang Cao,Tianyue Zhan,Haoyang Li,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Chenlin Ming,Keqi Zhu,Yizhi Chen,Shengcheng Fu,Zihao Zhou,Xinyi Peng,Kaixuan Zhang,Boyang Xing,Jiawei Bao,Yang Yang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PartiGen的框架，用于将文本描述转化为类人机器人运动，通过整合解剖学结构和运动历史，并引入了前向运动学监督和序列结束预测器，显著提高了运动的准确性、协调性和执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语言转化为人形机器人运动存在挑战，需要协调身体部位、确定运动时长并生成适合机器人执行的参考。现有方法在这些方面存在不足。

Method: PartiGen是一个部分感知的潜在扩散框架。它将解剖学结构整合到共享的全身运动表示中，通过部分特定的注意力机制处理局部肢体信息，并结合全身上下文。模型以文本和运动历史为条件，自回归生成运动原语。通过前向运动学监督来保证运动学一致性，并使用学习到的序列结束预测器来确定生成结束帧。

Result: 在HumanML3D数据集上，PartiGen的FID得分（3.165）比TextOp低16.8%，R@1提高了1.4个百分点。在时长研究中，序列结束预测器将平均绝对时长误差从12.471帧降低到3.080帧。使用GRIT追踪器评估，81.8%的生成参考在MuJoCo模拟中通过了稳定性测试，并且在真实G1机器人上，26次尝试中有26次（100%）成功执行。

Conclusion: PartiGen成功地实现了从文本到类人机器人运动的生成，通过考虑解剖学结构、运动历史和引入关键的生成后处理步骤（如时长预测和运动学约束），大幅提升了运动生成的质量和可执行性，并在仿真和真实机器人实验中得到了验证。

Abstract: Translating language into humanoid motion requires coordinating body parts, determining motion duration, and producing references suitable for robot execution. We introduce PartiGen, a part-aware latent diffusion framework that incorporates anatomical structure into a shared whole-body motion representation. Part-specific attention processes anatomically masked frame features, and its outputs are fused to combine local limb information with whole-body context. Conditioned on text and motion history, the model generates motion primitives autoregressively. Forward-kinematics supervision encourages kinematic consistency, while a learned end-of-sequence predictor selects a frame-level endpoint after generation. On HumanML3D retargeted to the Unitree G1, PartiGen achieves a segment-level FID of 3.165, a 16.8% reduction over TextOp, and improves R@1 by 1.4 percentage points. In the duration study with 16-frame primitives, endpoint prediction reduces mean absolute duration error from 12.471 to 3.080 frames compared with a fixed-length cutoff. We further evaluate robot execution using a pretrained, frozen GRIT tracker: 409 of 500 generated references (81.8%) pass the posture-based stability screen in MuJoCo. Following simulation screening and manual selection, 26 of 27 attempted motions are judged successful on the real G1.

</details>


### [333] [EndoRSI: Physical-Evidence-Driven Recursive Improvement of Endoscopic Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13376)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Tianchun Wu,Siu Hing Lui,Hanlin Zhao,Huxin Gao,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为EndoRSI的机器人自我改进系统，该系统利用机器人实验的物理证据来迭代地开发和改进内窥镜机器人的策略，从而显著提高了插入成功率，并展示了其在面对硬件和视觉变化时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的机器人策略研究严重依赖人类科学家和工程师来解释实验失败并指导控制器修改，效率低下。研究旨在自动化这一过程，实现机器人自我改进。

Method: 开发了EndoRSI系统，包含一个名为EndoForge的编码代理。该代理通过物理实验收集证据，迭代地开发和优化内窥镜机器人的策略。物理执行在人类授权的安全约束下进行。实验表明，代理能够根据物理反馈拒绝不恰当的策略，并改进策略以克服在离线数据中未观察到的问题。

Result: 在动力内窥镜检查任务中，EndoRSI系统显著提高了插入成功率，从初始代的2/21提升到第四代的17/21。最终确定的策略在视觉和硬件变化下仍保持高成功率，并且在六个独立的离体猪体样本上表现优于其他自主策略。

Conclusion: 物理机器人交互可以作为驱动机器人策略研究的实验证据，EndoRSI系统证明了人类控制下的机器人自我改进在科学发现中的有效性，并有望加速机器人领域的研究进程。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is beginning to automate scientific discovery, yet physical robot-policy research still relies on scientists and engineers to interpret experimental failures and decide how controllers should be revised. We present EndoRSI (Endoscopic Recursive Self-Improvement), a human-governed, self-improving robotic research system in which the embedded EndoForge coding agent iteratively develops and refines endoscopic robot policies using evidence from physical experiments. Physical execution remains isolated within a safety-constrained runtime under human authorisation. In motorised endoscopy, validation evidence first caused the agent to reject an initial action representation, while subsequent physical experiments falsified an offline-supported insertion strategy by revealing hazardous closed-loop behaviour absent from the source data. Failed physical rollouts were then incorporated into policy revision; insertion success subsequently increased from 2/21 in the first generation to 17/21 in the fourth. The frozen final policy retained competence across visual and hardware shifts and was the best performing autonomous policy across six independent ex-vivo porcine specimens. These results show that physical robot interaction can serve as experimental evidence for agent-driven robot policy research.

</details>


### [334] [Robot-WATCHDOG : failure detection through object-centric graph representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13403)
*Quentin Rolland,Fabrice Mayran de Chamisso,Jean-Baptiste Mouret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Robot-WATCHDOG的自监督框架，用于从正常演示中检测机器人策略中的故障，通过将场景建模为对象中心的时空图，并引入两种互补的故障评分机制：轨迹投影和相关性（TPC）以及图注意力网络与Transformer（GnnT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界环境中，显式定义所有可能的失败是不切实际的，因此需要一种仅凭正常演示就能检测失败的方法。现有方法在鲁棒性和捕捉语义/交互层面错误之间难以平衡。

Method: 将操作场景建模为对象中心的时空图。引入两种互补的故障评分：1. 轨迹投影和相关性（TPC），一种衡量几何偏差和关系一致性的非参数方法。2. GnnT，一个图注意力网络与Transformer结合，用于预测未来对象状态。

Result: TPC和GnnT在公共基准和新的语义复杂操作任务数据集上均优于基于图像的故障检测器，特别是在语义和交互层面故障检测方面。TPC在AUPR和MCC指标上表现最佳，且在不同随机种子下的方差较低。

Conclusion: Robot-WATCHDOG框架及其提出的TPC和GnnT故障评分机制能够有效地从正常演示中检测机器人操作中的故障，尤其擅长处理语义和交互层面的复杂错误，提供了比现有方法更优的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable real-time failure detection is critical for deploying learned robotic policies, yet defining failures explicitly is infeasible in open-world settings. This motivates failure detection from nominal demonstrations alone. Existing methods, however, struggle to balance robustness to benign variations with the ability to capture semantic and interaction-level errors. We introduce Robot-WATCHDOG, a self-supervised framework that models manipulation scenes as object-centric spatio-temporal graphs. Building on this representation, we investigate two complementary failure scores: Trajectory Projection and Correlation (TPC), a non-parametric measure of geometric deviation and relational consistency, and GnnT, a Graph Attention Network with a Transformer that predicts future object states. We evaluate both on a public benchmark and on a new dataset of semantically complex manipulation tasks, across six tasks and ten random seeds. Both scores outperform image-based failure detectors, particularly on semantic and interaction-level failures. TPC provides the strongest performance in AUPR and MCC on both dataset averages, with low variance across seeds. Qualitative results are made available at https://cea-list.github.io/Robot-WATCHDOG/.

</details>


### [335] [ManeuverFlow: Maneuver Hypothesis Generation with Future Evidence for Trajectory Evaluation and Refinement in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13409)
*Yuhao Wei,Yong Chen,Chen Yang,Yingchen Wang,Delin Ouyang,Lingfeng Qi,Qi Lan,Zhanhua Zhang,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManeuverFlow 是一种端到端的规划方法，它通过生成特定意图的轨迹，并结合当前和未来场景信息进行评估和优化，以解决复杂场景下的驾驶规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶规划方法在处理复杂场景时存在局限性，例如多查询回归可能导致模式崩溃，而基于锚点和生成式规划器则面临候选集大小的权衡问题，要么覆盖不足，要么成本过高。

Method: ManeuverFlow 采用一种新颖的端到端框架，首先生成不同意图的轨迹，然后利用当前和预测的未来场景信息（通过世界模型获取）来评估和优化这些轨迹。具体而言，它选择相关的驾驶意图模式，从中采样，并通过一个路由到特定意图的混合专家（MeanFlow）来生成轨迹。随后，它提取沿轨迹的当前和未来证据，用于预测 PDM 因子。一个精炼器利用同样的证据预测轨迹的局部 Frenet 残差，最后对精炼和保留的轨迹进行联合重新评分以进行选择。

Result: ManeuverFlow 在 NAVSIM v1/v2 navtest 基准测试中分别取得了 90.6% 的 PDMS 和 90.1% 的 EPDMS。在 WorldEngine 的 3DGS 闭环基准测试中，在记录交通和 IDM 控制交通的场景下，分别获得了最高的 75.19% 和 74.58% 的 PDMS* 分数。

Conclusion: ManeuverFlow 能够有效地处理复杂场景下的驾驶规划问题，通过生成并精炼特定意图的轨迹，并结合未来情景预测，取得了在多项基准测试中的领先性能。

Abstract: End-to-end planning in complex scenes must model diverse driving intentions and select the most appropriate trajectory using scene context and future risks. However, multi-query regression may collapse to one mode, while anchor-based and generative planners share a scaling bottleneck: small candidate sets limit maneuver coverage or discovery of high-quality trajectories, whereas large sets increase generation and scoring costs. We propose ManeuverFlow to generate trajectories for distinct intentions in one step and evaluate and refine them using current and future evidence. The model selects relevant maneuver modes, draws corresponding noise from the Gaussian mixture prior, and maps it to intention-specific trajectories through a maneuver-routed mixture-of-experts MeanFlow field. Current features and future features predicted by the world model are aggregated along each candidate path into trajectory-specific evidence for PDM-factor prediction. Using the same evidence, the refiner predicts local Frenet residuals for selected candidates; refined and retained candidates are then jointly re-scored for selection. With a lightweight TransFuser-style ResNet-34 backbone, ManeuverFlow achieves 90.6 PDMS and 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v1/v2 navtest, respectively. On WorldEngine's recent 3DGS closed-loop benchmark, it attains the highest listed PDMS$^\ast$ scores of 75.19 and 74.58 under logged and IDM-controlled traffic, respectively. Code: https://github.com/wyh-autonomy/maneuverflow.

</details>


### [336] [Reflect-VLA: Internalizing Reflective Guidance via Dual-Path On-Policy Self-Distillation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13453)
*Yuechen Luo,He Chong,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Hexin Zhang,Ruiqi Zhang,Ziying Song,Lei Yang,Zhi-Xin Yang,Fuxi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Reflect-VLA 模型，通过内部化“反射式引导”的规划优势，利用双路径策略内自我蒸馏（Dual-OPSD）技术，在单次推理中生成高质量驾驶轨迹，显著提高了规划性能并减少了训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习（RL）方法依赖于单一的轨迹奖励信号，无法精确定位规划错误或提供修正指令，尤其在长尾场景下效果不佳。反射式引导可以提供明确的诊断和纠正信息，但需要多次迭代，不适用于单次推理。

Method: 提出 Reflect-VLA 模型，将反射式引导作为训练时的特权信息。通过双路径策略内自我蒸馏（Dual-OPSD）将规划优势内化。Dual-OPSD 使用驾驶分数来路由学生生成的轨迹，并将教师模型的密集、面向 token 的修正分布蒸馏给高风险轨迹，同时通过自我模仿保留可靠轨迹。教师模型通过预训练、SFT 和重采样逐步构建。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1/v2 和 Bench2Drive 数据集上，Reflect-VLA 取得了具有竞争力的规划性能，并将训练时间缩短了 90% 以上。

Conclusion: Reflect-VLA 能够通过自我蒸馏有效利用反射式引导的优势，在单次推理中实现高效且准确的驾驶轨迹规划，克服了传统 RL 方法的局限性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) substantially improves planning performance in autonomous-driving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, existing RL methods rely on scalar trajectory rewards that measure overall quality but fail to identify specific planning errors or provide actionable correction signals. In long-tail scenarios, errors in scene understanding, behavior selection, and trajectory execution are compressed into a single score. We observe that Reflective Guidance turns trajectory evaluation into explicit diagnoses and corrective instructions, substantially improving planning accuracy. Yet such guidance requires an initial prediction and its evaluation results, making it unavailable for direct single-pass inference. We propose Reflect-VLA, which treats Reflective Guidance as training-time privileged information and internalizes its planning benefits through Dual-Path On-Policy Self-Distillation (Dual-OPSD). Unlike scalar-reward optimization, Dual-OPSD uses the driving score only to route student-generated trajectories. It distills dense token-level corrective distributions from the teacher for risky trajectories while applying self-imitation to preserve reliable ones. To provide reliable corrective distributions on student rollouts, the teacher is progressively constructed through Driving-Knowledge Pretraining, Planning-and-Refinement SFT, and Self-Refinement Rejection Sampling. At inference time, Reflect-VLA generates trajectories in a single pass without explicit guidance or iterative refinement. Experiments on NAVSIM v1/v2 and Bench2Drive demonstrate competitive planning performance while reducing training time by over 90% compared with RL.

</details>


### [337] [RESCUE: Is This Alarm Worth Acting On? Counterfactual Evaluation of VLA Runtime Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13516)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Abdelwahed Khamis,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为RESCUE的框架，用于评估机器人策略中运行时故障监控器在实现有用干预方面的价值。RESCUE通过比较不同干预措施的效果，确定了最早的可恢复检查点，并评估干预是否能降低任务完成成本。研究发现在某些情况下，即使是准确的检测也无法带来更低的干预成本，并且理想的探测器也有误报率的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时故障监控器评估方法仅关注检测准确性，但忽略了检测到的故障能否及时进行干预恢复，以及非必要的干预是否会干扰原本可能成功的执行。因此，需要一种新的评估方法来衡量监控器在实现有用干预方面的价值。

Method: RESCUE框架通过以下步骤进行评估：1. 在执行检查点恢复模拟器和策略状态。2. 比较不同替代动作的效果。3. 识别满足特定恢复标准的最新检查点。4. 评估在这些检查点上采取行动是否能降低预期的任务完成成本。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT和$π_{0.5}$策略，以及LIBERO-Spatial和LIBERO-Plus数据集上的大量反事实分支（128,340个）的评估显示：1. 可恢复性依赖于状态-干预对，而非仅机器人状态。2. 在$τ=0.5$时，OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO-Plus的66个故障中，14个在仅继续执行下可恢复，34个在继续执行或脚本式恢复下可恢复。3. 在某些条件下，恢复反而降低了可恢复性。4. 在强策略条件下，良好的检测分数并不一定意味着更低的干预成本。5. 在停止-重置模型下，即使是理想的检测器，其误报率也必须低于2.0-3.3%，否则不必要的重置会抵消早期停止故障带来的收益。

Conclusion: 监控器的价值不仅仅取决于其检测准确性，更与其可用的干预措施以及部署的工作负载共同决定。RESCUE框架能够更全面地评估监控器在实际机器人应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Runtime failure monitors for robot policies are typically evaluated by how accurately they distinguish failed from successful executions. Yet an accurate alarm may arrive too late for recovery, while an unnecessary intervention may disrupt an execution that would otherwise succeed. We introduce RESCUE, a counterfactual framework for evaluating whether a monitor's alarms enable useful interventions. By restoring simulator and policy states at execution checkpoints, RESCUE compares alternative actions, identifies the latest checkpoint satisfying a specified recovery criterion, and evaluates whether acting on alarms reduces expected task-completion cost. We evaluate RESCUE with OpenVLA-OFT and $π_{0.5}$ over LIBERO-Spatial and LIBERO-Plus using 128,340 counterfactual branches. Recoverability depends on the state--intervention pair rather than on robot state alone: at $τ=0.5$, 14 of 66 OpenVLA-OFT/LIBERO-Plus failures contain a recoverable checkpoint under continuation, compared with 34 of 66 under continuation or scripted recovery, while recovery reduces recoverability in other conditions. On the primary strong-policy conditions, favourable detection scores do not establish lower intervention cost. Under the evaluated stop-and-reset model, even an idealised detector can afford only a 2.0--3.3% false-alarm rate before unnecessary resets outweigh the savings from stopping failures early. These results show that monitor value depends jointly on the intervention available and the workload in which it is deployed.

</details>


### [338] [Beyond Successful Robot Demonstrations: Learning from Human Interactions and Modeling Failure Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13522)
*Peiyan Tu,Cong Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HF-WM的动作条件世界模型，它结合了人类交互学习和故障感知后训练，通过双分支框架（视频生成和点流预测）来弥合人类与机器人之间的具身差距，并通过GRPO后训练来学习故障动力学，以提高机器人智能的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集在交互动力学覆盖方面存在局限性，难以支持通用的世界模型。大规模人类视频虽然提供了丰富的交互经验，但人类手部和机器人执行器之间的具身差距阻碍了跨具身学习。此外，现有数据集主要包含成功演示，导致故障动力学表示不足，限制了世界模型预测非成功执行的能力。

Method: 提出HF-WM，采用双分支框架，结合视频生成和辅助点流预测，以稳定的点流作为统一的运动表示，来弥合具身差距并抑制自我中心相机运动。通过对成功演示的扰动动作进行GRPO后训练，利用动作对齐和物理合理性奖励，学习故障动力学。

Result: HF-WM在动作条件视频生成方面达到了最先进的性能，并在下游机器人任务的策略评估中展现出强大的效用。

Conclusion: HF-WM通过结合人类交互学习和故障感知后训练，成功地弥合了具身差距并提升了故障动力学的建模能力，为通用机器人智能奠定了基础。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models offer a promising foundation for generalist robotic intelligence by predicting the consequences of actions. However, existing robotic datasets provide limited coverage of the diverse interaction dynamics required for generalizable world modeling. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer complementary interaction experience, yet the embodiment gap between human hands and robotic manipulators hinders effective cross-embodiment learning. Meanwhile, the predominance of successful demonstrations leaves failure dynamics substantially underrepresented, limiting the ability of world models to anticipate unsuccessful executions. To address these limitations, we introduce HF-WM, an action-conditioned world model that combines Human interaction learning with Failure-aware post-training. To bridge the embodiment gap, we develop a dual-branch framework that couples video generation with auxiliary pointflow prediction, using stabilized pointflow as a unified motion representation across human and robot interactions while suppressing egocentric camera motion. To improve failure modeling, we further perform Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training on perturbed actions derived from successful demonstrations, guided by action alignment and physical plausibility rewards, enabling the model to learn plausible failure dynamics without paired ground-truth videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HF-WM achieves state-of-the-art performance in action-conditioned video generation and exhibits strong utility for policy evaluation in downstream robotic tasks.

</details>


### [339] [ATHLETE: Learning Reactive Humanoid Tennis via Trajectory-Guided Motion Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13572)
*Zhihao Tian,Zifan Wang,Enzheng Zhu,Xun Yang,Jialong Qin,Mengbo Wang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ATHLETE的框架，能够利用通用的运动数据和普通的网球视频，学习人形机器人的网球技能。该框架通过轨迹引导运动匹配（TGMM）生成任务相关的全身运动参考，并结合物理驱动的师生学习和在线DAgger蒸馏，最终训练出可以直接响应机器人和球体观察的反应式策略，无需在部署时输入参考动作。实验结果表明，该方法在真实人形机器人上实现了高命中率，并能与人类进行多拍来回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集用于训练人形机器人网球的特定任务动作捕捉演示成本高昂，尤其是在需要覆盖各种接近状态、接触位置和时机条件的情况下。受到人类通过结合可重用运动经验和特定任务演示来学习新技能的启发，作者希望开发一种更高效的学习方法。

Method: ATHLETE框架结合了LAFAN1运动数据和视频推导的击球动作，利用轨迹引导运动匹配（TGMM）来构建多样化的任务条件下的全身运动参考。然后，通过物理驱动的师生学习（physics-based teacher-student learning）结合在线DAgger蒸馏（online DAgger distillation）将这些参考转化为反应式策略，最后通过PPO算法进行微调。部署时，该策略可以直接响应机器人和球的观测，无需参考动作。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上，ATHLETE实现的网球正手击球率为90.00%，反手击球率为86.67%。此外，机器人还能够与人类玩家进行多拍来回的比赛。

Conclusion: ATHLETE框架能够有效地利用可重用的运动数据和视频演示，学习动态的人形机器人网球技能。该方法克服了传统数据收集的局限性，并在实际机器人平台上取得了优异的性能，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid tennis requires reactive whole-body coordination under rapidly changing ball dynamics, yet collecting task-specific motion-capture demonstrations that cover diverse approach states, contact locations, and timing conditions is costly. Inspired by how humans acquire new skills by combining reusable motor experience with task-specific demonstrations, we present ATHLETE, a framework for learning reactive humanoid tennis from generic locomotion data and ordinary tennis videos. ATHLETE combines LAFAN1 locomotion with video-derived striking motions and uses Trajectory-Guided Motion Matching (TGMM) to construct diverse task-conditioned whole-body references. These references are transformed into a reactive policy through physics-based teacher--student learning with online DAgger distillation, followed by PPO fine-tuning for the tennis task. The resulting policy responds directly to robot and ball observations without requiring reference-motion inputs at deployment. On a real humanoid robot, ATHLETE achieves forehand and backhand hit rates of $90.00\%$ and $86.67\%$, respectively, and enables multi-shot rallies with human players. These results demonstrate that ATHLETE can learn dynamic humanoid tennis skills from reusable locomotion data and video demonstrations. Result videos and code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/ATHLETE2027/.

</details>


### [340] [RARA: Robust Policy Fine-Tuning through Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13578)
*Xuhui Kang,Sung-Wook Lee,Yan Zhuang,Tianle Zhong,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RARA的两阶段机器人策略适应方法，通过检索预训练的表示并对其进行对齐，同时限制微调过程中的表示变化，以解决在少量演示下适应新任务的挑战，并取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接微调预训练的机器人策略以适应新任务时，容易导致表示失真，削弱泛化能力。现有方法难以在保持泛化能力的同时有效利用少量演示进行适应。

Method: RARA采用两阶段适应方法：1. 检索与目标演示任务意图和运动相似的预训练表示，并将目标演示的表示与检索到的表示对齐。2. 在目标演示上微调策略，同时限制检索到的表示在微调过程中的变化。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboCasa和真实机器人上的13个任务实验中，RARA在分布内成功率上平均提高了9.5个百分点，在分布外成功率上平均提高了13.6个百分点，优于所有对比基线。

Conclusion: RARA通过减少动作学习前的表示不匹配并保留预训练知识，有效限制了微调过程中的不必要表示漂移，提高了机器人策略在新任务上的适应能力和泛化能力。

Abstract: Pre-trained robot policies, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, provide a strong foundation for generalist robotic manipulation, but adapting them to new tasks with only a few demonstrations remains challenging. Directly fine-tuning a pre-trained policy often achieves strong in-distribution performance, yet it can distort the representations learned during pre-training and weaken the model's generalization ability under out-of-distribution shifts. We propose Retrieval-Anchored Representation Alignment (RARA), a two-stage adaptation method, to address this issue. First, RARA retrieves representation features from pretraining with similar task intent and motion, and aligns the policy's representations of the target demonstrations with the pre-trained representations of the retrieved demonstrations. Second, RARA fine-tunes the policy on the target demonstrations while limiting how much its representations of the retrieved demonstrations change during fine-tuning. By reducing the representation mismatch before action learning and preserving pre-trained knowledge during adaptation, RARA limits unnecessary representation drift during fine-tuning. Experiments with Diffusion Policy and GR00T on 13 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboCasa, and on a real robot show that RARA improves the average success of fine-tuning by 9.5 points in-distribution and 13.6 points out-of-distribution, and outperforms all compared baselines. Project page: https://shua-kang.github.io/RARA_robot/

</details>


### [341] [JitterVLA: A Replay Benchmark for Motion Quality in VLA-Driven Human-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13628)
*Faith Ogunwoye,Didar Seyidov,Konstantin Gubernatorov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为 JitterVLA 的方法，用于评估和改善视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 策略生成的机器人动作的平滑度，尤其是在存在延迟和不规则信号的情况下，通过重放指令和轨迹来隔离控制器影响，并展示了插值技术在减少加加速度（jerk）方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 策略生成的机器人动作在传输到控制器时存在延迟和不规则性，可能导致靠近人时产生突兀的运动，带来安全隐患。

Method: 开发了 JitterVLA 工具，通过重放相同的指令和到达轨迹来评估不同控制器在处理延迟和不规则信号时的效果。研究了插值和限制加加速度（jerk）两种方法来平滑动作，并分析了熵不连续性和熵门控的影响。在合成数据、人类轨迹数据和物理 UR3 机器人上进行了实验。

Result: 插值方法显著降低了模型预测的 p99 加加速度（jerk），在 200 毫秒延迟的人类运动数据上降低了 83%。该效果在新的实验中得到了复现。限制加加速度（jerk）的生成方法可以进一步降低 jerk，但会增加成本。熵不连续性的关联在不同 VLA 管道中表现相反，熵门控的效果也因管道而异。在 UR3 机器人上，插值方法将加速度计测得的 jerk 降低了 3.0%-14.0%，但位置导出的指标未能区分效果。

Conclusion: JitterVLA 工具能够有效评估和改善 VLA 策略生成的机器人动作的平滑度，特别是插值方法在降低机器人运动的突兀性方面效果显著，为在进行以人为中心的机器人研究之前进行筛选提供了支持。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies issue sparse targets whose arrival at the robot controller is delayed and irregular, so a reasonable predicted action can still produce abrupt motion near people. Remedies are evaluated on different streams, delays and metrics. JitterVLA replays identical commands and arrival traces through alternative controllers, isolating the controller's effect. We apply it to synthetic reaches, hand trajectories from 20 people, three open VLA pipelines (838 episodes) and a physical UR3. Interpolation lowered modeled p99 jerk for every source, by 83% (615 to 106 m/s^3) on human motion with 200 ms delay, at a 2.0 cm tracking cost, and the effect replicated on 598 new episodes; jerk-limited generation lowered jerk further at a larger cost. Entropy-discontinuity associations had opposite, replicated signs in two pipelines, and entropy gating helped in one but not the other. On the UR3, interpolation lowered accelerometer-derived jerk by 3.0-14.0% in all six exploratory pairs, while a position-derived metric did not resolve the contrast. JitterVLA supports screening before human-centred studies; code and data will be released.

</details>


### [342] [AutoBio+: Benchmarking Dexterous Manipulation with Dynamic Experimental Phenomena in Realistic Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13643)
*Xuanye Wu,Yue Shi,Yongcan Yang,Yiqun Wang,Zhaoqi Wan,Dayu Xia,Jiezhang Cao,Xiangfei Li,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoBio+，一个用于在逼真生物实验室环境中进行灵巧机器人操作的模拟基准和框架。它包含了14个任务，4种机器人实体，并引入了可控的动态实验现象以及混合渲染管线来提高真实感。此外，还开发了一个并行的模拟框架，用于大规模数据生成和评估，并展示了较小的sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逼真的视觉条件下，机器人需要执行灵巧的工具交互并处理动态的实验现象，这对生物实验室的自动化构成了重大挑战。需要一个系统性的评估方法来应对这些挑战。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含14个任务和4种机器人实体的模拟基准AutoBio+。
2. 引入了火焰、蒸汽、气泡等可控的动态实验现象。
3. 开发了混合渲染管线，结合了交互式模拟前景对象和高斯溅射背景。
4. 基于mjlab构建了并行模拟框架，提出了PFSM（并行有限状态机），集成了批量渲染和解耦轨迹回放。

Result: 1. 策略性能在不同任务之间存在显著差异，暴露了现有操作策略的优缺点。
2. 真实世界实验表明，sim-to-real差距减小。

Conclusion: AutoBio+为在逼真的生物实验室环境中进行灵巧操作的评估提供了一个有效的平台，并且所提出的并行模拟框架能够高效地生成数据和评估策略，缩小了模拟与现实之间的差距。

Abstract: Automating biological laboratories requires robots to perform dexterous tool interactions under realistic visual conditions while handling dynamic experimental phenomena, presenting significant challenges for robotic manipulation. To enable systematic evaluation under these conditions, we introduce AutoBio+, a simulation benchmark and framework for dexterous manipulation in realistic biological laboratory environments. AutoBio+ comprises 14 tasks across 4 robot embodiments, spanning functional tool use, precision manipulation, language-conditioned visual grounding, and instrument operation. We introduce controllable dynamic experimental phenomena, including flames, vapor, and bubbles, to capture the evolving visual conditions of laboratory procedures. To further enhance environmental realism, we develop a hybrid rendering pipeline that integrates interactive simulated foreground objects with Gaussian-splatting backgrounds reconstructed from 6 real laboratory scenes. For efficient large-scale data generation and evaluation, we further build a parallel simulation framework on top of mjlab, proposing Parallel Finite-State Machine (PFSM) and integrating batched rendering with decoupled trajectory replay for demonstration generation and model-independent closed-loop policy evaluation. Our evaluation reveals substantial variation in policy performance across tasks, exposing distinct strengths and weaknesses of current manipulation policies. Real-world experiments further demonstrate a reduced sim-to-real gap, supporting the validity of AutoBio+ for realistic manipulation evaluation. Supplementary materials are available at https://autobio-plus.github.io/AutoBioPlus.

</details>


### [343] [Cooperative Aerial Transportation of a Payload with a Sliding Cable Attachment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13665)
*Sebastian Tilaguy Lezama,Diego S D'Antonio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双旋翼直升机组成的带钩悬挂运输系统，用于非刚性连接的载荷的协同空中操作。该系统通过一个贯穿载荷的无摩擦钩子连接两个旋翼，允许电缆在两段之间滑动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空中操作系统通常需要将载荷刚性固定，限制了操作的灵活性。本研究旨在开发一种能够操作非刚性连接载荷的系统，以提高空中操作的通用性。

Method: 提出了一种参考轨迹生成框架，该框架考虑了期望的载荷姿态、电缆分布和系统形状，同时满足电缆长度约束和椭圆工作空间。旋翼跟踪生成的轨迹，并保持电缆张力为正，同时尊重执行器限制。载荷姿态通过系统几何进行估计。

Result: 该系统能够协同操作一个非刚性连接的载荷。通过实验验证了所提出的参考轨迹生成框架的可行性，证明了该方法能够有效跟踪生成的轨迹并满足约束条件。

Conclusion: 双旋翼直升机带钩悬挂运输系统是一种有效的方法，可以实现对非刚性连接载荷的协同空中操作。所提出的参考轨迹生成框架能够生成满足系统约束的可行轨迹，并通过实验得到了验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a dual-quadrotor hook- suspended transport system for cooperative aerial manipulation of a payload that is not rigidly attached to a fixed point along the cable. Two quadrotors are connected by a single inextensible cable that passes through a frictionless hook mounted on the payload, allowing the cable to slide between its two segments. This introduces a cable-distribution variable that couples the quadrotor motion, cable geometry, and payload dynamics. A feasible reference-generation framework is formulated using the desired payload pose, cable distribution, and system shape while satisfying the cable-length constraint and elliptical workspace. The quadrotors track the geometrically generated trajectories while maintaining positive cable tension and respecting actuator limits. The payload pose is estimated from the system geometry, and its measured position is used only to validate the desired trajectory. The approach is evaluated through experimental demonstrations.

</details>


### [344] [Towards a Unified Taxonomy of Agricultural Harvesting Technologies: Linking Physical Interaction, Automation, and Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13688)
*Renato Cristiano Torres,Fabiano Mariath D Oliveira,Claudio Gottschalg Duque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理作用原理和自动化程度的农业收获技术统一分类法，用于比较不同收获方法，并揭示了自动化程度与选择性之间的非直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的收获技术描述方式多样，难以进行直接比较，需要一个统一的分类框架来规范描述和比较。

Method: 通过两条主要分类轴：植物-机器交互的物理原理和自动化程度，提出了一种新的收获技术分类法。选择性被视为一种涌现的属性。

Result: 分析表明，自动化程度的提高并不一定带来更高的选择性。当感知和决策直接影响到产品层面的执行时，收获方式会从分布式或批量交互转向局部和选择性操作。

Conclusion: 提出的分类框架为定位和比较机械化、自动化和机器人收获技术提供了共同的、概念性的基础，有助于区分技术边界、识别中间配置，并支持未来收获系统的分析和开发。

Abstract: Harvesting technologies have evolved from conventional mechanized systems based on bulk mechanical interaction toward increasingly automated and robotic systems capable of perception, decision-making, and localized actuation. This evolution has also created a classification problem, as harvesting systems are commonly described according to crop type, mechanical removal principle, sensing technology, robotic architecture, or degree of automation, making direct comparison difficult. This paper proposes a unified taxonomy for agricultural harvesting technologies based on two primary classification axes: the physical principle governing plant-machine interaction and the level of automation incorporated into the harvesting process. Selectivity is treated as an emergent functional attribute that expresses the biological and spatial scale at which perception and decision-making influence physical actuation. Conventional mechanized methods, including cutting, pulling, vibration, impact, brushing, pneumatic action, and combined systems, are analyzed together with semi-automated and robotic harvesting approaches. The analysis shows that increasing automation does not necessarily result in greater selectivity. A qualitative transition occurs when perception and decision-making directly influence product-level actuation, shifting harvesting from distributed or bulk interaction toward localized and selective manipulation. The proposed framework provides a common conceptual basis for positioning and comparing mechanized, automated, and robotic harvesting technologies. By separating the physical mechanism of product removal from the automation level and resulting selectivity, the taxonomy clarifies technological boundaries, identifies intermediate configurations, and supports the analysis and development of future harvesting systems.

</details>
