### [345] [When LiDAR Fades, Radar Takes Over: LiDAR Reliability-Aware Adaptive Localization Under Airborne Obscurants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13788)
*Moumita Mukherjee,Avijit Banerjee,Anton Koval,George Nikolakopoulo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用正常数据训练的单类别时间学习框架，用于检测激光雷达（LiDAR）在地下环境中的退化，并根据检测结果自适应地在LiDAR惯性里程计（LIO）和雷达惯性里程计（RIO）之间切换，以保证定位的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地下等受烟雾、粉尘等影响的环境中，LiDAR的可靠性会显著下降，而雷达相对鲁棒。因此，需要一种方法来检测LiDAR的退化并保证恶劣环境下的定位精度。

Method: 通过提取空间、跨模态、几何/运动、强度和测量时效性等多种退化敏感特征，利用LSTM自编码器对这些特征的时间演变进行建模。该模型仅使用正常操作数据进行训练，能够检测传感器行为与健康状态的偏差。通过一个LiDAR可靠性估计值来控制LIO和RIO之间的自适应切换。

Result: 在真实的地下隧道（正常和浓烟条件）中进行了实验。结果表明，该框架能够有效检测LiDAR退化，实现模态的自适应切换，并在严重的LiDAR退化下仍能持续输出定位信息。特征消融研究表明，简化的特征表示在特定场景下能提高退化感知定位性能。

Conclusion: 提出的单类别时间学习框架能够有效地检测LiDAR在恶劣环境下的退化，并通过自适应地选择LIO或RIO来维持鲁棒的定位能力。特征选择的简洁性对于提高退化感知定位性能很重要。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for real-time localization and mapping, but its reliability can degrade substantially in subterranean environments affected by smoke, dust, and other airborne obscurants, whereas radar remains comparatively robust. This article presents a one-class temporal learning framework for LiDAR degradation detection and reliability-aware localization under such conditions. A broad set of degradation-sensitive cues, including spatial, cross-modal, geometric/motion, intensity, and measurement-recency features, is evaluated to characterize changes in LiDAR sensing quality. Their temporal evolution is modelled over sliding windows using an LSTM autoencoder trained exclusively on nominal operation, enabling deviations from healthy sensor behaviour to be detected without requiring labelled degradation data. The resulting LiDAR reliability estimate governs adaptive selection between LIO and radar inertial odometry (RIO), retaining LIO during reliable sensing and transferring localization authority to RIO when degradation is detected. The framework is evaluated using real-world ground robot data collected in GNSS-denied subterranean tunnels under nominal and dense-smoke conditions. Experimental results demonstrate degradation detection, adaptive modality switching, and continued localization output under severe LiDAR degradation. A feature family ablation study further shows that a reduced feature representation can improve degradation-aware localization performance in the evaluated dense-smoke scenarios, highlighting the importance of feature parsimony in one-class sensor degradation detection. The code will be released as open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [346] [SmolWAM: A Latent World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13740)
*Timi Owolabi,Hikmah Olawore,Frances Adelakun,Ayotomiwa Oyewumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SmolWAM的轻量级世界动作模型，它通过修改现有的大型模型Cosmos Policy的推理接口来减小计算量和内存占用，同时保持了预测动作和未来观察的能力，适用于资源受限的边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强大的世界动作模型（WAMs）计算成本过高，无法在资源受限的边缘设备上运行。研究旨在开发一个计算效率更高、内存占用更小的WAM。

Method: 通过对20亿参数的Cosmos Policy进行修改来构建SmolWAM。具体措施包括：保留预训练的骨干网络，重构推理接口，使用单帧视觉潜在表示，缓存文本特征以保留注意力机制，减少Transformer块的数量（12/28），并使用1008个tokens进行动作和未来视觉潜在表示的预测。预测过程在一个“流”步骤中完成。

Result: SmolWAM在400个LIBERO环境中达到了95.5%的成功率，推理速度为57毫秒/16动作块，计算量为2.3 TFLOP，GPU内存占用为2.4 GiB。与原始模型相比，SmolWAM快11倍，FLOPs减少18.5倍，内存占用减少3.2倍。其世界预测头仍然能够预测场景运动。

Conclusion: SmolWAM成功地将强大的世界动作模型的推理效率大幅提升，使其能够在资源受限的环境中部署，同时保持了关键的预测能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict a robot's actions together with the observations those actions will produce. However, the strongest models rely on video diffusion transformers that are far too computationally expensive for resource-constrained edge devices. We present SmolWAM, a latent WAM that we derive from the 2B-parameter Cosmos Policy by keeping its pretrained trunk and rebuilding its inference interface: single-frame visual latents, cached text features that preserve the trunk's attention sinks, 12 of 28 transformer blocks, and 1,008 tokens from which actions and future visual latents are read out in one rectified-flow step. Trained in 3.6 GPU-hours, SmolWAM succeeds in 95.5% of 400 LIBERO episodes at 57 ms and 2.3 TFLOP per 16-action chunk with 2.4 GiB of GPU memory, 11x faster than its teacher with 18.5x fewer FLOPs and 3.2x less memory, and its world head still forecasts scene motion.

</details>


### [347] [Dream to Remember: Continual Steering of Generalist Robot Policies with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13816)
*Bahey Tharwat,Iman Nematollahi,Marvin Alles,Xingyuan Zhang,Akshay L Chandra,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Dream to Remember (D2R) 的方法，用于持续训练机器人策略，使其能够学习新任务的同时保留旧任务的能力，解决了灾难性遗忘问题。D2R 通过在学习新任务时，利用预训练的世界模型对旧任务进行“想象式”演练来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略（如模仿学习和强化学习）在处理顺序任务时会遇到灾难性遗忘问题。无论是微调策略权重还是训练一个小型模块来引导生成策略的输入噪声，都会导致先前任务的性能下降。

Method: D2R 核心思想是，当新任务到来时，训练一个小的“转向”模块来引导一个固定的通用策略。同时，一个预训练好的“世界模型”会通过“想象式”的模拟来“排练”旧任务，从而将转向模块锚定在之前的行为上，而无需重放缓冲区或新数据。如果世界模型足够准确，新任务可以直接在想象中完成；否则，新任务在环境中学习，而世界模型仅用于排练旧任务。

Result: 在 CALVIN 数据集上，D2R 能够完全在想象中完成八个顺序任务，成功率从 63.7% 提升到 74.8%，而 LoRA 微调方法在此期间成功率骤降至 11.7%。在 LIBERO-90 数据集上，D2R 在学习新任务的同时，保持了通用策略在其他 LIBERO 套件上的性能。在真实机器人实验中，D2R 也能完全在想象中完成三个顺序任务，并保留了之前的改进。

Conclusion: D2R 是一种有效的持续学习方法，能够解决机器人策略中的灾难性遗忘问题。它通过世界模型的想象式排练，在不牺牲旧任务性能的情况下，成功地将新任务整合到机器人策略中，并且在不同数据集和真实机器人上都展现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained with imitation learning and are bounded by the quality and coverage of their demonstrations. Reinforcement learning can improve and extend them, either by fine-tuning the policy weights or by training a small module to steer the input noise of a frozen generative policy. When tasks arrive sequentially, both approaches suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Fine-tuning on a task degrades prior tasks, and steering only shifts forgetting to the shared steering module. We propose Dream to Remember (D2R), which continually steers a frozen generalist policy toward new tasks, while retaining its earlier skills. As the steering module learns a new task, a frozen pretrained world model rehearses prior tasks through imagined rollouts, anchoring the module to its earlier behavior without a replay buffer or new data. When the world model is faithful enough, D2R learns the new task entirely in imagination. Otherwise, it learns the task in the environment while using the world model only to rehearse prior tasks. On CALVIN, D2R improves eight tasks in sequence entirely in imagination, raising average success from 63.7% to 74.8%, while LoRA fine-tuning on the same sequence drops it to 11.7%. With a frozen off-the-shelf generalist policy and world model, $π_0$ and A2World, D2R learns unseen LIBERO-90 tasks in the environment while keeping $π_0$'s performance on the LIBERO suites it was fine-tuned on, even though the world model is not accurate enough for policy optimization. Finally, on a real Franka robot, D2R improves three tasks in sequence entirely in imagination while retaining the earlier improvements.

</details>


### [348] [MC-EvoBench: Auditing Experience-Driven Self-Improvement in Minecraft Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13829)
*Jingwei Song,Zhisheng Chen,Bingfan Zeng,Jing Tang,Yubo Hou,Jinhao Jing,Zhengwei Xie,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出了MC-EvoBench，一个在Minecraft中用于评估和衡量具身智能体可转移和持久化能力的新型基准测试。该基准测试通过控制交互历史、评估机制和特定环境设计，来区分真正获得的能力与外部资源的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身递归自我改进研究面临一个核心挑战：如何让智能体将交互历史转化为可持久化且能迁移到新任务的能力。简单地保存记忆或成功完成最终动作并不能证明能力的真正习得，需要更严谨的评估方法来区分真实能力增长和资源迁移、源暴露不均或早期执行失败等因素。

Method: 研究构建了一个名为MC-EvoBench的Minecraft基准测试。该基准测试包含19个注册的演化链，其中12个是主要的评估链，7个是校准或探测链。此外，还有三个图对齐的配对探测家族用于迁移性测试。每个任务被表示为任务-状态图，定义了阶段级环境验证器，匹配了源和目标角色，重置了目标世界，并只允许声明的智能体产物跨越源-目标边界。

Result: 在MC-EvoBench上的实验揭示了一个持续的、与控制器和规划器相关的直接能力差距。与此形成对比的是，相关经验并未带来稳定的迁移能力：它没有提高工具探测能力，在木材种类上的微小提升高度依赖于一个种子，并且所有置信区间都包含了零。阶段审计还表明，早期的木材收集和制作瓶颈常常导致后续能力无法被观察到。

Conclusion: MC-EvoBench提供了一个受控的测试平台，用于研究和评估具身智能体的持久化和可迁移能力。实验结果表明，当前方法在实现真正可迁移的能力方面仍存在显著挑战，并且早期瓶颈可能会阻碍对更高级能力的观察和评估。

Abstract: A central challenge in embodied recursive self-improvement is enabling agents to convert interaction history into capabilities that persist beyond one world and transfer to later tasks. A saved memory or successful final action does not by itself establish that a reusable capability was acquired; evaluation must separate capability change from carried-over resources, unequal source exposure, and early execution failures. We construct MC-EvoBench as a controlled Minecraft benchmark with a fixed catalog of 19 registered evolution chains, including 12 primary evaluation chains and seven calibration or probe chains, together with three graph-aligned paired probe families for transfer. The benchmark represents each task as a task-state graph, defines stage-level environment verifiers, assigns matched source and target roles, resets the target world, and permits only a declared agent artifact to cross the source-target boundary. For each probe, no-prior, related, unrelated, and shuffled source conditions are evaluated under matched environment-step budgets and five seeds; we report TSR, WGP, and VMP and retain provenance records. The benchmark reveals a consistent direct-capability gap between controller and planner-backed variants, while related experience yields no stable transfer: it does not improve either tool probe, its small wood-species gain hinges on one seed, and all intervals include zero. Stage audits further show that early wood-collection and crafting bottlenecks often leave later capabilities unobserved. MC-EvoBench thus offers a controlled testbed for durable, transferable embodied capabilities. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xzw-ustc/mc-bench-open-source

</details>


### [349] [Mapless, Stabilizing, and Anti-Stuck Flipper Control for Articulated Tracked Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13838)
*Valentýn Číhala,Pavel Sarga,Martin Pecka,Tomáš Svoboda,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAS（Stabilizing Anti-Stuck）的无地图翻转器控制器，该控制器结合了姿态稳定和防卡滞功能，仅依赖IMU、翻转器关节角度和运动提示作为输入，克服了传统基于地图控制器的漂移问题，并在模拟和真实环境中均表现出与最先进的基于地图控制器相当的通行能力，尤其在探测不到的障碍物上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主或半自主翻转器控制器严重依赖对机器人下方和前方地形的高程地图，而机器人底盘下的地形会被传感器遮挡，地图通过里程计积分维护，但里程计漂移会腐蚀地图信息。因此，研究一种无需高程地图的控制器是必要的。

Method: SAS控制器结合了姿态稳定项（用于控制身体滚动和俯仰）和防卡滞项（用于在机器人卡住时将其解脱）。其输入仅为IMU、翻转器关节角度和一个指示机器人是否正在移动的短期运动提示。该运动提示不累积，因此SAS不会继承里程计积分带来的漂移。

Result: SAS控制器在模拟和真实机器人上均实现了与最先进的基于地图的控制器相当的通行质量。在隐藏的、LiDAR无法探测到的障碍物上，SAS能够成功通过，而最佳的基于地图的控制器则会卡住。

Conclusion: SAS是一种有效的无地图翻转器控制器，它通过整合姿态稳定和防卡滞机制，并避免使用易漂移的里程计积分，在 terrenos 复杂且地形信息受限的情况下，能够实现可靠的机器人导航和通行能力，甚至优于一些基于地图的控制器。

Abstract: Nearly every autonomous or semi-autonomous flipper controller assumes access to an elevation map of the terrain under and in front of the robot. The terrain beneath the chassis is occluded in the sensors, so the map is remembered through odometry integration, whose drift corrupts it. We present Stabilizing Anti-Stuck (SAS), a mapless controller that combines a stabilization term for body roll and pitch with an anti-stuck term that frees the robot when it gets stuck. As input, SAS uses the IMU, the flipper joint angles, and a motion cue that only tells whether the robot is moving. The cue is computed over a short window and never accumulated, so SAS does not inherit the odometry-integration drift that corrupts the map. We deploy SAS on a tracked robot with four flippers and evaluate it against manual teleoperation and prior flipper controllers in a large comparison, both in simulation and on a real obstacle. In simulation and on the real robot, SAS achieves traversal quality comparable to state-of-the-art map-based controllers. On a log hidden in tall grass, invisible to the LiDAR, the best map-based controller gets stuck while SAS traverses the hidden obstacle without a problem.

</details>


### [350] [BT-Agent: Deploying Onboard Robot Agents with Behavior Trees and Multi-Fidelity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13862)
*Fabio Hübel,Adam Lilja,Yue Yin,Siming He,Claire Tomlin,Shankar Sastry,Jonas Frey,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BT-Agent的板载语言模型代理架构，结合了LLM的任务决策和行为树（BT）技能，用于在计算和内存受限的移动机器人上实现任务推理和响应式执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算和内存受限的移动机器人上部署需要任务推理和响应式执行能力的语言模型代理。

Method: 提出BT-Agent架构，结合LLM的任务决策和行为树（BT）技能。BT技能负责执行条件和结构化失败反馈。在四足机器人上使用9B模型进行了演示，涵盖五个任务类别。引入了一个包含161个任务的多保真基准，并在文本和Habitat模拟环境中进行了评估。

Result: BT-Agent在四足机器人上实现了完全板载操作。与Act/ReAct基线相比，在4B和9B模型下，成功率分别提高了约10%和5%。多保真基准显示文本评估吞吐量比模拟环境高10.9倍，模拟环境揭示了感知和运动失败对任务结果的影响。结构化工具调用和信息性失败反馈是关键的有效组件。

Conclusion: BT-Agent是一种有效的板载架构，能够实现LLM驱动的任务推理和响应式执行，尤其是在资源受限的移动机器人上。结构化工具调用和信息性失败反馈是提高性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Deploying language-model agents onboard mobile robots requires task reasoning and responsive execution within limited compute and memory. We propose BT-Agent, an onboard architecture combining LLM-driven task decisions with predefined behavior-tree (BT) skills. These skills enforce execution conditions and return structured failure feedback. We demonstrate fully onboard operation with a 9B model on a quadruped across five task categories. Intervention tests show that local checks stop ongoing navigation without waiting for another model decision, while the agent remains active to retry or report constraints. To compare agent designs efficiently, we introduce a 161-task multi-fidelity benchmark, evaluated in text on all tasks and in Habitat on 39. Text supports 10.9$\times$ higher evaluation throughput, while simulation reveals how perception and motion failures change task outcomes. BT-Agent's largest success gains over the strongest Act/ReAct baseline occur at 4B and 9B: approximately 10 and 5 percentage points. Ablations identify structured tool calls and informative failure feedback as the most useful components.

</details>


### [351] [HorizonNav: Geometry-Guided Video Diffusion for Anticipatory 3D Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13849)
*Hajira Amjad,Valerii Serpiva,Viacheslav Liubimov,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HorizonNav是一个基于视频扩散的机器人预测导航框架，它将RGB图像和文本提示转化为3D空间表示，通过几何引导的深度对齐来提高3D重建的几何一致性，并在导航任务中取得了显著的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人导航方法通常依赖于传感器数据（如激光雷达）来构建环境地图，这可能导致成本高昂且在某些场景下不适用。本研究旨在探索一种仅使用RGB图像和文本提示进行预测性导航的方法，以克服这些限制。

Method: 该框架首先利用视觉语言模型解析场景并生成结构化提示，然后输入到视频扩散模型生成未来观测的视频序列。接着，使用ORB-SLAM3进行相机姿态估计，并使用Video Depth Anything进行密集深度预测。核心贡献在于几何引导的深度对齐阶段，该阶段结合了相机姿态和三角化视觉地标来约束多视图的深度预测，从而提高3D重建的几何一致性。

Result: 在想象3D环境中的导航实验显示，规划可行场景的比例从13/19提升到15/19，成功的RRT试验次数从62/90提升到75/90。在走廊研究中，违反重建碰撞约束的相机轨迹比例降低，且RRT规划器能在大部分情况下恢复可行替代方案。此外，框架能够暴露重建和自由空间失败模式，而不是隐藏它们。在走廊重建中，平均重建误差约为0.30米。

Conclusion: HorizonNav框架能够有效地将单目RGB图像和文本提示转化为可用于预测导航的3D环境表示。通过几何引导的深度对齐，该方法显著提高了3D重建的几何一致性，并证明了其在改善机器人导航性能方面的潜力，尤其是在缺乏激光雷达等传感器的场景下。

Abstract: We present HorizonNav, a video diffusion-based framework for anticipatory robot navigation that transforms a single RGB image and a text prompt into a geometrically aligned representation of plausible surrounding 3D space. Given the initial observation and a textual description of the reconstruction objective, a vision-language model, guided by designed instructions, interprets the scene and constructs structured prompts for a video diffusion model. The resulting video sequence provides plausible future observations of the environment, which are subsequently processed using monocular ORB-SLAM3 for camera pose estimation and Video Depth Anything for dense depth prediction. Our central contribution is a geometry-guided depth alignment stage that combines the estimated camera poses with triangulated visual landmarks to constrain dense depth predictions across multiple views. This alignment improves the geometric consistency of the reconstructed 3D environment, addressing inconsistencies that temporal coherence of the generated video alone cannot resolve. The experimental evaluation of navigation in imagined 3D environment shows that across a 19-scene horizon study, planning-feasible scenes increase from 13/19 at P20 to 15/19 at P81, while successful RRT trials increase from 62/90 to 75/90. In a separate corridor study, 6 of 25 evaluable generated-camera trajectories violate the reconstructed collision constraints; RRT planner recovers feasible alternatives in 5 of those 6 cases. A larger archive contains 58 usable reconstructions from 67 attempted cases, of which 36 support a downstream plan and 22 do not, exposing reconstruction and free-space failure modes rather than hiding them without lidar. Across 15 corridor reconstructions, the mean reconstruction error is approximately 0.30 m at matched structural landmarks.

</details>


### [352] [Chronos Policy: Learning Robot Policies on Physical Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13929)
*Changhao Wang,Jaden Clark,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Chronos Policy 的机器人策略，该策略通过显式地将物理时间纳入模型（例如，使用时间感知 RoPE），来解决固定频率感知-动作序列中隐含的时间信息不足的问题，提高了在动态和多传感器任务上的性能，并增强了对感知和推理延迟变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略通常将时间隐含在固定频率的观察-动作序列中，当观察频率、延迟或推理延迟发生变化时，这种表示会失效。研究的动机是开发一种能显式处理物理时间的机器人策略，以提高其在动态和复杂时间条件下的鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 研究人员提出了一种将物理时间显式纳入深度学习模型（特别是扩散 Transformer）的方法。具体来说，他们探索了两种方式：1. 将时间戳嵌入添加到 token 特征中；2. 通过时间感知旋转位置编码（time-aware RoPE）将物理时间编码到位置编码中。此外，在训练过程中，通过随机化观察采样时间和动作块的起始时间，使策略能够适应更广泛的时间安排。

Result: 研究发现，时间感知 RoPE 比时间戳嵌入更能提高模型对物理时间的鲁棒性，因为它允许注意力机制直接依赖于物理时间而不改变 token 内容。Chronos Policy 在动态任务和多传感器任务上均取得了性能提升。在准静态任务上，该策略在保持原有性能的同时，也提高了对感知和推理时间变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将物理时间显式地纳入机器人策略（如 Chronos Policy）能够显著提高其在动态和多传感器环境下的性能和鲁棒性，尤其是在感知和推理时间可变的情况下。时间感知 RoPE 是一种有效的方法，可以实现这一点，并且能够处理异步、多速率的数据。

Abstract: Robot policies often convert sensing and control into fixed-rate observation-action sequences, leaving physical time implicit in sequence position. This assumption breaks when observation rates or delays vary, or when inference latency changes. We introduce Chronos Policy, which makes physical time explicit by conditioning on observation capture times and desired action execution times. We study two ways to incorporate time into a diffusion transformer: adding timestamp embeddings to token features, or encoding physical time in positional encoding through time-aware rotary positional encodings (time-aware RoPE). We find that time-aware RoPE is more robust, allowing attention to depend directly on physical time while keeping token content unchanged. During training, we temporally randomize which observations are sampled and when the action chunk begins, exposing the policy to a wider range of sensing and execution schedules that may occur during deployments. This also enables learning from asynchronous, multi-rate data without forcing all measurements onto a fixed temporal grid. Across simulation and real-world experiments, Chronos Policy improves performance on dynamic tasks, where observation-to-action timing is critical, and on multisensory tasks, where the relative timing between observations matters. On standard quasi-static tasks, it preserves nominal performance while improving robustness to changes in sensing and inference timing.

</details>


### [353] [Noise Space Learning for VLA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13943)
*Junjie Lu,Chuheng Zhang,Yucen Wang,Yuhua Jiang,Xinyao Qin,Min Xu,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为噪声空间学习（NSL）的新方法，用于在有限的机器人交互、计算资源和安全约束下，高效地将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型适应到特定任务。NSL通过学习一个条件于上下文的噪声分布，同时保持预训练的VLA模型固定，并可与多种学习算法（如PPO、SAC、AWR）结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练的VLA模型适应到特定任务面临着数据稀疏、计算资源有限和安全限制等挑战。需要一种高效的方法来解决这些限制。

Method: NSL通过学习一个上下文条件化的噪声分布来适应基于流匹配的VLA模型，同时固定预训练的VLA模型。该方法被表述为一个VLA适应接口，可以集成PPO、SAC和AWR等学习算法。理论上，文章进行了信息论分析来解释NSL如何实现高效适应。实验上，在LIBERO、RoboTwin和四个真实机器人操作任务上进行了评估。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实机器人操作任务上的实验表明，NSL算法在交互、计算和执行约束下表现良好。研究开发了一系列有效的NSL变体，并根据不同的资源和部署场景提出了实用的选择建议。

Conclusion: NSL是一种有效的VLA模型适应框架，能够克服机器人交互、计算和安全方面的限制，为不同资源和部署场景下的VLA模型适应提供了实用的指导。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models encode broad manipulation priors, yet adapting them to specific tasks requires balancing limited robot interaction, computational resources, and safety constraints. Noise space learning (NSL) adapts flow-matching-based VLAs by learning a context-conditioned distribution over noise while keeping the pretrained VLA fixed. Building on prior noise-steering methods, we formulate NSL as an interface for VLA adaptation that accommodates different learning algorithms and instantiate it with PPO, SAC, and AWR. Theoretically, we provide an information-theoretic analysis of how NSL enables efficient adaptation. Empirically, we study these NSL algorithms on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and four real-robot manipulation tasks under interaction, computation, and execution constraints. Across these settings, we develop a suite of effective NSL variants and derive practical recommendations for choosing among them under different resource and deployment regimes.

</details>


### [354] [Splat2Act: From Captured Scenes to Personalized VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13945)
*Xiaomeng Ye,Krishna Jaganathan,Gyubeum Lim,Gunjan Chhablani,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Splat2Act 是一种自动化管道，通过 3D 高斯喷溅和碰撞几何构建场景的数字孪生，然后生成用于定制视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的演示，从而实现 VLA 策略的个性化，仅需用户用手机扫描环境和物品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户家中部署通用操纵策略时，由于环境差异，其拾取成功率很低。收集实际演示成本高昂，因此需要一种自动化的方法来进行个性化。

Method: Splat2Act 使用 3D 高斯喷溅和碰撞几何构建大规模室内环境的数字孪生，自动识别容器，创建拾取任务，并使用模拟预言机生成演示。然后，在两个阶段对预训练的 VLA 策略进行微调：第一阶段适应公共对象资产，第二阶段根据用户特定对象和场景容器进行个性化。

Result: 在 101 个扫描场景中，Splat2Act 生成了大量演示。微调后的策略在目标场景中实现了 62.5% 的平均拾取成功率，并在真实机器人上取得了 63.9% 的成功率。

Conclusion: 自动化的真实到模拟到真实训练流水线，仅凭模拟预言机数据，就能有效地个性化 VLA 操纵策略，使其适用于特定场景和用户对象。

Abstract: A user who brings a generalist manipulation policy into their home or office wants it to pick up their objects from their shelves, counters, and tables. Yet even a strong generalist like $π_{0.5}$ achieves 0% pickup success in our captured deployment scenes without adaptation, and collecting real demonstrations in every user's environment is prohibitively expensive. We present Splat2Act, an automated real-to-sim-to-real pipeline that personalizes a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a specific scene and object set using only a handheld phone scan and images of the user's objects. Unlike prior real-to-sim work that reconstructs individual tabletops, Splat2Act operates on building-scale indoor environments: it builds a photorealistic digital twin with 3D Gaussian Splatting and collision geometry, automatically discovers tabletop and non-tabletop receptacles, constructs feasible pickup tasks, and generates successful demonstrations with a privileged simulation oracle, with only scene and object capture required from a human. Applied to 101 scanned campus environments with a median navigable area of 104.87 m$^2$ and 7 receptacles, the pipeline yields 852 receptacles and over 68,000 pickup demonstrations. We fine-tune $π_{0.5}$ in two stages: Stage 1 adapts to hundreds of public object assets, drawn either from the deployment scene alone or from all 101 scenes; Stage 2 personalizes to the user's own objects on the deployment scene's receptacles. Our strongest personalized policy reaches 62.5% aggregate pickup success in the target scene. Deployed on a real Stretch 3, the same policy achieves 63.9% success on a 12-task subset, closely tracking its 66.7% simulated success on those tasks. These results suggest that automated real-to-sim-to-real training can personalize VLA manipulation policies using only simulation-oracle data.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [355] [Minimum Bounding Sets for N-1-1 Branch Contingencies Under Thermal and Voltage Limits in Linear Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12606)
*Timothy N. Thacker*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了确定两支路故障对（N-1-1）故障分析中最小边界集的方法，该方法可以重现所有违规的热和电压限制的Worst Case值。在DC模型中，故障对的影响通过在网络周期空间中的余弦来度量，并提出了一个接近理想的界限。该方法进一步推广到其他线性模型，并提取了考虑电压幅值的模型。基于边界的最优优先搜索方法在实际系统中显著减少了故障对的评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于排序的N-1-1故障分析筛选方法无法保证遗漏的故障对是安全的，当故障对数量随网络规模呈二次方增长时，这一点尤为突出。因此，需要一种更有效的方法来确定能够保证覆盖最坏情况故障的最小故障集。

Method: 1. 确定两支路故障对的最小边界集，能够重现线性网络模型中所有违规的热和电压限制的Worst Case值。
2. 在DC模型中，利用故障对在网络周期空间中的余弦来衡量其影响，并推导了一个接近理想的界限。
3. 将此论证推广到更一般的线性模型。
4. 通过对无损网络能量函数进行一次牛顿迭代，提取了包含电压幅值的模型。
5. 构建了基于边界的最优优先搜索算法。

Result: 1. 提出了一种计算最小边界集的方法，可以保证覆盖最坏情况的故障。
2. 在DC模型中，推导出了一个接近理想的故障对影响界限。
3. 在公共系统中，基于该边界的最优优先搜索算法在故障对评估次数上比暴力搜索减少了130到3800倍（针对潮流）和170到23700倍（针对电压）。
4. 探索了线性模型到非线性模型的转换在IEEE 118节点系统上的差距。

Conclusion: 本文提出的最小边界集方法以及基于此的最优优先搜索算法，能够高效且可靠地进行N-1-1故障分析，显著减少了计算量，尤其是在处理大规模电力系统时。该方法为更准确和安全的电力系统分析提供了新的途径。

Abstract: N-1-1 contingency analysis studies the loss of one element followed by system adjustments and the loss of a second. The number of contingency pairs increases quadratically with the number of applicable elements, and traditional ranking-based screening methods cannot guarantee that omitted pairs are bounded. This paper determines the minimum bounding set of two-branch-outage pairs: the smallest set that can reproduce the worst value of every violated thermal and voltage limit of a linear network model. In the DC model, effects of an outage pair can be quantified by the extent of the coupling between the outages. The paper measures this by the cosine between the two outages in the network's cycle space, with a bound that splits into one term per outage and is within a near-ideal factor of $\sqrt{1+\varepsilon}$. This argument extends to every linear model in which an outage subtracts a positive semi-definite low-rank term from a symmetric positive definite matrix, with a tight bound and a collapse test. A model with voltage magnitudes is extracted by performing one Newton step on the energy function of the lossless network in rectangular coordinates. A best-first search built on these bounds reproduced brute force on public systems of up to 2{,}869 buses with 130 to 3{,}800 times fewer pair evaluations for flows and 170 to 23{,}700 times fewer for voltages. Gaps in translation to nonlinear models are explored on the IEEE 118-bus system.

</details>


### [356] [Symbolic-Permutation-Based Direct Sparse Jacobian Assembly for Accelerated Power-Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12654)
*Tianshi Cheng,Venkata Dinavahi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于符号置换的直接稀疏雅可比矩阵装配方法，消除了运行时总线类型投影开销，实现了单次通过、无分支的算法，并将计算速度提高了24.4-30.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿-拉夫逊潮流分析中稀疏雅可比矩阵的重复装配是主要的计算瓶颈。

Method: 通过对雅可比矩阵进行代数等变性分析，提出了一种基于符号置换的直接稀疏雅可比矩阵装配方法。在符号阶段预先应用置换，将雅可比微分和全局矩阵装配融合为单次操作。

Result: 与其他基线方法和优化方法相比，提出的装配方法速度显著提高（24.4-30.1倍）。端到端求解时间比pandapower快11.7-154.3倍，与LightSim2Grid相当或更快。

Conclusion: 该方法能够直接应用于具有静态网络算子稀疏雅可比矩阵的通用问题，如暂态稳定性分析、EMT仿真和OPF海森矩阵装配，并显著提高了计算效率。

Abstract: The repeated assembly of the sparse Jacobian matrix constitutes a primary computational bottleneck in the Newton-Raphson power-flow analysis. Through an algebraic equivariance analysis of the Jacobian matrix, this paper proposes a direct sparse Jacobian assembly method based on symbolic permutation. By pre-applying the permutation to the admittance matrix during a one-time symbolic phase, the runtime bus-type projection overhead is entirely eliminated. Consequently, complex Jacobian differentiation and global matrix assembly fuse into a single operation, yielding a single-pass, branchless algorithm that streams values directly into contiguous memory. The framework is realized in the open-source RustPower solver. Benchmarks on IEEE test systems and PEGASE 9241 demonstrate that the proposed assembly is 24.4-30.1x faster than the MATPOWER- style baseline and 6.0-7.8x faster than the Schafer-Braun optimized assembly. End-to-end, the grid-level solve time is 11.7x-154.3x faster than pandapower 3.5.4 with Numba, while leaving numerical results and convergence behavior unchanged. Compared with the native C++ LightSim2Grid 1.0.0, grid-level performance is comparable on IEEE 39 and 1.3x-1.8x faster on IEEE 118 and the PEGASE systems. The method extends directly to general problems where a sparse Jacobian inherits its structure from a static network operator, including transient stability analysis, EMT simulation, and OPF Hessian assembly.

</details>


### [357] [ROAM: Reinforcement Learning Optimized Adaptive Moving Target Defense for Smart Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12730)
*Mohammad Zakaria Haider,Prabin Mali,Muhammad Nadeem,Sumit Paudyal,Fei Ding,Mohammad Ashiqur Rahman*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ROAM的深度强化学习驱动的移动目标防御（MTD）框架，用于应对智能电网中通信层面的隐蔽性虚假数据注入（FDI）攻击，通过动态重构传感器ID映射来提高安全性，并兼容现有标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对智能电网PMU数据流的隐蔽FDI攻击能规避传统坏数据检测方法。现有的MTD方案存在静态或预定随机化、以及在速度（种子法）和鲁棒性（机器学习法）之间存在权衡，且无法根据实时威胁动态调整。

Method: ROAM框架利用深度强化学习（DRL），具体为深度Q网络（DQN）代理，在数据聚合层持续优化传感器到ID的映射。代理在每个决策步骤中选择欺骗覆盖范围、标记需要重映射的传感器，并决定重映射传感器中基于种子法和基于机器学习预测法的比例。整个过程无需修改PMU硬件，并保持IEEE C37.118兼容性。

Result: 在IEEE 14、57和118总线系统的FDI场景测试中，ROAM将隐蔽攻击的比例控制在3%以下，同时保持93%以上的检测率。面对观察到防御方配置的自适应软Actor-Critic（SAC）攻击者，ROAM将隐蔽攻击的比例降至2.6%以下，检测率维持在92%以上。硬件在环测试显示，平均执行时间小于18毫秒，平均重排盲区窗口占决策周期的2.6%。

Conclusion: ROAM是一种高效且自适应的MTD框架，能够显著提高智能电网抵御隐蔽FDI攻击的能力，同时保持了通信协议的兼容性和实时性要求。

Abstract: Smart grids rely on distributed phasor measurement units (PMUs) and wide-area communication networks whose data-acquisition pipelines are vulnerable to stealthy false data injection (FDI) attacks that evade conventional bad-data detection (BDD). Moving target defense (MTD) counters this exposure by continuously remapping sensor identities, but existing schemes apply static or pre-scheduled randomization and are each constrained by a single failure mode: seed-based methods are fast yet fragile to key compromise, while machine learning (ML) based prediction methods are resilient yet incur prohibitive latency. Neither adapts its randomization to live threat evidence. This paper presents ROAM, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) driven MTD framework in which a deep Q-network (DQN) agent continuously re-optimizes the sensor-to-ID mapping at the aggregation layer. At each decision step, the agent selects the deception coverage, flags the specific sensors to remap, and sets the split of the remapped sensors between seed-based and ML-prediction-based recovery, without modifying PMU hardware and while preserving IEEE C37.118 compatibility. On held-out BDD-compliant FDI scenarios across the IEEE 14-, 57-, and 118- bus systems, ROAM keeps the percentage of stealthy attacks below 3% while sustaining detection rates above 93%. Against an adaptive soft actor-critic (SAC) attacker that observes the defender's configuration, ROAM keeps stealthy attacks below 2.6% with detection above 92%. Hardware-in-the-loop testing on an OPAL-RT platform confirms real-time feasibility, with sub-18ms average shuffle execution and an average reshuffle blind window of 2.6% of the 3-s decision cycle.

</details>


### [358] [Distributionally Robust Adaptive Iterative Covariance Steering for Small-Body Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12774)
*Vivek Khatana,Aditya Gahlawat,Naira Hovakimyan,Petros Voulgaris*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 结合迭代协方差控制（iCS）和L1分布鲁棒自适应控制（L1-DRAC），实现自主小天体近距离操作中的风险感知避碰，能有效应对重力、太阳辐射压和随机扰动的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主小天体近距离操作中，需要连续推力制导来应对不确定的引力、太阳辐射压（SRP）和随机扰动，并确保碰撞避免。

Method: 采用迭代协方差控制（iCS）规划满足分布鲁棒条件风险值（CVaR）避碰约束的名义反馈策略，并结合L1分布鲁棒自适应控制（L1-DRAC）来补偿动力学不确定性，同时使用沃塞尔半径来约束剩余的分布失配。

Result: 在1000次配对蒙特卡洛试验中，L1-DRAC自适应控制将避碰违规率从32.7%降低到0.2%，物理碰撞率从16.4%降至零，且未发生执行器饱和。

Conclusion: iCS与L1-DRAC的结合能够有效地实现风险感知碰撞避免，显著提高自主小天体近距离操作的安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous small-body proximity operations require continuous-thrust guidance under uncertain gravity, solar-radiation pressure (SRP), and stochastic disturbances. We combine iterative covariance steering (iCS) with $L_1$ distributionally robust adaptive control ($L_1$-DRAC) to provide risk-aware collision avoidance despite mismatch between the nominal planning model and the true dynamics. Iterative covariance steering plans a nominal feedback policy satisfying distributionally robust conditional value-at-risk keep-out constraints, while $L_1$-DRAC compensates for dynamics uncertainty and bounds the residual distributional mismatch through a Wasserstein radius used by the planner. In $\mathbf{1000}$ paired Monte Carlo trials with gravity, SRP, and diffusion mismatch, $L_1$-DRAC adaptive control reduces the keep-out violation rate from $\mathbf{32.7\%}$ to $\mathbf{0.2\%}$ and the physical collision rate from $\mathbf{16.4\%}$ to zero, without actuator saturation.

</details>


### [359] [Closed-loop domain generation enhances battery health diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12859)
*Yue Wu,Ziyu Mo,Jinze Dai,Ziqing Xia,Shengyu Tao,Yusheng Zheng,Yunhong Che*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环域生成框架，用于在动态条件下实现跨域电池健康诊断，通过物理约束的域生成和贝叶斯优化来提高诊断的准确性，尤其是在新目标领域缺乏足够数据的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验室条件下开发的电池健康诊断模型在实际运行条件变化时会失效，而获取新领域的全生命周期数据既耗时又不可行。因此，需要一种能够适应动态条件并解决数据稀缺问题的跨域诊断方法。

Method: 提出一个闭环域生成框架。首先，使用基于变分自编码器的潜空间外插（Variational Autoencoder-based latent extrapolation）来实现物理约束的域生成，将源域和目标域的少量早期数据映射到共享潜空间，并生成物理边界内的跨域样本。然后，利用贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization）来自动校准采样、潜空间正则化、外插、扰动和生成数量等超参数，实现了生成-诊断的闭环控制，并严格排除了后期目标数据。

Result: 在四个跨域案例中，所提出的框架在正极活性材料损失、负极活性材料损失和锂库存损失的诊断上，平均均方根误差分别为1.53%、2.15%和2.02%。相比之下，传统方法则表现出显著更高的误差或完全失效。

Conclusion: 该框架为独立于下游诊断模型的、车载的、符合物理规律的电极级退化诊断提供了一条通用途径，能够有效解决跨域电池健康诊断中的数据稀缺和条件变化问题。

Abstract: Reliable electrode-level degradation diagnosis is essential for accurate health estimation and non-destructive monitoring in advanced battery management. Diagnosis models developed under controlled laboratory conditions can fail when operating conditions change, while acquiring sufficient full-lifespan data in new target domains remains prohibitively time-consuming and practically unfeasible. Here, we propose a closed-loop domain generation framework to enable cross-domain battery health diagnosis under dynamic conditions. Physics-constrained domain generation is achieved through Variational Autoencoder-based latent extrapolation, which maps the source data and few-shot early-life target domain data into a shared latent space and generates cross-domain samples within physical boundaries. Bayesian optimization closes the generation-diagnosis loop by automatically calibrating the sampling, latent regularization, extrapolation, perturbation, and generation-volume hyperparameters using leakage-free development validation, thereby strictly excluding later-life target data from optimization and ensuring the quality and quantity of generated data. Numerical results demonstrate that, across four cross-domain cases, the proposed framework achieves average root mean square errors of 1.53%, 2.15%, and 2.02% for positive electrode active material loss, negative electrode active material loss, and lithium inventory loss, respectively, whereas traditional methods suffer from notably higher errors or even complete failure. This framework provides a general pathway for onboard and physics-consistent electrode-level degradation diagnosis, independent of downstream diagnosis models.

</details>


### [360] [Fast-DeePC: Fast Data-Enabled Predictive Control using Soft-constrained Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12902)
*Terry Zhang,Jan Drgona,Malika Sader,Di Liu,Liang Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Fast-DeePC的框架，通过引入二次松弛项，将DeePC中的硬等式约束转化为软目标，从而可以在离线阶段消除坐标向量，将在线求解问题简化为一个固定大小的二次规划问题，并利用内点法加速求解，比通用求解器快100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的DeePC方法虽然使用数据驱动预测，但其硬等式约束导致在线求解变量维度随着数据量的增加而增大，计算成本过高。

Method: Fast-DeePC通过在控制目标中引入对坐标向量的二次松弛项，取代稀疏惩罚，将一致性约束转化为软目标。这使得坐标向量可以在离线阶段被消除，将在线问题简化为一个固定大小的、带箱约束的二次规划问题，并采用利用结构特性的内点法进行求解。

Result: 在相同的软二次正则化问题上，Fast-DeePC方法得到的结果与通用求解器一致，并且求解速度比通用求解器快了两个数量级。

Conclusion: Fast-DeePC框架有效地解决了DeePC在线计算成本随数据增长的问题，通过数学上的巧妙处理，实现了计算效率的大幅提升，同时保持了控制性能。

Abstract: Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) replaces the explicit system model of model predictive control with a data-driven predictor built directly from recorded input-output trajectories, but the original hard-equality formulation keeps its coordinate vector as an online decision variable whose size grows with the offline data length, so the online problem grows more expensive as more data is used to enrich the behavioral representation. This article proposes Fast-DeePC, a framework which relaxes this consistency relation into the control objective with a quadratic ridge on the coordinate vector in place of the customary sparsity-promoting penalty, admitting a closed-form, offline elimination of the coordinate vector and reducing the online problem to a fixed-size box-constrained quadratic program solved by a structure-exploiting interior-point method. On identical soft quadratically regularized problems, the result matches a general-purpose solver's solution and is up to two orders of magnitude faster.

</details>


### [361] [SecFD-FL: Frequency-Decoupled Federated Learning with Secure Aggregation for Charging Load Forecasting in Internet of Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12909)
*Mamunur Rashid,Nan Chen,Syed Ali Asad Rizvi,Amiya Nayak*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SecFD-FL的联邦学习框架，用于解决电动汽车充电负荷预测中的数据随机性和隐私敏感性问题。该框架结合了双流GRU网络来分别建模短期和长期负荷波动，并采用轻量级的安全聚合层（基于零和加法掩蔽协议）来保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车充电负荷预测对于互联网电动汽车（IoEV）的可靠性和效率至关重要。然而，充电需求的高度随机性和分布式传感器收集数据的隐私敏感性给预测带来了挑战。现有的联邦学习（FL）虽然保护数据隐私，但其协作训练过程仍易受诚实但好奇的服务器攻击，服务器可能通过模型更新重构私有数据。

Method: SecFD-FL框架包含两部分：1. 双流门控循环单元（GRU）网络，独立建模短期和长期充电负荷的波动性。2. 轻量级的安全聚合层，采用零和加法掩蔽协议。该协议通过向每个客户端的上传数据添加高方差噪声来进行扰动，从而防止服务器重构私有数据，同时又能精确地保留聚合结果。

Result: 在EV级和充电站级的真实数据集上进行的大量实验表明，SecFD-FL将全局RMSE降低了高达60%（相较于集中式基线），实现了高达0.991的Jain公平性，并将DLG攻击的重构相关性降低了8到15倍（相较于FedAvg）。

Conclusion: SecFD-FL成功地在保护数据隐私的同时，有效地解决了电动汽车充电负荷预测中的数据波动性问题，且通信成本没有额外增加。

Abstract: Accurate charging load forecasting is essential for the reliability and efficiency of the Internet of Electric Vehicles (IoEV), yet it is challenging on two fronts: charging demand is highly stochastic, and the data collected by distributed sensors is privacy-sensitive. Although federated learning (FL) preserves data privacy, collaborative training still remains vulnerable to an honest-but-curious server that can invert shared model updates to reconstruct private data. To address both data volatility and privacy concerns, we propose SecFD-FL, a frequency-decoupled federated learning framework for IoEV comprising: 1) a dual-stream gated recurrent unit (GRU) network that separately models short-term and long-term charging load volatility, and 2) a lightweight secure aggregation layer for privacy preservation. Specifically, the secure aggregation layer employs a zero-sum additive masking protocol that perturbs each client's upload with high-variance noise, preventing the server from reconstructing private data while preserving the exact aggregate. Extensive experiments have been conducted on both EV-level and charging station-level using real datasets. The experiment results show that SecFD-FL reduces global RMSE by up to 60% over centralized baselines, achieving Jain's fairness up to 0.991 across clients, and reduces DLG-attack reconstruction correlation by 8 times to 15 times relative to FedAvg -- all with no additional per-round communication cost beyond a one-time key exchange.

</details>


### [362] [Virtual Transmission Lines from Data Center Workload Migration: Coordinated Multi-Year Transmission Expansion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13032)
*Mengxin Zhu,Qian Kai,Yadong Liu,Yingjie Yan,Haoyu Zhao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种将地理工作负载迁移建模为虚拟输电线路（VTL）的方法，以解决AI数据中心快速增长的电力需求与电网扩建缓慢之间的时间和空间不匹配问题。通过混合整数线性规划模型，研究人员协同优化了物理输电线路建设和VTL工作负载迁移，以降低总系统成本、未服务的数据中心负荷和可再生能源弃电量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心电力需求激增，导致电力系统中快速增长的需求与缓慢的输电网络扩建之间存在时间上的不匹配，同时数据中心集中的负荷与分散的可再生能源之间存在空间上的不匹配。这种不匹配会加剧输电拥堵，导致数据中心负荷无法满足和可再生能源被弃用。

Method: 研究将地理工作负载迁移建模为虚拟输电线路（VTL），不传输物理电力，只改变节点处数据中心的需求。开发了一个混合整数线性规划模型，以多年为周期，协同优化候选线路建设和工作负载迁移。模型考虑了建设延迟、可再生能源弃电、未服务的数据中心负荷、工作负载平衡要求、通信带宽限制和链路延迟限制等约束。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点系统上进行的八年规划期评估显示，将VTL与物理输电扩建相结合，能够降低总系统成本、未服务的数据中心负荷和可再生能源弃电量，同时缓解输电拥堵。VTL感知的规划比仅建设规划少用一条候选线路。然而，单独的VTL无法满足系统需求，也不能完全取代物理输电增强的必要性。敏感性分析表明，在输电能力紧张、建设成本高、负荷增长快和数据中心灵活性更高的情况下，VTL的价值最大。

Conclusion: 将地理工作负载迁移（VTL）与物理输电网络扩建相结合是一种有效的方式，可以应对AI数据中心日益增长的电力需求带来的挑战。VTL能够在不进行物理输电建设的情况下，通过调整数据中心负荷来优化电力系统运行，降低成本和提高可靠性，但它不能替代必要的物理输电基础设施的投资。

Abstract: The rapid growth of electricity demand from AI data centers is creating a dual mismatch in power systems: a temporal mismatch between fast-rising demand and slow transmission expansion, and a spatial mismatch between concentrated data-center loads and geographically dispersed renewable resources. These mismatches can exacerbate transmission congestion, resulting in unserved data-center load and renewable-energy curtailment. This study models geographic workload migration as a virtual transmission line (VTL) that changes nodal data-center demand without transferring physical electricity. A mixed-integer linear programming model is then developed to co-optimize multi-year candidate-line construction and workload migration, subject to construction delays, renewable-energy curtailment, unserved data-center load, workload-balance requirements, communication-bandwidth constraints, and link-latency limits. The model is evaluated over an eight-year planning horizon on a modified IEEE 14-bus system. The results show that coordinating VTL with physical transmission expansion reduces total system cost, unserved data-center load, and renewable-energy curtailment while alleviating transmission congestion. VTL-aware planning also requires one fewer candidate line than build-only planning. However, VTL alone cannot meet system demand and does not eliminate the need for physical transmission reinforcement. Sensitivity analysis indicates that VTL provides the greatest value when transfer capacity is tight, construction costs are high, load growth is rapid, and data-center flexibility is greater. Additional validation on the IEEE 118-bus system over a five-year planning horizon shows that coordinated planning retains its cost and reliability benefits while reducing physical expansion requirements.

</details>


### [363] [Extended Neighborhood Message Passing for Inference in Loopy Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13146)
*Kuilong Yang,Zengfu Wang,Hua Lan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为扩展邻域消息传递（ENMP）的新算法，用于解决概率图模型中信念传播（BP）在存在环时收敛性和准确性差的问题，特别是对高斯情况下的方差估计不准确的问题。ENMP能够自动构建变量节点的扩展邻域，捕获环路引入的相关性，无需手动设计区域。该方法适用于一般分布，并可在精度-复杂度之间进行权衡，在高斯情况下还能实现精确的方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的信念传播（BP）算法在处理具有环路的因子图时，常出现不收敛和准确性差的问题，特别是在高斯情况下，方差估计不准确。现有方法如广义BP（GBP）需要手动设计或依赖特定启发式方法来构建区域图。

Method: 提出扩展邻域消息传递（ENMP）算法。ENMP通过分析二分因子图的拓扑结构，自动为变量节点构建扩展邻域，以捕获环路效应。该方法适用于一般分布，并具有可调的精度-复杂度权衡。对于高斯因子图，推导了闭式更新规则，允许在精确模式下进行精确的方差估计。

Result: ENMP在精度-复杂度之间提供了权衡。在精确模式下，消息更新能收敛到ENMP方程的唯一固定点，从而获得精确的边缘概率。在近似模式下，ENMP提供近似边缘概率。对于高斯因子图，ENMP能够实现精确的方差估计。在电力系统和离散Ising模型等具有密集环路的示例上的评估表明，ENMP的准确性随邻域大小的增加而提高，并且在某些系统上能够获得精确的边缘概率。在较大的高斯系统上，ENMP在均值和方差估计方面均优于正则化高斯BP（sGaBP-m）。

Conclusion: ENMP是一种能够自动构建扩展邻域以解决BP算法在处理环路问题时的不足的有效方法。它在精度和计算复杂度之间提供了灵活性，并且在高斯因子图上实现了精确的方差估计，克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Belief propagation (BP) is widely used for inference on probabilistic graphical models but often suffers from non-convergence and poor accuracy on loopy factor graphs, yielding inexact variance estimates in the Gaussian case. Generalized BP (GBP) improves accuracy but relies on manual design or application-specific heuristics for region-graph construction. We propose extended neighborhood message passing (ENMP), which automatically constructs extended neighborhoods of variable nodes from the bipartite factor graph topology, capturing loop-induced correlations without manual region design. The framework applies to general distributions on factor graphs with a tunable accuracy-complexity trade-off. In the exact regime, message updates converge to the unique fixed point of the ENMP equations in finitely many iterations, yielding exact marginals; in the approximate regime, ENMP provides approximate marginals. For Gaussian factor graphs, we derive closed-form update rules enabling exact variance estimation in the exact regime, addressing the long-standing inaccurate variance estimates of loopy Gaussian BP. We evaluate ENMP on densely loopy Gaussian factor graphs from power-grid benchmarks and on a discrete Ising factor graph. Results show accuracy improves with neighborhood size, with exact marginals achieved on several systems in both settings. On larger Gaussian systems, ENMP outperforms regularized Gaussian BP (sGaBP-m) in both mean and variance estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [364] [Modelling the Nordic power system expansion with PyPSA-Eur: a comparison with the Nordic Grid Development Perspective 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13167)
*Leonardo Rydin Gorjão,Heidi S. Nygård*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究比较了北欧电网发展视角（NGDP）与PyPSA-Eur模型在零碳目标下2025-2050年的低成本转型路径，发现在可再生能源扩张方面基本一致，但在氢能的作用和部署时间上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估北欧地区实现净零碳排放的电力系统转型路径，并理解不同规划方法（NGDP和低成本优化）在可再生能源和氢能部署上的差异。

Method: 使用PyPSA-Eur模型，在净零碳排放的约束下，联合优化发电、储能和输电的容量扩展，并结合小时级系统运行调度。模型约束了北欧发电结构需与NGDP的里程碑范围保持一致。

Result: 两种路径均支持大规模发展风能和太阳能，其中丹麦是海上风电和太阳能的主要部署地，芬兰是陆上风电的主要扩张地，挪威和瑞典更依赖现有水力发电和核电。主要分歧在于氢能的部署时机和作用：NGDP设想2030年起氢能即作为工业需求和系统发展的重要组成部分，而PyPSA-Eur模型则将大规模氢能部署推迟到2045-2050年，此时氢能主要作为应对强风能和太阳能波动的灵活性和季节性储能选项。

Conclusion: 早期氢能的部署并非纯粹成本最优化的必然结果，而是很大程度上取决于工业需求增长、部门耦合和前瞻性基础设施规划的假设。氢能是北欧转型中的主要战略不确定性。NGDP代表了更具前瞻性的规划视角，而PyPSA-Eur则代表了更保守的成本优化部署时机。

Abstract: We compare the Nordic Grid Development Perspective 2025 (NGDP) with a least-cost transition pathway for the Nordic power system obtained with PyPSA-Eur for the period 2025-2050 under a net-zero CO$_2$ constraint. The model co-optimises generation, storage, and transmission expansion with hourly system operation while constraining the Nordic generation mix to remain consistent with NGDP milestone ranges. Overall, the optimised capacity pathways reproduce the broad NGDP expansion of wind and solar power, with Denmark emerging as the main locus of offshore wind and solar deployment, Finland as a major site for onshore wind expansion, and Norway and Sweden relying more strongly on existing hydropower and nuclear capacity. The main discrepancy between the two pathways does not lie in the broad build-out of variable renewables, but in the timing and role of hydrogen. Whereas the NGDP treats hydrogen as an early and steadily increasing component of industrial demand and system development already from 2030, PyPSA-Eur delays substantial hydrogen deployment until 2045-2050, when it becomes cost-effective primarily as a flexibility and seasonal storage option in a strongly variable-renewable-energy-dominated system. This contrast suggests that early hydrogen build-out in the Nordics is not a robust outcome of cost optimisation alone, but depends strongly on assumptions regarding industrial demand growth, sector coupling, and anticipatory infrastructure planning. We therefore argue that hydrogen remains the principal strategic uncertainty in the Nordic transition, with the NGDP representing a more anticipatory planning perspective and PyPSA-Eur a more conservative least-cost timing of deployment.

</details>


### [365] [Minimum-Energy Control for Stochastic Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13424)
*Vaishnavi Sharma,Vaibhav Katewa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究随机线性时不变系统中的开环最小能量控制（MEC）问题，提出随机MEC（SMEC）问题，并将其转化为二次约束二次规划（QCQP）问题，给出了最优解，并引入了新的随机可控性和Gramian矩阵概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在考虑了多种随机约束以确保终端状态误差足够小时，研究开环MEC问题。

Method: 将SMEC问题公式化为QCQP问题，并推导出其最优解。引入了随机可控性和Gramian矩阵的变体。

Result: SMEC问题的最优解被导出，并且该问题被证明是确定性MEC问题的推广。发现了存在一个无法通过增加时间范围来克服的最优控制能量的下界。

Conclusion: 随机线性时不变系统的SMEC问题可以通过QCQP求解，并且存在一个固有的最优控制能量下界。

Abstract: The open-loop minimum-energy control (MEC) problem asks for control inputs that minimize the control energy while ensuring the terminal state reaches a desired value. In this paper, we study the open-loop MEC problem for stochastic linear time-invariant systems. We consider a variety of stochastic constraints that enforce the terminal error to be \emph{small}, and show that Stochastic MEC (SMEC) problem can be formulated as a QCQP. We obtain the optimal solution for SMEC and show that it generalizes the well-known Deterministic MEC problem by introducing novel stochastic variants of controllability and Gramian matrices. Finally, we show that there exists a fundamental lower bound on the optimal control energy which cannot be overcome by increasing the time horizon of the problem.

</details>


### [366] [Adaptive Incentive Design for Linear-Quadratic Dynamic Games: No-Regret Social Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13448)
*Lantian Zhang,Yibei Li,Georgios Vasileiou,Lihua Xie,Silun Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种自适应激励设计框架，用于在多主体线性二次动态博弈中，规划者可以同时学习主体的偏好并引导其行为以实现社会最优。该框架通过结合偏好学习和激励机制，实现了最优策略的执行和预算平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理人偏好未知的情况下，如何设计激励机制以使私人利益与集体目标一致，同时又能学习和引导代理人的行为，是该研究的动机。

Method: 研究首先确定了实现社会最优的仿射激励族，并确保了预算平衡。然后，开发了一种在线算法来估计代理人的偏好参数，并在弱激励条件下证明了估计的一致性。最后，将该估计器集成到一个结合确定性等效协调和衰减随机探测的主动学习激励机制中，并证明了该机制能通过代理人的均衡响应和系统动力学来学习偏好，从而使轨迹趋向最优。

Result: 证明了所提出的框架能够几乎肯定地使诱导轨迹收敛到社会最优轨迹，同时与完全信息预言家相比，累积社会成本遗憾和累积净预算不平衡均呈亚线性增长。仿真结果也证明了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新颖的自适应激励设计框架，能够在未知偏好的情况下，通过同时学习代理人偏好和激励其行为，实现动态博弈中的社会最优。该框架通过在线学习和主动探索相结合，有效地解决了偏好未知和学习困难的问题。

Abstract: Incentives can align private interests with collective goals, but their design becomes intertwined with learning when agents' preferences are unknown. This paper develops an adaptive incentive-design framework for multi-agent linear-quadratic dynamic games, where a system planner can simultaneously learn agents' private preferences and steer their strategic behavior towards the social optimum. To this end, we first characterize the complete family of affine incentives that implement the social optimum as the unique open-loop Nash equilibrium while ensuring exact budget balance. When agents' preferences are unknown, we develop an online algorithm to estimate their preference parameters and establish strong consistency of the estimates under a weak trajectory-dependent excitation condition, without requiring persistent excitation or i.i.d. trajectories. We then integrate this estimator into an active-learning incentive mechanism that combines certainty-equivalent coordination with decaying random probing. We prove that the probing generates sufficient information through the agents' equilibrium responses and the system dynamics, ensuring consistent preference learning from the resulting closed-loop trajectories. We show that the induced trajectories converge almost surely to their socially optimal counterparts, while cumulative social-cost regret relative to the full-information oracle and cumulative net-budget imbalance grow sublinearly. Simulations of a transboundary pollution game demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [367] [Hierarchical Graph Neural Network for Probabilistic Power Flow with Grid Topology Contingencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13495)
*Jinbao Wang,Jun Liu,Shiliang Zhang,Xuehui Ma,Lin Yan,Haolin Liu,Sabita Maharjan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种拓扑分解驱动的层级双向图神经网络（TDD-HBGNN），用于处理在电网拓扑发生变化（如设备故障）后，概率性潮流计算（PPF）的泛化能力不足的问题。该模型通过分解电网、捕捉低阶响应和学习高阶非线性残差，显著提高了计算精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动PPF模型在电网拓扑因设备故障而改变时，泛化能力差。需要一种能够适应不同电网拓扑的PPF方法。

Method: 将电网分解为基本拓扑单元（FTUs）、动态同质电压簇（DHVCs）和核心骨干子网（CBSG）。利用等效导纳表示单元间的连接，并结合基于灵敏度的线性先验模型和拓扑相关的非线性残差学习。通过自下而上的聚合和自顶向下的广播来处理局部扰动和系统全局信息。

Result: 在IEEE 118总线和Polish 2746总线系统上，TDD-HBGNN在N-1和N-2故障场景下，相角误差比基线方法低2.47-4.20倍，并且在所有测试故障中相角误差均更低。电压幅值误差在故障支路附近有显著改善。在联合电压-相角误差方面，TDD-HBGNN在所有对比中均占优。模型参数量少（0.658M），推理速度快（0.161-1.793 ms/case）。

Conclusion: TDD-HBGNN能够有效地处理未知的电网拓扑，在系统相角精度、故障局部电压精度以及参数效率和模型扩展性方面均表现出色。

Abstract: Data-driven surrogates reduce the nonlinear solves required for probabilistic power flow (PPF), but models tied to a fixed bus--branch graph often generalize poorly when outages change electrical coupling. This paper proposes a topology-decomposition-driven hierarchical bidirectional graph neural network (TDD--HBGNN) for PPF under unseen topologies. For each realized topology, the network is decomposed into fundamental topological units (FTUs), dynamically homogeneous voltage clusters (DHVCs), and a core backbone sub-grid (CBSG), with equivalent-admittance representations preserving electrical ports between units. A sensitivity-based linear prior captures the first-order voltage-magnitude and phase-angle response, while the HBGNN learns topology-dependent nonlinear residuals. Impedance- and uncertainty-aware bottom-up aggregation condenses local perturbations, while mode-modulated top-down broadcasting restores system context at buses. We evaluate our approach on the IEEE 118-bus and Polish 2746-bus systems under unseen N--1 and N--2 contingencies against six baseline methods. Compared with these methods, TDD--HBGNN achieves 2.47--4.20-fold lower all-bus phase-angle errors and lower phase-angle errors in every held-out event. Its voltage-magnitude advantage is concentrated near the outaged branches, where paired error ratios reach 1.75--2.58 on the IEEE system and 1.87--1.97 in Polish N--2 events. All system--contingency--baseline comparisons favor TDD--HBGNN in joint voltage--angle error. Moreover, shared hierarchical operators keep the model size at 0.658 million parameters across both grids and yield 0.161--1.793~ms forward latency per probabilistic case. These results demonstrate system-wide angular accuracy, outage-local voltage accuracy, and parameter-efficient scaling across unseen topologies.

</details>


### [368] [Learning-Based Power Flow Estimation with Sparse PMU Deployment in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13683)
*Van-Hai Bui,Quang Manh Hoang,Akhtar Hussain,Wencong Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的框架，通过选择性部署PMU来在降低成本的同时实现高精度的系统级潮流估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动的潮流估计方法需要密集部署PMU，成本高昂。本研究旨在找到一种在保证精度的前提下，最小化PMU部署数量的方法。

Method: 开发了一个神经网络来学习PMU测量值与系统潮流之间的非线性关系，并识别出能够提供足够信息的PMU子集，从而重建整个网络的支路潮流。

Result: 所提出的框架显著减少了所需的PMU数量，同时保持了准确的潮流估计。通过对IEEE 30总线测试系统的案例研究，量化了测量可用性与估计精度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为电力公司提供了一种在部署成本和预测性能之间取得平衡的成本效益型PMU选址策略。

Abstract: Accurate monitoring of power flow is fundamental to the secure and reliable operation of modern power systems. Existing data-driven power flow estimation methods typically assume that synchronized measurements are available at every bus, requiring extensive deployment of phasor measurement units (PMUs). Such dense sensing infrastructures substantially increase installation, communication, and maintenance costs. This paper proposes a learning-based framework that minimizes the number of installed PMUs while maintaining high-accuracy systemwide power flow estimation. Instead of relying on measurements from all buses, the proposed method identifies an informative subset of buses whose synchronized measurements are sufficient to accurately reconstruct branch power flow across the network. To this end, a neural network is developed to learn the nonlinear relationship between PMU measurements and system-wide power flows, and multiple PMU deployment scenarios are evaluated to quantify the trade-off between measurement availability and estimation accuracy. The results enable utilities to identify cost-effective PMU placement strategies that balance deployment cost and prediction performance. Case studies on the IEEE 30-bus test system demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly reduces the number of required PMUs while maintaining accurate power flow estimation.

</details>


### [369] [Direct Vector Field Guidance for Path Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13576)
*Amit Shivam,Nikhil Kumar Singh,Luís Tiago Paiva,Fernando A. C. C. Fontes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于有转弯速率限制的无人机的路径跟踪制导框架，该框架使用命令航向角作为侧向偏差的双曲正切整形函数，并通过李雅普诺夫分析证明了渐近收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是为具有转弯速率限制的无人机设计一种可靠且易于实现的路径跟踪制导方法，并与现有方法进行比较。

Method: 采用双曲正切函数来塑造命令航向角，以应对侧向偏差。使用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论证明了该方法的收敛性。推导了转弯速率的解析表达式及其最大值界限，用于直接增益选择。通过数值模拟在不同初始航向条件下进行验证，并与基于arctan、arcsine和arccos的矢量场制导律进行比较。

Result: 该方法能够实现可靠的边界收敛，并保持平滑的转向行为。提出的增益设计框架紧凑且具有解析性。数值模拟结果表明，该方法在各种条件下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于tanh的路径跟踪制导框架为具有转弯速率限制的无人机提供了一种有效的解决方案，具有稳定的性能、平滑的控制和简化的增益设计。

Abstract: This paper presents a path-following guidance framework for a turn-rate-constrained unmanned aerial vehicle (UAVs). The proposed guidance strategy uses commanded course angle as a tanh-hyperbolic shaping function of the lateral deviation from the desired path. Stability characteristics illustrating asymptotic convergence to the desired path are established using Lyapunov analysis. Analytical expressions for the turn-rate and corresponding maximum turn-rate bound are derived, enabling direct gain selection for a turn-rate-limited vehicle. Numerical simulations are performed for straight-line following scenarios under both ideal and non-ideal initial heading conditions. Furthermore, a comparative study against representative arctan-, arcsine-, and arccos-based vector-field guidance laws is conducted using analytically feasible gains selected from their respective turn-rate bounds. Comparative numerical results demonstrate the proposed formulation achieves reliable boundary convergence while maintaining smooth steering behavior and a compact analytical gain-design framework.

</details>


### [370] [Information-Weighted Distributed Kalman Filter for Multi-Target Tracking over Switching Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13893)
*Tirthankar Chakraborty,Arnab Maity*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种信息加权分布式卡尔曼滤波器（IWDKF），用于解决传感器网络中目标跟踪的挑战，该网络具有有限的感知覆盖范围和随时间变化的通信拓扑，并且无需全局网络参数知识，通信开销低，即使某些传感器无法直接观测到目标也能进行信息融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式滤波方法在目标跟踪中存在局限性，例如要求传感器或其邻居直接观测目标，依赖固定的网络拓扑、全局网络参数或多轮通信，这些限制了其在广域网络中的应用。

Method: 提出一种信息加权分布式卡尔曼滤波器（IWDKF），采用加权共识机制，适用于任意切换拓扑，前提是满足联合连通性。该方法不需要全局网络属性知识，每个时间步仅需一次共识交换，并允许在部分传感器未直接观测到目标的情况下进行信息融合。

Result: 证明了加权共识协方差矩阵保持正定，估计更新保持一致性。该加权策略在联合连通性下保留了基础共识协议的渐近一致性。仿真结果表明，IWDKF在固定和切换网络拓扑下均能提高估计精度，性能接近集中式卡尔曼滤波器。

Conclusion: IWDKF是一种有效的分布式目标跟踪方法，能够克服现有方法的局限性，在通信受限和网络拓扑变化的情况下仍能实现高性能的目标跟踪。

Abstract: Target tracking over wide areas using a network of sensor agents is a challenging problem when agents have limited sensing coverage and communication topologies vary over time. Existing distributed filtering approaches commonly assume that each agent, or at least one of its neighbors, can directly observe the target, and further rely on fixed network topologies, global network parameters, or multiple consensus rounds per time step. These requirements limit their applicability in wide-area network deployments. In this paper, we propose an Information-Weighted Distributed Kalman Filter (IWDKF) that relaxes these assumptions through a weighted-consensus mechanism applicable to arbitrary switching topologies satisfying joint connectivity. The proposed approach requires no prior knowledge of global network properties and limits communication overhead to a single consensus exchange per time step, while enabling information fusion even when some agents lack direct target measurements. The weighted-consensus covariance matrix is shown to remain positive definite, and the resulting consensus update is proven to maintain estimate consistency. It is also established that the proposed weighting strategy preserves the asymptotic agreement property of the underlying consensus protocol under joint connectivity. Numerical simulations of a multi-target tracking scenario involving a network of sensor agents demonstrate the improved estimation accuracy of the proposed IWDKF under both fixed and switching network topologies. The performance of the proposed filter is close to that of the centralized Kalman filter, and is compared with three existing distributed filters.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [371] [Ultrasound and Magnetic Resonance Elastography of the Neck: Biomechanical Basis, Clinical Applications and Methodological Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12584)
*Rashedul Hasan,Anna Yang,Kishani Kandasamy,Hossein Bahreinizad,Md Ashikuzzaman,Suman K. Chowdhury,Shahed Mohammed*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本综述整合了颈部组织力学特性、相关临床疾病以及超声弹性成像和磁共振弹性成像（MRE）在颈部的应用，发现超声弹性成像对甲状腺结节和淋巴结有诊断价值，而颈部MRE尚处于早期阶段，并提出测量建议以提高疾病管理和计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床颈部疾病评估主要依赖形态学、触诊和症状，而忽视了颈部组织的力学特性。尽管超声弹性成像和MRE已可进行体内测量，但相关研究方法各异且分散。因此，需要整合现有知识并提出标准化建议。

Method: 该综述回顾了颈部组织力学行为的生物力学基础、改变其力学特性的临床疾病，以及超声弹性成像和MRE在颈部的原理和应用。通过对比分析不同研究的结果，提出测量建议。

Result: 超声弹性成像已在甲状腺结节和颈部淋巴结的诊断中显示出价值。颈部MRE的可行性已得到证实，但仍处于早期发展阶段。两种成像模态的测量结果高度依赖于纤维方向、姿势、肌肉激活和采集条件。

Conclusion: 颈部组织的力学特性对其功能和疾病状态至关重要。超声弹性成像和MRE是评估这些特性的有前景的工具。未来需要标准化报告建议，并探索超越刚度测量（如各向异性、粘度和非线性）的方法，以期改进疾病管理并为颈部计算模型提供信息。

Abstract: Many neck disorders, from chronic neck pain and headache to thyroid and nodal malignancy and radiation-induced fibrosis, both arise from and alter the mechanical properties of neck tissues, yet clinical assessment still relies on morphology, palpation and symptoms. Ultrasound and magnetic resonance elastography (MRE) now allow these properties to be measured in vivo, but the neck literature differs widely in methodology and remains dispersed across modalities and tissues. This review brings together the biomechanical basis of neck tissue behavior, the clinical conditions that alter it, and the principles and applications of ultrasound elastography and MRE in the neck. We show that ultrasound elastography has demonstrated diagnostic value for thyroid nodules and cervical lymph nodes, while neck MRE has been shown to be feasible but remains at an early stage. Across both modalities, reported values depend strongly on fiber orientation, posture, muscle activation and acquisition conditions. We therefore propose reporting recommendations and discuss how measurements beyond stiffness, including anisotropy, viscosity and nonlinearity, could improve disease management and inform computational models of the neck.

</details>


### [372] [Radiomics-Based Thyroid Nodule Classification with Label-Conditional Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13387)
*Laura Testa,Pierpaolo Brutti,Umberto Ferraro Petrillo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合影像组学特征提取和广义相加模型（GAM）分类器的甲状腺结节恶性评估框架，并引入交叉保序预测（Cross-Conformal Prediction）来量化不确定性，以减少不必要的活检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的甲状腺结节恶性风险分层系统因观察者间变异性高，导致不必要的活检率居高不下，促使研究者寻求更客观、可重复的评估方法。

Method: 该研究采用PyRadiomics提取定量影像组学特征，并使用广义相加模型（GAM）进行分类。为提供不确定性估计，应用了具有标签条件保证的交叉保序预测，以识别模棱两可的病例。

Result: 在TN5000数据集上，筛选后的36个特征和GAM模型在验证集上取得了0.89的AUC、0.83的敏感性和0.82的特异性。在测试集上，63%的结节得到确定性预测，其中恶性确定性预测的条件精确率为0.94，良性确定性预测的条件阴性预测值为0.73。

Conclusion: 基于影像组学特征和GAM的框架结合交叉保序预测，能够有效减少不必要的活检，并通过提供不确定性估计，将需要临床进一步评估的病例（37%）有效地转交给临床医生，提高了评估的准确性和临床决策的可靠性。

Abstract: Thyroid nodule malignancy assessment relies on ultrasound imaging, yet current risk stratification systems suffer from substantial interobserver variability, contributing to a high rate of unnecessary biopsies. We propose a radiomics-based framework combining quantitative feature extraction via PyRadiomics with a Generalized Additive Model (GAM) classifier, selected among six candidates for its favorable trade-off between discriminative performance and interpretability. To complement point predictions with uncertainty estimates, Cross-Conformal Prediction with label-conditional guarantees is applied, flagging ambiguous cases for clinical review rather than forcing a binary decision. Experiments are conducted on TN5000, a publicly available ultrasound dataset of thyroid nodules. After Spearman correlation filtering, 36 features are retained and the GAM achieves an AUC of $0.89$, a sensitivity of $0.83$, and a specificity of $0.82$ on the validation set. At a significance level $ε=0.09$, selected to ensure empirical coverage guarantees on both classes, 63\% of nodules on the test set receive a certain (i.e., single-label) prediction, with conditional precision of $0.94$ for certain malignant and conditional Negative Predictive Value (NPV) of $0.73$ for certain benign cases, while the remaining 37\% are referred to the clinician.

</details>


### [373] [Stochastic Finite-Transition Learning and Exploration for Accelerated MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13095)
*Muhammad Usama Mirza,Esin Öztürk Işık,Tolga Çukur*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: FLEX-MRI 提出了一种新的生成式MRI重建方法，通过将重建过程划分为有限的阶段，并直接学习每个阶段的变化，从而在保持高重建质量的同时提高了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式MRI重建方法（如扩散和流模型）需要大量的网络评估，计算成本高。少步采样方法虽然降低了计算负担，但牺牲了重建保真度。因此，研究需要一种兼顾效率和保真度的方法。

Method: FLEX-MRI 将重建过程划分为一系列由k空间支撑变化定义的有限区间。它直接学习每个区间内的重建变化，结合了支持松弛的有限状态转移学习和随机探索。训练时，随机k空间采样掩码定义了具有不同支持的有限状态转移目标。推理时，通过扰动k空间系数来随机探索替代的转移路径，并通过数据一致性与测量值进行协调。

Result: FLEX-MRI 在多线圈MRI数据集上进行了实验，结果表明其性能优于现有方法，同时计算效率与少步基线方法相当。

Conclusion: FLEX-MRI 成功地解决了生成式MRI重建中的效率与保真度之间的权衡问题，通过新颖的阶段化学习和随机探索机制，实现了高效且高质量的MRI重建。

Abstract: Generative MRI reconstruction maps an initial source distribution to the target image distribution along a learned recovery trajectory. Common generative methods, such as diffusion and flow, typically learn fine-grained local dynamics whose integration defines this global mapping, hence requiring many network evaluations at inference. Existing few-step sampling approaches can lower computational burden by approximating large transformations from local dynamics, albeit at the expense of reconstruction fidelity. FLEX-MRI instead partitions recovery into a small number of finite intervals between retained states defined by progressive changes in k-space support, and directly learns the reconstruction change across each interval. Because such finite transitions can admit multiple plausible outcomes, FLEX-MRI combines support-relaxed finite-transition learning with stochastic exploration. During training, randomized k-space sampling masks define finite-transition targets with varying support, while the prediction loss remains unrestricted by each individual mask. At inference, FLEX-MRI perturbs a random subset of currently estimated k-space coefficients, allowing the learned predictor to stochastically explore alternative transition paths. Each predicted state is then reconciled with the acquired measurements through data consistency. We theoretically show that this stochastic exploration induces nonzero transition-level variation through the learned recovery map and data consistency without requiring an auxiliary generative sampler. Experiments on multi-coil MRI datasets show that FLEX-MRI outperforms competing methods while maintaining computational efficiency comparable to few-step baselines.

</details>


### [374] [The Predictability Bias of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13237)
*Fabian Drexel,Bernhard Kainz,Jakob N. Kather,Daniel Rueckert,Lisa C. Adams,Keno K. Bressem,Cosmin I. Bercea*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究提出的TAP-JEPA模型在CT图像中学习时，发现预测性目标（如解剖结构）虽然易于从上下文中预测，但无法捕捉到临床诊断的关键（如病灶）。实验表明，模型在学习过程中会过度关注可预测的解剖结构，而模糊了重要的病灶信息。因此，在医学图像表示学习中，应避免以可预测性作为代理目标，并保留诊断相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在学习时通过预测被遮掩区域的教师表示，但这种方法存在“可预测性偏差”，即对于上下文中无法完全确定的变化（如CT中的病灶）无法有效学习。CT图像中，解剖结构易于预测，而病灶则不然，这阻碍了模型的临床诊断能力。

Method: 作者提出TAP-JEPA，一个基于ViT-B/3D的模型，在113,611个CT影像上进行训练。通过重放训练过程中的遮掩、教师和预测器计算，审计了学习到的预测路径。分析了预测对解剖结构和病灶特征的影响，并评估了移除病灶相关token对下游任务的影响。

Result: TAP-JEPA在扫描级分类任务上表现与SOTA模型相当。然而，在预测过程中，病灶的Skill值从0.388降低到0.271，而解剖结构的Skill值仅从0.921降低到0.873。预测使得病灶特征向对照组偏移34.7%，对照组特征向病灶偏移13.6%。移除病灶token对下游任务的影响有限。

Conclusion: 在医学表示学习中，上下文的可预测性不足以作为临床相关性的代理目标。训练目标应保留诊断性特征，而不是过度依赖于其可预测性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn by predicting teacher representations of masked regions from visible context. Point prediction summarizes the conditional target distribution and cannot reproduce variation that the context does not resolve. We call this constraint predictability bias. In computed tomography (CT), recurrent anatomy is predictable from context, whereas the sporadic, localized pathology that determines clinical interpretation often is not. We introduce TAP-JEPA, a ViT-B/3D trained on 113,611 CT volumes that is competitive with state-of-the-art encoders (COLIPRI, Merlin, VoxelFM) in scan-level classification. We then audit the learned predictive pathway by replaying the exact masking, teacher, and predictor computations used during training. Prediction lowers the mean Skill (prevalence-normalized average precision) from 0.388 to 0.271 for pathology, but only from 0.921 to 0.873 for anatomy. Along the teacher direction, separating pathology from matched control regions in the same organ, prediction moves pathology 34.7% toward the controls and controls 13.6% toward pathology, with larger shifts farther from visible context. Replacing visible matched-control representations increases surrounding-target prediction loss more than fully replacing pathology representations across four pathology categories. Downstream, removing all final-layer pathology tokens reduces the decision margin by at most 0.45% in four of seven scan-level probes. Together, these results show that contextual predictability is not a sufficient proxy for clinical relevance in medical representation learning. Training objectives should therefore retain diagnostic features regardless of their predictability from surrounding anatomy.

</details>


### [375] [Desertification Forecasting in Semi-Arid Regions: Using Deep Learning to Probe the Observability Limits of Land Degradation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13406)
*Filipe Moura Da Silva,Cláubio Landney Lima Bandeira*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 研究使用卷积长短期记忆网络（ConvLSTM）结合十年地球观测数据，预测了巴西塞阿拉州荒漠化退化指数（IDV）的年际变化。结果表明，ConvLSTM在预测区域趋势方面优于基线模型，但对个体单元的预测能力有限，并引入了不确定性量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究侧重于绘制荒漠化地图，而对其进行预测的研究较少。本研究旨在探索使用地球观测数据和深度学习模型预测荒漠化演变的可能性，以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用卷积长短期记忆网络（ConvLSTM）模型，结合2000-2023年间10个年度地球观测（EO）变量，对巴西塞阿拉州约2.2公里分辨率的数据进行训练。模型采用两阶段训练（单步预测后进行递归预测），并在一块独立的时空区域进行评估。此外，还使用了梯度提升模型、蒙特卡洛 dropout 和随机情景图来评估和量化预测的不确定性。

Result: ConvLSTM模型在预测一年后IDV的RMSE相对持久性预测降低了1.6%，四年后降低了4.4%。模型能够捕捉到区域性的退化趋势，并复制了53-69%的区域性变化。单独的EO变量分析表明，海拔和EVI会降低预测精度，而坡度和坡向则无显著影响。在气候强迫下，预计到2030年平均IDV将从2023年的0.350升至0.370。

Conclusion: 基于地球观测数据的荒漠化预测对于区域总体趋势是可靠的，但对于单个单元的预测能力有限。ConvLSTM模型能够捕捉到主要的区域性退化趋势，并提供不确定性估计，为荒漠化监测和预警提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Forecasting desertification has received far less attention than mapping it. We use a convolutional LSTM (ConvLSTM) trained on ten annual Earth Observation (EO) variables (2000-2023) over the semi-arid state of Ceará, Brazil, on a 256x206 grid at roughly 2.2 km resolution, to measure how much of the year-to-year evolution of a MapBiomas-derived degradation index (IDV) can be predicted from satellite data. The network predicts a bounded change on top of the last observed state, is trained in two stages (single-step, then recursive rollout) and is evaluated on a contiguous spatial and temporal holdout. State-wide mean IDV rose from 0.238 to 0.350 between 2000 and 2023, about 0.005 per year, but cell-level change is dominated by year-to-year jumps, and a gradient-boosting model given all channels explains only 4.7% of its variance. Against this ceiling, the ConvLSTM lowers RMSE relative to persistence by 1.6% one year ahead and 4.4% four years ahead in a held-out southern band (p < 0.001), beats persistence with a constant drift and reproduces 53-69% of the regional drift. Drift-aware baselines only help from the third year onward, by up to 4.4% in RMSE at six years. An earlier formulation that redrew the whole field each year predicted changes unrelated to the observed ones (correlation -0.06) and produced a spurious decline when run recursively. Channel knockouts suggest that elevation and EVI degrade the forecast, while slope and aspect have no measurable effect. Monte Carlo dropout and stochastic scenario maps convey the uncertainty that the expected-value forecast hides. Under climatological forcing, mean IDV rises from 0.350 in 2023 to 0.370 in 2030. EO-based desertification forecasting is therefore reliable for the regional trend, not for individual cells.

</details>


### [376] [Model-agnostic Knowledge Augmented Machine Learning for Weakly Annotated Bioimaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13637)
*Jayanie Bogahawatte,Sachith Seneviratne,Simon Maksour,Dzung Do-Ha,Elizabeth Qian,Samuel Brennan,Matthew Keon,Lezanne Ooi,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的知识增强方法，用于提高弱标注生物影像数据的机器学习分类性能，特别是在TDP-43错位分析中，为理解肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）病理学及其潜在治疗方法提供了新的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自动化特征提取的机器学习方法在弱标注生物影像数据集上的分类性能不稳定，因此需要一种能够利用领域知识的方法来提高性能。

Method: 提出了一种模型无关的知识增强方法，并将其与掩码自编码器（MAE）进行结合，利用自监督学习，在TDP-43错位分析方面进行了评估。研究使用了三组独立的运动神经元显微镜数据集，这些数据集来源于人类诱导多能干细胞，用于研究与ALS相关的TDP-43核质错位表型。

Result: 该方法在七种不同的机器学习架构上均提高了分类性能。与MAE自监督学习结合后，在三组内部显微镜数据集和三个公共细胞形态学基准数据集上，其精确度分别提高了2.9%和平均6.8%。该模型在ALS相关TDP-43错位研究中发现了剂量依赖效应、散发性和家族性ALS捐赠者运动神经元病理的差异性，以及edaravone和niclosamide对SA诱导的TDP-43错位缺乏保护作用。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的知识增强流水线能够有效地进行体外细胞图像分析，从而深化对ALS相关病理学和潜在治疗方法的理解。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly used for automated feature extraction from weakly annotated bioimaging data, such as microscopy and cytomorphology images. Due to the sole dependency of automated feature extraction, existing methods yield inconsistent classification performance across weakly annotated datasets. Here, we propose a model-agnostic knowledge augmentation method that enables ML models to exploit domain knowledge beyond what automated feature extraction can capture. The proposed approach improves classification performance across seven ML architectures. When integrated with masked autoencoder-based self-supervised learning, our method achieves the strongest overall performance, improving precision by 2.9% over the next-best method on three in-house microscopy datasets and by an average of 6.8% across three public cytomorphology benchmarks. We perform nucleus-level classification based on mislocalization of TAR DNA-binding protein 43 (TDP-43) from the nucleus to the cytoplasm, a phenotype linked to Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis (ALS), using three independent microscopy datasets of motor neurons derived from human induced pluripotent stem cells. Accurate quantification of this phenotype at scale remains challenging and may contribute to inconsistencies across studies. Application of the best-performing model to these microscopy datasets reveals biological interpretations related to ALS, including dose-dependent effects of the proteasomal stressor, MG132, and the oxidative stressor, sodium arsenite (SA), on TDP-43 mislocalization, variable pathology in motor neurons from sporadic and familial ALS donors, and a lack of protection of the pharmacological compounds, edaravone and niclosamide, against SA-induced TDP-43 mislocalization. Together, our pipeline enables in-vitro cellular image analysis to advance understanding of ALS-associated pathology and potential treatments.

</details>


### [377] [A Miniaturized Wi-Fi HaLow-Based Wireless Imaging Transfer System for Capsule Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13815)
*Michele Bertozzi,Lorenzo Amelio,Sebastiano Strangio,Nikita J. Greenidge,Benjamin Calmé,Pietro Valdastri,Giuseppe Iannaccone Fellow,Alessandro Catania*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于Wi-Fi HaLow (IEEE 802.11ah) 的微型无线成像传输（WIT）系统，用于胶囊内窥镜，实现了稳定的高分辨率彩色图像无线传输，并验证了其在体内的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内窥镜在尺寸、功率和数据传输方面存在严重限制，尤其是在通过生物组织传输彩色图像方面。本研究旨在克服这些挑战，提高图像传输的实用性。

Method: 设计并实验验证了一个基于sub-GHz IEEE 802.11ah (Wi-Fi HaLow) 无线链路的微型WIT系统。并在临床相关的猪模型中进行了初步的体内验证，以评估Wi-Fi HaLow在穿透生物组织方面的通信稳定性。

Result: 系统实现了320x320像素分辨率的稳定无线图像传输，端到端数据速率超过12 Mbps，能量效率为20.7 nJ/bit。总功耗（包括相机、数字处理、RF传输和LED照明）约为477 mW，完整链式能耗约为39.6 nJ/bit。

Conclusion: 基于sub-GHz Wi-Fi HaLow的WIT系统为无线胶囊内窥镜提供了一种有前景的解决方案，在吞吐量、鲁棒性和能效之间取得了良好的平衡，并具有体内通信的潜力。

Abstract: Wireless capsule endoscopy is constrained by severe limitations in size, available power, and reliable data transmission through biological tissues. Although recent advances in miniature imaging sensors have improved image quality, the wireless transmission of color images at practical frame rates remains a challenging task under the tight capsule constraints.
  We present the design and the experimental validation of a miniaturized Wireless Imaging Transfer (WIT) system for capsule endoscopy based on a sub-GHz IEEE 802.11ah (Wi-Fi HaLow) wireless link. In addition, a preliminary in-vivo validation of Wi-Fi HaLow communication is reported, showing that a stable through-body wireless link can be established across biological tissues in a clinically relevant porcine model. These in-vivo results provide direct experimental support for the adoption of sub-GHz Wi-Fi HaLow for capsule endoscopy applications.
  Experimental results demonstrate stable wireless image transmission at a resolution of 320x320 pixels, with measured end-to-end data rates above 12 Mbps and a transmission energy efficiency of 20.7 nJ/bit. We also report a detailed power consumption analysis, with a breakdown of the contributions from the imaging sensor, illumination, digital processing, and RF transmission. When all active subsystem contributions are considered, including the camera, digital processing, RF transmission, and illumination at 60% LED brightness, the total power consumption is approximately 477 mW, corresponding to a complete-chain energy consumption of approximately 39.6 nJ/bit. Overall, the measured performance confirms that sub-GHz Wi-Fi HaLow provides a promising trade-off among throughput, robustness, and energy efficiency for wireless imaging in capsule endoscopy.

</details>


### [378] [Can Multimodal Decision Models Classify Breast Ultrasound Images? Benchmarking CLEF and CLEF-Flash Across Prompts and Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13675)
*Tran Cao Minh,Hai Tran,Ha Minh Tan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了 CLEF 和 CLEF-Flash 模型在乳腺超声图像分类任务中的表现，发现在不同数据集和提示词下，模型的准确率、F1分数、吞吐量和延迟等指标表现各异，CLEF-Flash在准确率上表现更优，但CLEF在某些指标上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估多模态决策模型（CLEF 和 CLEF-Flash）在乳腺超声图像分析中的应用潜力，并了解不同提示词和数据集对模型性能的影响。

Method: 在三个乳腺超声数据集（Dataset B, BUSI, BUS-BRA）上，对 CLEF 和 CLEF-Flash 模型进行了基准测试。使用了三种不同的提示词：最小标签、明确分类任务和超声标准。评估指标包括准确率、宏 F1 分数、平衡准确率、宏精确率、吞吐量和延迟。

Result: CLEF-Flash 在所有数据集上使用临床提示词时准确率最高。CLEF-Flash 的延迟比 CLEF 低。临床提示词能提高 CLEF-Flash 的准确率，但会降低 CLEF 的准确率。在 BUSI 数据集上，CLEF 使用任务提示词时获得最高的宏 F1 和平衡准确率。模型在区分良性和恶性病变时容易出错，尤其是在 BUS-BRA 数据集上。

Conclusion: 多模态决策模型在乳腺超声分析中的性能受数据集和提示词的影响很大。在应用这些模型之前，进行针对特定类别的评估至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal decision models return probabilities over predefined alternatives, offering a structured interface for image classification. This study benchmarks inference with CLEF and CLEF-Flash on 2818 breast ultrasound images from Dataset B, BUSI, and BUS-BRA. Dataset B and BUS-BRA use binary benign-malignant classification, whereas BUSI includes normal, benign, and malignant categories. Model parameters remain unchanged, and original images are submitted without user-defined preprocessing. Three prompts provide minimal labels, an explicit classification task, or sonographic criteria. We report accuracy, macro F1, balanced accuracy, macro precision, throughput, and latency. CLEF-Flash with the clinical prompt achieves the highest accuracy on all three datasets: 74.85% on Dataset B, 66.28% on BUSI, and 51.63% on BUS-BRA. On BUSI, however, CLEF with the task-guided prompt achieves the highest macro F1 (64.51%) and balanced accuracy (68.72%). CLEF-Flash has lower latency in every matched comparison. Clinical instructions improve CLEF-Flash accuracy relative to the minimal prompt but reduce CLEF accuracy on all datasets. Confusion matrices reveal frequent benign-to-malignant errors, particularly on BUS-BRA, where all configurations fall below the 67.63% accuracy of a majority-class predictor. These findings demonstrate dataset-and prompt-dependent performance and motivate class-specific evaluation before applying multimodal decision models to breast ultrasound analysis.

</details>
